We study continuous TTT in multi-turn agent episodes, where each update changes the policy that generates later training text. This creates a self-training loop that helps when new trajectory information appears, but can amplify drift when the agent gets stuck and repeatedly trains on similar text. We find that update-text repetition distinguishes these regimes and introduce Agentic Test-Time Training (aTTT), a token-level reweighting method that downweights the loss on tokens appearing in repeated $n$-grams from prior updates while leaving novel tokens fully weighted. To run such updates inside live episodes, we build a concurrent serving system using vLLM's runtime LoRA API, limiting overhead to 1.9$\times$ the no-TTT cost. aTTT improves success by up to 5.0 points on ALFWorld and 4.9 points on SWE-bench Lite. The gains concentrate where models already have task competence but drift over long trajectories, suggesting that aTTT mainly preserves existing competence rather than teaching new abilities.

</details>


### [71] [Best-of-Better-$N$: Generating Pre-Aligned Responses with In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03453)
*Eric Lei,Hsiang Hsu,Chun-Fu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Best-of-Better-N (BoBN)方法，通过检索高奖励样本并利用上下文学习重写以匹配目标任务格式，从而提升LLM推理时对齐的效果，在固定N时获得更高质量响应，减少达到目标性能所需的N值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时对齐方法（如Best-of-N）受限于参考LLM自身生成响应的质量：若参考LLM对高奖励响应赋予极低概率，则任何选择策略都无法找到对齐的输出。需要一种方法提升参考LLM的采样分布质量。

Method: 提出BoBN框架：1) 从高奖励示例中检索与输入查询和任务相关的样本；2) 引入重写步骤（restyling），由参考LLM将检索到的响应重写以匹配目标任务格式和风格；3) 将重写后的示例作为上下文输入，使采样分布向高奖励区域偏移。

Result: 在安全对齐和数学推理基准测试上，BoBN在固定N时生成更高质量的响应，显著提升了性能；同时达到目标性能所需的N值更小。理论上证明了上下文学习能使预训练Transformer的输出分布向高奖励区域偏移，带来可证明的收益。

Conclusion: BoBN通过检索加重写的上下文学习策略，有效克服了参考LLM对高奖励响应概率不足的限制，是一种高效且理论上可证明的推理时对齐增强方法。

Abstract: Inference-time alignment methods, such as Best-of-$N$, offer a flexible alternative to training-based alignment by using reward models to select high-quality responses generated by a reference LLM. However, the efficacy of these methods is inherently limited by the response quality: if the reference LLM assigns negligible probability to high-reward responses, no selection strategy will succeed in finding aligned outputs. In this work, we propose Best-of-Better-$N$ (BoBN), an in context learning-based generation framework to address this challenge. Our method utilizes retrieval from high-reward examples relevant to the input query and task. Crucially, we introduce a restyling step where retrieved responses are rewritten by the reference LLM to align with the target task's format and style. These restyled examples are used in-context to shift the sampling distribution toward the high-reward region. We analytically characterize how in-context learning shifts the output distribution of pretrained transformers toward the high-reward region, resulting in provable benefits on the target task. We then evaluate BoBN on safety alignment and mathematical reasoning benchmarks across several reference LLMs. BoBN's higher-quality responses enable better performance to be achieved when the number of responses $N$ is fixed, and smaller $N$ required to achieve a target performance.

</details>


### [72] [Towards Diverse and Comprehensive Benchmarks for Mutual Information Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03487)
*Alberto Foresti,Ivan Butakov,Alexander Tolmachev,Giulio Franzese,Alexey Frolov,Pietro Michiardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于copula理论的互信息估计统一基准框架，包含两大测试族，系统评估了非参数、判别式和生成式三类估计器，发现不存在通用最优方法，不同估计器在特定设置下各有优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有互信息估计基准通常仅在简化低维分布上评估，缺乏对复杂真实数据场景下估计器性能的系统研究。

Method: 提出统一的copula理论基准框架：(1) copula优先族——通过合成和流式变换系统变化真实MI、维度和边际复杂度；(2) 边际优先族——将真实图像数据与受控依赖结构结合，扩展同类配对范式。在该框架下评估三类估计器（非参数、判别式、生成式）。

Result: 没有通用最优估计器；每一类估计器都能在特定设置下系统性地优于其他所有估计器。

Conclusion: 通过分析各类估计器优势场景，识别出基本估计障碍，并提出更能针对这些局限性的新测试。开源代码已发布。

Abstract: Mutual information (MI) estimation is a central problem in machine learning and statistics; however, existing benchmarks typically evaluate estimators on simplified, low-dimensional distributions, leaving their performance on complex, realistic data largely unexplored. We address this gap with a comprehensive benchmarking framework grounded in a unified copula-theoretic perspective that subsumes existing benchmarks as special cases. Within this framework, we propose two complementary families of tests: a copula-first family that systematically varies ground-truth MI, dimensionality, and marginal complexity using synthetic and flow-based transformations; and a marginals-first family that couples real-world image data with controlled dependency structures, extending the classic same-class-pairing paradigm. We use this suite to extensively evaluate three classes of estimators: non-parametric, discriminative, and generative. Contrary to prevailing assumptions, our results indicate that there is no universal winner: each category can systematically outperform all other estimators under specific setups. By analyzing these cases, we identify fundamental estimation barriers and propose new tests that more effectively stress these specific limitations. We share the open source code at https://github.com/VanessB/mutinfo.

</details>


### [73] [A Near-Linear-Time Solver for Graph $p$-Laplacian Semi-Supervised Learning via Continuation in $p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03503)
*Oren E. Livne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于p-Laplacian的非线性半监督学习方法，通过阻尼弦-牛顿延拓在p上求解，使得每个线性化系统保持良态，可借助近线性Laplacian求解器高效处理。在228个SuiteSparse图上的实验表明，复杂度为m^1.19，可直接处理6.8×10^7条边的社交网络，速度比现有FCL求解器快1.5-14倍，且在MNIST上p=3比p=2准确率高28个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准二次型(p=2)的半监督学习在标签稀缺时存在退化问题：当维度d≥2时，增加无标签数据反而使估计趋近于常数函数（Nadler-Srebro-Zhou）。解决这一问题需要使用p>d的非线性p-Laplacian能量，但现有求解器在大p值时面临权重退化导致的近奇异系统问题，且难以直接利用近线性Laplacian求解器。

Method: 将p-Laplacian半监督学习重写为源形式非线性Laplacian流Bρ_p(B^T x)=b，采用阻尼弦-牛顿延拓在参数p上逐步求解。每个线性化系统都保持良态条件，可交由近线性Laplacian求解引擎处理。通过实现O(m)层次结构避免Cholesky填充导致的巨量内存消耗。

Result: 在规模缩放图族上，壁钟时间经验为m^0.96-m^1.02；在228个SuiteSparse图上拟合复杂度为m^1.19（对比直接分解的m^1.45）；可处理6.8×10^7条边的社交网络；内存方面Cholesky填充达到图非零元的10-280倍，而本方法为O(m)；比FCL求解器快1.5-14倍且精度相当；MNIST 10-NN上p=3在每类一个标签时准确率64%，p=2仅36%。

Conclusion: 提出的非线性p-Laplacian半监督学习方法在理论上克服了二次型在标签稀缺时的退化问题，在实践上实现了近线性的时间复杂度和O(m)的内存开销，可扩展到大规模图数据，且分类性能显著优于传统方法。

Abstract: Graph-based semi-supervised learning (SSL) propagates a few labels over a similarity graph by minimizing a Dirichlet-type energy. The standard quadratic ($p=2$) energy reduces to a single graph-Laplacian solve, but it degenerates exactly where SSL is most useful when labels are scarce: gathering more unlabeled data drives the $p=2$ estimate to a near-constant function whenever $d\ge2$ (Nadler-Srebro-Zhou). Well-posedness requires the nonlinear $p$-Laplacian energy with $p>d$. Existing solvers reduce this to a sequence of weighted Laplacian solves, but their reference implementations use a direct sparse factorization or ichol-preconditioned CG instead. Plugging a near-linear Laplacian solver is not straightforward: at large $p$ the conductance weights degenerate near flat-gradient edges, making the system nearly singular and causing stagnation without a damped outer iteration. We close this gap. Recasting $p$-Laplacian SSL as a source-form nonlinear Laplacian flow $Bρ_p(B^\top x)=b$ and solving by damped chord-Newton continuation in $p$, every linearized system stays well-conditioned and can be delegated to a near-linear Laplacian engine. On size-scaled graph families the wall-clock is empirically $m^{0.96}$-$m^{1.02}$ per family (approximate Cholesky default), and a pooled fit across 228 SuiteSparse graphs gives $m^{1.19}$ vs.\ $m^{1.45}$ for direct factorization; the solver handles a $6.8\times10^7$-edge social network in minutes. Memory is the binding constraint: Cholesky fill reaches $10$-$280\times$ the graph nonzeros vs.\ our $O(m)$ hierarchy. Against the released FCL solver we are $1.5$-$14\times$ faster at matched accuracy. On MNIST $10$-NN, $p=3$ scores $64\%$ at one label per class vs.\ $36\%$ for $p=2$. Code: https://github.com/orenlivne/np.

</details>


### [74] [Mixture-of-Gaussians-Guided Schedule Design for Brownian Bridge Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03517)
*Ron Levi,Michael Elad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对布朗桥扩散模型（BBDM）中调度设计缺乏理论框架的问题，提出了一种基于混合高斯（MoG）先验的分析框架，推导了闭合形式的理想后验和MMSE去噪器，并基于此提出了两个互补的调度设计目标（Wasserstein感知质量准则和MSE重建保真度准则），揭示了二者之间的内在权衡，证明了与退化和先验无关的通用调度存在性。实验在MoG设定和FFHQ数据集上验证了理论的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BBDM的调度设计通常依赖启发式方法，缺乏系统性的理论分析框架来指导调度选择，这限制了模型在图像恢复和逆问题中的性能优化。

Method: 1. 在MoG先验下对BBDM逆动力学进行解析分析，得到闭合形式的理想后验和MMSE去噪器；2. 构建可解析表达的BBDM诱导重建律的替代模型；3. 提出两个互补的调度设计目标——Wasserstein准则（侧重感知质量）和MSE准则（侧重重建保真度）；4. 理论证明存在与退化和先验无关的通用调度。

Result: 在受控MoG设定下，理论与实验完全对齐；在FFHQ数据集上的修复、去模糊和超分辨率任务中验证了所提调度设计准则的实用价值。

Conclusion: 本文建立了BBDM调度设计的理论分析框架，揭示了感知质量与重建保真度之间的内在权衡，并证明了通用调度的存在性，为实际应用中的调度选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Brownian Bridge Diffusion Models (BBDM) offer an appealing framework for image restoration and inverse problems by constructing a stochastic bridge from the clean signal directly to the degraded observation, rather than to pure noise. Despite their promise, the choice of bridge schedule is typically inherited from heuristics, and a principled analytical framework for schedule design has been lacking. In this work, we develop such a framework by offering a novel analysis of BBDM reverse dynamics under a Mixture-of-Gaussians (MoG) prior. This setting yields a closed-form ideal posterior and a corresponding MMSE denoiser, while the BBDM-induced reconstruction law is captured analytically through a tractable surrogate. Building on these expressions, we formulate two complementary schedule-design objectives: a Wasserstein criterion targeting perceptual quality and an MSE criterion targeting reconstruction fidelity. Our work exposes an inherent tradeoff between the two and proves the existence of universal schedules for both that are independent of the degradation and prior. Extensive experiments on controlled MoG settings confirm full alignment between theory and practice, and experiments on the FFHQ dataset across inpainting, deblurring, and super-resolution tasks validate the practical value of our schedule-design criteria.

</details>


### [75] [On the Convergence of Adam, Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03519)
*Steven Heilman,Sampad Mohanty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于任意动量衰减参数β₁,β₂∈[0,1)，投影Adam算法在在线优化中的平均遗憾可以非零。该结果推广了Reddi等人2018年的结论（要求β₁<√β₂），且适用于多种Adam变体及i.i.d.变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示Adam算法在特定条件下存在的收敛性缺陷，即其平均遗憾可以非零，从而警示对Adam算法理论性质的过度乐观。

Method: 利用[-1,1]上的三周期线性函数序列（斜率c,-1,-1），其中c略大于2，构造反例证明平均遗憾非零。该方法放宽了此前Reddi等人对β₁<√β₂的限制条件。

Result: 对于任意β₁,β₂∈[0,1)，投影Adam的平均遗憾可以非零；该结论扩展至AdamW、RMSProp、NAdam、Adan、AdaMax、Muon等变体，以及i.i.d.变体。

Conclusion: Adam及其变体在在线优化中可能存在非零平均遗憾，其收敛性保障需要更严格的条件，对实际使用具有警示意义。

Abstract: We show that projected Adam for online optimization with arbitrary moment decay parameters $β_1,β_2\in[0,1)$ can have average regret bounded away from zero. A similar result of Reddi-Kale-Kumar from 2018 required $β_1<\sqrt{β_2}$. Similar to their result, we use a three-periodic sequence of linear functions on $[-1,1]$ with slopes $c,-1,-1$, though we use $c$ slightly larger than $2$. This nonzero average regret result extends to Adam variants such as AdamW, RMSProp, NAdam, Adan, AdaMax, Muon, and to an i.i.d. variant of the three-periodic sequence of slopes for Adam.

</details>


### [76] [Co-Adaptive Multi-Task LoRA: Transfer-Aware, Label-Free Control of Domain Participation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03522)
*Wei Zhang,Lin Tang,Ming Zhao,Yuxuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoDA，一种无需额外参数、仅需前向传播的自适应控制器，通过在线调整各领域在LoRA多任务微调中的参与权重（损失权重+数据采样比例），显著提升异构多领域联合学习效果，平均超越均匀混合、学习型混合、梯度手术优化器和在线数据选择方法，同时使用一半数据并降低梯度冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效微调（LoRA）在处理多领域时采用固定的均匀混合训练策略，忽略了两个关键问题：每个领域应参与多少训练，以及哪些领域应共同训练（因为领域间存在正迁移和干扰）。这两个问题相互耦合且未被充分解答。

Method: CoDA包含两个核心信号：1）能力信号（competence signal）：通过对当前共享适配器在少量无标签探测数据上的前向传播获得，其水平追踪剩余提升空间，其轨迹追踪学习速度；2）跨领域亲和力（cross-domain affinity）：通过探测表征的漂移量计算有符号的成对迁移度量。控制器求解一个熵正则化的二次规划问题，动态调整各领域的参与权重（联合控制损失权重和数据采样比例），奖励高剩余空间、仍在学习、相互协同的领域，抑制干扰领域。

Result: 在五个异构领域和两种骨干网络上，CoDA超越均匀混合、学习型混合、梯度手术多任务优化器和在线数据选择方法，同时使用一半数据；有效降低跨领域梯度冲突；理论证明了能力信号追踪领域风险、参与程序存在唯一不动点且通过压缩映射达到、解执行迁移感知的水库填充策略。

Conclusion: CoDA是一种前向传播、无训练参数、可插入任何多任务LoRA管线的通用控制器，通过廉价的无标签探测信号实现领域间的自适应协同训练，有效解决了多领域微调中的负迁移和样本效率问题。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a single low-rank adapter on many domains at once is multi-task learning: the domains must be co-learned, and how they share the adapter decides whether they help or hurt one another. Most efficient fine-tuning pipelines ignore this and train on a fixed, uniform mixture, leaving two coupled questions unanswered: how much should each domain participate, and which domains should be co-trained given that some transfer positively and others interfere? We show that both answers can be read off cheaply and without labels. A forward pass of the current shared adapter over a small unlabeled probe yields, per domain, a competence signal whose level tracks remaining headroom and whose trajectory tracks learning speed; the drift of these probe representations yields a signed cross-domain affinity that predicts pairwise transfer. We fold both into CoDA, a co-adaptive controller that solves a small entropy-regularized quadratic program on the simplex to set each domain's participation -- jointly its loss weight and its share of the sampled data -- rewarding high-headroom, still-learning, mutually synergistic domains and damping interfering ones. The controller is forward-only, adds no trainable parameters, and wraps any multi-task LoRA pipeline. Across five heterogeneous domains and two backbones, CoDA improves the average over uniform mixing, learned mixtures, gradient-surgery multi-task optimizers, and online data selection while using half the data, and lowers cross-domain gradient conflict. We prove that the competence signal tracks domain risk, that the participation program has a unique fixed point reached by a contraction, and that its solution performs transfer-aware water-filling; analysis, ablations, and controls corroborate each claim.

</details>


### [77] [Aligning Language Models with Selective Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03528)
*Gaoxiang Luo,Yifan Wu,Sinian Zhang,Aryan Deshwal,Ju Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RLSR框架，通过在LLM后训练对齐阶段优化风险-覆盖曲线下面积（AURC）指标，实现选择性预测（SP），从而提升LLM在高风险决策场景中的可靠性，在域内和域外任务上均优于多种对齐基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被部署在高风险真实世界AI系统中作为关键决策组件，其可靠性成为首要关注问题。现有LLM对齐方法主要关注正确性或校准指标，缺乏针对选择性预测性能的优化，无法有效平衡风险-覆盖权衡以实现人机协作。

Method: 提出强化学习选择奖励（RLSR）框架，将选择性预测性能指标AURC（风险-覆盖曲线下面积）作为对齐目标，在LLM后训练对齐阶段进行优化，通过奖励机制引导模型在可能正确的输入上进行预测，其余交由人工处理。

Result: RLSR在域内和域外任务上均实现了显著更好的风险-覆盖权衡，优于多种对齐基线方法。

Conclusion: 将选择性预测指标纳入LLM对齐目标能有效提升模型可靠性，RLSR为高风险场景下的人机协作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as critical decision-making components in high-stakes real-world AI systems, rendering LLM reliability a foremost practical concern. In this paper, we focus on enhancing LLM reliability through selective prediction (SP), a strategy that allows an LLM to only predict for inputs where it is likely to be correct (i.e., coverage) and hence reduce the error rate (i.e., risk) on that portion of inputs -- flagging the remaining inputs for future human discretion. In other words, SP improves LLM reliability by balancing the risk-coverage trade-off and enabling seamless human-AI collaboration. To integrate SP into LLMs, we focus on the LLM post-training alignment stage and propose to align LLMs with SP performance metrics, in contrast with existing LLM alignment methods that focus primarily on correctness or calibration metrics. Specifically, we propose a novel alignment framework, Reinforcement Learning for Selection Reward (RLSR), which targets the area under the risk-coverage curve (AURC) -- a popular SP performance metric -- as its alignment objective. RLSR achieves substantially better risk-coverage trade-off compared to multiple alignment baselines on both in-domain and out-of-domain tasks.

</details>


### [78] [WeightCLIP: Aligning Datasets and Models for Weight Space Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03551)
*Aron Asefaw,Konstantinos Tzevelekakis,Damian Falk,Léo Meynent,Damian Borth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WeightCLIP提出了一种通过对齐神经网络权重表示与数据集信息的方法，利用对比学习将权重空间和数据集嵌入对齐，从而提升检索、生成和微调等下游任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有权重空间学习方法无法利用训练模型所使用的数据集信息来塑造权重空间表示，限制了下游应用的性能。

Method: 使用自编码器将神经网络编码为潜在表示，同时用数据集编码器编码数据集样本，通过对比学习目标对齐两种表示，实现数据集信息引导的权重空间重塑。

Result: 在检索、生成和模型微调等下游任务中，显式引入数据集信息显著提升了权重空间表示的性能；提出的潜在精炼过程优于标准微调。

Conclusion: 将数据集信息显式融入权重空间表示学习能够有效提升多种下游任务的效果，代码已开源。

Abstract: Weight space learning aims to learn representations of neural network (NN) weights, enabling different downstream tasks. Existing approaches show promising performance, but lacking a way to shape these weight-space representations using information about the datasets the models were trained on, thus limiting downstream applications. We propose WeightCLIP, a method for learning a dataset-aligned latent space for neural networks, where datasets information is induced during training. The NNs are encoded as latent representations using an autoencoder, while dataset samples are encoded using a dataset encoder. The two representations are aligned using a contrastive objective, effectively reshaping the weight-space representations according to the datasets. We demonstrate that such representations can be used for different downstream tasks, including mapping dataset information to a weight-space representation that decode to strong models. In addition, we introduce a latent refinement process for generating models that outperforms standard fine-tuning. Overall, our results demonstrate that explicitly incorporating dataset information improves what can be achieved with weight-space representations across retrieval, generation, and refinement. Code will be available at https://github.com/HSG-AIML/WeightCLIP.

</details>


### [79] [Teacher Supervision over Representation Equivalence Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03572)
*Sang Il Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出知识蒸馏应匹配教师模型的等价类（正交-各向同性缩放变换下不变的结构），而非绝对特征；实验表明输出函数（logit）匹配恢复能力，而隐藏层特征匹配仅对齐几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏方法假设教师表示具有绝对坐标可匹配，但预训练表示仅在正交和各向同性缩放变换下可识别（等价类），因此绝对特征匹配是不适定的。

Method: 建立几何框架：教师输出函数是类不变量的商映射，监督应针对类不变量（Gram结构、CKA、主子空间）或先对齐坐标；在Qwen2.5和Llama-3.1上通过恢复实验和移植实验验证。

Result: 恢复实验可恢复模型表示（CKA~0.99）但无法恢复能力；消融实验表明logit匹配驱动能力恢复，隐藏特征匹配仅对齐几何；恢复局限于语料覆盖区域；移植实验表明边界重叠是必要非充分条件。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏应聚焦等价类不变量的匹配，logit匹配是关键，特征匹配仅是几何对齐；边界重叠预测移植成功但非充分。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation is usually framed as a choice of what to match in the teacher - its logits, hidden features, or sample relations - which presupposes that the teacher's representation has absolute coordinates to match. It does not: a pretrained representation is identifiable only up to an orthogonal-and-isotropic-scaling equivalence class, so a student should learn the teacher's equivalence class, not its features. The organizing fact is that capability is the teacher's output function, a class invariant that factors through the quotient by the class action, so an objective recovers capability exactly when it is defined there. This makes absolute feature matching ill-posed, and admissible supervision a matter of targeting class invariants (Gram structure, CKA, principal subspaces) or aligning coordinates first, unifying feature matching, relational distillation, alignment, and grafting in one geometric account. We validate our framework on Qwen2.5 and Llama-3.1. A restoration study recovers a corrupted model's representation (CKA ~ 0.99) but not its capability, and an ablation isolates the cause: output-function (logit) matching drives capability, while matching hidden representations aligns geometry without restoring function. Recovery is confined to the corpus-covered region, and a graft study confirms that boundary overlap predicts transplant success but is necessary, not sufficient.

</details>


### [80] [Differentiate the Evaluator, Not the Program: An Efficient Runtime Representation for Neuro-Symbolic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03574)
*Lucas Sheneman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出原生可微分虚拟机(NDVM)，将程序符号结构与可微数值状态分离，通过密集批处理缓冲区和执行轨迹的精确反向模式梯度记录，实现无需为每个候选程序单独编译即可对可执行程序进行微分，在程序-参数协同搜索中达到约24倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统生成的科学模型同时包含符号结构和连续参数，参数校准成为程序-参数协同搜索的瓶颈：外层循环生成大量候选程序，每个程序需要内层基于梯度的优化才能评估。现有方法要么将每个候选编译为单独计算图（牺牲程序即数据特性），要么基于解释器（解释器开销主导数值计算）。

Method: NDVM将符号结构（标签、符号、环境、控制流）保留为原生运行时数据，而数值载荷存于密集批处理缓冲区中，沿实际执行轨迹记录精确反向模式梯度。一个求值器遍历可分摊到大量参数向量上。设计受真实可微自托管Scheme解释器的锁定成本模型启发。

Result: 与参考后端的正向和梯度等价，每通道约60倍批处理分摊，近线性多核扩展，支持两个独立前端。在固定预算的LLM提议程序协同搜索中，NDVM在墙上时钟时间上约24倍更快达到高质量解。

Conclusion: 运行时微分可作为科学发现工作流的实用系统基础。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly propose executable scientific models whose value depends on both their symbolic structure and their fitted continuous parameters. This makes parameter calibration the bottleneck of program-and-parameter co-search: an outer loop can generate thousands of candidate programs, but each needs an inner gradient-based optimization before it can be assessed. Staging each candidate into its own differentiable graph makes individual models fast but sacrifices the program-as-data property that keeps search fluid; interpreter-based approaches preserve programs as runtime data but pay interpreter overhead that dominates the numerical work.
  We present the Native Differentiable Virtual Machine (NDVM), a runtime representation that differentiates executable programs without compiling each candidate into a separate graph. NDVM separates symbolic structure from differentiable numeric state: tags, symbols, environments, and control remain native runtime data, while numeric payloads live in dense batched buffers with exact reverse-mode gradients recorded along the realized execution trace, so one evaluator walk is amortized across large populations of parameter vectors. A locked cost model of a real differentiable self-hosted Scheme interpreter motivates the design. We realize NDVM as a native runtime with forward and gradient equivalence to the reference backend, about 60x per-lane batch amortization, near-linear multicore scaling, and two independent front ends. In fixed-budget co-search over LLM-proposed programs, NDVM reaches high-quality solutions about 24x sooner in wall-clock time, suggesting runtime differentiation as a practical systems foundation for scientific discovery workflows.

</details>


### [81] [When Geometry Aligns: Dihedral Hidden-State Transformations in UNet, ViT, and DiT Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03580)
*Mojtaba Faramarzi,Alex Lamb,Irina Rish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入二面体群反射变换作为可控内部干预，系统研究了扩散模型架构（UNet、DiT、ViT）中几何结构扰动对隐藏状态稳定性的影响，发现几何一致性变换可提升特征稳定性，而不一致变换会导致可预测的架构特定失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散架构（卷积UNet、基于Transformer的DiT等）中几何结构扰动的影响尚不明确，缺乏统一的分析框架来理解这些扰动如何影响隐藏状态的稳定性。

Method: 提出统一框架，将二面体群反射元素作为可控内部干预应用到中间隐藏状态；使用激活级诊断指标（自一致性偏移SCS、激活质量散射AMS、漂移Drift）分析特征稳定性；在Stable Diffusion 2.1 U-Net上评估7种干预模式，并辅以图像级指标（FID、KID、CLIP分数、LPIPS多样性）及ViT/DiT对照实验。

Result: 几何一致性变换提升稳定性，不一致变换导致可预测的架构特定失效。多架构实验共同验证了这一结论。

Conclusion: 几何一致性是空间结构视觉和扩散模型中隐藏状态稳定干预的关键原则。

Abstract: Diffusion architectures now encompass convolutional UNets as well as transformer-based designs such as Diffusion Transformers (DiTs), inspired by Vision Transformers (ViTs), yet the effects of structured geometric perturbations within these architectures remain poorly understood. We study this question through a unified framework that applies reflection-based elements of the dihedral group to intermediate hidden states as controlled internal interventions, contrasting geometrically consistent and inconsistent variants. Using activation-level diagnostics, including Self-Consistency Shift (SCS), Activation Mass Scatter (AMS), and Drift, we analyze feature stability and geometric drift. We find that consistent transformations improve stability, while inconsistent ones induce predictable, architecture-specific failures. In the main Stable Diffusion 2.1 U-Net study, we evaluate seven intervention modes over three seeds and complement the internal diagnostics with image-level FID, KID, CLIP score, and LPIPS diversity. Taken together with supporting ViT and controlled DiT analyses, these results establish geometric consistency as a key principle for stable hidden-state interventions in spatially structured vision and diffusion models.

</details>


### [82] [Modular Foundation Models for Time-Series Perception in Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03585)
*Quang Hung Pham,Ryad Zemouri,Martin Gagnon,Luc Vouligny*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模块化时间序列基础模型，通过预训练表征编码器集合、门控选择机制和Transformer自注意力聚合模块，实现跨故障预测与健康管理（PHM）任务的迁移学习，在ETT基准和工业虚拟传感案例中取得竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列感知方法多为任务特定、数据饥饿且难以集成到可扩展的监控决策管道中，纯数据驱动模型在变工况下缺乏鲁棒性和迁移能力。

Method: 1) 基于多预训练表征编码器的模块化框架；2) 自监督学习在异构数据集上学习任务无关表征；3) 门控机制动态选择相关编码器实现条件计算；4) Transformer自注意力模块显式建模跨编码器交互并投影到共享隐空间；5) 冻结预训练编码器，仅训练轻量任务头适配下游任务。

Result: 消融实验验证了自监督预训练、编码器选择、表征对齐和自适应聚合的互补作用；ETT基准上展现竞争性能；工业案例（水轮发电机转子温度虚拟传感）证实了实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 所提模块化时间序列基础模型通过可组合的预训练编码器架构，有效增强了PHM任务的可迁移性和适应性，在数据稀缺场景下具有实用潜力。

Abstract: Engineering Digital Twins and Prognostics and Health Management (PHM) systems rely on robust perception modules to extract actionable information from heterogeneous and non-stationary time-series data. However, most existing approaches remain task-specific, data-hungry, and difficult to integrate into scalable monitoring and decision-making pipelines. Moreover, purely data-driven models often lack robustness and transferability across varying operating conditions. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a modular foundation model for time-series perception based on a collection of pretrained representation encoders. The framework leverages self-supervised learning on heterogeneous datasets to learn transferable and task-agnostic representations, which can be reused across multiple PHM tasks. A gating mechanism is introduced to dynamically select relevant encoders for a given target dataset, enabling conditional computation and adaptive model composition. The selected representations are projected into a shared latent space and aggregated using a Transformer-based self-attention module that explicitly models cross-encoder interactions. The resulting architecture supports multiple downstream tasks, including imputation, long-term forecasting, and few-shot learning, through lightweight task-specific heads, while keeping pretrained encoders frozen during adaptation. Extensive ablation studies demonstrate the complementary roles of self-supervised pretraining, encoder selection, representation alignment, and adaptive aggregation. Experimental results on the ETT benchmark show competitive performance across tasks, while a real-world industrial case study on virtual sensing for hydro-generator rotor temperature highlights the practical relevance of the approach.

</details>


### [83] [Graph Classification via Network Usable Information: From Representation Evaluation to Structure Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03587)
*Abdullah Shaik,Anwar Said*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NetinfoGC是一个面向图分类任务的框架，扩展了网络可用信息(NUI)范式到图级别。它利用基于传播的机制和经典结构描述符（如图中心性度量）构建置换不变的图表示，并引入无需训练的NUI估计方法（基于聚类一致性和真实标签）以及稀疏组LASSO正则化自动选择信息性描述符。实验表明经典中心性度量与学习到的传播表示具有竞争力甚至更优，且NUI与分类准确率高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经网络方法依赖端到端训练的黑盒嵌入，缺乏可解释性和对表示质量的直接评估手段。作者希望提出统一且可解释的框架，无需端到端神经训练即可评估和利用图表示。

Method: (1) 构建基于传播机制和经典结构描述符（如图中心性度量）的置换不变图表示族；(2) 提出基于聚类一致性与真实标签的无训练NUI估计方法，作为任务相关信息的代理指标；(3) 利用稀疏组LASSO正则化自动选择信息性结构描述符并抑制冗余特征。

Result: 实验发现经典中心性度量与学习到的传播表示相比具有竞争力甚至更优；NUI估计值与下游分类准确率之间存在强相关性，验证了NUI作为表示效用指标的有效性。

Conclusion: NetinfoGC提供了一个统一、可解释的图表示评估与利用框架，无需端到端神经训练即可实现有效的图分类，且NUI可作为可靠的表示质量代理指标。

Abstract: We propose NetinfoGC, a framework for graph classification that extends the Network Usable Information (NUI) paradigm to graph-level learning. Unlike conventional graph neural network approaches that rely on end-to-end training of black-box embeddings, NetinfoGC constructs a family of permutation-invariant graph representations derived from propagation-based mechanisms and classical structural descriptors, including graph centrality measures.
  To evaluate representation quality, we introduce a training-free NUI estimation procedure based on clustering consistency with ground-truth labels, providing a proxy for task-relevant information without supervised learning. We further exploit the same representations using sparse-group LASSO regularization, enabling automatic selection of informative structural descriptors while suppressing redundant ones.
  Experiments on benchmark datasets show that classical centrality measures are highly competitive with learned propagation-based representations, and in several cases yield superior performance. Moreover, we observe a strong correlation between estimated NUI and downstream classification accuracy, validating NUI as an effective measure of representation utility.
  Overall, NetinfoGC provides a unified and interpretable framework for evaluating and exploiting graph representations without requiring end-to-end neural training.

</details>


### [84] [Implicit Bias of SGD in Multivariate ReLU Networks: Effective Width Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03613)
*Shuang Liang,Tom Jacobs,Guido Montúfar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了噪声随机梯度下降在训练宽两层ReLU网络用于多元回归时的隐式偏差。在平均场机制下，训练动力学被近似为Wasserstein梯度流，收敛于唯一稳态测度。尽管网络无限过参数化，但学习到的预测器具有有效有限表示：输入权重和偏置沿有限方向对齐，导致有效宽度坍缩。解函数是连续分段仿射函数，其仿射区域由有限超平面排列的细胞决定。学习方向数（即超平面数）上界为2𝒫-1，其中𝒫是训练输入上可实现线性二分法的数量。此外，每个学习方向在训练数据上诱导唯一的三值激活模式，体现了学习的非冗余性。因此，学习预测器的复杂度由训练数据的组合几何决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解噪声随机梯度下降在训练宽两层ReLU网络时诱导的隐式偏差，特别是无限过参数化网络如何通过隐式正则化产生有效有限表示，以及这种表示如何与训练数据的组合几何结构相关联。

Method: 在平均场框架下，将训练动力学近似为Wasserstein梯度流，分析其收敛到稳态测度的性质。通过刻画该稳态测度的结构，研究输入权重和偏置的分布特性，并利用超平面排列和线性二分法理论分析学习到的表示。

Result: 1) 无限过参数化网络发生有效宽度坍缩，权重沿有限方向对齐；2) 学习到的函数是连续分段仿射函数，由有限超平面排列决定；3) 学习方向数上界为2𝒫-1；4) 每个学习方向在训练数据上诱导唯一的三值激活模式，具有非冗余性。

Conclusion: 噪声SGD在训练宽两层ReLU网络时存在隐式偏置，倾向于学习与数据组合几何对齐的有限复杂度表示，从而解释了过参数化网络在实践中的泛化能力。

Abstract: We study the implicit bias of noisy stochastic gradient descent in training wide two-layer ReLU networks for multivariate regression. In a mean-field regime, the training dynamics are approximated by a Wasserstein gradient flow that converges to a unique stationary measure. We characterize the structure of this stationary measure and the predictor it represents. We show that, despite the network being infinitely overparameterized, the learned predictor admits an effectively finite representation: the input weights and biases align along finitely many directions, leading to an effective width collapse. In particular, the solution function is continuous piecewise affine, with affine regions determined by the cells of a finite hyperplane arrangement. The number of learned directions, and hence hyperplanes, is bounded above by $2\mathcal{P}-1$, where $\mathcal{P}$ denotes the number of linear dichotomies realizable on the training inputs. We further establish a non-redundancy property of the learned representation by proving that each learned direction induces a unique ternary activation pattern on the training data. Consequently, the complexity of the learned predictor is governed by the combinatorial geometry of the training data.

</details>


### [85] [Reflected Schrödinger Bridge Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03626)
*Marcus Häggbom,Viktor Nilsson,Pierre Nyquist,Joakim andén*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个部分免模拟的框架，使反射薛定谔桥（Reflected Schrödinger Bridges）能够像流匹配（flow matching）一样进行高效训练，通过新的采样方法和回归目标，在保持或略微提升生成性能的同时，几乎不增加训练和推理的计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反射薛定谔桥依赖于基于前向-后向SDE理论的复杂训练，需要昂贵的高阶导数和在训练期间对整个路径进行采样，计算成本高。而最近流匹配启发的部分免模拟训练方法虽然加速了标准（非反射）SB的计算，但尚未覆盖具有反射动力学的SB，而反射动力学能保证生成样本始终位于数据域内，是一个有用的模型选择。

Method: 提出一个部分免模拟的框架，包含：(1) 一种新的采样方法；(2) 一个新的回归目标。该方法使得反射SB的训练方式与流匹配类似，无需高阶导数和全路径采样。

Result: 在多个高维图像数据集上进行成对耦合实验，反射动力学在训练和推理过程中增加的额外壁钟时间可以忽略不计，同时生成性能保持或略有提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的部分免模拟框架成功地将反射SB的训练简化为类似于流匹配的形式，既保持了反射动力学的域内约束优势，又避免了复杂的SDE训练过程，实现了高效且性能优良的生成。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative modeling have enabled the efficient computation of Schrödinger bridges (SB) in high-dimensional settings by leveraging partially simulation-free training methods inspired by flow matching. However, these have not covered SBs with reflecting dynamics, a useful model choice with built-in guarantees that generated samples stay in the data domain. Existing alternatives for reflected SBs instead rely on more complex training based on forward--backward SDE theory, requiring expensive higher-order derivatives and sampling entire paths during training. In this article, we introduce a partially simulation-free framework that allows reflected SBs to be trained similarly to flow matching, using a new sampling method and regression target. We demonstrate our results by coupling pairs of well-known high-dimensional image datasets. Using reflected dynamics incurs negligible additional wall-clock time during both training and inference while maintaining or slightly improving generative performance.

</details>


### [86] [LLM-Guided Transportation Hub Capacity Planning with Textual Business Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03651)
*Xiaoyue Liu,Zheng Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种LLM智能体框架，通过链式推理协议和优化模型反馈循环，将自然语言业务上下文融入枢纽容量规划，在真实货运网络中实现2.8%最优性差距（传统模型为11.0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统枢纽容量规划模型虽能有效优化定量输入，但无法消化定性商业上下文信息，导致决策与真实业务需求脱节。

Method: LLM智能体通过链式推理协议构建结构化决策表，将每项上下文映射到具体容量调整；随后通过优化模型的反馈循环验证决策，利用基于路径的绩效指标引导智能体选择。

Result: 在美国东南部13枢纽货运网络上，该框架的最优性差距为2.8%，显著优于未使用文本业务输入的传统优化模型（11.0%）。

Conclusion: LLM可作为上下文桥梁，将定性商业洞察有效整合进运筹学工作流中，提升容量规划决策质量。

Abstract: While traditional hub capacity planning models optimize effectively for quantitative inputs, they often fail to digest qualitative business context. We propose a novel framework where a large language model (LLM) agent iteratively proposes hub capacity decisions guided by natural-language business context descriptions. The key mechanism is a chain-of-thought reasoning protocol: the LLM constructs a structured decision table that maps each contextual item to specific capacity adjustments based on the implied direction and magnitude of changes. The new capacity decision is then validated through a feedback loop with an optimization model, which provides routing-based performance metrics to guide the agent's selection. On a real-world 13-hub freight network in the southeastern US, our framework achieves a 2.8% optimality gap relative to the hidden ground-truth, a significant improvement over the 11.0% gap produced by the traditional optimization model without textual business inputs. This demonstrates that LLMs can serve as a contextual bridge, integrating qualitative business insights into Operations Research workflows.

</details>


### [87] [Phase-Preserving Trimodal Transformer for Tropical Forest Biomass Estimation Using Optical and PolInSAR Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03663)
*Luiz Felipe Parente Santiago,Rosiane Rodrigues de Freitas,Daniel Rodrigues dos Santos,Felipe Ferrari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对热带成熟林AGB估算中SAR饱和与云遮挡问题，提出Trimodal Coherent Co-attention Transformer (TCCT)，融合光学（Landsat-5）与P/L波段PolInSAR复值数据，通过复值编码器保持相位相干性、动态共注意力机制自适应降低云污染光学像素权重，并利用Levenberg-Marquardt优化进行局部空间异速校准。在亚马逊Paracou地区，全局RMSE为4.19 m，局部微调后CHM的RMSE为3.78 m、R²为0.33，AGB相对RMSE为4.51%，满足ESA BIOMASS任务要求（<20%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带成熟林AGB估算面临SAR信号在密林区饱和及光学影像持续云覆盖的物理限制，现有方法难以突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出TCCT架构：1) 复值编码器保留PolInSAR空间相位相干性；2) 动态共注意力机制作为自适应门控模块，降低云污染光学像素权重、转向微波相位数据；3) 利用Levenberg-Marquardt优化进行局部空间异速校准。

Result: 全局交叉验证RMSE 4.19 m；局部微调后CHM的RMSE 3.78 m、R² 0.33；AGB相对RMSE 4.51%（>50 Mg/ha密林区），优于RF、CNN、ViT基线。消融实验证实保持相位相干性可缓解深冠层信号饱和。

Conclusion: TCCT满足ESA BIOMASS任务<20%误差要求，为热带生物群落连续碳储量制图提供了鲁棒框架。

Abstract: The accurate estimation of Above-Ground Biomass (AGB) in mature tropical forests remains a critical challenge in remote sensing, primarily due to the saturation of Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) signals in high-density areas and persistent cloud cover affecting optical imagery. To overcome these physical limitations, we propose the Trimodal Coherent Co-attention Transformer (TCCT), a physics-informed deep learning architecture. The TCCT natively fuses optical surface reflectance (Landsat-5) with complex-valued Polarimetric SAR Interferometry (PolInSAR) data from both P and L bands. Unlike traditional fusion methods, our architecture employs complex-valued encoders to preserve spatial phase coherence, coupled with a dynamic co-attention mechanism that acts as an adaptive gating module, reducing the weight of cloud-corrupted optical pixels and shifting reliance to microwave phase data. We also derived a localized spatial allometric calibration model via Levenberg-Marquardt optimization, tailored to the specific wood density of the Paracou region in the Amazon basin. Evaluated using a two-stage protocol, the TCCT first underwent a rigorous 5-fold cross-validation to establish robust global weights (achieving a global RMSE of 4.19 m). Subsequently, following a localized spatial fine-tuning phase over 200 epochs, the model attained an absolute RMSE of 3.78 m and an $R^2$ of 0.33 for Canopy Height Models (CHM), outperforming standard Random Forest, CNN, and Vision Transformer baselines. Our ablation study confirms that preserving phase coherence mitigates deep-canopy signal saturation. When converted to AGB, the fine-tuned TCCT map yielded a Relative RMSE (rRMSE) of 4.51% in dense forest areas above 50 Mg/ha. By meeting the European Space Agency (ESA) BIOMASS mission requirement of less than 20% error, the TCCT provides a robust framework for continuous carbon stock mapping in tropical biomes.

</details>


### [88] [A Structural Interpretation of GELU and Threshold-Transmission Activations via the First-Order Loss Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03664)
*Roberto Rossi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从高斯互补一阶损失函数的角度重新解释了GELU激活函数，将其视为硬线性门控在随机高斯阈值下的期望盈余的信号传输项，并推广至包含ReLU、GELU、SiLU/Swish和hard swish的阈值-传输家族。统一阈值情形导出了显式阈值宽度的hard-swish类紧致分段多项式门控，实验表明其在紧凑视觉和语言模型中与现有激活函数具有竞争力甚至更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GELU的动机通常基于随机伯努利门控的期望输出，缺乏从互补损失函数角度的理解。本文旨在提供一种新的解释视角，并推广至统一的激活函数家族。

Method: 从高斯互补一阶损失函数出发，将GELU重新解释为硬线性门控在服从正态分布的随机阈值下的期望盈余，并分离出损失核算项与信号传输项。通过引入不同的阈值分布，统一了多种激活函数；特别地，采用均匀阈值分布导出了具有显式宽度参数的hard-swish类分段多项式门控。

Result: 在紧凑视觉和语言模型上的受控实验表明，标定或学习的均匀阈值门控在多数测试设置中与GELU、ReLU、SiLU/Swish具有竞争力甚至更优，且有限过渡区域被非平凡地利用。

Conclusion: 高斯损失函数视角为理解GELU等激活函数提供了新的理论框架，均匀阈值门控作为一种简单有效的替代方案，在实际应用中表现出色。

Abstract: The Gaussian Error Linear Unit is usually motivated as the expected output of an input-dependent stochastic Bernoulli gate. This work gives a complementary interpretation based on the Gaussian complementary first-order loss function: GELU is the signal-transmission term of the expected surplus of a hard linear gate with a Gaussian random threshold. This view separates loss accounting from forward signal transmission and generalises to a threshold-transmission family that includes ReLU, GELU, SiLU/Swish, and hard swish as special cases. The uniform-threshold case recovers a hard-swish-like compact piecewise-polynomial gate with an explicit threshold-width parameter, yielding fixed- and learned-width variants. Controlled experiments on compact vision and language models show that calibrated or learned uniform-threshold gates are consistently competitive with GELU, ReLU, and SiLU/Swish, improve over them in most tested settings, and use the finite transition region nontrivially.

</details>


### [89] [Validation-Induced Shapley Shifts: How Validation Structure Distorts Data Valuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03675)
*Yinan Shen,Ziao Yang,Hongfu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示Shapley值在数据估值中对验证集噪声高度敏感：噪声导致Shapley值向零压缩，归因于噪声引发的邻域重排效应。作者提出归一化和边界感知验证策略以增强估值鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战现有研究对Shapley值稳定性的默认假设，发现验证集微小扰动（如添加噪声）会系统性改变Shapley分布，揭示数据估值中的新脆弱性维度。

Method: 基于KNN-Shapley框架，通过合成数据和真实数据实验，系统分析噪声对Shapley值分布的影响，识别噪声诱导的邻域重排效应，并提出归一化与边界感知验证两种缓解策略。

Result: 噪声添加导致Shapley值向零压缩，且这种变化具有一致性和可复现性；验证集扰动会改变训练样本与验证样本间的局部秩序，导致估值景观平坦化。

Conclusion: Shapley值并非稳定可靠，对验证集选择存在系统性脆弱性；所提策略可缓解失真，推动机器学习市场中更鲁棒、可解释的数据估值方法。

Abstract: Shapley values are widely used to attribute value to training data based on their marginal contribution to performance on a validation set. Existing practice often assumes these values are stable once the training data and model are fixed. In this work, we uncover a systematic vulnerability: even modest changes to the validation set, such as introducing noises, cause directional shifts in Shapley distributions. As noises are added, Shapley values of training samples compress toward zero. We trace this to a noise-induced neighborhood reshuffling effect: perturbations alter the local rank order between validation and training samples, flattening the valuation landscape. Using the KNN-Shapley framework, we show through synthetic and real data that these shifts are consistent and reproducible. Our findings challenge the assumption of Shapley stability and reveal a new axis of fragility in data valuation. We propose normalization and boundary-aware validation strategies to mitigate these distortions and enable more robust, interpretable valuation in machine learning marketplaces.

</details>


### [90] [Rethinking AI-Generated Text Detection: A Strong Baseline and the Distribution-Shift Problem That Remains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03680)
*Zhuoer Shen,Mingyi Wang,Shaofeng Zou,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个关键发现：简单微调的RoBERTa在多项AI生成文本检测基准上匹配或超越了专用检测器，表明现有架构复杂性并非检测性能的核心驱动力。真正的挑战在于分布偏移（主题领域或生成模型变化），且简单的数据增强无法解决。作者发现了一个关键失败模式：分布偏移下，检测器会错误地将人类撰写的文本（来自未见领域）高置信度地判定为机器生成。为此，研究了两种轻量级域适应方法：基于LoRA的K-shot MAML适应和置信度加权集成。结论强调，AI文本检测的进展应同时衡量分布内性能和分布偏移鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成文本检测研究常引入新基准和定制化检测器，但缺乏对简单基线的充分对比，导致对架构复杂性的真实贡献认知不清。本文旨在从基线优先视角重新审视该领域，揭示性能瓶颈所在。

Method: 1) 在多个基准上将简单微调的RoBERTa与专用检测器进行对比；2) 系统评估分布偏移（主题领域变化、生成模型变化）对检测性能的影响；3) 识别分布偏移下的关键失败模式（将人类文本误判为机器生成）；4) 采用两种轻量级域适应方法：基于LoRA的K-shot MAML适应（一阶）和置信度加权集成。

Result: 微调RoBERTa在分布内设置中匹配或超越专用检测器；分布偏移下性能急剧下降，简单增加数据无法弥补；检测器在未见领域的人类文本上产生高置信度的错误机器标签；两种轻量级域适应方法能在一定程度上缓解问题。

Conclusion: AI生成文本检测的进展应同时以分布内性能和分布偏移鲁棒性来度量；架构复杂性的边际收益有限，域适应和鲁棒性才是真正的挑战所在。

Abstract: Recent AI-generated text detection work often introduces a new benchmark together with a specialized detector tailored to it. We revisit this practice from a baseline-first perspective. Across several benchmarks, we show that a plain, fully fine-tuned RoBERTa matches or exceeds the specialized detectors those benchmarks are built around. This suggests that much of the recent architectural complexity is not what drives strong in-distribution detection. The remaining challenge is the distribution shift. The same strong baseline degrades sharply when the topic domain or generating model changes at test time, and simply adding more source data does not close the gap. We identify a key failure mode: under distribution shift, the detector can assign high-confidence machine labels to human-written text from unseen domains. We then study two lightweight domain adaptation methods to address this problem: $K$-shot adaptation with first-order MAML over LoRA adapters, and a per-sample confidence-weighted ensemble built on top of the adapted detector. Overall, our results suggest that progress in AI-generated text detection should be measured not only by in-distribution performance, but also by robustness under distribution shift.

</details>


### [91] [PIEFS: Physics-Informed Eigenfunction Features with Learnable Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03692)
*Varvara Nazarenkko,Timur Lidzhiev,Alexander Tarakanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PIEFS是一种基于可学习度量的物理信息特征框架，通过修改Dirichlet能量和Gram正交性约束，在监督学习中学习自适应谱表示，相较于固定核或图拉普拉斯方法更具灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统谱方法依赖固定的核函数、图拉普拉斯或人工选择的特征缩放，缺乏任务自适应性，难以在监督学习中灵活调整表示空间。

Method: 提出PIEFS框架：训练标量坐标映射，施加经验Gram正交性约束、监督线性读出和Dirichlet惩罚项；其中输入梯度由可学习度量A(x)=Λ(x)U(x)变换，Λ(x)控制各向异性缩放，U(x)通过Givens旋转的乘积结构参数化。

Result: 在合成、表格和图像基准上，实验比较了恒等度量、对角度量、旋转缩放度量三种设置，并与经典基线及NeuralEF方法对比，验证了PIEFS作为紧凑监督谱表示方法的有效性。

Conclusion: PIEFS提供了一种任务自适应的Dirichlet正则化坐标学习方法；未来方向包括优化稳定性、显式算子特征问题验证以及更丰富的度量参数化。

Abstract: Spectral methods are widely used to construct representations from the geometry of data, but they often rely on a fixed kernel, graph Laplacian, or manually selected feature scaling. We propose Physics-Informed Eigenfunction Features with Learnable Scaling (PIEFS), a supervised neural representation-learning framework with a spectral inductive bias, based on a modified Dirichlet energy. In PIEFS, scalar coordinate maps are trained under empirical Gram orthogonality, a supervised linear readout, and a Dirichlet penalty in which the input gradient is transformed by a learnable metric $A(x)=Λ(x)U(x)$. The diagonal factor $Λ(x)$ controls anisotropic scaling, while the orthogonal factor $U(x)$ is parameterized by a structured product of Givens rotations. This construction yields task-adaptive Dirichlet-regularized coordinates rather than eigenfunctions of a fixed supervision-independent operator. Experiments on synthetic, tabular, and image-based benchmarks study the effect of identity, diagonal, and rotation-scaling metrics, and compare the resulting coordinates with classical baselines and NeuralEF. The results support PIEFS as a compact supervised spectral representation method and identify optimization stability, validation on explicit operator eigenproblems, and richer metric parameterizations as the main directions for future work.

</details>


### [92] [Social Networks of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03695)
*Kaixuan Liu,Guojun Xiong,Weinan Zhang,Shengpu Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SNLA框架揭示了LLM智能体群体中注意力受限导致羊群效应（有效样本量有界），而广泛注意力仅在无向正则图上恢复群体智慧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统社会网络模型假设网络结构决定信念聚合，但LLM智能体的有限注意力使其只能接收部分信息，导致模型高估信息聚合程度，无法区分真实共识与羊群效应。

Method: 提出SNLA框架，建模每个智能体对其他智能体的实际影响力（取决于网络位置和注意力聚焦程度）；理论上通过可处理代理进行推导，实证上通过受控测试平台和多智能体LLM基准验证。

Result: 窄注意力导致羊群效应，有效样本量不随种群规模增长；宽注意力仅在无向度正则图上恢复群体智慧；预测在受控测试平台和三个多智能体LLM基准上得到验证。

Conclusion: 注意力机制是决定LLM群体信念聚合质量的关键因素，窄注意力导致虚假共识，群体智慧的实现依赖于网络结构（无向、度正则）与注意力宽度的匹配。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in interacting populations, raising the question of what such populations come to believe collectively. Whether a population aggregates genuine knowledge or collapses into a false consensus directly affects how much such systems can be trusted. Classical social-network models assume that the network itself determines how beliefs combine. This assumption breaks down for LLM agents, whose limited attention takes in only part of what they are exposed to, so these models overstate how much information a population actually pools and cannot tell genuine consensus from herding. We introduce SNLA, a framework that models how much each agent actually influences others, rather than merely how the network connects them. This influence depends on each agent's position in the network and on how sharply attention focuses. Theoretically, we show on a tractable proxy that narrow attention causes herding, where the effective sample size stays bounded regardless of population size, while wide attention recovers wisdom-of-crowds behavior only when the exposure graph is undirected and degree-regular. Empirically, a controlled testbed validates these predictions directly, and the herding-wisdom transition reproduces on operator-controlled variants of three multi-agent LLM benchmarks.

</details>


### [93] [SAVER: Stochastic Adaptive Variance-Driven Exploration and Reconstruction for Low-Dose Computed Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03761)
*Shunta Nonaga,Koji Tabata,Junya Honda,Hiroyuki Kudo,Wataru Yashiro,Tamiki Komatsuzaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAVER是一种自适应CT数据采集框架，通过基于统计方差的实时投影角度选择和Softmax随机调度算法，动态将辐射剂量分配到信息量最大的方向，在减少辐射剂量的同时保持或提升重建图像质量，尤其适用于结构各向异性强的对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统低剂量CT协议采用固定的均匀角度采样，忽略了患者器官结构的个体差异，无法在降低辐射剂量的同时最优化图像质量。亟需一种能根据患者解剖结构复杂度自适应调整采样策略的方法。

Method: 提出SAVER框架，基于已采集数据的统计方差实时选择投影角度，采用Softmax随机调度与模拟退火机制，优先选择结构信息丰富的方向同时保留必要的探索能力。

Result: 在8种不同体模上的数值实验表明，SAVER在重建保真度上持续优于传统随机采样，尤其对结构各向异性高的对象效果显著，且在强测量噪声下依然表现稳健。

Conclusion: SAVER通过将辐射剂量动态重新分配到信息量最大的投影方向，以数学上严谨的方式最大化每单位辐射剂量的诊断质量，标志着向样本依赖、数据驱动的CT采集模式的转变。

Abstract: Computed Tomography (CT) is indispensable in clinical diagnostics, yet minimizing radiation dose without compromising image quality remains a critical challenge. Conventional low-dose protocols often rely on fixed, uniform angular sampling, independent of the underlying structural complexity of organs of individual patients. We propose ``Stochastic Adaptive Variance-Driven Exploration and Reconstruction'' (SAVER), an adaptive data acquisition framework that selects projection angles in real-time based on the statistical variance of acquired data. Utilizing a Softmax-based stochastic scheduling scheme with simulated annealing, SAVER prioritizes directions with high structural information while maintaining necessary exploration. Numerical experiments across 8 diverse phantoms demonstrate that SAVER achieves consistently higher reconstruction fidelity than conventional random sampling, particularly for objects with high structural anisotropy. Furthermore, the proposed method exhibits robust performance under significant measurement noise. By dynamically reallocating radiation dose to the most informative projections, SAVER provides a mathematically-grounded approach to maximize diagnostic quality per unit of radiation dose, marking a shift toward sample-dependent, data-driven CT acquisition.

</details>


### [94] [FedACT: Federated Adaptive Coordinate Trust Modulation for Robust Transformer Training under Data Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03763)
*Shuai Li,Qinglin Wang,Ping Luo,Jiahuan Wang,Hongyang Hu,Haotian Mo,Yigui Feng,Ziang Liu,Qisong Xiao,Jie Liu,Tao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedACT，一种面向联邦AdamW训练的全局感知坐标信任调制方法，通过坐标级信任评分重新分配更新幅度，在视觉Transformer、CNN、LLM预训练和微调等任务上优于现有联邦自适应优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦自适应优化器主要在客户端更新或通信轮次层面解决信任不匹配问题，但在坐标维度上仍然均匀地应用修正后的自适应方向；在异构客户端数据下，即使经过全局修正的AdamW更新在坐标级可靠性上仍高度不均匀（坐标信任不匹配），需要更细粒度的协调机制。

Method: FedACT首先形成全局修正的自适应方向，然后根据坐标级信任评分重新分配更新幅度：对由局部梯度和全局修正共同支持的坐标分配较大步长，对剩余坐标保留较小的非零更新。

Result: 在联邦视觉Transformer、CNN、LLM预训练和微调上的大量实验表明，FedACT一致优于强联邦自适应基线，在更强的数据异构性下对Transformer模型提升最大。机制分析表明FedACT提高了跨客户端的更新方向一致性。

Conclusion: 坐标级信任分配有效补充了轮次级全局-局部修正，为联邦自适应训练提供了更精细的优化视角。

Abstract: Federated Transformer training increasingly relies on local AdamW, whose adaptive updates can provide much stronger local progress than SGD-based training. However, under heterogeneous client data, even globally corrected AdamW updates may remain highly uneven in coordinate-wise reliability. We refer to this phenomenon as coordinate trust mismatch. Existing federated adaptive optimizers mainly address mismatch at the client-update or communication-round level, but still apply the corrected adaptive direction densely and uniformly across coordinates. In this paper, we propose FedACT, a global-aware coordinate trust modulation method for federated AdamW training. FedACT first forms a globally corrected adaptive direction and then reallocates update magnitudes according to a coordinate-wise trust score, assigning larger steps to coordinates jointly supported by local gradients and global correction, while preserving smaller non-zero updates on the remaining coordinates. Extensive experiments on federated vision Transformers, CNNs, LLM pre-training, and LLM fine-tuning show that FedACT consistently improves over strong federated adaptive baselines, with the largest gains on Transformer models under stronger data heterogeneity. Mechanism analyses further show that FedACT improves cross-client direction consistency, suggesting that coordinate-level trust allocation effectively complements round-level global-local correction. Code will be released.

</details>


### [95] [Conservative Subject Invariant EMG-based Gesture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03783)
*Hamed Rafiei,Ali Mousavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种保守多目标学习框架，通过多头架构联合优化手势分类、对抗性主题混淆和三元组度量学习，并引入Lipschitz自适应加权机制，在sEMG手势识别中实现了跨主体泛化性能的显著提升（UCI EMG数据集84.48% vs SOTA 78.2%；NinaPro DB5数据集61.44% vs 41.30%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: sEMG手势识别中跨主体泛化是一个根本性挑战，现有深度学习方法依赖主体特定数据，难以在不变性和判别性之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出保守多目标学习框架，采用多头架构联合优化三个目标：手势分类损失、通过梯度反转层实现的对抗性主题混淆损失、以及三元组度量学习损失；引入基于Lipschitz的自适应加权机制动态平衡各辅助目标。

Result: 在UCI EMG数据集上准确率84.48%（SOTA为78.2%）；在NinaPro DB5上准确率61.44%（SOTA为41.30%，相对提升49%）；降低了跨主体预测方差，产生了更结构化的潜在表征。

Conclusion: 通过自适应多目标优化联合施加不变性和判别性，能够实现更稳定的训练和更好的跨主体泛化性能。

Abstract: Cross-subject generalization remains a fundamental challenge in surface electromyography (sEMG)-based gesture recognition. Although deep learning methods have improved within-subject performance, they often rely on subject-specific data and struggle to balance invariance and discriminability. In this work, we propose a conservative multi-objective learning framework for subject-invariant sEMG gesture recognition. The proposed model adopts a multi-head architecture that jointly optimizes gesture classification, adversarial subject confusion through gradient reversal, and triplet-based metric learning to encourage discriminative and subject-invariant representations. To improve optimization stability, a Lipschitz-inspired adaptive weighting mechanism is introduced to dynamically balance the auxiliary objectives according to their relative magnitudes during training. The proposed method is evaluated on two benchmark datasets: UCI EMG (36 subjects, 6 gestures) and NinaPro DB5 (10 subjects, 10 gestures). On the UCI EMG dataset, the method achieves 84.48\% accuracy compared to 78.2\% reported by state-of-the-art methods. On NinaPro DB5, it achieves 61.44\% accuracy versus 41.30\%, corresponding to a 49\% relative improvement. In addition, the proposed framework reduces cross-subject prediction variance and produces more structured latent representations. These results indicate that jointly enforcing invariance and discriminability through adaptive multi-objective optimization leads to more stable training and improved cross-subject generalization in sEMG-based gesture recognition systems.

</details>


### [96] [Tensor-Train Joint Modeling for Few-Step Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03788)
*Byoungkwon Kim,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个通过张量分解（CPD/TTD）对离散扩散模型进行显式联合分布建模的框架，解决了条件独立假设导致的多步并行去噪偏差问题，尤其适用于少步快速生成场景。TTD对序列数据具有天然的结构优势，通过轻量微调即可显著提升预训练掩码扩散模型（MDM）的少步生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散模型基于条件独立假设，在少步生成（快速生成所需）时存在系统性并行化偏差，该偏差随每步去噪的token数量增加而累积，严重制约了离散扩散在少步快速生成场景下的潜力。

Method: 提出基于张量分解的显式联合分布建模框架：1）将条件干净分布表示为可控制表达能力的低秩张量；2）支持Canonical Polyadic (CPD) 和 Tensor-Train (TTD) 两种分解；3）针对TTD，通过Oseledets定理形式化其对邻近token间依赖的结构偏好；4）设计针对预定位序调度的迭代边缘推理过程以实现高效生成；5）通过轻量微调集成到预训练MDM中。

Result: 该框架通过轻量微调即可显著提升预训练掩码扩散模型在少步生成场景下的性能，且训练成本远低于从头训练。

Conclusion: 张量分解提供了一种可控表达能力的显式联合分布建模方式，TTD因对序列数据邻近依赖的结构偏好尤其适合自然语言和分子线性表示等序列数据，为离散扩散模型在少步快速生成场景下的实际应用开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion promises orders-of-magnitude faster generation than autoregressive (AR) models for sequential discrete data, yet its full potential of few-step generation has remained out of reach due to a fundamental structural limitation. The conditional-independence assumption underlying current discrete diffusion models introduces a systematic parallelization bias that compounds with the number of tokens unmasked per step, becoming severe in the few-step regime that fast generation requires. We address this with the first framework for explicit joint distribution modeling in discrete diffusion via tensor decomposition, which represents the conditional clean distribution as a low-rank tensor with controllable expressivity. The framework supports both Canonical Polyadic (CPD) and Tensor-Train (TTD) decompositions, and we identify a structural bias of TTD toward dependencies between nearby tokens, formalized through Oseledets' theorem relating TT-rank to unfolding-matrix rank, which is well-suited to sequential data such as natural language and line notations for molecular data. To enable efficient generation, we present an iterative marginal inference procedure with specialization for predetermined position schedules. Our framework integrates into pretrained MDMs through lightweight fine-tuning, yielding substantial improvements in few-step generation at a fraction of the cost of training from scratch.

</details>


### [97] [Foundations of Equivariant Deep Learning: Unifying Graph and Sheaf Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03798)
*Yoshihiro Maruyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出序等变神经网络（OENN），将几何深度学习扩展到更丰富的对称结构。通过面偏序集上的等变丛理论推广图消息传递和层神经网，刻画了所有线性序等变映射，构建OENN层，并证明了连续序等变映射的通用逼近定理（UAT）。进一步扩展为范畴等变神经网络（CENN），给出了等变神经网络及UAT的一般形式，能够利用数据中的范畴对称性（如多对象上的非可逆对称及组合关系）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何深度学习利用数据中的对称性提升性能，但现有方法（如图神经网络、层神经网）仅能处理有限的对称结构。本文旨在利用更丰富的对称性（如序关系、范畴结构），建立更具一般性的等变神经网络框架。

Method: 基于面偏序集（face posets）上的等变丛理论，发展序等变神经网络（OENN）。首先刻画所有线性序等变映射，构建OENN层，然后证明连续序等变映射的通用逼近定理。进一步将OENN推广为范畴等变神经网络（CENN），利用范畴对称性。

Result: (i) 完整刻画了所有线性序等变映射；(ii) 构建了OENN层；(iii) 证明了连续序等变映射的通用逼近定理（UAT），该结果即使局限在层神经网（之前未知UAT）也是新的；(iv) 将图神经网络和层神经网的UAT推广到更一般的设定；(v) 进一步推广为CENN，给出等变神经网络及UAT的一般形式。

Conclusion: 序等变神经网络（OENN）成功将几何深度学习扩展到更丰富的对称结构，其通用逼近定理是新的突破。范畴等变神经网络（CENN）则提供了利用任意范畴对称性的统一框架，为处理具有复杂组合对称性的数据开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Symmetry is everywhere in nature and society. Geometric deep learning exploits symmetries in data to improve the performance and efficiency of deep learning systems. In this paper, we extend geometric deep learning to utilize richer symmetry structures. Specifically, we develop order-equivariant neural networks (OENN), which generalize standard graph message passing and sheaf neural networks via the theory of equivariant bundles over face posets (face categories). We (i) characterize all linear order-equivariant maps, (ii) build OENN layers, and (iii) prove universal approximation theorems (UATs) for continuous order-equivariant maps, which are new results even when restricted to sheaf neural networks (for which no UAT was known before). We illustrate the framework on graph and sheaf models. Our results can also be seen as extending the known UAT for graph neural networks to a more general setting that subsumes sheaf neural networks as well. In addition, we show that OENN can be extended further to CENN, Category-Equivariant Neural Network, which gives the general form of equivariant neural networks as well as of equivariant universal approximation theorems, allowing us to leverage categorical symmetry in data (e.g., non-invertible symmetries on multiple objects with compositional relations on those symmetries).

</details>


### [98] [Punching Above Their Weight: Classification-Head Fine-Tuning of Tiny Language Models (TLMs) for Verifiable Multiple-Choice Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03801)
*Bhavesh Sood,Jaromir Savelka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了小型语言模型（<3B参数）在可验证多选题任务上的适配方法，对比了三种LoRA微调范式，发现分类头微调优于标签生成（+2-3%），微调后的TLMs可与GPT-3/PaLM/GPT-4竞争，且Qwen3-0.6B和1.7B在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于大型语言模型，TLMs在消费级设备上的应用潜力未被充分探索，且缺乏针对可验证多选题任务的统一微调范式对比。

Method: 定义了TLMs为<3B参数模型；对比三种LoRA微调范式：标签生成、仅金标准、判别式分类头；在Qwen3系列（0.6B-8B）上统一实验设置，使用HellaSwag、WinoGrande、PIQA、SciQ和ARC-C五个基准。

Result: 分类头微调在0.6B和1.7B尺度上优于标签生成2-3%；微调后的TLMs与GPT-3(175B)、PaLM(540B)、GPT-4零样本/少样本性能相当；Qwen3-0.6B和1.7B在HellaSwag、WinoGrande、PIQA上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 判别式分类头微调是TLMs在可验证多选题任务上的有效方法，小模型通过适当微调可达到与超大模型竞争的性能，验证了TLMs在消费级设备上的实用价值。

Abstract: We define Tiny Language Models (TLMs) as models below roughly 3B parameters that fit on mainstream consumer devices. We study how to adapt them for and use them on verifiable multiple-choice tasks. We compare three LoRA-based fine-tuning paradigms (label generation, gold only, and our discriminative classification head) on a unified setup across several Qwen3 models from 0.6B to 8B and five benchmarks: HellaSwag, WinoGrande, PIQA, SciQ and ARC-C. Classification-head fine-tuning reliably outperforms label generation (+2-3%) at the 0.6B and 1.7B scales. Further, TLMs fine-tuned using the discriminative method are competitive to zero-/few-shot GPT-3 (175B), PaLM (540B) and GPT-4. The performance we report for Qwen3-0.6B and Qwen3-1.7B are SOTA on HellaSwag, WinoGrande, and PIQA.

</details>


### [99] [Stable Global Weighting of Flow Mixtures using Simplex Exponential Moving Average](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03809)
*Benjamin Wiriyapong,Oktay Karakus,Can Eyupoglu,Kirill Sidorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AMF-VI-sEMA，一种两阶段混合归一化流框架，通过基于单纯形指数移动平均（sEMA）的稳定全局加权机制，实现了对异构后验几何的鲁棒近似推断，在多个后验基准上优于单流基线和前代方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有归一化流架构在异构后验几何（如多峰、弯曲、环形等复杂形状）上难以泛化，单一架构往往仅擅长特定类型分布，缺乏自适应分配模型容量的能力。

Method: 两阶段框架：阶段1独立训练多个异构专家流（RealNVP, MAF, RBIG）；阶段2冻结专家参数，通过温度控制的softmax平均对数似然学习全局混合权重，并采用单纯形指数移动平均（sEMA）进行平滑更新，无需逐样本门控或梯度回传。

Result: 在10个后验基准（6个2D合成+4个实际贝叶斯目标）上，AMF-VI-sEMA在NLL、KL散度、Wasserstein-2距离和MMD指标上一致优于AMF-VI和单流基线（NICE, ResFlow等），避免了灾难性传输失败，有效组件数Neff>1.4，计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 基于sEMA的全局混合加权机制是一种简单、稳定且计算高效的后验近似策略，能够自适应分配专家容量并避免组件坍塌，为混合流变分推断提供了一种实用解决方案。

Abstract: Normalising flows provide a powerful variational family for approximate inference, yet individual architectures often fail to generalise across heterogeneous posterior geometries. We revisit mixture-based flow formulations and introduce \emph{AMF\mbox{-}VI\mbox{-}sEMA}, a two-stage framework featuring a \emph{stable global weighting} mechanism based on a \emph{Simplex Exponential Moving Average} (sEMA) update. In Stage~1, a heterogeneous set of experts (\textsc{RealNVP}, \textsc{MAF}, \textsc{RBIG}) are trained independently to specialise in distinct structural regimes. In Stage~2, expert parameters are frozen and global mixture weights are learned through a temperature-controlled softmax of average log-likelihoods, followed by a smooth EMA update on the probability simplex. This design produces a tractable, data-agnostic gating mechanism (without per-sample gating or gradient backpropagation through weights) that adaptively reallocates capacity while avoiding component collapse. We evaluate the framework on ten posterior benchmarks: six canonical 2D synthetic families (Banana, X-Shaped, Bimodal, Multimodal, Two-moons, Rings) and four real/low-dimensional Bayesian targets (BLR, BPR, Weibull, Real-GMM2), with stronger baselines (\textsc{NICE}, \textsc{ResFlow}, and EM-Mixing). Comprehensive evaluation covers NLL, KL divergence, Wasserstein-2 distance, and MMD, together with diagnostics of mixture dynamics, hyperparameter sensitivity, and cross-seed robustness. Empirically, \emph{AMF\mbox{-}VI\mbox{-}sEMA} achieves consistent NLL improvements over its predecessor \emph{AMF\mbox{-}VI} and avoids the catastrophic transport failures of single-flow baselines, while maintaining stable weight trajectories ($N_{\mathrm{eff}}{>}1.4$ on all datasets) with minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [100] [Adversarial LassoNet: Robust Feature Selection via Stability-Driven Sparse Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03839)
*Zhen Huang,Peicheng Xu,Junbiao Pang,Yulong Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Adversarial LassoNet (AdLNet)，将输入空间对抗扰动与 LassoNet 的层次稀疏机制结合，提升高维稀疏特征选择的稳定性与分布外泛化能力。在多种数据集上，AdLNet 在保持竞争性稀疏选择性能的同时，显著提升了分布外鲁棒性和特征支持可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 ℓ1 正则化稀疏特征选择方法在高维环境下对观测噪声和虚假相关脆弱，导致特征支持集不稳定、泛化能力差。尽管对抗训练常用于提升模型鲁棒性，但其与层次化稀疏特征选择的交互机制尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出 Adversarial LassoNet (AdLNet)，将输入空间对抗扰动与 LassoNet 的层次稀疏机制融合。在局部平滑性假设下推导出可计算的一阶对抗近似，并借助 NTK 启发的频谱分析表征扰动训练如何降低梯度集中度。

Result: 在 ColoredMNIST 上，AdLNet 在近匹配支持稀疏度条件下，分布外鲁棒性提升 4.4%，特征支持可重复性提升 6.3%。在高维肺癌筛查数据集上，测试准确率提升 5.3%，AUC 提升 6.0%。在高维 SERS 数据、6 个公开基准数据集上也保持 competitive 稀疏选择性能。

Conclusion: AdLNet 有效结合对抗训练与层次稀疏特征选择，在保持特征选择性能的同时显著提升模型鲁棒性与稳定性，为高维噪声环境下的可靠特征选择提供了新范式。

Abstract: Sparse feature selection is critical for high-dimensional machine learning, yet traditional $\ell_1$-regularized methods are often brittle under observational noise and spurious correlations, leading to unstable feature supports and degraded generalization. Although adversarial training has been widely used to improve model robustness, its interaction with hierarchical sparse feature selection remains underexplored. In this work, we propose Adversarial LassoNet (AdLNet), a stability-driven sparse feature selection framework that integrates input-space adversarial perturbations with the hierarchical sparsity mechanism of LassoNet. We derive a tractable first-order adversarial approximation under local smoothness assumptions and provide an NTK-inspired spectral analysis to characterize how perturbation-driven training can reduce gradient concentration. Experiments on high-dimensional SERS data, six public benchmark datasets, and ColoredMNIST show that AdLNet maintains competitive sparse-selection performance while improving out-of-distribution robustness by 4.4\% and feature support reproducibility by 6.3\% under nearly matched support sparsity on ColoredMNIST. On the high-dimensional lung cancer screening dataset, AdLNet achieves a 5.3\% test accuracy gain and a 6.0\% AUC improvement over vanilla LassoNet. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/719573/Adversarial-LassoNet.

</details>


### [101] [NeSy-CSA: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Open-Ended Critical Scenario Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03847)
*Qitong Chu,Xunjie He,Chen Deng,Huaxin Pei,Yufeng Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NeSy-CSA神经符号框架，将开放式的关键场景归因问题转化为结构化、可追溯的推理过程，通过选择相关因素、构建依赖感知证据图、结合符号推理与神经推理实现灵活归因，在四个决策环境中将归因有效性提升18.32%和13.67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于场景的测试中，理解已发现场景为何变得关键对于决策系统至关重要，但关键性的原因涉及多样化的状态变量、交互模式和失效机制，使归因成为开放性问题，现有方法难以平衡开放推理灵活性与关键场景推理所需的可解释性和可追溯性。

Method: 提出NeSy-CSA神经符号框架：通过选择相关因素缩小归因空间；构建依赖感知证据图使推理过程可追溯；基于原子操作执行符号推理过程，结合证据约束的神经推理支持开放归因；引入过程和结果两级评估模块评估归因结构有效性和行为有效性。

Result: 在四个决策环境中，NeSy-CSA相比基于LLM的基线在两个干预驱动的归因有效性指标上分别提升18.32%和13.67%。

Conclusion: NeSy-CSA能够将发现的关键场景转化为可复用知识，用于后续测试和安全分析。

Abstract: Understanding why discovered scenarios become critical in scenario-based testing is essential for effectively leveraging them in decision-making systems. Reasoning about such criticality can be formulated as an attribution problem. However, across different decision-making tasks, the causes of criticality may involve diverse state variables, interaction patterns, and failure mechanisms, making attribution an inherently open-ended problem beyond predefined explanation spaces. Existing attribution methods still struggle to balance open-ended reasoning flexibility with the interpretability and traceability required for critical scenario reasoning. To address this limitation, we propose NeSy-CSA, a neuro-symbolic framework that transforms open-ended critical scenario attribution from unconstrained explanation generation into structured and traceable reasoning. NeSy-CSA narrows the attribution space by selecting relevant factors, makes the reasoning process traceable through a dependency-aware evidence graph, and executes symbolic reasoning procedures derived from atomic operations, coordinated with evidence-constrained neural inference to support flexible open-ended attribution. We further introduce a process-level and result-level assessment module to evaluate the structural validity of the attribution process and the behavioral effectiveness of the attribution results under controlled interventions. Experiments across four decision-making environments show that NeSy-CSA improves two intervention-based measures of attribution effectiveness by 18.32% and 13.67% over LLM-based baselines. These results demonstrate its potential to transform discovered critical scenarios into reusable knowledge for subsequent testing and safety analysis.

</details>


### [102] [A Unified Framework for Quantized and Continuous Strong Lottery Tickets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03860)
*Aakash Kumar,Emanuele Natale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在量化设置下对强彩票假设（SLTH）进行了严格分析，通过研究离散随机子集和问题（RSSP），获得了指数级改进的失败概率界，并统一了连续与量化两种设置的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLTH理论在量化（离散权重）设置下的结果远不如任意精度（连续权重）设置完善，缺乏统一的理论框架来处理近似表示与精确表示之间的关系。

Method: 对离散设置下的随机子集和问题（RSSP）进行分析，推导出量化情况下找到强彩票的失败概率的紧界，并通过极限分析统一连续近似表示与量化精确表示。

Result: 获得了量化设置下SLTH失败概率的指数级改进的紧界，证明连续近似表示和量化精确表示均可作为所提结果的极限情况。

Conclusion: 统一了连续与量化两种设置下的SLTH理论，提供了一种同时处理近似误差和舍入误差的 cohesive 框架，显著改进了现有界。

Abstract: The Strong Lottery Ticket Hypothesis (SLTH) asserts that sufficiently overparameterized, randomly initialized neural networks contain sparse subnetworks that, even without any training, can match the performance of a small trained network on a given dataset. A key mathematical tool in the theoretical study of SLTH has been the Random Subset Sum Problem (RSSP). The SLTH has recently been extended to the quantized setting, where the network weights are sampled from a discrete set rather than from a continuous interval. These new results are however far from those in arbitrary-precision setting in several ways. In this work, we provide an analysis of the RSSP in the discrete setting, and use it to derive tight SLTH guarantees in the quantized case. Our analysis obtain tight bounds on the failure probability of finding a strong lottery ticket in the quantized regime, providing an exponential improvement over previous results. Most importantly, it unifies the literature by showing that both approximate representations in the continuous setting and exact representations in quantized settings naturally emerge as limiting cases of our results. This perspective not only sharpens existing bounds but also provides a cohesive framework that simultaneously handles approximation and rounding errors.

</details>


### [103] [A Gradient Flow Perspective on Minimum MMD Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03871)
*Sophia Seulkee Kang,Louis Sharrock,Xiaoyuan Cheng,François-Xavier Briol,Zonghao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对最小最大均值差异（MMD）估计中的优化难题，提出了预条件梯度下降（PGD）方案，在显式梯度优势和投影残差条件下建立了渐近全局收敛性，并在参数估计和复合假设检验问题中优于标准梯度下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMD估计虽在实践中成功，但其优化问题缺乏理论保证，现有算法依赖现实中很少成立的凸性假设。

Method: 提出预条件梯度下降（PGD）方案，受MMD梯度流（概率测度空间上的非参数下降方案）启发，建立渐近全局收敛性理论。

Result: PGD方案在多种参数估计和复合假设检验问题上优于标准梯度下降。

Conclusion: PGD方案在非凸设定下为MMD估计提供了理论收敛保证和实际性能提升。

Abstract: Minimum maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) estimation has emerged as a robust and likelihood-free alternative to maximum likelihood estimation for parameter estimation. Yet, despite its practical success, the associated optimization problem remains poorly understood, with theoretical guarantees for existing algorithms hinging on convexity assumptions that rarely hold in practice. We address this gap by proposing a preconditioned gradient descent (PGD) scheme, establishing its asymptotic \emph{global} convergence under explicit gradient-dominance and projection-residual conditions. Our approach is inspired by recent progress on MMD gradient flows, a nonparametric descent scheme on the space of probability measures. We provide extensive empirical evidence that our PGD scheme outperforms standard gradient descent across a range of challenging parameter estimation and composite hypothesis testing problems.

</details>


### [104] [AdaptiveSD A Stability-Aware, Runtime-Adaptive Speculative Decoding Framework with Multi-Policy Orchestration for CPU-Constrained LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03876)
*Sadra Saremi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaptiveSD是一个全运行时自适应推测解码框架，通过四个紧密耦合的组件在连续反馈循环中协同工作，确保在不同模型类型和工作负载下实现稳健可靠的执行，即使在严重内存带宽约束下也能有效部署小型量化GGUF语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定深度推测解码在实践中常因带宽饱和、不稳定性或资源耗尽导致性能下降甚至系统崩溃，而现有方法仅关注最大化吞吐量，忽视了在严重内存带宽约束下的可靠执行问题。

Method: 提出AdaptiveSD框架，包含四个组件：运行时监控引擎（跟踪多个计算信号）、自适应草稿控制器（执行11条规则策略层次结构，优先保护系统资源）、动态策略引擎（结合启发式和强化学习动态调整策略）、KV缓存协调层（通过INT8影子缓冲区和位置感知驱逐实现细粒度缓存管理）。

Result: 通过浪费的草稿计算量、令牌间延迟离散度以及标准推测效率指标等多个关键指标评估有效性，而非仅关注传统吞吐量最大化。

Conclusion: AdaptiveSD通过全运行时自适应机制解决了固定深度推测解码在内存带宽约束下的性能退化问题，为小型量化模型在CPU环境下的可靠部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: With the rise of small quantized GGUF-based language models and their increasing use for on-device inference tasks, we have seen the growing need for an approach capable of reliably delivering these models at scale even under severe memory bandwidth constraints such as those imposed by pure CPU implementations. Fixed-depth speculative decoding has emerged as one promising technique, but in practice, it often leads to performance degradation due to either bandwidth saturation, instability, or even catastrophic resource exhaustion resulting in system failure. To overcome this problem, we introduce AdaptiveSD, a fully runtime-adaptive speculative decoding framework aimed at ensuring robust, reliable execution across the spectrum of model types and workloads. Our solution consists of four tightly-coupled components working together in a continuous feedback loop: a Runtime Monitoring Engine tracking multiple signals relevant to ongoing computation, an Adaptive Draft Controller enforcing an eleven rule policy hierarchy prioritizing system resource preservation over raw draft count, a Dynamic Policy Engine employing a suite of heuristic and reinforcement learning techniques to dynamically modify policies depending upon workload behavior, and finally, a KV Cache Coordination Layer managing cache states with fine-grained control through INT8 shadow buffers and position aware evictions. While conventional approaches focus solely on maximizing throughput, we instead assess the effectiveness of our approach based on several key metrics including wasted drafted compute and inter-token latency dispersion alongside standard measures of speculative efficiency.

</details>


### [105] [CDCP: Conditional Diffusion Model with Contextual Prompts for Multi-task Offline Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03903)
*Jiayi Guan,Tianle Zhang,Li Shen,Ruiqi Zhang,Ao Zhou,Lusong Li,Guai Chen,Mengjie Li,Alois Knoll,Xiaodong He,Changjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出条件扩散模型结合上下文提示（CDCP），将多任务离线安全强化学习转化为条件生成问题，通过无分类器引导的成本约束策略、上下文提示方法和梯度损失同步策略，在多个任务场景下实现了比现有方法更高的性能和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务离线安全强化学习面临多任务、安全约束和分布外动作的三重挑战，现有方法难以在保证安全的同时最大化奖励回报。

Method: 1) 将多任务约束优化问题转化为条件生成问题，使用扩散模型；2) 设计无分类器引导的成本约束策略，通过监督学习消除分布外动作的外推误差；3) 引入上下文提示方法增强多任务表示准确性和对未见任务的适应性；4) 提出梯度损失同步策略消除梯度干扰，提升训练稳定性。

Result: 在多个多任务场景下，CDCP算法比当前最先进的基线方法表现出更高的性能和安全性，且无需进一步训练即可满足不同的成本约束。

Conclusion: CDCP为多任务安全强化学习提供了一种更灵活的成本约束解决方案，有效解决了多任务、安全约束和分布外动作的三重挑战。

Abstract: Multi-task offline safe reinforcement learning (RL) promises to learn a shared optimal safe policy from offline data across multiple tasks. This paradigm provides an effective means for the widespread application of RL in multi-task scenarios with high risk and interaction costs. However, the triple challenges of multi-tasking, safety constraints, and out-of-distribution (OOD) actions pose a significant hurdle for existing methods to ensure safety while maximizing reward returns. In this work, we propose a Conditional Diffusion model with Contextual Prompts (CDCP) to address these challenges. Concretely, we first rethink the requirements and challenges in current multi-task decision-making and control scenarios and establish the objectives of multi-task offline safe RL. Subsequently, we transform the multi-task constrained optimization problem into a conditional generation problem using the diffusion model. Based on this, we design a classifier-free guided cost-constraint strategy to provide flexible cost constraints and eliminate extrapolation errors from OOD actions via supervised learning. Additionally, we introduce a novel contextual prompting method to enhance multi-task representation accuracy and adaptability to unseen tasks. A gradient loss synchronization strategy is also introduced to eliminate gradient interference, improving training stability. Finally, extensive experiments demonstrate that the CDCP algorithm exhibits higher performance and safety in multi-task scenarios than the current state-of-the-art baseline methods. It meets different cost constraints without further training, providing a more flexible cost-constraint solution for the multi-task safe RL.

</details>


### [106] [NeuroOnline: Bridging Pretraining and Online Adaptation for EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03925)
*Weibin Li,Wendu Li,Yushan You,Chen Wei,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出在线神经适应框架NeuroOnline，通过多视图一致性学习和上下文感知表征调制解决EEG基础模型在在线场景中的分布偏移与任务目标对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型采用预训练-静态部署范式，存在两个关键局限：(1)预训练目标与下游任务之间不对齐；(2)在线环境中面对分布偏移时适应性有限。

Method: NeuroOnline包含两个互补机制：(1)多视图一致性学习——通过跨视图对齐促进一致且任务相关的表征；(2)上下文感知表征调制——利用可学习上下文提示与交叉注意力动态适应数据分布变化。两者统一了表征对齐与动态适应。

Result: 在多个EEG基准上，NeuroOnline在在线设置下持续优于强基线方法，在分布偏移场景下表现更佳。消融与敏感性研究验证了各组件的必要性和整体设计的有效性。

Conclusion: NeuroOnline框架有效统一了表征对齐与动态适应，为EEG基础模型在在线场景中的部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: EEG foundation models have shown strong potential in learning generalized representations across subjects and tasks. However, most existing approaches follow a pretraining-static deployment paradigm, which suffers from two key limitations: (1) misalignment between pretraining objectives and downstream tasks, and (2) limited adaptability to distribution shifts in online settings. We propose Online Neural Adaptation (NeuroOnline), a unified framework that enables continuous adaptation in online scenarios. NeuroOnline integrates two complementary mechanisms: (1) multi-view consistency learning, which enforces cross-view alignment to promote consistent and task-relevant representations, and (2) context-aware representation modulation, which leverages a learnable context prompt with cross-attention to dynamically adapt representations to evolving data distributions. Together, these mechanisms unify representation alignment and dynamic adaptation. Experiments on multiple EEG benchmarks show that NeuroOnline consistently outperforms strong baselines in online settings, achieving better performance under distribution shifts. Ablation and sensitivity studies further validate the necessity of each component and the effectiveness of the overall design.

</details>


### [107] [MPSelectTune: Prompt-type Selection for Fine-tuning improves Concept Unlearning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03932)
*Shubhadip Nag,Srinjoy Das,Agniva Saha,Anushree Ghosh,Soumi Das,Tarun Kumar,Suparna Bhattacharya,Sourangshu Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MPSelectTune方法，通过对抗性多提示学习来消除预训练LLM中的有害概念，在不牺牲主任务性能的前提下，将最坏情况下的概念准确率降低高达17%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘方法通常忽略提示变体对模型不安全响应的影响，无法在不同提示类型下鲁棒地消除偏见或有害概念。

Method: 提出MPSelectTune两阶段方法：首先使用多种提示类型进行多任务损失微调，然后最小化具有最高概念准确率的提示类型对应的概念准确率（即最坏提示类型对抗优化）。

Result: 在四个基准测试上，相比近期基线方法，主任务准确率提升2-15%，最坏情况概念准确率降低高达17%。

Conclusion: 通过针对最坏提示类型的对抗性优化，能够更有效地实现跨提示类型的鲁棒概念遗忘。

Abstract: LLMs can be conveniently adapted to a diverse set of tasks, e.g, prediction, question-answering tasks, etc, using appropriate prompts with few-shot examples. Biased or harmful concepts, e.g. gender or bio-weapons, present in pre-trained LLMs can lead to unsafe or unethical responses for many such prompts.
