We propose a novel physics-informed message passing operator that encodes linear advection on metric graphs as an inductive bias. In the purely advective setting, the operator provably recovers the exact dynamics up to a theoretically derived discretization error without any training. Combined with trainable components like MLPs, our message passing operator extends to realistic advection-reaction dynamics in water distribution systems, where we achieve superior performance compared to baselines and zero-shot generalization across different graph topologies.

</details>


### [213] [Platonic Projection Structures: Operator-Induced Observability in Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05175)
*Kazuo Ishii,Bishnu Prasad Gautam,Jieling Wu,Javaid Saher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Platonic投影结构（PPS），一个基于算子理论的框架，用于表征部分观测下表示学习的可观测性。通过自伴正半定观测算子在潜表示空间上诱导商几何，统一描述了量子测量、表示推理、知识蒸馏等场景中表示的可访问性与可解释性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示学习缺乏对部分观测下表示可访问性的形式化刻画。观测到的输出并非潜表示的直接反映，传统方法难以区分哪些潜成分是本质可观测的、哪些是不可恢复的，导致可解释性方法存在根本性局限。

Method: 将系统建模为三元组(H, Π, O)，其中H为潜表示空间，Π为自伴正半定观测算子，O(v)=⟨v,Πv⟩为诱导标量可观测。可观测性通过商空间H/ker(Π)刻画，代表观测下不可区分的潜状态等价类。

Result: ①量子测量与线性观测下的表示推理共享相同的算子-商结构，差异仅在于观测算子的代数性质；②表示迁移与知识蒸馏可解释为ΦΠ_T≈Π_SΦ的近似保持；③输出基可解释性存在结构限制：ker(Π)中的潜成分完全不可观测，对归因与解释方法施加内在约束；④实验验证了核不变可观测性、投影诱导归因差距及秩控制的可观测几何。

Conclusion: PPS通过算子诱导的商几何显式刻画了可观测性，为表示可访问性、可解释性与投影介导推理提供了统一视角。

Abstract: We characterize observability in representation learning through Platonic Projection Structures (PPS), an operator-theoretic framework for analyzing representation accessibility under partial observation. Rather than treating observable outputs as direct reflections of latent representations, PPS models observation through a self-adjoint positive semidefinite operator acting on a latent representation space. A system is represented as a triple $(H, Π, O)$, where $H$ is a latent representation space, $Π\succeq 0$ is an observation operator, and $O(v)=\langle v,Πv\rangle$ defines an induced scalar observable. Observability is characterized by the quotient geometry $H/\ker(Π)$, representing equivalence classes of latent states indistinguishable under observation. We show that quantum measurement and representation inference under linear observation models share this operator-theoretic structure while differing in the algebraic properties of their observation operators; the correspondence is structural rather than physical. Representation transfer and knowledge distillation can likewise be interpreted as approximate preservation of observable geometry through $ΦΠ_T \approx Π_S Φ$. PPS also reveals a structural limitation of output-based interpretability: latent components in $\ker(Π)$ are inaccessible from induced observables, imposing intrinsic constraints on attribution and explanation methods. Controlled empirical validations demonstrate kernel-invariant observability, projection-induced attribution gaps, and rank-controlled observable geometry in latent representation spaces. PPS thus provides an explicit characterization of observability through operator-induced quotient geometry and a unified perspective on representation accessibility, interpretability, and projection-mediated inference.

</details>


### [214] [Relational Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Dynamic Pricing in High-Speed Railway Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05179)
*Enrique Adrian Villarrubia-Martin,David Muñoz-Valero,Luis Rodriguez-Benitez,Giovanni Montana,Luis Jimenez-Linares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对铁路定价中部分可观测的多智能体强化学习问题，提出了一种基于实体图建模的方法，将环境表示为运营单元图，并通过图卷积网络和注意力机制扩展MATD3算法，实验表明该方法在收入与稳定性上优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在放松管制的铁路系统中，运营商需在部分可观测环境下动态定价，且受监管约束无法直接通信，必须仅从市场数据中推断策略互动。标准多智能体强化学习将观测视为非结构化向量，忽略了市场拓扑结构，导致学习效率低下。

Method: 提出实体图建模方法，将环境表示为运营单元（而非智能体或基础设施）的图结构，编码竞争、协调和连通关系；在此基础上扩展MATD3算法，通过多层关系图卷积网络处理实体特征，并利用注意力机制进行聚合。

Result: 在铁路定价强化学习环境中，所提框架在两种不同市场复杂度设置下均实现了比一组关系型和非关系型基线方法更高的收入和更好的稳定性。

Conclusion: 图结构建模能够有效捕捉市场拓扑中隐含的战略互动关系，结合图表示学习的多智能体强化学习在部分可观测的铁路定价场景中具有显著优势，代码已开源。

Abstract: In liberalised railway systems, operators must set prices dynamically in an environment with partial observability, as they retain private information about their objectives and performance, where regulatory constraints prohibit communication or direct information exchange between competitors to prevent explicit collusion. Consequently, agents must learn to infer strategic interactions only from observable market data which presents a significant challenge for multi-agent reinforcement learning, where standard approaches typically treat observations as unstructured vectors, ignoring the underlying market topology that governs strategic interactions. To address this, an entity graph modelling approach is proposed, which represents the environment as a graph of operational units, rather than decision-making agents or static infrastructure, encoding competition, coordination, and connectivity relations between entities. Then, an extension of the multi-agent twin delayed deep deterministic policy gradient algorithm with graph-based representation learning processes the features of the entities through a multi-layer relational graph convolutional network and aggregates them via a learnt attention mechanism. Experimental results in a rail pricing reinforcement learning environment show that this novel framework achieves higher revenue and stability in two different settings of increasing market complexity compared to a representative selection of relational and non-relational baselines. The code is publicly available at: https://github.com/Kinrre/RelationalRailPricing-RL

</details>


### [215] [SMART: A Machine Learning and Monte Carlo Framework for Rapid Analysis of Stochastic Transistor Aging and Process Variation in Digital Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05187)
*Arash Esshaghi,Siavash Es'haghi,Gholamreza Shahabadi,Alireza Moradi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SMART是一个将机器学习（随机森林回归）与蒙特卡洛模拟相结合的框架，用于高效预测数字电路门延迟分布，在ISCAS85基准测试中实现了94.54%的分析时间缩减和1.63%的平均精度误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着CMOS技术进入深纳米时代，BTI和工艺变化的随机效应严重威胁数字电路可靠性。传统可靠性分析方法依赖计算密集型仿真或大型查找表，在大规模设计中难以扩展，成为设计空间探索的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出SMART框架，采用随机森林回归直接预测门延迟分布，绕过耗时的原子模型参数提取；结合贝叶斯优化进行超参数自动调优，确保跨库的预测鲁棒性；将计算开销转移到离线训练阶段。

Result: 在ISCAS85基准电路上，相比最先进方法，分析时间减少94.54%，平均精度误差仅为1.63%。

Conclusion: SMART通过离线训练转移计算复杂度，为设计弹性、可靠性感知的数字系统提供了可扩展且准确的解决方案。

Abstract: As CMOS technology scales into the deep nanometer regime, digital circuit reliability is increasingly threatened by the combined stochastic effects of Bias Temperature Instability (BTI) and Process Variation (PV). Traditional reliability analysis methods, which rely on computationally intensive simulations or extensive lookup tables, fail to scale efficiently for large designs, creating a critical bottleneck in design space exploration. To address this, we propose SMART, a novel framework that integrates Machine Learning (ML) with Monte Carlo simulation to enable rapid, high-fidelity reliability analysis. SMART employs Random Forest regression to predict gate delay distributions directly, bypassing time-consuming atomic model parameter extractions. Crucially, the model utilizes Bayesian Optimization for automated hyperparameter tuning, ensuring maximum predictive robustness across diverse libraries. Experimental validation on ISCAS85 benchmark circuits demonstrates that SMART achieves a 94.54% reduction in analysis time compared to state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining a remarkable average accuracy error of just 1.63%. By shifting computational complexity to an offline training phase, the proposed framework offers a scalable, accurate solution for designing resilient, reliability-aware digital systems.

</details>


### [216] [Latent Programming Horizons in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05188)
*André Silva,Han Tu,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现编码智能体中语言模型的隐层状态线性编码了程序属性（如语法正确性、测试通过率等），并且这些表征能提前预测未来编辑结果（最远约25步），称为“潜在编程视界”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对编码智能体内部表征（特别是模型如何理解正在编写的程序）知之甚少，缺乏对智能体运行时语言模型内部状态的机理分析。

Method: 在编码智能体运行时，从语言模型的残差流中提取隐层状态，训练逻辑回归探针（logistic-regression probe）来解码当前代码属性（是否可解析、是否通过测试、失败测试数变化、回归引入等），并预测未来编辑结果。

Result: 探针在正确性预测上AUC最高达0.83；能提前约25步预测未来编辑结果（超过随机水平）；探针无需重新训练即可跨基准迁移。

Conclusion: 编码智能体的语言模型隐层状态不仅编码当前程序属性，还超前表征未来状态，揭示了“潜在编程视界”现象，呼吁更多面向编码智能体的机械可解释性研究。

Abstract: A coding agent solving a software-engineering task spends dozens of steps reasoning, editing code, and running tests, yet little is known about what the underlying language model internally represents about the program it is working on. We show that the residual streams of language models under coding agents linearly encode properties of the evolving program: a logistic-regression probe on hidden states is able to decode whether the current code parses, passes its test suite, reduces the number of failing tests, and introduces regressions, reaching AUC up to 0.83 for correctness across two models and two benchmarks. Our second finding is more surprising: these representations run ahead of the agent's own edits. Probes trained to predict the outcome of future edits (before they are materialized and written on disk) achieve performance above chance up to roughly 25 steps in advance. We call this the agent's latent programming horizon. As a proof of external validity, we show that the probes transfer across benchmarks without retraining. Our positive results open calls for more research in mechanistic interpretability of coding agents.

</details>


### [217] [Noisy-Channel Minimum Bayes Risk Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05198)
*Yusuke Sakai,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于噪声通道分解的最小贝叶斯风险（MBR）解码方法，通过引入双向似然（假设到参考和参考到假设）、假设先验和参考先验四个组件，统一解释了现有MBR变体，并揭示了不同指标和任务下的通道贡献特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MBR解码在选择假设时只计算给定伪参考下的期望效用分数，而常用评估指标（如BLEU、COMET）是非对称的。这种设计上的不一致性导致需要同时考虑假设到参考和参考到假设的双向方向效应。

Method: 将MBR解码分解为四个交互组件：假设到参考似然、参考到假设似然、假设先验和参考先验。通过噪声通道分解自然地融合了双向效应，以解决指标不对称问题。

Result: 通道级贡献在不同指标下表现出不同特征，但在任务间保持一致性；适当的通道加权可能带来优于原始MBR解码的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该分解为现有MBR变体提供了统一解释，并实现了指标和任务特定的可解释性，为改进MBR解码提供了方向。

Abstract: Minimum Bayes Risk (MBR) decoding yields more robust and higher-quality text generation than maximum a posteriori (MAP) decoding by selecting hypotheses that maximize expected utility over sampled pseudo-references. However, there exists a discrepancy in the design: hypothesis selection calculates expected utility scores conditioned on given pseudo-references, while commonly used evaluation metrics, e.g., BLEU and COMET, are asymmetric. Therefore, it is important to consider both hypothesis-to-reference and reference-to-hypothesis directional effects. In this study, we introduce a noisy channel decomposition of MBR decoding that naturally incorporates bidirectional effects to account for these asymmetries. We decompose MBR decoding into four interacting components: hypothesis-to-reference likelihood, reference-to-hypothesis likelihood, hypothesis prior, and reference prior. This decomposition provides a unified interpretation of existing MBR variants and enables metric- and task-specific interpretability by isolating the contribution of each channel. Our comprehensive analysis reveals that channel-wise contributions exhibit distinct characteristics across metrics while remaining consistent across tasks, and suggests that appropriate channel weighting may lead to improvements over original MBR decoding.

</details>


### [218] [FlatManifold: Robust Continual Learning under Severe Label Noise and Domain Shifts via Intrinsic Manifold Flattening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05201)
*Rai Hisada,Kanji Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlatManifold提出了一种基于Nyström流形展平映射和正交化RKHS投影的鲁棒持续学习框架，通过特征空间的数学结构线性化同时对抗灾难性遗忘和严重标签噪声，在含40%对称标签噪声的多会话机器人数据上显著优于baseline。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳流式环境中，模型需要同时应对复杂的非线性域漂移（持续学习）和严重的非校准标签噪声，现有基于样本过滤的管线复杂且易出错，缺乏数学层面的鲁棒性保证。

Method: 利用核技巧的Nyström流形展平映射，将特征分布投影到具有ridge正则化的固定正交目标拓扑（正交化RKHS），通过持续拓扑制动项（利用历史协方差矩阵）防止灾难性遗忘，利用展平空间的固有数学鲁棒性平滑极端标签噪声对梯度的影响。

Result: 在含40%对称标签噪声的真实多会话机器人数据集上，以及跨季节、光照条件的极端域漂移场景下，FlatManifold显著优于标准序列优化基线，有效缓解梯度破坏并展现高泛化能力。

Conclusion: 结构线性化本身可作为对抗分布式标签破坏的强大数学屏障，证明了在RKHS中实现拓扑固定的流形展平是一种简洁且鲁棒的持续学习范式。

Abstract: In non-stationary streaming environments, simultaneously adapting to complex, non-linear domain shifts via continual learning while mitigating the catastrophic effects of severe, uncalibrated label noise poses a fundamental mathematical challenge. In this paper, we propose \FlatManifold{}, a novel, streamlined robust continual learning framework that utilizes a Nyström manifold flattening map based on the kernel trick and projection onto an orthogonalized Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space (RKHS).
  Unlike traditional methods that rely on complex, error-prone sample-filtering pipelines, the proposed approach exploits the intrinsic mathematical robustness of the flattened space itself. By mapping feature distributions onto a fixed orthogonal target topology with a ridge regularizer, the framework naturally smoothes and counteracts the influence of extreme label noise during the optimization process. Concurrently, catastrophic forgetting is prevented via a continual topology brake term that leverages the covariance matrix of past experiences.
  Extensive evaluation on real-world multi-session robotics datasets demonstrates that even under severe conditions featuring 40\% symmetric label noise, \FlatManifold{} successfully mitigates gradient corruption. Under extreme cross-session domain shifts spanning various seasons and lighting conditions, the proposed framework establishes high generalization capabilities, significantly outperforming standard sequential optimization baselines and proving that structural linearization itself serves as a powerful mathematical barrier against distributed label corruption.

</details>


### [219] [Video-based detection of cessation of breathing in pre-term infants using machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05230)
*Dineo Serame,Lionel Tarassenko,Mauricio Villarroel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于摄像头的非接触式视频监测技术用于早产儿呼吸暂停（COBE）检测。在30名早产儿数据上，纯视频模型达到76.9%的平衡准确率，而视频与阻抗呼吸图（IP）结合的混合模型将准确率提升至90.6%，表明视频信号能提供超越传统生理信号的呼吸信息，可作为新生儿重症监护室呼吸监测的有效补充手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早产儿因呼吸控制发育不成熟，易发生呼吸暂停导致缺氧损伤。现有接触式呼吸监测（如阻抗呼吸图、心电图衍生呼吸等）在NICU中面临运动伪影、传感器移位和皮肤脆弱等挑战。因此需要探索非接触式视频监测作为补充方案，实现更可靠的呼吸暂停检测。

Method: 使用30名早产儿的视频和临床记录数据，从动态跟踪的躯干区域提取呼吸运动生成视频时间序列信号。采用残差网络（ResNet）架构训练纯视频模型；同时构建混合模型，将视频衍生信号与阻抗呼吸图（IP）、心电图衍生呼吸（EDR）及光电容积脉搏波呼吸包络相结合。

Result: 纯视频模型检测COBE的平衡准确率为76.9%；视频特征与IP结合的混合模型平衡准确率提升至90.6%，优于任一单独模态的表现。

Conclusion: 视频衍生信号包含临床相关的呼吸特征，能够增强COBE检测性能。非接触式视频监测可作为自动COBE检测的补充模态，有潜力提高新生儿呼吸监测的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Pre-term infants are susceptible to potentially harmful apnoea-related cessations of breathing due to immature respiratory control. However, reliable respiratory monitoring in the neonatal intensive care unit (NICU) remains challenging because motion artefacts, sensor displacement, and skin fragility can compromise contact-based measurements. Non-contact video monitoring offers a complementary approach that does not depend on adhesive sensors while providing additional respiratory information.
  We investigated whether camera-based signals can detect apnoea-related cessation of breathing (COBE) and provide complementary information to routinely acquired physiological signals. Using video and clinical recordings from 30 pre-term infants, respiratory motion was extracted from dynamically tracked torso regions to generate camera-derived time-series signals. Camera-only models were trained using residual network (ResNet) architectures, while hybrid models combined video-derived signals with impedance pneumography (IP), ECG-derived respiration (EDR), and the PPG-derived respiratory envelope.
  Camera-only models achieved a balanced accuracy of 76.9%, demonstrating the feasibility of non-contact COBE detection. Combining video-derived features with IP improved balanced accuracy to 90.6%, outperforming either modality alone and indicating that video provides respiratory information beyond standard physiological signals.
  These findings show that video-derived signals contain clinically relevant respiratory features and enhance COBE detection when combined with conventional physiological signals. This supports non-contact video as a complementary modality for automated COBE detection and highlights its potential to improve the robustness of neonatal respiratory monitoring.

</details>


### [220] [CanniUplift: A Holistic Framework for Mitigating Seller and Incentive Cannibalization in E-commerce Uplift Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05242)
*Zuwang He,Shihao Shu,Yuli Qu,Hanyu Gao,Ziliang Zhang,Diwei Chen,Xiangda Yan,Buyu Gao,Tanchao Zhu,Yumeng Li,Junxiong Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CanniUplift框架，通过平台级全局对齐（PGA）和基于赎回的分解去噪（RDD）机制，解决多卖家环境下因违反SUTVA假设导致的卖家级和激励级双重蚕食效应，在合成和工业数据集上显著提升增量效果，线上部署实现平台增量GMV提升4.08%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统提升模型在多卖家复杂电商环境中违反SUTVA假设，面临卖家级蚕食（激励导致支出在店铺间转移而非平台增长）和激励级蚕食（自然转化或替代奖励引入噪声）两大挑战，亟需统一框架来缓解双重蚕食效应。

Method: 提出CanniUplift框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）平台级全局对齐（PGA）通过全局GMV一致性约束捕捉跨店替代效应；（2）基于赎回的分解去噪（RDD）利用赎回行为在全空间框架内分解处理结果并减少归因噪声；（3）Treat-Attention机制建模用户历史行为与当前处理选项间的复杂交互。

Result: 在合成和工业数据集上显著超越SOTA基线；消融实验证明PGA和RDD持续提升wAUUC和wQINI；线上部署实现平台增量GMV相对提升4.08%，并改善ROI。

Conclusion: CanniUplift框架有效解决了多卖家环境下的双重蚕食问题，能准确估计个体处理效应，驱动平台整体增长。

Abstract: Personalized incentive allocation is vital for e-commerce, where uplift modeling is the standard for estimating Individual Treatment Effects (ITE). However, traditional models often fail in complex multi-seller environments with violations of the Stable Unit Treatment Value Assumption (SUTVA). We identify two critical challenges: Seller-level Cannibalization, where incentives shift expenditure between shops without growing the platform, and Incentive-level Cannibalization, where organic conversions or alternative rewards introduce significant noise into incrementality estimation. In this paper, we propose CanniUplift, a unified framework to mitigate these dual-source cannibalization effects. Specifically, we design Platform-level Global Alignment (PGA) to capture cross-shop substitution through global GMV consistency constraints. To tackle incentive-driven noise, we introduce Redemption-based Decomposition Denoising (RDD), which uses redemption behavior to decompose treated outcomes and reduce attribution noise within an entire-space framework. Furthermore, a Treat-Attention mechanism is designed to model intricate interactions between users' historical behaviors and current treatment options. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and large-scale industrial datasets demonstrate that CanniUplift significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Ablation studies confirm that the integration of PGA and RDD consistently improves wAUUC and wQINI. Successfully deployed online, our framework achieved a 4.08% relative increase in platform-wide incremental GMV (Delta GMV) over the production baseline and improved ROI in online A/B tests, proving effective in driving global platform growth.

</details>


### [221] [FUSE: FK-Steered Multi-Modal Flow Matching for Efficient Simulation-Based Posterior Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05252)
*Weichen Qin,Yufan Xie,Peihao Wang,Chia-Jui Chou,Minghui Du,Peng Xu,Ziren Luo,Yi Yang,Jingyi Yu,Bo Liang,Jiakai Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FUSE方法，通过双轨架构保留多模态输入异构特征，并利用FK引导采样策略优化生成轨迹，在SBI基准和真实系外行星轨道估计任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于生成模型的SBI方法在多模态建模中依赖暴力融合策略，忽略了参数与观测之间的结构性差异，限制了估计保真度。

Method: 提出双轨架构分别处理参数和观测数据的异构特征并实现动态交互；设计FK（Feynman-Kac）引导采样策略，利用中间观测似然指导生成轨迹，提升推理阶段样本质量。

Result: 在标准SBI基准上超越现有基线，生成的后验分布与真实MCMC高度吻合；在真实系外行星轨道估计任务中成功解析了现有方法难以处理的参数简并问题。

Conclusion: FUSE有效提升了SBI多模态推理的精度，有望加速天体物理学及其他领域的科学发现。

Abstract: Simulation-Based Inference (SBI) is critical for scientific discovery, with generative models offering a promising path toward efficient inference. However, existing methods struggle with effective multimodal modeling. They often rely on brute-force fusion strategies that ignore the structural disparities between parameters and observations, thus limiting estimation fidelity. In this work, we introduce FUSE (Feynman-Kac steered mUlti-modal flow matching for efficient Simulation-based posterior Estimation). Unlike prior work, FUSE employs a dual-track architecture that preserves the distinct features of multimodal inputs while facilitating dynamic interaction. Additionally, we propose an FK-steered sampling strategy that leverages intermediate observation likelihoods to guide the generative trajectories, effectively improving the sample quality during inference. Our approach outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on standard SBI benchmarks, producing posteriors that closely match ground-truth MCMC. Furthermore, in a real-world exoplanet orbital estimation task, FUSE successfully resolves complex parameter degeneracies that challenge existing methods, highlighting its potential to accelerate complex scientific discoveries in astrophysics and beyond.

</details>


### [222] [GeoFlow: Geo-Aware Modeling of Inter-Area Relationships in Origin-Destination Flow Prediction and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05257)
*Zherui Huang,Guanjie Zheng,Hao Xue,Linghe Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoFlow是一个融合地理空间属性的OD流建模框架，通过结合图注意力和坐标感知编码器，并采用轴向-全局注意力解码器与流匹配模型，在预测精度和生成质量上均达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于图的OD流建模方法往往忽略了显著的地理属性，限制了对长距离和多区域依赖关系的建模能力。

Method: GeoFlow包含三个核心设计：(i) 通过相对位置、k跳距离和测地距离增强区域表示；(ii) 几何-内在融合编码器，结合图注意力处理内在区域信号与坐标感知编码器捕捉全局结构；(iii) 轴向-全局注意力解码器捕获OD特定的竞争依赖关系，并与流匹配模型结合生成更真实的样本。

Result: GeoFlow在预测精度上达到最优性能，同时显著提升了生成的真实性和多样性。消融实验验证了各组件的贡献。

Conclusion: GeoFlow通过显式引入地理空间属性，有效解决了图方法在长距离和多区域依赖建模上的局限性，为OD流预测与生成提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Origin-destination (OD) flow modeling underpins urban planning and mobility analysis, but prevailing graph-based methods often neglect salient geographic attributes, limiting their ability to model long-range and multi-area dependencies. In this paper, we introduce GeoFlow, a novel framework that (i) augments area representations with geospatial attributes, including relative positions, k-hop and geodesic distances, (ii) employs a specialized geometric-intrinsic fusion encoder design that combines graph attention for intrinsic area signals with coordinate-aware encoders for global structure, and (iii) adopts an axial-global attention decoder to capture OD-specific competitive dependencies. For OD flow generation, GeoFlow is paired with flow matching models to produce more authentic and diverse mobility samples. Empirically, GeoFlow achieves superior performance in predictive accuracy, while substantially improving generative fidelity and diversity. Ablation and analytical studies confirm the contribution of each component. Code is available at https://github.com/ZheruiHuang/GeoFlow.

</details>


### [223] [Target-Guided Selective Reweighting for Physics-Informed Neural Network Inverse Problems: A Transfer Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05271)
*Qian Hu,Bin Fan,Yao Xiao,Zhicheng Lin,Meixin Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出目标引导选择性重加权PINN（TGSR-PINN），通过神经元目标评分、高斯混合模型弱适应信号估计和选择性软衰减，解决PINN逆向迁移学习中的负迁移问题，在保持场精度的同时提升目标参数恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN在PDE逆问题中面临不适定优化、损失竞争和参数补偿问题；迁移学习中直接微调在源域与目标域物理机制、控制参数或观测噪声差异较大时，虽能降低场误差但会恢复错误的物理参数（负迁移），亟需一种目标证据驱动的表示矫正方法。

Method: TGSR-PINN仅迁移源PINN的权重和偏置，独立初始化目标物理参数；经过短目标适应阶段后，利用一阶泰勒敏感度和预激活方差计算神经元目标评分，通过高斯混合模型（含秩回退）将低分神经元证据转化为连续弱适应信号，对低分神经元的输入权重行和偏置进行选择性软衰减（而非硬剪枝或随机重置）。

Result: 在高Péclet数二维对流扩散任务、Allen-Cahn到Burgers跨PDE族迁移任务中，TGSR-PINN在保持相当场精度的同时改善了目标参数恢复；5%噪声反应扩散案例在较温和源-目标失配下提供补充证据。消融实验表明神经元目标评分、弱适应信号估计、层保护及选择性软衰减共同贡献效果。

Conclusion: TGSR-PINN通过目标证据驱动的选择性软衰减策略，有效缓解了PINN逆向迁移学习中的负迁移问题，为物理信息神经网络在跨域PDE逆问题中的应用提供了可行的表示矫正框架。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) encounter ill-posed optimization, loss competition, and parameter compensation in partial differential equation (PDE) inverse problems. Transfer learning can reuse representations from source tasks, but direct fine-tuning may introduce negative transfer when dominant physical mechanisms, governing parameters, or observation noise differ between source and target domains: the model achieves low field error yet recovers incorrect target physical parameters. To mitigate, we propose Target-Guided Selective Reweighting PINN (TGSR-PINN), a target-evidence-driven representation correction method for PINN inverse transfer learning. TGSR-PINN transfers only the weights and biases from the source PINN, while target physical parameters are independently initialized; after a short target-adaptation phase, the method computes neuron target scores using first-order Taylor sensitivity and pre-activation variance on fixed scoring batches, and converts evidence associated with low-scoring neurons into continuous weak-adaptation signals via a Gaussian mixture model (GMM) with rank fallback. TGSR-PINN then applies selective soft decay to input weight rows and biases of low-scoring neurons instead of hard pruning or random resetting. In experiments, TGSR-PINN improves target parameter recovery while maintaining comparable field accuracy in the high-Péclet 2D advection-diffusion task and in the Allen--Cahn to Burgers cross-PDE-family transfer task; a 5%-noise reaction--diffusion case provides supplementary evidence under milder source-target mismatch. Ablation studies suggest that neuron target scoring, weak-adaptation signal estimation, layer protection, and selective soft decay jointly contribute to the benefits.

</details>


### [224] [Adaptive Inference Batching using Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05272)
*Ruslan Sharifullin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨强化学习（RL）在推理服务系统中学习自适应批处理和路由策略的可行性。主要发现：在单GPU设置下，良好调整的静态批处理策略已接近最优，RL仅带来边际提升（+0.1%至+1.0%）；而在多GPU异构路由场景中，RL能发现工作负载分离策略，消除队头阻塞，相比Round-Robin提升3.5倍（348%），相比最强启发式基线（Shortest-Queue）提升48%，吞吐量提高60%，延迟降低25%且满足SLA约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理服务系统需要在突发性、异构性工作负载下平衡吞吐量和延迟，但行业标准仍采用需要手动调优且无法适应流量变化的静态批处理策略。研究旨在探索RL是否能学习出超越这些启发式方法的自适应批处理和路由策略。

Method: 将问题建模为基于队列状态、请求类型和GPU可用性的MDP，在经排队论和生产轨迹（Azure Functions, BurstGPT）验证的离散事件模拟器上训练REINFORCE和PPO智能体，在标准泊松流量、极端突发、真实世界轨迹和异构多GPU路由等多种场景下评估。

Result: 单GPU场景下RL仅带来边际提升（+0.1%至+1.0%）；多GPU异构路由中，RL发现工作负载分离策略，实现3.5倍（348%）优于Round-Robin、48%优于Shortest-Queue的效果，吞吐量提高60%，延迟降低25%且满足SLA约束。注意力增强策略网络相比MLP基线收敛速度快约20%。

Conclusion: RL相对于工程化启发式方法的优势集中在组合性、多资源决策问题上，而非单资源时序调度。这一区分为生产中决定何处值得投入学习策略的工程成本提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Inference serving systems must balance throughput and latency under bursty, heterogeneous workloads, yet the industry standard remains static batching policies that require manual tuning and cannot adapt to shifting traffic. We investigate whether reinforcement learning (RL) can learn adaptive batching and routing policies that outperform these heuristics, training REINFORCE and PPO agents on a discrete-event simulator validated against queuing theory and production traces (Azure Functions, BurstGPT). We formulate the problem as an MDP over queue state, request type and GPU availability, evaluating across standard Poisson traffic, extreme bursts, real-world traces and heterogeneous multi-GPU routing.
  Our central finding is a clear boundary condition for RL's value in systems problems. In single-GPU settings, a well-tuned static batching policy is already near-optimal under Poisson-like arrivals and RL offers only marginal gains (+0.1% to +1.0%). In multi-GPU heterogeneous routing, however, where fast and slow requests compete for shared resources, the agent discovers a workload-segregation policy that eliminates Head-of-Line blocking, yielding a 3.5x (348%) improvement over Round-Robin and a 48% improvement over the strongest heuristic baseline (Shortest-Queue), with 60% higher throughput and 25% lower latency while respecting SLA constraints. The policy generalizes to unseen bursty and real-world traffic despite training only on synthetic Poisson arrivals and an attention-augmented policy network converges roughly 20% faster than an MLP baseline.
  These results suggest RL's advantage over engineered heuristics concentrates in combinatorial, multi-resource decisions rather than single-resource temporal scheduling, a practical distinction for deciding where learned policies justify their engineering cost in production inference infrastructure.

</details>


### [225] [Advances in Neural Controlled Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05280)
*Benjamin Walker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对神经控制微分方程（NCDE）在训练和可扩展性上的计算瓶颈，提出了三种改进方法：Log-NCDE（利用Log-ODE方法加速近似求解）、Linear NCDE（使用线性向量场实现闭式解和并行计算）以及Structured Linear NCDE（采用结构化线性向量场进一步提升效率），在保持理论表达能力的同时将每步训练时间降低三个数量级，并在多个时间序列基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NCDE虽然具有最大表达能力和连续时间灵活性，但其非线性向量场导致前向传播计算昂贵且 inherently sequential，限制了可扩展性和实际应用。

Method: 1) Log-NCDE：基于神经粗糙微分方程，采用Log-ODE方法高效近似NCDE的解；2) Linear NCDE：用线性向量场替代非线性向量场，实现闭式解和时序并行计算；3) Structured Linear NCDE：使用结构化线性向量场进一步提升效率。

Result: 每步训练时间降低最多三个数量级，在多个时间序列基准上达到 state-of-the-art 性能。

Conclusion: 所提方法在不牺牲理论表达能力的前提下，显著提升了NCDE的训练效率和可扩展性，推动了连续时间模型在大规模时间序列任务中的实际应用。

Abstract: Many real-world systems evolve continuously, yet most machine learning models interpret time series as discrete sequences. Continuous-time approaches instead treat time series as samples from an underlying input path, a formulation that naturally accommodates irregularly sampled or oversampled data. Among these, Neural Controlled Differential Equations (NCDEs) are a maximally expressive class of models that parametrise a vector field using a neural network and evolve their hidden state by solving a dynamical system driven by the input path. NCDEs typically use a non-linear vector field, so their expressive power and continuous-time flexibility come at the cost of a forward pass that is both computationally expensive and inherently sequential, limiting their scalability and practical applicability.
  This thesis advances the training and scalability of NCDEs through three complementary contributions. First, building on neural rough differential equations, Log-NCDEs apply the Log-ODE method to efficiently approximate an NCDE's solution during training, improving both computational speed and empirical performance. Second, Linear NCDEs replace the non-linear vector field with a linear one, enabling closed-form solutions and parallel-in-time computation without sacrificing theoretical expressivity. Third, Structured Linear NCDEs use structured linear vector fields to further enhance efficiency while maintaining theoretical expressiveness and empirical performance.
  Collectively, these methods reduce the time per training step for an NCDE by up to three orders of magnitude while achieving state-of-the-art performance across diverse time series benchmarks.

</details>


### [226] [Air Quality Downscaling with Station-Guided Pseudo-Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05292)
*Guorun Wang,Simone Foti,Andreas D. Demou,Leonidas Kotoulas,Theodoros Christoudias,Alexandros Koliousis,Mihalis Nicolaou,Stefanos Zafeiriou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向欧洲PM2.5降尺度的站点引导框架，利用多尺度Transformer网络和多种辅助数据，在×40超分辨率下同时实现降尺度和偏差校正，并通过时间无关的传播策略解决稀疏站点监督问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗分辨率大气场与局部PM2.5变化之间存在空间支撑不匹配问题：像素代表区域平均值，而地面真值观测是离散、未对齐的连续空间信号样本，现有方法难以弥合这一差距。

Method: 构建多尺度Transformer网络，融合粗分辨率CAMS大气成分场及人类活动、土地覆盖、高程、卫星气溶胶观测、风场等异构侧信息，实现×40（约1 km）超分辨率；提出时间无关的传播策略，利用OpenAQ观测的空间高斯混合插值生成密集监督信号。

Result: 在欧洲范围的定性和站点级评估中，模型恢复了细粒度空间结构，有效缓解了局部的CAMS偏差。

Conclusion: 该框架无需时间序列建模即可实现粗分辨率大气场到局部PM2.5的高分辨率降尺度与偏差校正，验证了多源异构信息融合与空间高斯传播策略的有效性。

Abstract: Super-resolving coarse atmospheric fields to local PM$_{2.5}$ variations is uniquely challenged by a mismatch in spatial support: while pixels represent regional averages, ground-truth observations are discrete, unaligned samples of a continuous spatial signal. To bridge this gap, we present a station-guided framework for high-resolution PM$_{2.5}$ downscaling over Europe. Taking coarse CAMS atmospheric composition fields alongside heterogeneous side information (i.e., human activity, land cover, elevation, satellite aerosol observations, and wind fields) our framework jointly super-resolves ($\times 40$, $\approx$ 1 km) and bias-corrects CAMS rasters, without relying on temporal sequence modelling. To address the challenge of densely supervising our multi-scale transformer network with sparse in-situ data, we introduce a time-agnostic propagation strategy that utilises spatial Gaussian blending of interpolated OpenAQ observations. Extensive qualitative and station-level evaluations across Europe demonstrate that our model recovers fine-grained spatial structures and effectively mitigates localised CAMS biases.

</details>


### [227] [Learning Only What Valid Adapters Can Express: Subspace-Constrained Adaptation Against Fine-Tuning Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05300)
*Fabien Polly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种基于可信适配器池子空间约束的参数高效微调方法，证明该方法能有效防止后门攻击和标签反转，同时保持清洁数据上的性能，但牺牲了峰值可塑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调（如LoRA）虽高效，但仍存在大量行为改变的可达更新空间，使得中毒目标可被表示和优化。作者希望探索一种更安全的替代方案，通过将适应约束在从可信适配器池中估计的子空间内，来限制可能的行为变化范围。

Method: 基于flan-t5-large和196个公共LoRA适配器，估计低维共享子空间（128维），将梯度适配限制在该子空间内，与完整LoRA微调进行比较。在清洁分类数据、标签反转攻击、智能后门攻击等场景下进行评估。

Result: (1)适配器的功能相关内容位于低维共享子空间，30-38%的权重范数冗余；(2)子空间约束的128维协调匹配全LoRA性能，标签反转下LoRA崩溃至3-26%精确匹配，约束学习器保持62-96%；(3)约束学习器无法拟合损坏数据，适应损失区分清洁与垃圾数据达120倍；(4)针对智能后门攻击，在目标行为不在池中时阻止成功（8% vs LoRA的100%），目标与池中常见行为一致时仅部分阻止（85%）。

Conclusion: 基于可信适配器池的子空间约束提供了一种有效的安全微调机制，能抵御多种攻击，但以峰值可塑性为代价，且保护依赖于池本身的可靠性。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning still leaves a broad space of behavior-changing updates reachable, so a poisoned objective can be represented and optimized. We study an alternative: adaptation constrained to the subspace estimated from a trusted pool of existing task adapters. On flan-t5-large with 196 public LoRA adapters, we show that (1) the functionally relevant content of an adapter lies in a low-dimensional shared subspace, 30 to 38 percent of its weight norm being redundant under the evaluated task distributions; (2) gradient adaptation restricted to 128 coordinates on this subspace matches full LoRA fine-tuning on clean classification data, while under targeted label inversion LoRA collapses to 3-26 percent exact match and the constrained learner keeps 62-96 percent on the tasks the pool covers; (3) the constrained learner cannot fit corrupted data, its adaptation loss separating clean from garbage by two orders of magnitude (120x), an out-of-distribution signal without an extra detector; and (4) against an adaptive backdoor attacker who optimizes within the subspace, the attack is blocked (8 percent success versus 100 for LoRA) on the task where its target behavior is unlike anything in the pool, and only partially blocked (85 percent) when the target coincides with a common pool behavior. On these two tasks the outcome is consistent with how close the target is to the pool's directions, which suggests but does not establish a pool-relative boundary. The mechanism trades peak plasticity for these properties: on tasks the pool covers poorly, unconstrained fine-tuning wins, and the protection assumes the pool itself is trusted. Code and data are public.

</details>


### [228] [Biologically Informed Deep Neural Networks for Multi-Omic Integration, Pathway Activity Inference and Risk Stratification in Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05306)
*Pedro Henrique da Costa Avelar,Le Ou-Yang,Min Wu,Sophia Tsoka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多组学数据整合的通路活性自编码器框架，通过通路约束的架构设计在保持高表征能力的同时实现内在可解释性，在乳腺癌生存预测和亚型分类任务中验证了多组学整合的积极效果，并揭示了基因、蛋白质和miRNA表达层的关键贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在模型可解释性与表征能力之间存在权衡，多数方法要么依赖事后解释，要么使用线性模型从而忽略复杂的组学交互关系，亟需一种既能保持高表征能力又具备内在可解释性的多组学整合方法。

Method: 提出通路活性自编码器（Pathway Activity Autoencoders），通过将先验知识（通路信息）嵌入到架构约束中，构建多组学框架；在乳腺癌数据上应用于生存预测和亚型分类任务，并进行单组学层贡献分析、重复性实验以及潜在空间可视化。

Result: 多组学整合对生存预测和亚型分类有积极效果；基因、蛋白质和miRNA表达层贡献最强；dropout可提升鲁棒性和一致性，但过度正则化会降低预测性能；可视化展示了框架的内在透明性和临床相关性。

Conclusion: 该框架能生成具有生物学意义且直接可转化为临床相关见解的优越潜在表征，验证了多组学整合的价值，并为组学层贡献提供了可操作的指导原则。

Abstract: Integrating complex, multi-omics data presents significant challenges. Existing approaches often face a trade-off between model interpretability and representational capacity, with most either relying on post-hoc interpretation or use linear models that may overlook complex interactions. We report Pathway Activity Autoencoders for the multi-omics setting, which embed prior knowledge via pathway-informed architectural constraints, fostering interpretability, while preserving representational power. Our multi-omic framework is applied in the context of breast cancer and is evaluated in survival prediction and subtype classification with results indicating a positive effect of integration. We conduct analysis of individual omics layer impact on end-task performance, revealing that gene, protein, and microRNA expression layers provide the strongest contribution. Repeatability studies indicate that, while dropout improves model robustness and consistency, excessive regularisation can reduce predictive performance. Finally, visualizations of the learned feature space illustrate the framework's intrinsic transparency and clinical relevance. The results underscore the value of multi-omic integration and delineate the impact of individual omics layers, establishing practical guidelines for integration within our framework. Overall, our pathway activity autoencoder frameworks yield superior latent representations that are biologically meaningful and are directly translatable into clinically relevant insights.

</details>


### [229] [How Far is Too Far? Defining the Distance Threshold for Verification Siamese Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05329)
*Heloísa Dias Viotto,Cauê Samonek,Lucas Garcia Pedroso,Marcos Sunye,André Abed Grégio,Paulo Lisboa de Almeida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无监督方法，通过拟合孪生验证网络输出距离的双峰分布并取两峰间最低点作为阈值，无需标注数据即可实现与等错误率方法相当的94%平均验证准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孪生验证网络中的阈值设定通常依赖标注数据，成本高昂且难以在部署环境中动态更新，亟需一种无监督的阈值确定方法。

Method: 假设孪生验证网络输出的距离分布可近似为双峰函数，通过识别两峰之间的最低点来自动确定验证阈值，无需任何标注样本。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10、LFW和PKLot四个数据集上平均验证准确率达94%，与有监督的等错误率方法性能相当。

Conclusion: 所提出的无监督阈值选取方法有效消除了对标注数据的依赖，可在部署环境中直接更新阈值，兼具实用性与高效性。

Abstract: Siamese verification networks are widely used to compare items such as faces, cars, or signatures. In these scenarios, the network is trained to learn an embedding space in which similar objects are mapped closer together, while dissimilar objects are mapped further apart. Two objects are considered to belong to the same class (e.g., the same person in two different images) when the distance between their embeddings falls below a predefined threshold. Defining this threshold, however, is a non-trivial task and typically requires labeled data. In this work, we assume that the distribution of distances produced by a siamese verification network can be approximated by a bimodal function. Based on this assumption, we propose an unsupervised method to determine the verification threshold by identifying the minimum point between the two modes. The proposed approach does not require annotated samples, enabling the verification threshold to be updated directly in the deployment environment without the cost of manual labeling. We evaluate our method on four datasets: MNIST, CIFAR-10, LFW, and PKLot. The results indicate that the proposed approach achieves an average verification accuracy of 94%, comparable to the Equal Error Rate method, while eliminating the need for labeled data.

</details>


### [230] [TREK: Distill to Explore, Reinforce to Refine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05339)
*Yuanda Xu,Zhengze Zhou,Kayhan Behdin,Jelena Markovic-Voronov,Hejian Sang,Xiaomin Li,Wenhui Zhu,Xinchen Du,Aida Rahmattalabi,Ran He,Sen Na,Zhipeng Wang,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TREK是一种通过教师路由的前向KL散度扩展探索支持的两阶段方法，先识别学生模型难以解决的提示，利用教师模型生成候选解并用前向KL将正确模式拉入学生支持分布，再回归标准GRPO优化。在数学推理和智能体任务上显著优于纯GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在策略已能采样有用推理轨迹时有效，但在困难提示上会停滞，因为正确答案模式位于学生当前策略的在线支持之外。需要一种方法来扩展学生的探索支持，引入外部知识而不破坏原有优化过程。

Method: TREK包含四步：(1) 识别学生模型通过率极低的困难提示；(2) 查询提议源（外部黑盒教师、白盒教师或带额外推理上下文的同模型）生成已验证的候选解；(3) 按当前学生似然保留前r个候选，进行短期的前向KL阶段将这些模式拉入学生支持分布；(4) 回归标准在线GRPO精炼。

Result: 在数学推理上，TREK+DeepSeek-V4教师提升Qwen3各规模模型在AIME 2024/2025上的性能；Qwen3-8B在AIME 2025从36.9提升至40.3，AIME 2024从47.9提升至51.1（avg@16）；自上下文变体无外部教师达38.5和49.6。在智能体任务上，ALFWorld成功率从75.8提升至82.8，ScienceWorld从12.5提升至26.7。困难任务类型上TREK在训练早期即达到高成功率。

Conclusion: TREK是一种通用、高效的探索支持扩展框架，不依赖教师内部信息，仅需已验证的输出轨迹，可显著提升GRPO在困难问题上的表现，适用于多种任务和模型配置。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is effective when the current policy already samples useful reasoning trajectories, but it stalls on hard prompts whose correct solution modes lie outside the student's on-policy support. We propose TREK (Teacher-Routed Exploration via Forward KL), a simple staged procedure that uses distillation not for imitation but for exploration support expansion. A key advantage of TREK is its generality: because it only consumes verified output trajectories, it can use an external black-box teacher, a white-box teacher, or the same model given additional inference-time context, and it can efficiently identify which hard-prompt samples are most worth consolidating even when teacher internals are unavailable. TREK first identifies prompts where the unaided student has very low pass rate, queries a proposal source to produce verified candidate solutions, keeps the top-$r$ proposals ranked by current student likelihood, applies a short forward-KL phase to pull those verified modes into the student's support, and then returns to standard on-policy GRPO refinement. On mathematical reasoning, TREK with DeepSeek-V4 proposals improves Qwen3 models across all tested scales on AIME 2024 and AIME 2025; for Qwen3-8B, it improves AIME 2025 from 36.9 to 40.3 and AIME 2024 from 47.9 to 51.1 (avg@16), while the self-context variant reaches 38.5 and 49.6 without an external teacher. On agentic tasks, TREK raises ALFWorld success rate from 75.8 to 82.8 and ScienceWorld success rate from 12.5 to 26.7; notably, on the hardest task types, TREK achieves high success rates early in training while unaided GRPO requires substantially more optimization steps to reach comparable levels.

</details>


### [231] [CompactionRL: Reinforcement Learning with Context Compaction for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05378)
*Yujiang Li,Zhenyu Hou,Yi Jing,Jie Tang,Yuxiao Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CompactionRL，一种结合上下文压缩的强化学习策略，用于训练长程智能体LLM。该方法联合优化任务执行与摘要生成，通过token级损失归一化和跨轨迹广义优势估计，使LLM能够从压缩后的长程轨迹中学习。在多项编码任务上取得显著性能提升，并已部署于GLM-5.2模型的RL训练流程中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体LLM受限于有限的上下文窗口，交互轨迹可能超出最大上下文长度。上下文压缩虽能通过摘要化缩短上下文，但将压缩机制融入强化学习训练尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出CompactionRL强化学习策略，联合优化任务执行与摘要生成，采用token级损失归一化和跨轨迹广义优势估计（GAE），使LLM能在压缩后的长程轨迹上有效学习。

Result: 在GLM-4.5-Air模型上，SWE-bench Verified Pass@1达66.8%（+7.0），Terminal-Bench 2.0达24.5%（+3.1）；在GLM-4.7-Flash上分别提升5.5和6.8个百分点，达56.0%和20.2%。

Conclusion: CompactionRL有效提升了长程智能体LLM的编码任务性能，成功部署于GLM-5.2模型的RL训练流程中，证明了上下文压缩与强化学习结合在长程智能体训练中的可行性。

Abstract: Long-horizon agentic LLMs are increasingly limited by finite context windows, as extended interaction trajectories can exceed the maximum context length before a task is completed. Context compaction offers a natural solution by summarizing previous interaction states and continuing the rollout under a compressed context, but incorporating compaction into reinforcement learning remains underexplored. We propose CompactionRL, a reinforcement learning strategy to train long-horizon agentic LLMs with context compaction. Our approach jointly optimizes task execution and summary generation with token-level loss normalization and cross-trajectory generalized advantage estimation. This design enables the LLM agents to learn from compacted long-horizon trajectories. We train CompactionRL on top of open models and observe consistent performance gains on agentic coding tasks. CompactionRL enables the open GLM-4.5-Air model (106B-A30B) to achieve Pass@1 scores of 66.8% on SWE-bench Verified and 24.5% on Terminal-Bench 2.0, with absolute gains of 7.0 and 3.1 points, respectively. Built upon GLM-4.7-Flash (30B-A3B), CompactionRL improves Pass@1 by 5.5 and 6.8 points, reaching 56.0% on SWE-bench Verified and 20.2% on Terminal-Bench 2.0, respectively. CompactionRL is thus deployed in the RL pipeline for training the open GLM-5.2 model (750B-A40B).

</details>


### [232] [TabPack: Efficient Hyperparameter Ensembles for Tabular Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05380)
*Yury Gorishniy,Akim Kotelnikov,Ivan Rubachev,Artem Babenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPack是一种高效的MLP集成方法，通过并行采样和训练不同超参数的多个MLP，并在训练过程中动态选择集成成员，实现了无需精细调参即可获得强劲性能，在中等至大规模表格数据集上，默认设置即可与经过大量调优的先前方法相媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高效MLP集成方法对所有底层MLP使用相同的超参数，需要超参数调优才能达到最佳性能，这增加了计算开销和人工成本。

Method: TabPack在单次运行中并行采样并训练多个具有不同超参数的MLP，在训练过程中动态选择集成成员；用户只需指定超参数采样范围而非精确值。

Result: 在中等至大规模公开数据集上，TabPack的默认配置与经过大量调优的先前方法性能相当；在MacBook上运行默认配置比在某些工业级GPU上调优基线方法耗时更少。

Conclusion: TabPack显著降低了在表格数据任务上取得竞争性结果所需的调参努力和计算资源，实现了开箱即用的强性能。

Abstract: In deep learning for tabular data, efficient ensembles of multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) have recently emerged as effective and practical architectures. Existing methods of this kind use the same hyperparameters for all underlying MLPs, which requires hyperparameter tuning for achieving the best performance. In this work, we introduce TabPack, an efficient MLP ensemble with strong out-of-the-box performance and reduced reliance on traditional tuning. In a single run, TabPack samples and trains many MLPs with different hyperparameters efficiently in parallel and selects ensemble members on the fly during training. Thus, TabPack only requires specifying ranges from which to sample MLP hyperparameter rather than exact hyperparameter values, which naturally demands less precision for good performance. In experiments on medium-to-large public datasets, TabPack with default settings performs on par with extensively tuned prior methods, thus substantially reducing effort and compute resources needed to achieve competitive results on tabular tasks. Notably, running the default TabPack configuration on a modern MacBook took less time than tuning some baselines on an industry-grade GPU.

</details>


### [233] [What Does a Discrete Diffusion Model Learn?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05381)
*Rodrigo Casado Noguerales,Bernhard Schölkopf,Thomas Hofmann,Aran Raoufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文严格推导了连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）的证据下界（ELBO），证明了Oracle距离定理：负ELBO等于数据熵加上路径KL散度，其唯一优化器是真实反向跳跃率的条件期望。对于令牌因子化噪声，推导出优化器的三种精确坐标形式（去噪器、空腔/桥插件、分数），并揭示了不同参数化下ELBO的收敛行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清离散扩散模型中神经网络学习对象的本质——在跳跃率层面，去噪器、分数比率和桥插件预测器不过是同一对象在不同坐标下的表示。错误坐标读取会导致训练和采样过程的失配。

Method: 从连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）ELBO的严格推导出发，包括边界项；证明Oracle距离定理；推导令牌因子化噪声下优化器的三种精确坐标及其闭式转换；通过可精确求解的模型进行数值验证。

Result: 1) 负ELBO恰好等于数据熵加上从Oracle反向过程到学习过程的路径KL散度；2) 所有噪声过程共享相同的最佳可达负ELBO：数据熵；3) 去噪器、空腔（桥插件）和分数三者之间存在闭式转换关系；4) 去噪器参数化下均匀扩散ELBO在初始化时发散，而桥插件保持有限。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了MDM、UDM、SEDD、GIDD等现有方法，解释了掩码扩散与均匀扩散中不同参数化的行为差异，并支持ELBO实现的精确初始化校准。

Abstract: What does a discrete diffusion model learn: a denoiser, a score ratio, or a bridge plug-in predictor? At the level of jump rates, these are one object in different coordinates, and reading a neural network in the wrong coordinate changes the process being trained and sampled. Starting with a rigorous derivation of the continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) ELBO for any noising process, boundary terms included, we prove the \emph{Oracle Distance} theorem: the negative ELBO is exactly equal to the data entropy plus the path KL from the oracle reverse process to the learned one, not merely a bound. Its unique optimizer is therefore the conditional expectation of the true reverse jump rate given the current noisy state, and its irreducible cost is the rate at which the forward process $Z_t$ destroys information about the clean data $Z_0$, $-\tfrac{d}{dt}I(Z_0; Z_t)$, so every noising process shares the same best achievable negative ELBO: the data entropy. For sequences with token-factorizing noise, the oracle projection yields three exact coordinates for the optimizer: denoiser, cavity (bridge plug-in), and score, with closed-form conversions among them. This framework identifies which law each loss in the literature actually optimizes, recovering MDM, UDM, SEDD, and GIDD as special cases; explains why denoiser and cavity coincide for masked diffusion but not for uniform diffusion; proves that a denoiser parameterization makes the uniform ELBO diverge at initialization while the bridge plug-in stays finite; and calibrates ELBO implementations exactly at initialization. Every identity is verified numerically, without approximation, on an exactly solvable model.

</details>


### [234] [Weak-to-Strong Generalization via Direct On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05394)
*Shiyuan Feng,Huan-ang Gao,Haohan Chi,Hanlin Wu,Zhilong Zhang,Zheng Jiang,Bingxiang He,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Direct On-Policy Distillation (Direct-OPD)方法，通过将弱模型经过RL训练后的策略偏移（与自身预RL参考模型的log-ratio）作为隐式奖励信号，直接蒸馏到强学生模型上，从而避免在强模型上重复运行昂贵的RL训练。实验表明该方法能在极低成本下显著提升强模型性能，如Qwen3-1.7B在AIME 2024上从48.3%提升至62.4%，仅需8张A100训练4小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型规模扩大，基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在强模型上的后训练成本急剧增加，成为训练瓶颈。现有方法需要强模型在训练中生成大量rollouts，成本高昂。作者希望探索一种弱到强的替代方案：在廉价的小模型上运行RL，然后将RL收益复用到强模型上，避免重复的高成本RL训练。

Method: 提出Direct On-Policy Distillation (Direct-OPD)：1) 在弱教师模型上运行RL训练得到post-RL模型；2) 计算post-RL教师与其自身pre-RL参考模型之间的log-ratio，将其作为稠密隐式奖励信号；3) 该奖励信号直接作用于强学生模型自身on-policy的状态-动作对上，指导学生模型学习教师模型的策略偏移方向；4) 无需训练显式奖励模型，也无需在目标模型上运行稀疏奖励的RL。

Result: 在AIME 2024上，Qwen3-1.7B从48.3%提升至62.4%，仅需8张A100训练4小时；方法优于step-matched直接RL；支持多个策略偏移的顺序组合。

Conclusion: RL训练的产出可以跨模型规模复用为隐式奖励信号，而不仅仅是作为最终模型进行模仿。Direct-OPD有效实现了弱到强的RL收益迁移，为大规模模型的后训练提供了一种低成本高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is a powerful recipe for improving language-model reasoning, but it is expensive to repeat on every new strong model because the target model must generate many rollouts during training. As models scale, post-training itself becomes a bottleneck. We study a weak-to-strong alternative: run RL on a smaller model where rollouts are cheaper, then reuse what that RL run learned to improve a stronger target model. Directly distilling the post-RL weak teacher is not enough, because the teacher's final policy mixes useful RL gains with the limitations of the smaller model. We propose Direct On-Policy Distillation (Direct-OPD), which transfers the teacher's RL-induced policy shift instead. Direct-OPD compares the post-RL teacher with its own pre-RL reference and treats their log-ratio as a dense implicit reward for the student. In plain terms, the checkpoint pair tells us which actions RL made the weak model more or less likely to take, and Direct-OPD applies that signal on the stronger student's own on-policy states. This directly reuses the weak model's RL supervision signal without training an explicit reward model or running sparse-reward RL on the target model. Empirically, Direct-OPD consistently leverages weaker teachers to improve stronger target models; notably, it boosts Qwen3-1.7B from 48.3% to 62.4% on AIME 2024 in just 4 hours on 8 A100 GPUs. It outperforms step-matched direct RL and enables the sequential composition of multiple policy shifts. Our results show that RL outcomes can be reused across model scales as implicit reward signals, not merely as final models to imitate.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [235] [CodeJeNN: A simple C++ neural network generator for physics applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02746)
*Jay Arcities,Pavel Popov,Eric J Ching,Kamal Viswanath,Ryan F Johnson*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: CodeJeNN是一个将Keras训练模型自动生成独立C++推理代码的工具，消除了外部依赖，在CFD等物理应用中实现加速且不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Python机器学习库集成到高性能C++数值求解器中会造成性能瓶颈，需要一种桥接方案来消除这一障碍。

Method: 从训练好的Keras模型自动生成自包含的C++推理代码，使用最小化内联函数消除外部依赖，支持多种网络架构。

Result: 在Keras的eager和JIT模式下的推理基准测试中实现加速，并在氢气-空气混合层黏度建模的CFD测试案例中展现加速效果且不牺牲精度。

Conclusion: CodeJeNN有效地弥合了Python机器学习库与高性能C++求解器之间的鸿沟，为物理应用中的机器学习集成提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Machine learning has shown speedups for numerical methods in physics applications, but integrating Python-based libraries into high-performance C++ solvers creates performance bottlenecks. We present CodeJeNN, which bridges this gap by auto-generating self-contained C++ code from trained Keras models for inference. This eliminates external dependencies through minimal inlined functions, allowing seamless integration into existing frameworks. We describe the Keras-to-C++ workflow, supported architectures, and limitations. CodeJeNN is demonstrated through inference benchmarks against Keras in eager and JIT modes and a CFD test case modeling viscosity in a hydrogen-air mixing layer, showing speedups without sacrificing accuracy.

</details>


### [236] [Restoring the uniform density limit in Perdew-Zunger self-interaction correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03997)
*Benjamin G. Janesko*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种替代PZ-SIC中轨道密度假设的新方案，该方案恢复了均匀密度极限，减少了轨道依赖性和瓣状密度误差，并在局部标度的自相互作用校正广义梯度近似中展示了广泛准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PZ-SIC虽然对单电子密度精确，但牺牲了均匀密度的精确性。需要一种替代方案来恢复均匀密度极限，同时减少轨道依赖性和瓣状密度误差。

Method: 提出了一种替代PZ-SIC中轨道密度假设的新方案（局部标度的自相互作用校正），该方案无需从平滑多电子密度中提取瓣状单电子密度来评估近似密度泛函，从而恢复均匀密度极限并减少误差。

Result: 新方案恢复了均匀密度极限，减少了轨道依赖性和瓣状密度误差，并在非经验局部标度自相互作用校正的广义梯度近似中表现出广泛的准确性。

Conclusion: 提出的替代轨道密度假设的方案能同时满足单电子密度和均匀密度的精确性要求，是一种更优的自相互作用校正方法。

Abstract: The Perdew-Zunger self-interaction correction (PZ-SIC) makes approximate density functionals exact for all one-electron densities, but sacrifices exactness for uniform densities. I show that an alternative to the orbital density ansatz employed in PZ-SIC restores the uniform density limit. The new ansatz also eliminates the need to evaluate approximate density functionals on lobed one-electron densities extracted from smooth many-electron densities, thereby reducing orbital dependence and lobed density error. I demonstrate the alternative ansatz in a broadly accurate nonempirical locally scaled self-interaction-corrected generalized gradient approximation.

</details>


### [237] [Hyper Boris integrators for kinetic plasma simulations and their connection to 3D rotation representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04465)
*S. Zenitani,T. N. Kato*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了用于非相对论动力学模拟的新型高精度粒子积分器——超Boris积分器，并探讨了其与3D旋转表示的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Boris方法在半世纪以来被广泛用于等离子体物理的PIC模拟中且精度良好，但日益增长的高精度数值求解器需求促使研究者开发新的积分器。

Method: 提出了新型高精度粒子积分器（超Boris积分器），用于非相对论动力学模拟，并分析了其与3D旋转表示之间的理论联系。

Result: 成功构建了超Boris积分器，在保持Boris方法基本框架的基础上提高了数值精度。

Conclusion: 超Boris积分器为非相对论PIC模拟提供了更高精度的替代方案，且与3D旋转表示的关联揭示了其数学结构上的深层含义。

Abstract: Particle-in-cell (PIC) simulation is one of the most important research tools in theoretical plasma physics. To solve the motion of charged particles, the Boris method (a.k.a. the Boris integrator/pusher/solver) has been used for more than a half century. Although the Boris solver has good accuracy, the demand for high-accuracy numerical solvers has been increasing, and new integrators have been actively developed. In this contribution, we present novel high-accuracy particle integrators, the hyper Boris integrators, for nonrelativistic kinetic simulations. We further discuss their connection to 3D rotation representations.

</details>


### [238] [Accelerating Multi-scale Simulations of Nuclear Components via PCYS Interpolation Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04556)
*Fabrizio Aguzzi,Martin S. Armoa,Cesar I. Pairetti,Cesar M. Venier,Alejandro E. Albanesi*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多尺度计算框架，通过5D插值表和泰勒级数线性化方案替代传统VPSC-FEM耦合方法，在保证微观组织保真度（误差<1%）的前提下，实现核反应堆锆合金构件的工程尺度模拟，并在格架结构仿真中验证了计算效率的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微观力学晶体塑性模型（如VPSC）能够准确描述锆合金辐照蠕变与生长导致的各向异性尺寸变化，但其与宏观有限元求解器的直接耦合计算成本过高，无法应用于工程尺度构件分析。因此，需要开发一种计算高效的跨尺度方法。

Method: 基于开源FEM求解器Code_Aster实现多尺度框架：采用5D插值表作为静态材料代理，控制瞬时粘塑性响应；结合周期性重新校准和一阶泰勒级数线性化方案，追踪辐照损伤引起的微观结构漂移而无需实时更新数据库。该Polycrystal Yield Surface (PCYS)插值方法通过VPSC-FEM仿真进行基准验证。

Result: 材料层面：线性化方案在代表性变形路径下将相对误差控制在1%以内，保持结构相容性；结构层面：间隔格架域仿真显示显著的计算成本节约，克服了多尺度计算惩罚，同时保留了微观结构保真度。

Conclusion: 该框架具有潜力应用于反应堆内部构件的多物理场结构评估及全寿期安全裕度评价。

Abstract: Zirconium alloy core components in nuclear reactors, such as spacer grids and fuel cladding, undergo anisotropic dimensional changes driven by coupled irradiation creep and growth. While micromechanical crystal plasticity frameworks like the Viscoplastic Self-Consistent (VPSC) formulation capture these microstructurally driven phenomena, their integration into macroscopic Finite Element Method (FEM) solvers is computationally prohibitive for engineering-scale components. To bridge this gap, this work presents a multi-scale framework implemented within the open-source FEM solver Code_Aster. The developed interface uses a 5D Interpolation Table (IT) as a static material surrogate to govern instantaneous viscoplastic responses, coupled with a periodic recalibration and first-order Taylor series linearization scheme to track microstructural drift due to radiation damage without on-the-fly database updates. The predictive accuracy, numerical stability, and performance of this Polycrystal Yield Surface (PCYS) interpolation approach are benchmarked against VPSC-FEM simulations under continuous high-dose irradiation scenarios. Material-level assessments demonstrate that the linearization scheme bounds relative errors below 1% for representative deformation paths, maintaining structural compatibility. Furthermore, structural simulations of a spacer grid domain revealed meaningful computational savings, overcoming the multi-scale computational penalty while preserving microstructural fidelity. The proposed framework shows potential for multiphysics structural assessments and safety margin evaluations of core internals over operational lifespans.

</details>


### [239] [dpti: An Automated Thermodynamic Integration Workflow for Phase Diagram Calculations with Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05015)
*Fengbo Yuan,Xin Zhong,Donghao Zheng,Jinzhe Zeng,Linfeng Zhang,Han Wang,Yifan Li*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了dpti，一个用于自动化热力学积分（TI）工作流的开源Python包，可基于机器学习原子间势（MLIP）计算相图。它通过可逆积分路径连接参考系统与MLIP描述的体系，自动生成并运行分子动力学任务、计算自由能贡献、估计误差，并传播共存点得到相界。通过二氧化硅和冰-水两个Deep Potential模型示例进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于MLIP的TI计算在技术上具有挑战性，需要精心设计可逆积分路径，并对每个相和状态点执行大量密切相关的MD任务，现有工具缺乏自动化支持。

Method: dpti是一个开源Python包，通过可逆积分路径将具有解析自由能的参考系统与MLIP描述的固/液体系连接起来。用户提供JSON输入文件后，dpti自动生成并运行MD任务，计算自由能贡献，估计误差，并将共存点传播为相界线。

Result: 通过两个Deep Potential模型示例展示了dpti的应用：二氧化硅相图（β-石英、柯石英和熔体）以及冰Ih-液态水的相界线。

Conclusion: dpti为基于MLIP的材料相图自动计算提供了实用工具，有效解决了传统TI工作流中手动操作多、路径设计复杂等问题。

Abstract: Thermodynamic integration (TI) is a widely used approach for computing free energies and phase diagrams. However, TI calculations driven by machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) remain technically challenging because they require careful design of reversible integration paths and many closely related molecular dynamics (MD) tasks for each phase and state point. To address these challenges, we present dpti, an open-source Python package that automates TI workflows for phase diagram calculations with MLIPs. dpti connects reference systems with analytically known free energies to MLIP-described atomic and molecular solids and liquids through reversible integration paths. Given JSON input files, dpti generates and runs the required MD tasks, computes free energy contributions, estimates errors, and propagates coexistence points into phase boundaries. We demonstrate the usage of dpti with two examples driven by Deep Potential models: a silica phase diagram involving beta-quartz, coesite, and melt, and the ice Ih-liquid water phase boundary. dpti provides a useful tool for automated phase diagram calculations of materials modeled by MLIPs.

</details>


### [240] [Watts per event: evaluating Sustainability of HEP Event Generators beyond the LHC era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05018)
*Szabolcs Molnár,Gábor Bíró,Gábor Papp,Gergely Gábor Barnaföldi*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过容器化工具集77rev/propripy对HIJING++重离子蒙特卡洛事件生成器进行基准测试，发现通过优化多线程级别可降低事件生成成本，从而减少高能物理事件生成器的能源足迹，缓解计算挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC后时代蒙特卡洛事件生成器的开发、调优和运行需要大量资源，其可持续性面临挑战。

Method: 使用容器化工具集77rev/propripy对HIJING++重离子蒙特卡洛事件生成器进行基准测试，分析不同CPU架构的性能，并通过调整多线程级别优化事件生成成本。

Result: 通过合理选择多线程级别，可以优化事件生成成本，减少能源足迹。

Conclusion: 该方法是可持续的，能降低高能物理事件生成器的能源消耗，缓解日益增长的计算挑战。

Abstract: The development, tuning and operation of Monte Carlo event generators beyond the LHC era require vast amount of resources. In this study we investigate the sustainability of these software with a containerized set of tools (named 77rev/propripy), by benchmarking the HIJING++ heavy-ion Monte Carlo event generator. We analyze the performance of various CPU architectures and show that by choosing the level of multithreading properly, the cost of event generation can be optimized. The presented approach can reduce the energy footprint of high-energy physics event generators and therefore alleviate the ever-increasing, ubiquitous computational challenges.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [241] [MLIR for Quantum Beyond Gate Cancellation: Quantum Circuit Mapping Reimagined](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02616)
*Matthias Reumann,Yannick Stade,Robert Wille,Lukas Burgholzer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于MLIR框架的量子电路映射方案，通过重新实现A*搜索算法进行量子比特路由和SWAP插入，在保证无缝集成的同时，在解质量和运行时间上均超越了非MLIR方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIR框架已成为构建可扩展领域专用编译器的基石，量子社区已利用它建模量子程序并实现基础优化，但量子编译流程中计算密集型的任务（如量子电路映射）在MLIR生态中尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出一种MLIR原生的蓝图，通过重新实现一种成熟的、最先进的A*搜索映射算法，用于量子比特路由和SWAP插入。

Result: 该方法不仅无缝集成到基于MLIR的量子编译器集合中，而且在解质量和运行时间上都超越了之前的非MLIR解决方案。

Conclusion: MLIR-native的量子电路映射方案是可行且优越的，为量子编译流水线中非局部的、量子特定的优化例程提供了有效的MLIR集成路径。

Abstract: The Multi-Level Intermediate Representation (MLIR) framework has become a cornerstone for building extensible, domain-specific compilers, with the quantum computing community already leveraging it to model quantum programs and implement basic optimizations. However, computationally intensive tasks in the quantum compilation pipeline, such as quantum circuit mapping, remain underexplored within the MLIR ecosystem. This paper proposes an MLIR-native blueprint for these non-local, quantum-specific optimization routines by reimplementing a well-established, state-of-the-art mapping A* search algorithm for qubit routing and SWAP insertion. Our evaluation demonstrates that this approach not only integrates seamlessly into an MLIR-based quantum compiler collection but also surpasses previous non-MLIR solutions in both solution quality and runtime. The implementation is open-source and publicly available at https://github.com/munich-quantum-toolkit/core.

</details>


### [242] [Comparing the Performance of Leading VQE Algorithms for Computing Ground-State Energies of Amino Acids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02620)
*Sanskriti Shindadkar,Clyde Villacrusis,Jasper Andrews,Brandon Yan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个包含10多种可重复基准测试的集成仓库，用于比较不同分子模拟变分量子本征求解器(VQE)的ansatz方法，并通过四组实验系统评估了噪声鲁棒性、可训练性、自适应与固定ansatz的比较以及精度与表达能力的关系，应用于多种氨基酸分子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估NISQ量子计算机在分子模拟任务中的实际就绪程度，解决当前不同ansatz方法缺乏统一、公平、可重复比较框架的问题。

Method: 构建一个集成基准测试仓库，包含10多种已发表论文中的ansatz和两种截断方法，统一处理映射后的哈密顿量；在QMProt数据集的氨基酸分子上进行四组实验：1) 噪声鲁棒性量化（扫描PennyLane噪声通道和强度）；2) 贫瘠高原相关可训练性分析（梯度方差诊断和优化轨迹）；3) 自适应vs固定ansatz在相同参数预算下的对比；4) 精度与表达能力（保留自适应算子数量vs基态能量误差）。

Result: 通过四组实验从噪声韧性、可训练性、算法效率（迭代次数、时钟时间、成本函数评估次数）和精度-表达能力权衡等多维度给出了量化比较结果。

Conclusion: 该集成仓库为VQE方法的公平比较提供了统一流水线，揭示了不同ansatz在NISQ设备上的性能差异，有助于指导实际应用中的算法选择和优化策略。

Abstract: Simulating molecules is a major application of quantum computing, with the potential to overcome exponential scaling constraints of classical computation. Researchers use different methods in order to evaluate the readiness of NISQ computers in order to test current simulation capabilities. We present an integrated repository with reproducible benchmarks of over 10 different ansatzes from published papers and two different truncation methods, applicable to any set of mapped hamiltonians, providing a single pipeline for comparing performance along multiple axes, including variance and computational time, among others. We apply them to simulate different amino acids, using hamiltonians taken from the QMProt Dataset. We then ran four separate experiments. First, we quantified noise resilience by optimizing the same hardware-efficient ansatzes under identical initialization while sweeping PennyLane noise channels and strengths, and measuring parameter drift, cosine similarity of optimal parameters, and energies evaluated on noiseless versus noisy backends. We then studied barren-plateau-related trainability via gradient-variance diagnostics and optimization trajectories across initialization strategies and ansatzes depth on small systems. We then compared adaptive versus fixed ansatzes at matched parameter budgets, reporting outer-loop iterations, wall time, and especially total cost-function evaluations to fairly contrast greedy adaptive growth with layered hardware-efficient circuits. Lastly, we mapped accuracy versus expressive capacity by sweeping the number of retained adaptive operators and recording ground-state energy error relative to classical references.

</details>


### [243] [COMET: Combinatorial Optimization for Multiplex Editing Targets Via Constraint-Preserving QAOA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02622)
*Priyansh Singhal,Sumit Maheshwari,Piyush Joshi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了在多重CRISPR-Cas9基因编辑中，通过惩罚项与XY-mixer两种方式在QAOA中施加单热约束的效果。在三个基因、十二量子比特的实例上，XY-mixer在模拟中深度p=3时最优解概率超过95%，而惩罚变体在所有深度下均低于6%。在IBM量子处理器上，XY-mixer的模拟器-硬件能量差距保持在|0.8|以内，而最差惩罚变体差距达+53.9。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重CRISPR-Cas9基因编辑中，每个目标基因需选择一个引导RNA，且存在跨基因相互作用，这是一个受约束的组合优化问题。传统上通过添加二次惩罚项来强制实施单热约束，但惩罚系数的选择依赖启发式，且惩罚项会放大硬件噪声。

Method: 将问题建模为QUBO，分别采用惩罚项法和XY-mixer法在QAOA中实施单热约束，并在三个免疫检查点基因（PDCD1、LAG3、HAVCR2）的十二量子比特实例上进行系统比较。在模拟环境和IBM ibm_kingston量子处理器上进行验证。

Result: 模拟中，XY-mixer在深度p=3时最优解概率超过95%，三种惩罚变体均低于6%。在量子硬件上，XY-mixer的模拟-硬件能量差距在|0.8|以内，最差惩罚变体差距达+53.9。

Conclusion: XY-mixer在约束保持方面优于传统惩罚项方法，能有效抑制硬件噪声影响，但结构保证在门级噪声下会部分失效。该工作为生物动机领域的约束实施策略提供了方法论比较和硬件验证。

Abstract: Multiplex CRISPR-Cas9 gene editing requires selecting one guide RNA per target gene subject to cross-gene interactions: a constrained combinatorial problem that can be formulated as a Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) and solved via the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA). The one-hot per-gene constraint is conventionally enforced by adding quadratic penalty terms to the cost Hamiltonian, but penalty coefficient selection is heuristic and penalties amplify hardware noise. An alternative is to enforce the constraint structurally via the XY-mixer, which preserves feasibility by construction. We present COMET, a systematic comparison of penalty-based and XY-mixer QAOA on a three-gene, twelve-qubit multiplex editing instance targeting the immune-checkpoint genes PDCD1, LAG3, and HAVCR2. In simulation, the XY-mixer exceeds 95% probability of the optimum by QAOA depth p=3, while three penalty variants spanning an order of magnitude in penalty coefficient remain below 6% at every depth. On IBM's ibm_kingston (Heron r2) processor, the XY-mixer's simulator-hardware energy gap stays within |0.8| across all depths, while the worst-tuned penalty variant's gap reaches +53.9. We provide an honest account of where the structural guarantee partially breaks under gate-level noise. The twelve-qubit instance is classically trivial; our contribution is a methodological comparison of constraint-enforcement strategies in a biologically motivated domain, with real-hardware validation.

</details>


### [244] [Krylov-Lie Algebras for Variational Quantum Algorithms: Geometric, Depth-Aware Insights into Expressivity and Trainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02626)
*Anžej Margeta-Cacace*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入Krylov代数作为变分量子算法(VQA)损失景观的理论框架，推导了非Haar方差公式，证明非Haar贡献可能通过重新加权损失景观的可见扇区来缓解贫瘠高原问题，表明VQA的可训练性可能优于现有文献结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VQA景观理论基于动力学李代数、近似t-design和Haar随机电路等框架，忽略了有限深度真实ansätze的几何结构，不适用于浅层电路区域——而该区域中VQA是2-design的糟糕近似且可训练性最高。

Method: 引入Krylov代数（有限生成元集作用于种子向量所诱导的代数结构），用Krylov-李代数和群近似VQA可达流形；推导加权非Haar方差公式，将标准李代数Haar公式作为特例恢复，并将非Haar效应显式分离为修正项。

Result: 证明了充分深度的电路集成必须收敛到Haar的启发式在一般情况下不成立，识别了Naive Haar收敛的具体障碍，并在自然必要充分遍历条件下恢复收敛；非Haar贡献可通过重新加权损失景观扇区缓解贫瘠高原。

Conclusion: VQA的可训练性可能优于现有文献的悲观评估，Krylov代数为VQA景观理论提供了数值稳健、几何保真的新框架。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms (VQAs) are a leading approach to near-term quantum computation, but their utility is limited by barren plateaus and other pathologies in their loss landscapes. Existing landscape theories based on dynamical Lie algebras, Jordan-algebraic Wishart systems, approximate t-designs, and Haar-random circuits are foundational, but they often neglect the finite-depth geometry of realistic ansätze and are therefore poorly suited to the shallow-depth regime, where VQAs are poor approximators of 2-designs and trainability is most feasible. This thesis introduces Krylov algebras, algebraic structures induced by the Krylov span of a finite generator set acting on one or more seed vectors, as a framework for VQA landscape theory. We show that VQA reachable manifolds can be approximated in a numerically robust, geometrically faithful way by Krylov-Lie algebras and groups, and that these structures induce canonical invariant measures for computing expectation values and variances under general sampling measures. In particular, we derive weighted non-Haar variance formulas that recover the usual Lie-algebraic Haar formulas as a special case while isolating non-Haar effects into explicit correction terms. We also show that the common heuristic that sufficiently deep circuit ensembles must converge to Haar fails in general without additional hypotheses, identify concrete obstructions to naive Haar convergence, and recover convergence under natural necessary and sufficient ergodic conditions. Lastly, our formulas further imply that non-Haar contributions may mitigate barren plateaus by reweighting the visible sectors of the loss landscape, suggesting that VQAs may be more trainable than recent literature has posited.

</details>


### [245] [Minimally invasive measurement of work in coherent quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02652)
*Cyril Elouard,Karen Hovhannisyan,Giulia Rubino*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于海森堡绘景哈密顿量变化概念的操作性方案，用于获取封闭量子系统的功统计信息。该方案在保持能量相关相干性的同时忠实再现未测量的功，导出了修正的Jarzynski关系、Crooks关系及热力学不确定关系，并在相干引擎和麦克斯韦妖协议中展现出优于传统双点能量测量方案的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子热力学中的核心挑战在于：如何在获取相干过程中功的涨落信息时，不破坏未测量演化的能量学特征。标准双点测量方案中，初始能量测量会使相干输入退相，导致测量的平均功与未测量演化产生偏差。

Method: 基于海森堡绘景中哈密顿量变化的抽象概念，发展了一套仅需单次测量即可获取封闭量子系统功统计的操作性方案。

Result: 1. 该方案能保持能量相关相干性，忠实再现未测量的功，同时产生正概率；2. 导出了包含相干诱导修正项的修正Jarzynski关系、Crooks关系及热力学不确定关系；3. 在相干引擎中，当双点能量测量会抑制功输出时，该方案仍能可靠量化引擎性能；4. 构建的麦克斯韦妖协议在相干功提取方面优于基于能量的反馈引擎。

Conclusion: 该方案建立了一个在不抹除驱动量子热力学性能的相干性的前提下，获取相干功涨落信息的框架。

Abstract: A central challenge in quantum thermodynamics is to access work fluctuations in coherent processes without distorting the energetics of the unmeasured evolution. In standard two-point schemes, the initial energy measurement dephases coherent inputs, causing the measured average work to differ from that of the unmeasured evolution. Here, we develop an operational scheme for accessing work statistics for closed quantum systems based on the abstract notion of variation in the Heisenberg picture Hamiltonian. This scheme preserves energetically relevant coherences, thereby faithfully reproducing unmeasured work, while still producing positive probabilities. We derive modified Jarzynski and Crooks relations, as well as a thermodynamic uncertainty relation, identifying coherence-induced correction terms. Furthermore, we show that this scheme can reliably quantify the performance of a coherent engine in situations where the two-point energy measurement would suppress work output. In addition, the scheme requires only a single measurement and can predict the work associated with a subsequent unitary transformation. We exploit this feature to construct a Maxwell-demon protocol that can outperform energy-based feedback engines for coherent work extraction. Our results establish this scheme as a framework for accessing coherent work fluctuations without erasing the coherence that drives quantum thermodynamic performance.

</details>


### [246] [Quench Spectroscopy of Magnetic Excitations on a Superconducting Quantum Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02673)
*D. A. Millar,G. W. Pennington,N. T. M. Siow,S. Brandhofer,J. Crain,F. H. L. Essler,A. G. Green,S. J. Thomson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在超导量子处理器上利用淬火光谱学提取了101个自旋的XXZ自旋链的激发谱，成功解析了自由磁振子、多磁振子束缚态和双自旋子连续谱，并展示了一种无需基态制备即可在易制备乘积态上直接提取谱的协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体量子系统的激发谱包含输运、热化、基态结构等关键信息，但强关联系统的动力学结构因子实验测量困难且经典计算极具挑战性，因此需要一种高效、可扩展的量子计算方法。

Method: 在超导量子处理器上实现淬火光谱学，通过定制淬火协议与可观测量组合，选择性访问自旋-1/2 XXZ链不同相中的不同激发扇区；在无能隙区域直接从易制备的乘积态淬火动力学中提取谱，无需基态制备。

Result: 在L=101自旋链上成功分辨出自由磁振子、多磁振子束缚态和双自旋子连续谱，证明了协议在无能隙区域的可行性。

Conclusion: 淬火光谱学是数字量子硬件上快速、灵活的多体激发谱探测手段；新协议无需昂贵态制备，为经典模拟可能变得棘手的区域提供了可扩展的路径。

Abstract: The elementary excitation spectrum of a many-body quantum system encodes many key properties, including phenomena as diverse as transport, thermalisation and ground state structure. Excitation spectra of strongly correlated systems are typically encoded in dynamical structure factors, which are demanding to measure experimentally and challenging to compute classically. Here we use quench spectroscopy on a superconducting quantum processor to extract excitation spectra of spin chains of $L=101$ spins. By tailoring the combination of quench protocol and observable, we selectively access distinct excitation sectors across several phases of the spin-$1/2$ XXZ chain, resolving free magnons, multi-magnon bound states, and two-spinon continua. Notably, we demonstrate that the protocol does not rely on ground state preparation: in the classically challenging gapless regime, we extract spectra directly from the quench dynamics of easily prepared product states, a procedure that is natural and straightforward on quantum hardware. Our work establishes quench spectroscopy as a fast and flexible probe of many-body excitation spectra on digital quantum hardware, introduces a novel quench protocol that does not require costly state preparation routines, and provides a scalable route towards regimes where classical simulation may become intractable.

</details>


### [247] [Parametrized-circuit-free quantum regression with variance regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02696)
*Yerassyl Balkybek,Andrey Kardashin,Vladimir V. Palyulin,Konstantin Antipin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需变分量子电路的量子回归方法，通过线性组合适当地选取的可观测量并编码对称性来预测量子态属性，训练高效且资源消耗低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子电路虽表达能力强、精度高，但训练需要大量时间和资源，因此需要一种更高效的量子回归建模方法。

Method: 利用问题相关的对称性编码和方差正则化，通过线性组合适当地选取的可观测量来构建回归模型。该方法无需参数化量子电路，可观测量测量后即可高效完成训练。

Result: 在伊辛模型的横向场强度预测和两体量子比特系统的纠缠量化两个任务上，该方法准确且比传统变分方法资源消耗更低。

Conclusion: 该方法通过利用问题对称性避免了变分训练的瓶颈，在保持精度的同时显著降低了资源开销，是一种高效的量子回归替代方案。

Abstract: Quantum regression tasks for predicting properties of quantum states are commonly addressed using variational quantum algorithms. While variational quantum circuits are highly expressive and allow to achieve reasonable accuracy, training these circuits may demand a considerable amount of time and resources. In this work, we propose an approach of constructing problem-specific quantum regression models with encoding relevant symmetries and regularizing the variance. The proposed method is based on finding the coefficients of the linear combination of suitably chosen observables. Although it requires the knowledge of the symmetries of the problem in question, the method does not involve parameterized quantum circuits, and the training is done efficiently once the observables are measured. We demonstrate this method on two examples: Prediction of the transverse field strength in the Ising model, and quantification of entanglement in bipartite qubit systems. Our approach is accurate and less resource-intensive than conventional variational methods.

</details>


### [248] [Encoding matroids into quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02736)
*Nathan Ferreira,Alison A. Silva,Giuliano G. La Guardia,Fabiano M. Andrade*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于图态和超图态的公理化框架，提出了与拟阵相关联的多体量子态——拟阵态，给出了两种构造方式（基于回路和独立集），建立了图态、拟阵态与超图态之间的层次关系，并展示了如何利用拟阵态生成任意图态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为多体量子态提供更高效、更具一般性的表示方法，将拟阵理论这一组合数学工具引入量子信息领域，以更好地刻画和描述量子纠缠结构。

Method: 基于公理化方法，分别以拟阵的回路和独立集为出发点，构造全局算符并定义拟阵态；通过与图态和超图态的对比分析，建立层次结构关系。

Result: 成功构造了两类拟阵态，证明了其算符满足局域性、对称性、对易性等理想性质，建立了图态-拟阵态-超图态的层次包含关系，并证明了任意图态可由特定拟阵态族生成。

Conclusion: 拟阵理论为多体量子态的描述提供了一种自然的组合语言，为量子纠缠及相关组合结构的研究开辟了新视角。

Abstract: Efficient representations of multipartite quantum states play a fundamental role in quantum information theory, providing both conceptual insight and practical tools for characterizing entanglement. Motivated by the axiomatic framework for graph states [Phys. Rev. A 85, 062313 (2012)] and its subsequent extension to hypergraph states [Phys. Rev. A 87, 022311 (2013)], we introduce an axiomatic construction of \emph{matroid states}, a new family of multipartite quantum states associated with matroids. Our constructions are based on a set of axioms analogous to those that define graph and hypergraph states, yielding a consistent quantum representation of arbitrary matroids. Two ways of constructing matroid states are proposed: the first is defined in terms of circuits, and the second in terms of independent sets. In both approaches, we establish the existence of universal global operators that satisfy desirable properties such as locality, symmetry, commutativity, and are associated with the combinatorial structure of matroids. Furthermore, we establish a hierarchy connecting graph, matroid, and hypergraph states within a unified framework. Additionally, we show how to obtain an arbitrary graph state by applying suitable families of matroid states, whose corresponding operators are the generators of the stabilizer subgroup of the graph state. These results identify matroid theory as a natural combinatorial language for the efficient description of multipartite quantum states and open new perspectives for the investigation of quantum entanglement and related combinatorial structures.

</details>


### [249] [Self-Attention for Quantum Entanglement Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02767)
*Anuj Gore,Roopayan Ghosh,Dylan Lewis,Sougato Bose*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出前馈神经网络和注意力模型两种方法，从量子态的投影测量中准确预测二分第二Renyi纠缠熵，相比经典影子估计器具有更高的精度和样本效率，展示了机器学习在可扩展高效量子关联估计中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠是量子增强技术的关键资源，但其可靠量化面临两个挑战：希尔伯特空间随系统大小指数扩展导致完整态层析不可行，以及实验估计纠缠需要大量测量样本带来显著开销。因此需要更高效、可扩展的纠缠估计方法。
