Method: 提出了两种机器学习模型：前馈神经网络和基于注意力的模型，用于从量子态的投影测量中预测二分第二Renyi纠缠熵，并与经典影子估计器进行性能基准比较。

Result: 机器学习方法在多种系统大小下均比经典影子估计器实现了更高的精度和更好的样本效率。

Conclusion: 机器学习在可扩展且高效地估计量子关联方面具有潜力，为量子纠缠的可靠量化提供了新途径。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement is a powerful resource for quantum-enhanced technologies. However, its reliable quantification remains challenging due to the exponential scaling of the Hilbert space with system size, which renders full state tomography infeasible. Moreover, experimentally estimating entanglement typically requires a large number of measurement samples leading to a significant overhead. In this work, we present two models, a feed-forward neural network and an attention-based model, to accurately predict the bipartite second Renyi from projective measurements of quantum states. We benchmark their performance against standard classical shadow estimators and find that the machine-learning approaches achieve higher accuracy and improved sample efficiency across a range of system sizes. Our results demonstrate the potential of machine learning for scalable and efficient estimation of quantum correlations.

</details>


### [250] [Mapping open quantum dynamics onto graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04721)
*Kyuho Kim,Dayeong Lee,Seungkyun Park,Xianji Piao,Namkyoo Park,Sunkyu Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于马尔可夫开放量子动力学的通用图论框架，将开放量子动力学映射到两个唯一定义的图上，将量子主方程解释为算子值信号在图上的平均波特征。通过开放量子Rabi模型，展示了开放系统推广的Fock态晶格、耗散的图拓扑特征以及弱到超强耦合转变的分类。图剪枝揭示了开放量子动力学的骨干结构，实现了高效的图神经网络学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放量子动力学公式只使用部分跳变算子或统计普适热库进行代数化表述，忽略了底层连接结构。为了探索开放量子系统的图论连接结构，提出一种通用的图论框架。

Method: 提出通用图论框架，将马尔可夫开放量子动力学映射到两个唯一定义的图上，将量子主方程严格解释为算子值信号在图上的平均波特征。通过开放量子Rabi模型验证，并采用图剪枝方法提取骨干结构。

Result: 实现了开放系统Fock态晶格的推广、耗散的图拓扑特征表征以及弱到超强耦合转变的分类。图剪枝揭示了动力学骨干结构，使图神经网络学习性能显著提升。

Conclusion: 该框架桥接了图论与开放量子动力学，实现了高维复杂性的高效数据驱动分析。

Abstract: Graph-theoretic frameworks have been widely employed in quantum physics to address the high-dimensional complexity of quantum systems. Although open quantum dynamics incorporates system-bath coupling via numerous interacting operators, it has been formulated algebraically with a partial set of jump operators or statistically universal reservoirs, leaving the underlying connectivity structure largely unexplored. Here, we propose a universal graph-theoretic framework for Markovian quantum dynamics. The framework maps open quantum dynamics onto two uniquely defined graphs, where the quantum master equation is rigorously interpreted as the average wave characteristic of operator-valued signals across the graphs. Applying this framework to the open quantum Rabi model, we demonstrate an open-system generalization of Fock-state lattices, characterize graph-topological signatures of dissipation, and classify the weak-to-ultrastrong coupling transition. Building on these representations, graph pruning reveals the backbone of open quantum dynamics, which enables superior graph neural-network learning. Our results bridge graph theory and open quantum dynamics, achieving efficient data-driven analysis of high-dimensional complexity.

</details>


### [251] [Reducing quantum measurements in qubit-based overlapping grouping methods for quantum energy estimation through better initializations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02794)
*Isaac L. Huidobro-Meezs,Rodrigo A. Vargas-Hernández*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出方差感知排序插入（VarSI）算法族，通过利用协方差字典构建更好的非重叠Pauli分组，显著降低了变分量子算法中哈密顿量期望值估计的测量成本。在130个分子基准测试中，VarSI相比标准SI方法实现了38%的非重叠测量改进，并将重叠方法（ICS）的测量成本进一步降低9-15.3%，最高达70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法中估计哈密顿量期望值的测量成本是核心瓶颈，现有重叠分组方法依赖非重叠分组（如SI算法）作为初始化，而不同初始化可能影响重叠方法的测量成本。作者旨在通过改进非重叠分组来进一步降低整体测量开销。

Method: 提出VarSI算法族，包含三种变体：全局贪心分组插入规则、方差感知SI类比算法、以及从SI或VarSI初始化的局部精化步骤。该方法利用重叠方法已需要的协方差字典（来自近似波函数）来指导非重叠分组的构建，并用于初始化ICS重叠方法。

Result: 在130个分子哈密顿量基准测试中，VarSI的非重叠分组测量改进比SI平均提升38%；初始化ICS后，相比标准SI初始化实现平均9-15.3%的测量减少，最高可达70%。

Conclusion: 非重叠分组即使是作为重叠估计器的初始化步骤，仍是影响最终测量成本的重要设计环节。VarSI有效弥合了非重叠与重叠分组方法之间的差距。

Abstract: The measurement cost for estimating expectation values of Hamiltonians is a central bottleneck in variational quantum algorithms. Grouping strategies significantly reduce this cost, with overlapping techniques being the state of the art in the field. Overlapping grouping methods require i) a non-overlapping grouping of the Hamiltonian, typically obtained from the Sorted Insertion (SI) algorithm as initialization, and ii) the construction of covariance dictionaries from approximate wavefunctions to guide the optimization. It was recently shown that different initializations can potentially reduce measurement costs for overlapping methods. Motivated by these findings, we introduce variance-aware SI (VarSI), a family of covariance-informed non-overlapping Pauli grouping heuristics to reduce measurement counts. VarSI grouping leverages the covariance dictionaries, already required by overlapping methods, to construct better non-overlapping groups. We propose three variants: a global greedy grouping insertion rule, a variance-informed SI analog, and a local refinement step initialized from SI or our variance-informed variant. We showcase the use of groupings generated by our VarSI heuristic algorithms to initialize overlapping methods using the iterative coefficient-splitting (ICS) algorithm. Molecular benchmarks with 130 Hamiltonians demonstrate consistent, non-overlapping measurement improvements over SI of 38\% and enhanced downstream ICS results when initialized from VarSI groups. We find that the initializations considered here achieve up to 70\% measurement reductions for ICS, compared to the standard SI initialization with mean reductions of 9--15.3\% depending on qubit mappings and covariance dictionaries used. These results show that non-overlapping grouping remains a consequential design step even when the final estimator uses overlapping fragments.

</details>


### [252] [Fault-tolerant quantum computation with static atomic buses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02804)
*Matteo Bergonzoni,Laura Pecorari,Sam Norrell,Cody Poole,Guido Pupillo,Mark Saffman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于静态原子总线的中性原子量子计算架构，利用辅助介体原子实现长距离纠缠操作，避免了原子运输带来的延迟和加热问题。通过最优控制协议设计，实现了保真度接近99.9%、时长几百纳秒的总线介导受控Z门。大规模量子纠错模拟表明，与原子穿梭架构相比，逻辑错误率提升一个数量级以上，逻辑门时间约100微秒，纠错周期约1毫秒（代码距离d<12）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在中性原子量子计算机中，可扩展、高保真度的长距离连接是实现高效量子纠错和容错量子计算的关键，但传统的原子运输方式会引入延迟和运动加热，且随系统规模增大而恶化。

Method: 提出静态原子总线架构，使用辅助介体原子实现长距离纠缠操作；将微观中性原子动力学与架构约束相结合，设计时间最优控制、相互作用平坦性和鲁棒性约束条件下的总线介导受控Z门最优控制协议。

Result: 总线介导门保真度接近99.9%，门时长仅几百纳秒；与原子穿梭架构相比，逻辑错误率提升一个数量级以上；逻辑门时间约100微秒，纠错周期约1毫秒（代码距离d<12）。

Conclusion: 静态原子总线是可扩展容错中性原子量子计算中原子穿梭架构的实用替代方案。

Abstract: Efficient quantum error correction and fault-tolerant quantum computing require scalable, high-fidelity long-range connectivity. In neutral-atom quantum computers, this is commonly achieved through atom transport, but shuttling introduces latency and motional heating that worsen with system size. Here, we introduce a neutral-atom architecture based on static atomic buses, in which auxiliary mediator atoms enable long-range entangling operations without qubit transport. The architecture naturally supports long-range stabilizer measurements in high-rate LDPC codes and transversal logical gates between neighboring surface-code patches, enabling a modular framework for efficient logical memories, Clifford computation, and magic-state distillation. To realize these capabilities, we co-design optimal-control protocols for bus-mediated controlled-Z gates that incorporate both microscopic neutral-atom dynamics and architectural constraints. We obtain smooth bus-mediated gates with fidelities approaching 99.9% and durations of a few hundred nanoseconds by combining time-optimal control with interaction-flatness and robustness constraints. Large-scale simulations of quantum error correction and logical entangling operations between neighboring surface-code patches predict more than an order-of-magnitude improvement in logical error rates compared with atom-shuttling architectures under realistic noise. The architecture achieves logical gate times of approximately 100 us and quantum-error-correction cycle times of about 1 ms for code distances d<12. These results establish static atomic buses as a practical alternative to atom shuttling for scalable fault-tolerant neutral-atom quantum computing.

</details>


### [253] [Locally Passive, Globally Charged Quantum Batteries: Coherence-Controlled Work and the Robustness of the Stored Charge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02810)
*Asad Ali,Saif Al-kuwari,James Q. Quach*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可解的充电器-电池模型，其中量子相干性控制电池的电荷存储位置及其抗噪鲁棒性。充电过程将充电器的相干性转化为充电器-电池纠缠，并将功分为局域可提取部分和关联锁定部分。对于量子比特，两者满足精确互补关系；最大相干时电池局域被动，全部电荷锁定在关联中。鲁棒性依赖于局域可访问性：可提取功基于布居数，抗纯退相干，仅受弛豫限制；关联锁定功对退相干和弛豫都脆弱。通过增益-损耗竞争代数统一处理了退相干、全局/局域退极化及振幅阻尼，并用超导transmon参数给出了具体存储寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子相干性如何影响量子电池的电荷存储位置及其在噪声环境中的存储寿命，尤其是区分局域可提取功与关联锁定功的不同鲁棒性。

Method: 构建可解的充电器-电池模型，通过增益-损耗竞争代数统一处理退相干、退极化和振幅阻尼等多种噪声，并利用超导transmon参数进行数值分析。

Result: 局域可提取功基于布居数，抗纯退相干且仅受弛豫限制；关联锁定功对退相干和弛豫都脆弱；两者存在精确互补关系。

Conclusion: 量子电池的存储寿命取决于能量存储形式：局域可提取功更鲁棒，关联锁定功更脆弱，这为设计抗噪量子电池提供了指导。

Abstract: A solvable charger--battery model is introduced in which quantum coherence controls both where a quantum battery's charge is stored and how robustly it survives noise. Charging converts the charger's coherence into charger--battery entanglement and splits the deposited work between a locally extractable part and a correlation-locked part accessible only through joint operations; for a qubit, the split obeys an exact complementarity, and at maximal coherence, the battery is locally passive with the entire charge locked in correlations. Robustness follows local accessibility: the stored energy and locally extractable work are population-based, immune to pure dephasing, and limited only by relaxation, with an energy half-life, whereas the correlation-locked work is fragile to both dephasing and relaxation. Dephasing, global and local depolarization, and amplitude damping are treated through a single gain--loss competition algebra, and the resulting storage lifetimes are made concrete with superconducting-transmon parameters.

</details>


### [254] [An approach for calculating astrophysical opacities on quantum computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02811)
*Shivesh Pathak,Alina Kononov,Andrew D. Baczewski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于计算天体物理学不透明度的量子算法协议，采用第一和第二量子化表示处理电子-光子相互作用系统，并通过相互作用图像哈密顿量模拟实现。通过光子寄存器的动量分辨测量推断不透明度，建立了量子比特数与光谱范围/分辨率之间的直接关系。对于经典上具有挑战性的太阳铁不透明度问题，其逻辑资源估计与另一个高能量密度物理问题相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天体物理不透明度计算是经典计算中极具挑战性的问题，特别是太阳铁不透明度。量子计算有望提供指数级加速，因此需要设计专门的量子算法协议来高效解决此类高能量密度物理问题。

Method: 采用第一和第二量子化表示描述相互作用的电子-光子子系统，利用相互作用图像进行哈密顿量模拟；通过对光子寄存器进行动量分辨测量来推断不透明度，建立量子比特数与光谱范围/分辨率间的直接映射关系。

Result: 实现了一种量子算法协议，可有效计算天体物理不透明度；逻辑资源估计表明，解决太阳铁不透明度问题所需资源与Rubin等人（PNAS 2024）报道的另一高能量密度物理问题相当。

Conclusion: 该量子算法协议为计算天体物理不透明度提供了可行的量子方案，资源需求与已知的高能量密度物理问题相近，展示了量子计算在天体物理领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: We propose a quantum algorithmic protocol for calculating astrophysical opacities. Our implementation uses first- and second-quantized representations of interacting electronic and photonic subsystems and Hamiltonian simulation via the interaction picture. Inferring opacity from momentum-resolved measurements of the photonic register yields a direct relationship between qubit count and spectral range/resolution. Logical resource estimates for the classically challenging problem of solar iron opacity are comparable to another high-energy-density physics problem (Rubin et al., PNAS 121(3) (2024)).

</details>


### [255] [Tunable M-Ary Quantum Secure Direct Communication via Correlation-Histogram Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02858)
*Todd M. W. Hodges*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于连续参数编码和联合直方图解码的双向量子安全直接通信（QSDC）协议，通过时间窗口内纠缠对的联合结果概率分布来扩展字母表，无需增加希尔伯特空间维度，并建立了非理想条件下的迹距离安全性分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统QSDC协议通常依赖固定维度的希尔伯特空间来编码信息，字母表大小受限于空间维度。本文旨在通过时间维度上的统计积分来扩展有效字母表，同时保持双光子极化希尔伯特空间的固定，并解决实际非理想信道和设备带来的参数依赖性泄漏问题。

Method: 协议采用双向量子纠缠源，信息编码为连续可调参数，接收端通过标准符合计数偏振测量估计联合结果直方图来解码。在理想模型中，编码操作严格局域，信道不引入参数依赖特征，飞行光子的约化态为最大混合态且与编码参数无关。建立了基于迹距离的定量框架来界定非理想效应导致的参数依赖性泄漏。

Result: 理想条件下被动截获飞行子系统无法获取任何编码信息。协议引入了运行时可调字母表和连续时间模拟调制能力，支持根据信道条件动态调整协议参数。

Conclusion: 该协议在保持固定双光子极化希尔伯特空间的前提下，通过时间窗口积分实现了可扩展字母表，增强了实际环境适应性，并提供了非理想条件下的安全性量化工具。

Abstract: We propose a two-way entanglement-based quantum secure direct communication (QSDC) protocol in which information is encoded as the value of a continuously tunable parameter and decoded from empirically estimated joint-outcome histograms over windows of detected pairs. The protocol uses a fixed two-qubit polarization Hilbert space and only standard coincidence-based polarization measurement at the receiver, with the symbol alphabet enlarged not by increasing the carrier Hilbert-space dimension but by exploiting temporal integration over a window of pairs to resolve distinguishable joint-outcome probability distributions. Under an idealized model in which the source is maximally entangled, the encoding operation is strictly local, and the channel imprints no parameter-dependent signature on the traveling photon, the reduced state of the traveling photon is maximally mixed and independent of the encoded parameter. Consequently, passive interception of the in-flight subsystem alone yields no information about the encoded message. We further develop a quantitative trace-distance framework for bounding any parameter-dependent leakage that may arise from non-ideal channel and device effects in practical implementations. The protocol introduces protocol-level design parameters not available in fixed-alphabet two-way QSDC, specifically a runtime-tunable alphabet size and native support for continuous-time analog modulation, which may be advantageous in operating environments where channel conditions vary on protocol-relevant timescales.

</details>


### [256] [Photon Squeezing and Its Signatures of Quantum Phase Transitions in the Open Quantum Rabi-Stark Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02868)
*Tian Ye,Xinghan Chen,Chen Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究开放量子Rabi-Stark模型中的稳态光学正交压缩，发现正(负)Stark耦合增强(抑制)压缩效应，压缩行为在一阶和二阶量子相变中表现出不同特征，并在二阶相变附近展现有限尺寸标度行为，有望实现近乎完美的压缩态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩光是非经典光的重要标志，具有重要的理论意义和量子技术应用前景，但在光-物质相互作用系统中对稳态光学压缩的理解仍不充分，特别是在量子相变背景下的行为有待探索。

Method: 采用量子缀饰主方程（quantum dressed master equation）对开放量子Rabi-Stark模型的稳态光学正交压缩进行数值和解析研究。

Result: (1) 正Stark耦合增强压缩，负Stark耦合抑制压缩；(2) 一阶QPT处压缩急剧消失，可作为敏感探针；(3) 二阶QPT附近压缩因子展现有限尺寸标度行为；(4) 建立了热涨落破坏量子临界性的定量判据。

Conclusion: 该研究加深了对光-物质相互作用系统中非经典光的理解，为设计强光学压缩态提供了有用见解，并为未来实验提供了理论指导。

Abstract: As a hallmark of nonclassical light, squeezed light is of profound theoretical interest and holds broad practical promise for emerging quantum technologies. In this work, we investigate steady-state optical quadrature squeezing in the open quantum Rabi-Stark model by employing the quantum dressed master equation. Both numerically and analytically, we find that positive (negative) Stark coupling tends to enhance (suppress) the squeezing effect. The quadrature squeezing exhibits distinct signatures associated with both first- and second-order quantum phase transitions (QPTs). Notably, a sharp vanishing of squeezing is observed across the first-order QPT, suggesting its potential as a sensitive probe of such transitions. In the vicinity of the second-order QPT, we further demonstrate that the squeezing factor displays finite-size scaling behavior, indicating a promising route toward the realization of near-perfect squeezing. Moreover, we establish a quantitative criterion for the disruption of quantum criticality induced by thermal fluctuations, which may offer valuable guidance for future experiments. These findings contribute to a deep understanding of nonclassical light in light-matter interacting systems and provide useful insights for the design of strong optical squeezing states.

</details>


### [257] [Decision Kernels for Quantum Error Mitigation: Why Accuracy Gains Need Not Improve Downstream Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02888)
*Vicenzo Scavino*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出量子误差缓解（QEM）通常用期望值精度来评估，但近期的量子计算工作流实际依赖的是基于间隙的决策（如argmin选择、排序等），而非绝对精度。作者提出了一个商空间理论，证明决策完备的最小对象是残差间隙律，并展示了QEM决策核是物理噪声通过缓解映射的拉回。实验表明，基于期望值精度的选择可能导致决策风险恶化，而基于残差间隙几何的决策感知选择可适度改善性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QEM通常用期望值精度来衡量，但许多近期的量子计算工作流（如argmin选择、排序、top-k过滤、优化步接受、相位标记）只依赖相对间隙而非绝对精度，导致评估指标与下游决策之间存在结构性错配。

Method: 开发了有限次QEM下游决策的商空间理论，定义了决策完备的最小对象——残差间隙律，在Gauss有限次场景下由有效边界和决策核刻画。证明了商因子分解、间隙律最小性、边际不可行定理、QEM拉回定理、Gauss决策风险公式和固定分配有限次逆界。通过Qiskit Aer仿真、Clifford数据回归、概率误差消除等方法进行实验验证。

Result: Clifford数据回归在改善均方误差的同时可能使决策"平坦"；概率误差消除虽提高精度但通过采样开销恶化决策风险；决策感知选择相对于基于精度的选择适度降低静态保持失败率，但未达到动态成功目标；在评估的机制中，应基于残差间隙几何而非期望值精度选择QEM方法。

Conclusion: 在评估的机制中，操作含义是应通过残差间隙几何来选择QEM方法，而非仅依据期望值精度。

Abstract: Quantum error mitigation (QEM) is usually benchmarked by expectation-value accuracy, but many near-term workflows use those values only to make downstream choices such as argmin selection, ranking, top-k filtering, optimizer-step acceptance, or phase labeling. This creates a structural mismatch: accuracy is measured in the ambient landscape space, whereas shift-invariant decisions depend only on gaps. We develop a quotient-space theory of finite-shot QEM for downstream decisions. The minimal decision-complete object is the residual gap law; in Gaussian finite-shot regimes it is summarized by effective margins and a decision kernel. The QEM-specific point is that this kernel is not free: it is the pullback of shared physical device noise through the mitigation map. We prove quotient factorization, gap-law minimality, a marginal no-go theorem, a QEM pullback theorem, Gaussian decision-risk formulas, and a fixed-allocation shot-level converse. Finite-shot Qiskit Aer simulations demonstrate the predicted divergence in the evaluated regimes. Clifford-data regression can be decision-flat while improving mean-squared error, and probabilistic error cancellation can improve accuracy while worsening decision risk through sampling overhead. Decision-aware selection modestly reduces static held-out failure relative to accuracy-based selection, often by retaining Raw, but the dynamic success target is not reached. Pre-registered stress tests under a calibrated device-noise model and on a hardware micro-cell probe robustness beyond these regimes. The operational implication in the evaluated regimes is to select QEM methods through residual gap geometry, not from expectation-value accuracy alone.

</details>


### [258] [Coherent Control of Three-Level System Using Shaped Free Electrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02906)
*Dixuan Wu,Jing Li,Yuhan Jiang,Yunquan Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由光学近场整形的自由电子与Λ型三能级系统的相干相互作用，揭示了电子调制与跃迁路径之间的可调谐干涉效应，实现了电子介导的相干布居捕获（CPT），并找到了可实现完全布居转移和高相干叠加态（暗态）的参数区间，这些稳态与初始状态无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三能级系统具有二能级系统所不具备的量子干涉效应，对量子光学具有重要意义。本文旨在探索利用光学近场整形的自由电子作为量子驱动源，与Λ型三能级系统进行相干相互作用的机制。

Method: 将电子束列视为量子驱动，通过研究自由电子（经光学近场整形）与Λ型三能级系统的相干相互作用，分析电子调制与三能级系统跃迁路径之间的干涉效应。

Result: 实现了电子介导的相干布居捕获（CPT），找到了可实现两个下能级之间完全布居转移的参数区间，并制备了具有高相干性的暗态；这些驱动-耗散稳态与系统初始状态无关。

Conclusion: 提出将整形自由电子作为三能级系统稳态相干控制的平台，实现原子尺度的态工程。

Abstract: Three-level systems exhibit quantum interference effects absent in two-level systems, making them important for quantum optics. Here, we study the coherent interaction of a Lambda-type three-level system with free electrons shaped by optical near fields. By treating the electron train as a quantum drive, we show that the interplay between electron modulation and the three-level system's transition pathways induces tunable interference patterns. This interaction effectively realizes electron-mediated coherent population trapping (CPT). We identify a regime that enables complete population transfer between the two lower states and the preparation of a high-coherence superposition, manifested as dark states. In particular, these driven-dissipative steady states are independent of the initial state. Our work proposes shaped free electrons as a platform for steady-state coherent control of three-level systems, enabling atomic-scale state engineering.

</details>


### [259] [Full-Period Optical Phase Estimation with Heisenberg Scaling Using Displaced Squeezed States and Gaussian Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02960)
*Marco A. Rodríguez-García,Luis Medina-Dozal,Francisco E. Becerra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段优化策略，用于固定能量约束下单模高斯态与高斯测量的全周期光学相位估计。第一阶段使用位移压缩探针和零差测量进行相位粗定位，第二阶段使用压缩真空探针与自适应零差测量进行精确定位。推导了广义Cramér-Rao界，并在E≤25光子、压缩≤12dB条件下评估了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定能量约束下，实现全周期光学相位估计的高效策略，克服传统方法在全局相位估计中的局限性，提升相位估计精度。

Method: 提出两阶段高斯策略：第一阶段采用位移压缩探针+零差测量实现相位粗定位；第二阶段采用压缩真空探针+自适应零差测量实现精确相位估计。推导了适用于该两阶段策略的广义Cramér-Rao界。

Result: 在E≤25光子、压缩≤12dB条件下，使用位移压缩态的协议相比相干态协议降低了两阶段优化界，且距离理想局部压缩真空量子Cramér-Rao界仅差3到30倍。

Conclusion: 两阶段高斯策略在有限能量和压缩限制下能有效逼近量子极限，位移压缩态在第一阶段优于相干态，为实际光学相位估计提供了可行的优化方案。

Abstract: We propose two-stage optimized strategies for full-period optical phase estimation with single-mode Gaussian states and Gaussian measurements under a fixed energy constraint. In the first stage (Stage I), displaced squeezed probes and heterodyne measurements provide coarse localization of the phase to a window on the circle. In the second stage (Stage II), squeezed-vacuum probes with adaptive homodyne measurements perform efficient phase estimation inside the selected window. We derive a generalized Cramér-Rao bound for this family of two-stage Gaussian strategies, which contains the contribution from local parameter estimation in Stage II plus an overshoot penalty from coarse localization errors in Stage I. For E <= 25 photons and squeezing limited to 12 dB, protocols using displaced squeezed states in Stage I reduce the optimized two-stage bound relative to protocols using coherent states in Stage I, and remain within a factor of 3 to 30 of the idealized local squeezed-vacuum quantum Cramér-Rao bound.

</details>


### [260] [Higher-order noise statistics restore Heisenberg scaling under collective dephasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02962)
*Jiaxin Liu,Xing Heng,Zuoxian Wang,Danyue Ma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明噪声量子计量学中，仅用单原子相干时间T₂描述退相干不足以预测纠缠探针的性能。两种具有相同单原子T₂但不同高阶统计量的集体浴会导致相反的纠缠增强标度。高斯马尔可夫集体退相下GHZ探针达到与原子数无关的灵敏度下限，而有限速率复合泊松浴中GHZ探针可恢复海森堡标度δω∝1/N。高斯下限是最坏情况，任意有限速率踢统计都严格优于它。这一机制是纯指数且CP可分的，不同于Zeno、非马尔可夫等路径。集体噪声的全计数统计成为噪声量子计量学的新控制轴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统噪声计量学理论仅用两点相关函数（即单原子相干时间T₂或噪声谱）刻画退相干，本文质疑这种描述对于纠缠探针是否充分。作者试图揭示集体浴的高阶统计量对纠缠增强计量灵敏度的影响，并寻找超越高斯噪声极限的可行路径。

Method: 本文通过对比高斯马尔可夫集体退相与复合泊松浴（有限速率相位踢序列）下的纠缠探针性能，解析计算Dicke相干性衰减率Γ_q=Γ[1-Re φ(q)]，其中φ为踢特征函数。分析了任意绝对连续踢律下的饱和行为，并推广到一般Lévy相位噪声。建立了高斯下限为最坏情况的严格证明，并给出逆界证明GHZ标度不可超越。

Result: 1) 两种具有相同单原子T₂但不同高阶统计量的集体浴导致相反的纠缠增强标度。2) 高斯马尔可夫集体退相下GHZ探针灵敏度与原子数无关（无纠缠增强）。3) 复合泊松浴中，对于任意绝对连续踢律，衰减率在大q处饱和而非按q²增长，GHZ探针可在有限速率噪声主导窗口恢复海森堡标度δω∝1/N。4) 高斯下限是严格最坏情况，任意有限速率踢统计都优于它。5) 逆界证明无输入态、辅助或测量可超越GHZ标度。

Conclusion: 集体噪声的全计数统计（而非仅噪声谱）是决定纠缠探针计量性能的关键控制轴。这一机制纯指数且CP可分，不同于Zeno效应、非马尔可夫性、非线性生成器或纠错等已知路径。该发现为噪声量子计量学开辟了超越传统谱分析的新方向，并揭示了Dicke超辐射猝灭的耗散类似物。

Abstract: Noisy-metrology theory characterizes decoherence by its two-point correlation function, equivalently the single-atom coherence time or noise spectrum. We show this is insufficient for entangled probes: two collective baths with identical single-atom $T_2$ but different higher-order statistics yield opposite entanglement-enhanced scaling. Under Gaussian Markovian collective dephasing a Greenberger--Horne--Zeilinger (GHZ) probe reaches an atom-number-independent sensitivity floor. For a fully Markovian compound-Poisson bath, in which collective dephasing is generated by a finite-rate sequence of unitary phase kicks, a Dicke coherence of order $q$ (a difference of $J_z$ eigenvalues) decays at $Γ_q=Γ[1-\mathrm{Re}\,\varphi(q)]$, with $\varphi$ the kick characteristic function; for any absolutely continuous kick law this rate saturates at large $q$ instead of growing as $q^2$, and a GHZ probe recovers Heisenberg scaling $δω\propto1/N$ over the window in which collective finite-rate noise dominates residual independent decoherence. We prove that the Gaussian floor is the exact worst case: at fixed single-atom coherence time every finite-rate kick statistics strictly beats it, and for arbitrary Lévy phase noise the asymptotic entangled-probe sensitivity is set exclusively by the diffusive component. A converse bound shows that no input state, ancilla, or measurement improves on the GHZ scaling. The mechanism is purely exponential and CP-divisible, distinct from the Zeno, non-Markovian, nonlinear-generator, and error-correction routes. A dissipative analogue caps the Dicke superradiant burst. The full counting statistics of common noise thus emerge as a control axis for noisy quantum metrology, beyond the spectrum.

</details>


### [261] [Robust phase sensitivity in Mach-Zehnder interferometer using photon added and subtracted squeezed coherent state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02984)
*Shivani Singh,Priya Malpani,Anirban Pathak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Mach-Zehnder干涉仪(MZI)中使用光子加减压缩相干态(PASCS和PSSCS)的不同组合作为输入态，利用量子Fisher信息进行相位估计。发现两个输入模式均使用PASCS时能获得最精确的相位估计，且PASCS在低光子损耗情况下具有鲁棒性。同时分析了单强度检测和强度差检测两种测量方案，指出强度测量并非MZI相位估计的最优测量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索通过非经典输入态（如光子加减压缩相干态）来提升Mach-Zehnder干涉仪相位估计精度的可能性，寻找最优的输入态组合和测量方案。

Method: 使用量子Fisher信息计算相位估计精度上限，比较不同输入态组合（PASCS和PSSCS的各种组合）在MZI中的表现；分析单强度检测(SID)和强度差检测(IDD)两种测量方案；研究光子损耗对相位估计的影响。

Result: 两个输入模式均使用PASCS时可获得最精确的相位估计；强度测量（SID和IDD）不是MZI相位估计的最优测量方案；PASCS在低光子损耗条件下具有较好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PASCS输入态能显著提升MZI相位估计精度，且在一定损耗条件下保持优势，但需要寻找比强度测量更优的测量方案以充分发挥其潜力。

Abstract: For the precision-based measurements, Mach-Zehnder interferometry is a widely used technique. There are various ways to enhance the precision of Mach-Zehnder interferometer (MZI), e.g., having a non-classical input state is one of the ways to enhance the precision of the phase estimation performed by MZI. The phase estimation performed by MZI is investigated here by considering that the input states of MZI are different combinations of photon added and subtracted squeezed coherent states (PASCS and PSSCS). Using quantum Fisher information, it is shown that the use of PASCS in both the input modes of MZI, provides the most precise estimate of the unknown phase. This system is also analyzed in two different measurement scenarios -- single intensity detection (SID) and intensity difference detection (IDD). Systematic analysis has established that the intensity measurement might not be an optimal measurement scheme for phase estimation in MZI as phase and intensity correspond to non-commuting observables. The impact of the photon loss on the MZI-based phase estimation setup is also studied and it is found that PASCS is robust against photon loss, when the loss in MZI is low.

</details>


### [262] [Time-dependent multiparameter estimation for quantum experiments via online-offline sequential Monte-Carlo method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02987)
*Chattamas Manoworakul,Jason F. Ralph,Nattaphong Wonglakhon,Areeya Chantasri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合估计方法，将在线顺序蒙特卡洛采样器与离线时间批处理估计技术相结合，用于处理高噪声测量和快速检测率的系统。该方法通过将信号分批平均得到平均轨迹，使用批平均Kraus映射演化系统动力学，并在超导量子比特实验（荧光测量和色散z测量）中验证，结果显示在荧光情况下优于标准校准方法，在色散情况下能发现标准方法无法检测到的参数突变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线估计方法在处理高噪声测量和快速检测率的系统时表现不足，需要更鲁棒的混合估计方案。

Method: 将顺序蒙特卡洛采样器与时间批处理估计技术结合，通过连续监控系统将信号分批平均为平均轨迹，利用批平均Kraus映射演化系统动力学，并推导了高效的近似方法。算法采用模块化推导，并在超导量子比特实验的两种测量设置（荧光测量和色散z测量）上实现。

Result: 在荧光测量情况下，算法优于标准校准方法；在色散测量情况下，算法能够发现标准方法无法检测到的参数值意外跳变。超参数通过独立数值模拟选择，估计精度通过信号重建方法验证。

Conclusion: 提出的混合估计方法在高噪声、快速检测率的量子系统中比传统方法更准确、更灵敏，尤其能检测到参数突变等非平凡动态行为。

Abstract: While typical online estimation methods can estimate the multiparameter dynamics of many systems, they may not be sufficient for a system with highly noisy measurement and rapid detection rate. In this paper, we create a hybrid estimation method by augmenting the sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) sampler, an online estimation method with an offline technique known as the time-batch estimation technique. By continuously monitoring the system, we may divide signals into batches and average them into an averaged trajectory. The system dynamics is then evolved with batch-averaged Kraus maps, for which we derive a highly efficient approximation. To facilitate the adoption of our algorithm, we present a modular derivation of the SMC methods and showcase our algorithm as an explicit example. We then implement our algorithm on the measurement signals obtained from superconducting-qubit experiments under two types of measurement setting: a fluorescence measurement and a dispersive $z$-measurement. The algorithm's hyperparameter values are chosen from independent numerical simulation, while the accuracy of our estimation is validated by a signal reconstruction method. Our results show that, for the fluorescence case, our algorithm can estimate the system's parameters better than the standard calibration method, and, for the dispersive case, our estimation is capable of finding an unexpected jump in parameter values that the standard calibration method could not find.

</details>


### [263] [Error-tolerant secure key leasing for quantum decryption keys in public-key encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02989)
*Duo Xu,Yuki Takeuchi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个容错的安全密钥租赁（SKL）协议，用于公钥加密。在该协议中，出租方将私钥编码为量子态并租赁给承租方，即使私钥存在噪声，承租方仍能正确解密，出租方也能在密钥返还时进行验证。协议不改变私钥编码方式，无额外量子信息处理开销，并通过将容错SKL与容错认证删除问题桥接，分析了鲁棒性与安全性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全密钥租赁（SKL）协议假设密钥在传输和使用过程中无噪声，但实际量子系统中噪声不可避免。因此，作者希望设计一种在噪声环境下仍能保持正确性和安全性的SKL协议。

Method: 协议不改变私钥的量子编码方式，因此无额外量子信息处理开销。作者通过将容错SKL与容错认证删除（使用缩短码）进行理论桥接，利用编码理论分析安全性与鲁棒性之间的权衡。当错误量低于特定阈值时，承租方能正确解密，出租方能正确验证密钥返还。

Result: 提出了首个容错SKL协议，在噪声环境下仍能保持解密正确性和验证正确性。建立了容错SKL安全性与容错认证删除（使用缩短码）之间的理论联系，为安全性分析提供了新框架。

Conclusion: 本文提出的容错SKL协议填补了现有SKL在噪声环境下的空白，其理论框架为后续研究安全性与鲁棒性的权衡奠定了基础，并开辟了编码理论与量子密码安全分析的新交叉方向。

Abstract: We propose the first error-tolerant secure key leasing (SKL) for public-key encryption. As with SKL in prior works, our protocol consists of a lessor and lessee. In the protocol, the lessor encodes its secret key into quantum states and leases the key to the lessee. Then, the lessor can ask the lessee to return the secret key at a later point. The lessor is able to check whether the lessee has returned its key honestly. However, our protocol works even when the leased secret key is subject to noise. The lessee decrypts the ciphertext correctly, and the lessor verifies the return of the secret key correctly when the amount of error is below a certain threshold. Our improved protocol does not change the encoding of the secret key, and thus adds no overhead to the quantum information processing. Our most significant result is a framework to analyze the trade-off between robustness against error and security. We bridge the security of the error-tolerant SKL and that of the error-tolerant certified deletion with shortened codes, which is a relatively less explored concept in coding theory.

</details>


### [264] [Symmetry-Resolved Parent Hamiltonians for Entangled Bosonic Cat Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02997)
*Šimon Bräuer,Klaus Mølmer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了多模玻色子猫资源态的父哈密顿量，通过分离通用分支哈密顿量和状态依赖约束哈密顿量，构建了GHZ、团簇和W型猫态的父哈密顿量，并在大|α|极限下退化为稳定的逻辑量子比特哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立从相干态玻色子工程到基于稳定子的量子信息处理的直接桥梁，为多模玻色子猫态提供系统性的父哈密顿量构造方法。

Method: 将哈密顿量构造分为两部分：通用分支哈密顿量（将每个模式限制在相干态支撑|±α⟩上）和状态依赖约束哈密顿量（在分支流形内选择所需的关联和对称性扇区），逐步消除低能流形中的简并性。

Result: 得到了GHZ态、团簇态和W型猫态的显式父哈密顿量；在大|α|极限下，玻色子父哈密顿量退化为作用于有效逻辑量子比特基的稳定子或交换哈密顿量。

Conclusion: 该构造为相干态玻色子工程与稳定子量子信息处理之间建立了直接联系。

Abstract: We derive parent Hamiltonians in terms of oscillator operators for multimode bosonic cat resource states. The construction separates a universal branch Hamiltonian, which confines each mode to the coherent-state support $ |\pmα\rangle$, and state-dependent constraint Hamiltonians, which select the desired correlations and symmetry sectors inside the resulting branch manifold. This framework progressively removes degeneracies in the low-energy manifold and yields explicit parent Hamiltonians for GHZ-, cluster-, and W-type cat states. In the large-$|α|$ limit, the bosonic parent Hamiltonians reduce to stabilizer or exchange Hamiltonians acting on an effective logical-qubit basis. The present construction provides a direct bridge between coherent-state bosonic engineering and stabilizer-based quantum information processing.

</details>


### [265] [Fringe field induced spin-OAM mixing of twisted electrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03002)
*S. V. Gatalina,S. S. Baturin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了扭曲电子在轴对称磁线圈入口或出口区域传播时的自旋效应，通过Foldy-Wouthuysen约化推导出傍轴自旋子方程，发现边缘场梯度会产生横向Pauli耦合，并利用Ermakov映射精确处理标量横向动力学，在一阶微扰下给出了任意轴对称螺线管轮廓下的自旋-轨道混合定量描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解扭曲电子在真实磁透镜和螺线管元件中的自旋-轨道混合机制，为可控的自旋-OAM转换提供理论框架。

Method: 从Dirac方程的Foldy-Wouthuysen约化出发，推导含纵向变化螺线管场的傍轴自旋子方程；利用Ermakov映射将依赖纵向的聚焦吸收为度量缩放变换，将轨道演化约化为二维谐振子问题；将横向Pauli项作为微扰处理，利用轴对称性下的总角动量投影守恒进行分析。

Result: 得到了任意实际螺线管轮廓下一阶修正的显式表达式；自旋翻转伴随轨道角动量的单量子变化；一阶动力学限制在每个入射振子分量最多两个相邻径向边带；推导了跃迁振幅，其相位由Ermakov累积和对角自旋-轨道旋转共同决定。

Conclusion: 该框架为量化真实磁透镜和螺线管束流元件中的扭曲电子自旋-轨道混合提供了直接方法，并指出了在工程化磁场边缘序列中实现可控自旋-OAM转换的途径。

Abstract: We study spin effects in twisted-electron propagation through the entrance or exit region of an axially symmetric magnetic coil. Starting from the Foldy-Wouthuysen reduction of the Dirac equation, we derive a paraxial spinor equation in which the longitudinally varying solenoidal field produces, in addition to the usual diagonal Zeeman term, a transverse Pauli coupling proportional to the fringe-field gradient. The scalar transverse dynamics is treated exactly by the Ermakov mapping, which absorbs the longitudinally dependent focusing into a metaplectic scaling transformation and reduces the orbital evolution to that of a stationary two-dimensional oscillator. On this background, the transverse Pauli term is treated perturbatively and yields an explicit first-order correction for arbitrary realistic solenoidal profiles. Axial symmetry implies conservation of the total projection of angular momentum, so each spin flip is accompanied by a compensating one-quantum change of orbital angular momentum. In addition, the linear coordinate structure of the perturbation restricts the first-order dynamics to at most two neighboring radial sidebands for each incoming oscillator component. We derive the corresponding transition amplitudes and show how their phases are governed jointly by the Ermakov accumulation and the diagonal spin-orbital rotation. The resulting framework provides a direct way to quantify spin-orbit mixing of twisted electrons in realistic magnetic lenses and solenoidal beam-line elements, and it identifies a route toward controlled spin-OAM conversion in engineered sequences of magnetic-field edges.

</details>


### [266] [Single-acquisition tomography of photonic qubits with structured media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03052)
*Francesco Di Colandrea,Yingwen Zhang,John Grace,Dilip Paneru,Alessio D'Errico,Ebrahim Karimi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于液晶超表面的结构各向异性介质平台，可在单次采集内实现光子偏振量子态的信息完备测量，无需重新配置测量装置，且与光子数无关。实验展示了单帧量子态层析在单光子和双光子偏振态上的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子态层析需要对大量相同副本进行一系列投影测量，需频繁重配置测量装置，且随希尔伯特空间维度增加测量次数急剧增长，亟需更高效、可扩展的单次测量方案。

Method: 利用三块具有空间变化光轴取向的液晶超表面，将输入偏振态转换为远场离散横向动量模式分布，通过衍射图案唯一确定偏振态。该方法与光子数无关，可通过后选择对应n重符合事件选取所需光子数扇区。

Result: 实验上实现了单光子和双光子偏振态的单帧量子态层析，证明了该平台的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种简单、可扩展的高效量子态表征路径，避免了传统方法中的顺序测量和装置重配置，为量子技术中的态表征提供了新范式。

Abstract: Quantum state tomography is an essential tool for characterizing quantum systems and underpins nearly every experimental realization of quantum technologies. Conventional tomography relies on performing a sequence of projective measurements on many identical copies of a quantum state, requiring the measurement apparatus to be reconfigured between successive acquisitions. As the Hilbert-space dimension increases, the number of required measurements grows rapidly; in practice, additional overcomplete measurements are often performed to improve robustness to experimental imperfections. Here, we introduce a tomography platform based on structured anisotropic media that performs informationally complete measurements of photonic polarization qubits within a single acquisition. The approach employs three liquid-crystal metasurfaces with spatially varying optic-axis orientations that transform the input polarization into a far-field distribution of discrete transverse-momentum modes. Each diffraction pattern uniquely determines the polarization state, enabling its reconstruction without sequential changes to the measurement apparatus. Unlike previous implementations, our scheme is intrinsically photon-number independent: the same optical device operates identically for arbitrary photon numbers, while the desired photon-number sector can be selected afterwards through post-selection of the corresponding $n$-fold coincidence events. We experimentally demonstrate single-frame quantum state tomography of both single- and two-photon polarization states, providing a simple and scalable route toward efficient quantum-state characterization.

</details>


### [267] [ORBIT-Q: Dual-axis benchmarking of autonomous agents in scientific quantum programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03105)
*Shi-Xin Zhang,Yu-Qin Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: ORBIT-Q是一个针对量子计算领域科研编程的基准测试，结合多层级验证管道，支持智能体框架和量子软件框架的对比评估。实验表明TensorCircuit-NG性能最优，Codex+GPT-5.5组合最强，但自主智能体与人类专家之间仍存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编程基准测试多聚焦于常规编程任务，而科学计算（尤其是量子计算）需要超越简单功能测试的严格验证范式，涵盖物理保真度、可微分工作流、框架原生语义和可扩展表示等维度，因此需要专门的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了一套精心策划的复杂科研级量子工作流套件，结合多层级验证管道，支持两种正交对比：(1) 固定量子软件框架下不同智能体组合的对比；(2) 固定智能体下不同量子软件框架的对比。

Result: TensorCircuit-NG (TC) 在自主编程驱动下展现出最高的能力和性能效率；Codex + GPT-5.5 是TC上测试最强的智能体配置；但前沿自主智能体与人类专家参考实现之间仍存在显著的性能和设计差距。

Conclusion: ORBIT-Q可作为自主科研编程、框架-智能体协同以及量子软件性能的严谨基准，揭示了当前自主智能体在复杂科研编程任务中的局限性。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents perform well on many conventional programming tasks, but scientific computing demands a rigorous validation paradigm that extends beyond simple functional test completion: generated code must preserve physical fidelity, differentiable workflows, framework-native semantics, and scalable representations. We introduce Open Research Benchmark for Integrated Tasks in Quantum Computing (ORBIT-Q) to address this gap. At its core, ORBIT-Q contributes a carefully curated suite of complex, research-level quantum workflows that serves as a challenging testbed for modern scientific programming. ORBIT-Q combines a rigorous multi-tier verification pipeline to support two orthogonal comparisons: different agent harness and model configurations at a fixed quantum software framework, and different quantum software frameworks at a fixed agent. In our systematic evaluations, TensorCircuit-NG (TC) exhibits the highest capability and performance efficiency among the evaluated quantum software frameworks under agent-driven programming, and Codex with GPT-5.5 is the strongest tested agent configuration on TC. However, a significant performance and design gap remains between frontier autonomous agents and human expert reference implementations. We further evaluate two efficiency dimensions: agent-side resource use and artifact-side runtime. Together, these results establish ORBIT-Q as a rigorous benchmark for autonomous scientific programming, framework-agent synergy, and quantum software performance.

</details>


### [268] [Geometric modulation of transition and survival intensities in non-Hermitian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03164)
*Ryu Frieson,Takashi Oka,Hideaki Obuse*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了几何调制框架到多能级系统，展示了复贝里相位如何导致非厄米系统中跃迁和生存强度的非互易性，并以非厄米Landau-Zener问题为例进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米系统的非酉时间演化产生多种新现象，其中复贝里相位诱导的状态放大/抑制是重要课题。此前标准非厄米LZ问题中跃迁强度不呈现非互易性的物理起源尚不明确，本文旨在揭示这一机制。

Method: 将几何调制框架从两能级推广到多能级系统，并应用于非厄米Landau-Zener问题的两个变体（半LZ问题和标准LZ问题），通过分析复贝里相位对跃迁和存活概率的影响来揭示非互易性的起源。

Result: 在半LZ问题中，跃迁和生存概率均因复贝里相位展现出非互易性；在标准LZ问题中，揭示了此前未知的跃迁强度非互易性实际上也起源于复贝里相位。

Conclusion: 复贝里相位是非厄米Landau-Zener系统中非互易行为的根本物理起源，这一发现统一解释了半LZ和标准LZ问题中的非互易现象。

Abstract: The time evolution of non-Hermitian systems is generally nonunitary. Dynamics governed by time-dependent non-Hermitian Hamiltonians lead to a variety of novel phenomena, one of which is state amplification or suppression induced by the complex Berry phase. Here, we extend the framework of geometric modulation to multi-level systems and show that both transition and survival intensities can be modulated. We apply our theory to the non-Hermitian Landau-Zener (LZ) problem. First, we show that, in the half-LZ problem, both the transition and survival probabilities exhibit nonreciprocity due to the complex Berry phase. In the non-Hermitian standard LZ problem, only the survival intensity is known to exhibit nonreciprocity, whereas the transition intensity does not. However, the physical origin of this nonreciprocal behavior remains unclear. In this work, we show that the nonreciprocity originates from the complex Berry phase.

</details>


### [269] [Quantum Kolmogorov--Arnold representation theorem for continuous unitary-valued maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03187)
*Sviatoslav V. Dzhenzher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子版本的Kolmogorov-Arnold表示定理，证明在单位矩阵的1-邻域内，任何连续酉算子值函数可分解为单变量矩阵指数的加法或乘积形式，并证明该表示无法全局扩展到整个酉群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受经典Kolmogorov-Arnold表示定理及其在KAN和量子KAN（QKAN）中应用的启发，探索该定理在量子域中酉算子值连续多变量函数上的类比表示。

Method: 首先，在单位矩阵的1-邻域内，对反厄米矩阵值映射建立加性分解表示定理；其次，利用量子算子的非交换性，推导出目标酉映射可分解为单变量矩阵指数的有限序列乘积形式；最后，基于SU(2)的提升性质构造拓扑反例。

Result: 在O₁(I)邻域内成功建立了酉算子值函数的加性和乘性分解表示；同时通过反例证明这些局部表示定理无法全局扩展到整个酉群U(n)。

Conclusion: 量子Kolmogorov-Arnold表示定理仅在局部成立，酉群的整体拓扑结构本质上阻碍了该表示的全局推广。

Abstract: The classical Kolmogorov--Arnold representation theorem states that any continuous multivariate function can be exactly decomposed into a finite composition of univariate continuous functions and addition operations.
  This foundational result has recently inspired the development of Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs) in classical machine learning, as well as their extensions into the quantum domain (QKANs). In this paper, we establish two quantum analogues of the Kolmogorov--Arnold representation theorem for continuous unitary-valued maps of several variables within an open $1$-neighbourhood of the identity matrix \(O_1(\mathbf{I}) \subset \mathcal{U}(n)\).
  First, we prove a representation theorem that yields an exact additive decomposition inside the matrix exponent of anti-Hermitian-valued maps.
  Second, due to the non-commutative nature of quantum operators, we derive a factorised version expressing the target unitary map as a finite sequential product of univariate matrix exponentials. Finally, we provide a concrete topological counterexample based on the lifting property of \(\mathcal{SU}(2)\) to demonstrate that these local representation theorems cannot be globally extended to the entire unitary group \(\mathcal{U}(n)\) without encountering fundamental structural obstructions.

</details>


### [270] [Self-Specializing Vision-Language Transmon Chip Calibration in a Physics-Grounded Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03193)
*Animesh Tripathy,Aswanth Krishnan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套基于视觉语言智能体的超导transmon芯片校准系统，通过物理仿真环境、端到端运行智能体和无梯度在线自适应机制，在不更新权重的情况下实现了芯片校准性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导transmon芯片校准是一个受噪声、漂移和有限预算影响的序贯决策问题，专家需要选择实验、解读模棱两可的曲线、判断拟合质量并在芯片漂移时修正过时的信念。现有方法缺少能够闭环运行并自适应单个物理器件的视觉语言智能体。

Method: 三组件协同设计：(1) 基于scqubits的物理仿真环境，包含磁通线畸变、墙钟时间尺度漂移和栅泄露等真实物理效应；(2) 端到端视觉语言智能体，调用工具、读取曲线、维护结构化笔记并提交参数；(3) 无梯度在线自适应：反射器从过往尝试中提取无真值异常签名，生成设备笔记追加至提示词，通过配对快照验收门控隔离策略改进与漂移。

Result: 在预算受限的硬层级芯片上，六次迭代将最差CZ保真度从0.678提升至0.787并降低方差，在四量子比特尺度复现；单次接受的笔记在其配对快照上将CZ保真度从0.678提升至0.913。植入故障实验确认笔记具有因果性，可在无真值条件下诊断硬件故障，主要价值在于提升故障下限并降低方差。

Conclusion: 该智能体、评分和奖励机制可通过替换测量后端迁移至真实硬件；仅验收门控是仿真环境特有的人为设计，可简化为留出切片或重复平均形式。该方法有效实现了无需权重更新的芯片级自适应校准。

Abstract: Calibrating a superconducting transmon chip is a sequential decision problem under noise, drift, and a finite budget: an expert must choose experiments, read ambiguous plots, judge fit quality, and revise stale beliefs as the chip drifts. We study whether a vision-language agent can close this loop and specialize itself to one physical device without weight updates, via three co-designed artifacts. The first is a physics-grounded simulation environment for transmon chips: calibration observables derive from circuit-quantized parameters via scqubits, with realistic flux-line distortion, wall-time-scaled and mid-scan drift, and gate leakage, concerns a toy simulator would omit; each tool call advances a modeled clock so drift accrues by wall time, not call count. The second is a vision-language agent that runs the loop end to end, calling tools, reading plots, maintaining a structured notebook, and submitting parameters without hidden truth, scored against hidden parameters and gate fidelities measured on the device. The third is gradient-free online adaptation: a reflector reads truth-free anomaly signatures from past attempts and grows a small, human-readable device note appended to the prompt, admitted by a paired-snapshot accept gate that isolates strategy improvement from drift. On a hard-tier chip under budget pressure, six iterations raised the worst-case CZ fidelity from 0.678 to 0.787 and cut its variance, reproducing at four-qubit scale; a single accepted note raised CZ fidelity from 0.678 to 0.913 on its paired snapshot. A planted-fault study confirms the note is causal, diagnosing a hardware fault truth-free, its principal value raising the failure floor and cutting variance. The agent, scoring, and reward transfer to real hardware via a measurement-backend swap; only the accept gate is a simulation affordance, reducing to a held-out-slice or repeat-and-average form.

</details>


### [271] [Broadband Characterization of Polarization Mode Dispersion for Quantum Communication Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03202)
*Vadim Rodimin,Konstantin Kravtsov,Rui Ming Chua,Xingjian Zhang,Aleksei Ponasenko,Yury Kurochkin,Alexander Ling,James A. Grieve*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种表征宽带量子信号偏振光纤信道的方法，利用奇异值分解（SVD）获得最优输入态、互无偏测量基及其不保真度，并通过奇异值分离一阶与高阶偏振模色散（PMD），在Masdar City部署光纤链路中验证了该方法，展示了双通道级联下的PMD抑制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽带量子信号在光纤中传输时，窄带滤波会浪费光子通量，而偏振模色散（PMD）会引入随波长变化的偏振演化，导致信道退化。现有方法缺乏对宽带条件下偏振信道的紧凑表征和优化策略。

Method: 将波长相关的PMD映射为庞加莱球上的轨迹，对频带平均旋转矩阵进行奇异值分解，闭式导出最优输入态、互无偏测量基及其不保真度，三个奇异值作为带宽相关的信道签名，区分一阶与高阶PMD，并定义5%不保真度带宽作为滤波预算。

Result: 在Masdar City部署的光纤链路中实现了信道表征，并通过单个偏振控制器级联两个信道实现了PMD抑制。

Conclusion: 该方法为宽带量子信号的偏振信道提供了紧凑的解析表征工具，有效分离了PMD的阶次，并给出了实用的滤波预算指导，实验验证了其在实际部署光纤中的可行性。

Abstract: We present a method for characterizing polarization fiber channels carrying broadband quantum signals, where narrowband filtering would waste photon flux. Wavelength-dependent polarization mode dispersion (PMD) maps each input state to a trajectory on the Poincaré sphere; we show that the singular value decomposition of the band-averaged rotation matrix yields, in closed form, the optimal input states, the mutually unbiased measurement bases, and their infidelities. The three singular values provide a compact, bandwidth-dependent channel signature that separates first- from higher-order PMD, and the resulting 5%-infidelity bandwidth gives a practical filtering budget. We characterize deployed fiber links in Masdar City and demonstrate PMD mitigation by concatenating two channels through a single polarization controller.

</details>


### [272] [False vacuum decay in long-range interacting quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03274)
*Valerio Pagni,Laura Batini,Nicolò Defenu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有长程相互作用的混合场伊辛链中的假真空衰变问题，发现非局域反弹解具有各向异性和代数拖尾，改变了标准薄壁图像。对于不同长程指数σ，寿命标度行为不同，但薄壁指数在主导项中保持不变，修正出现在次主导项中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解长程相互作用如何改变量子模拟器中的泡核化（假真空衰变）过程，挑战传统短程相互作用的薄壁图像。

Method: 将问题映射为非局域欧几里得φ⁴理论，其中分数阶空间动能项∼|q|^σ（σ=α-1）。结合薄壁论分析和全非局域鞍点方程的数值求解。

Result: 对于0<σ<1，寿命指数B∼h^{-1/σ}；对于1<σ<2，主导项恢复Coleman标度B∼h^{-1}，长程效应体现在次主导项∼h^{σ-2}中。非局域反弹解在时空上各向异性，具有代数拖尾。

Conclusion: 可调长程相互作用从根本上重塑了泡核化过程，改变了量子模拟器中的假真空衰变行为，为实验调控真空衰变提供了新途径。

Abstract: We formulate false-vacuum decay in a mixed-field Ising chain with $1/r^α$ interactions as a spatially nonlocal Euclidean $φ^4$ theory featuring a fractional spatial kinetic term $\sim |q|^σ$, where $σ=α-1$. The nonlocal bounce is anisotropic in space-time and develops algebraic spatial tails, challenging the standard thin-wall picture of a compact droplet. Combining thin-wall arguments with numerical solutions of the full nonlocal saddle, we show that these tails preserve the leading thin-wall exponents, manifesting instead in subleading corrections. For $0<σ<1$, the lifetime exponent scales with the energy bias $h$ of the metastable state as $B\sim h^{-1/σ}$; for $1<σ<2$, the leading Coleman scaling $B\sim h^{-1}$ is recovered, while long-range effects are retained in the subdominant term $\sim h^{σ-2}$. Our results show that tunable long-range interactions fundamentally reshape bubble nucleation and alter false-vacuum decay in quantum simulators.

</details>


### [273] [Quantum Annealing for Dynamic Portfolio Optimization under Realistic Transaction Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03218)
*Escolástico Sánchez-Martínez,Senaida Hernandez Santana,Ventura Sarasa Laborda,Pablo Serrano Molinero,Guillermo Botella Juan,Alberto del Barrio García*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在现实交易和配置约束下，量子与古典投资策略在投资组合构建中的对比，通过QUBO建模和混合量子-经典优化求解，并从业务绩效和计算性能两个维度进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子退火在受约束投资组合优化中的实际作用，厘清其相对于经典方法的建模优势与当前实现局限。

Method: 将投资组合选择问题通过有限二进制编码重构为QUBO模型，嵌入约束二次模型(CQM)并采用混合量子-经典优化工作流求解；同时引入经典基准分配策略进行对比评估。

Result: 从业务绩效（收益、波动率、夏普比率）和计算性能（求解器结构、约束处理、硬件执行特征）两个互补视角评估了组合效率。

Conclusion: 量子退火在受约束投资组合优化中具有建模优势，但当前实现仍存在相对于成熟经典方法的局限性。

Abstract: This paper investigates and compares quantum and classical investment strategies for portfolio construction under realistic trading and allocation constraints. The study considers a multi-period portfolio re-balancing setting in which asset weights are subject to lower and upper bounds, class-level exposure restrictions, and turnover limitations. On the quantum side, the portfolio selection problem is reformulated as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) model through a finite binary encoding of asset weights, and subsequently embedded into a constrained quadratic model (CQM) solved by a hybrid quantum-classical optimization workflow. On the classical side, benchmark allocation strategies are introduced to provide an economically meaningful reference for performance assessment. The proposed framework jointly evaluates portfolio efficiency from two complementary perspectives: business performance, measured through return, volatility, and a measure of the trade-off between them, the Sharpe ratio; and computational performance, assessed through solver structure, constraint handling, and hardware execution characteristics. The resulting comparison provides a rigorous basis for understanding the practical role of quantum annealing in constrained portfolio optimization, while clarifying both its modeling advantages and its current implementation limitations relative to established classical approaches.

</details>


### [274] [Matter-Field Exchange Generates Entanglement, Not Classical Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03429)
*Nicetu Tibau Vidal,Aditya Varna Iyer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了Aziz-Howl混合理论中经典引力诱导纠缠的机制，指出其非可分离项实际来源于同一量子物质场在两干涉仪间的传播通道，而非经典引力自由度介导的纠缠。通过使用不同物质种类或插入势垒可消除该效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Aziz-Howl提出的混合理论（量子物质+经典引力）能否产生类似BMV型纠缠 witnesses 所关注的纠缠，澄清经典引力是否真的能介导纠缠。

Method: 在微扰QFT框架下分析Aziz-Howl机制，追踪其非可分离项的来源，检查传播子结构和后选择投影操作对最终态的影响。

Result: Aziz-Howl效应依赖于同一量子物质场在两干涉仪间的相干传播振幅，且后选择投影去除了记录物质场污染、模式变形或交换的扇区。若使用不同物质场或插入势垒，交叉传播子消失，纠缠图为零。

Conclusion: Aziz-Howl效应是经典背景下的物质扇区串扰效应，而非经典引力自由度介导的纠缠，可通过使用不同物质种类或抑制传播的势垒消除。

Abstract: Aziz and Howl have argued that a hybrid theory with quantum matter and a classical gravitational field can generate entanglement between two massive systems. In their construction, the branch-dependent contribution appears at fourth order through propagators of the quantum matter field in a fixed classical gravitational potential. We analyse this mechanism within the same perturbative QFT framework and show that the effect should not be interpreted as classical gravity mediating entanglement in the sense relevant to BMV-type witnesses. The non-separable term relies on a quantum-matter exchange channel between the two interferometers and is present only when the two systems are modelled as excitations of the same matter field. If distinct, non-interconverting matter fields describe the systems, the corresponding cross-propagator is absent and the Aziz-Howl entangling diagram vanishes. The effect relies on coherent propagation amplitudes of the quantum matter field between the two interferometers, together with postselection onto the original localised branch subspace. Moreover, the inference of entanglement is made after projecting the full QFT evolution onto a restricted final subspace containing the original localised $N$-particle branch states. This projection removes precisely the sectors that would record matter-field contamination, mode deformation, or exchange between the two interferometers. We therefore argue that the Aziz-Howl mechanism is a matter-sector cross-talk effect in a classical background, not entanglement mediated by classical gravitational degrees of freedom. Having identified the channel responsible for the Aziz-Howl contribution, we predict that it can be eliminated by using distinct, non-interconverting matter species in the two interferometers, or by inserting a barrier that suppresses matter-field propagation between them.

</details>


### [275] [A Galton-Watson estimate for Dyson series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03235)
*Hans Maassen,Dmitri Botvich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了以有根树标记的特定积分级数的收敛性问题，给出了收敛的充要条件及级数和的显式表达式。所用技术强烈 reminiscent of Galton-Watson 分支过程的生成函数方法。该级数源于量子力学中自由动力学被局域势微扰的 Dyson 级数展开：级数收敛意味着微扰动力学存在且与自由动力学酉等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源于量子力学中自由动力学受局域势微扰的 Dyson 级数展开问题，该级数的收敛性直接决定微扰动力学是否存在以及是否与自由动力学酉等价，因此需要严格分析这类积分级数的收敛条件。

Method: 采用 reminiscent of Galton-Watson 分支过程的生成函数方法，将积分级数与有根树结构对应，通过组合分析建立收敛的充要条件并推导级数和的显式表达式。

Result: 得到了该类以有根树标记的积分级数收敛的充要条件，并给出了级数和的显式表达式。

Conclusion: 该积分级数的收敛性可等价于微扰动力学存在且与自由动力学酉等价，从而为量子力学中局域势微扰问题提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: We consider the question of convergence of particular series of integrals, which are labeled by rooted trees. Necessary and sufficient criteria for convergence are obtained, together with an explicit expression for the sum. The technique used is strongly reminiscent of the generating function approach of Galton and Watson to branching processes. The interest in these series derives from the Dyson series expansion for the perturbation of a free quantum dynamics by a local potential: the convergence of the series imlies that the perturbed dynamics exists and is unitarily equivalent with the free one.

</details>


### [276] [Generating one-way computations with flow: flow-preserving rewriting that ignores the interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03250)
*Miriam Backens*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出仅使用三种流保持重写规则，就能从具有所需输入输出数量的平凡图生成任意具有流的一路计算图，且这些规则不要求保持语义解释，适用于生成测试实例和量子机器学习拟设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流保持重写方法同时保持了图的语义解释，限制了其应用范围。为了扩展流保持重写的用途（如生成测试实例和量子机器学习拟设），需要研究不保持解释但保持流存在的重写规则。

Method: 研究并证明了一组仅包含三种流保持重写规则（几乎等同于Pauli测量下完整的一套流与解释保持重写规则，是任意测量下流与解释保持重写规则的一小部分）足以从平凡图生成任意具有流的一路计算图。

Result: 发现仅三种流保持重写规则就足以从具有所需输入输出数量的平凡图生成任意具有流的一路计算图。该规则集几乎等同于Pauli测量下完整的一套流与解释保持重写规则，是任意测量下该规则集的一小部分。

Conclusion: 不保持解释的流保持重写规则可以扩展一路计算的应用场景，为软件测试实例生成和量子机器学习拟设生成提供了新方法。

Abstract: The one-way model is a universal model of quantum computation, driven by successive adaptive single-qubit measurements on an entangled resource state. Measurements are non-deterministic, yet if the computation satisfies one of several related families of conditions known as 'flows', the computation can be made deterministic overall by modifying later measurements depending on the outcomes of earlier ones. Flow properties also enable efficient translation from one-way computations to circuits, motivating research into rewriting one-way computations while preserving the existence of flow. Existing approaches to flow-preserving rewriting are used for compilation or optimisation and preserve both the interpretation and the existence of flow.
  Here, we broaden our perspective to consider flow-preserving rewriting that does not necessarily preserve the interpretation, with applications to creating test instances for software that works with flow, as well as to generating ansätze for quantum machine learning. We show that a family of just three flow-preserving rewrite rules suffices to generate any diagram with flow from a trivial diagram with the desired number of inputs and outputs. This rule set is nearly the same as the complete set of flow- and interpretation-preserving rewrite rules for one-way computations in which all measurements are Pauli; and just a small subset of the flow- and interpretation-preserving rewrite rules for arbitrary measurements.

</details>


### [277] [Comparing and learning figures of merit for quantum circuit compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03275)
*Harshdeep Singh,Marvin Richter,Mats Granath,Anton Frisk Kockum*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子编译中不同品质因数（FoM）的优缺点，提出了加权成功试验概率（wPST）作为改进指标，并设计了机器学习模型来快速预测PST和wPST，在数值模拟和量子处理器实验中，预测的FoM相比常用FoM与真实值的相关性提升了50%以上。此外，还开发了两步法预测非编译量子电路的wPST。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有品质因数（如门数、电路深度）易于计算但无法准确反映电路结构和噪声的影响，而精确的FoM（如PST）需要完整电路执行，耗时过长。需要在计算简便性与预测准确性之间找到平衡。

Method: 提出加权PST（wPST）作为改进指标；设计机器学习模型同时考虑量子电路和量子硬件数据来预测PST和wPST；开发两步法：先预测非编译电路所需的额外量子门，再预测wPST并考虑退相干时间。

Result: 机器学习预测的FoM与真实PST或wPST的相关性相比常用FoM提升了50%以上。

Conclusion: 机器学习预测的FoM能有效替代传统FoM，在保证计算效率的同时大幅提升预测准确性，可实际应用于量子编译器中的电路选择。

Abstract: To make quantum algorithms executable on a particular quantum device, they need to be compiled into circuits that respect constraints of the quantum hardware. This compilation usually involves multiple steps, where many hardware-compatible circuits are generated, and the best circuit is selected. To say which circuit is best, the quality of a circuit is generally quantified by a $\textit{figure of merit}$ (FoM). For FoMs, there is a trade-off between ease of calculation and accuracy in predicted execution quality. Commonly used FoMs, e.g., the number of gates, circuit depth, etc., are easy to evaluate, but do not directly capture the effects of circuit structure and noise. On the other end of the spectrum are FoMs that require full circuit execution and take a prohibitively long time to evaluate. One example is the probability of successful trials (PST), i.e., the probability of obtaining the initial state after running the quantum circuit followed by its inverse. Here, we investigate advantages and disadvantages of different FoMs, and formulate the properties of an ideal FoM. Based on our results, we propose wPST, a weighted version of the PST that accounts for individual qubits, not just the whole state. To quickly predict PST and wPST, we design machine learning models that take into account both the quantum circuit and quantum hardware data. In numerical simulations and experiments on quantum processors, we find that our machine learning-predicted FoMs outperform commonly used FoMs, increasing the correlation with the true PST or wPST by over 50%. To make our model useful for quantum compilers, we devise a two-step process to predict the wPST for non-transpiled quantum circuits: first, we predict the additional quantum gates required for the given quantum circuit, and then we predict the wPST, accounting for coherence times in the quantum device.

</details>


### [278] [Complexity of Normalized Persistence Problems for Topological Data Analysis and Local Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03278)
*Dominic Lowe,M. S. Kim,Roberto Bondesan,Ryu Hayakawa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究归一化持续同调（normalized persistence）问题，证明其变体为 $\mathsf{DQC}_1$-hard 且属于 $\mathsf{BQP}$，表明 TDA 在量子计算中可能具有指数级加速。同时揭示了归一化持久性与局域哈密顿量低能子空间谱量估计的紧密联系，并引入具有完美完备性的 $\mathsf{SDQC}_1$ 刻画精确核归一化问题的难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑数据分析（TDA）中的持续同调是关键概念，其归一化版本具有实际可解释性。作者希望研究归一化持续同调的计算复杂性，探索其量子优势的证据，并建立与局域哈密顿量谱问题之间的联系。

Method: 通过证明归一化持续同调变体为 $\mathsf{DQC}_1$-hard 且属于 $\mathsf{BQP}$，给出指数级量子加速的证据；研究低能归一化子迹与谱密度等问题的 $\mathsf{DQC}_1$-hardness；引入 $\mathsf{SDQC}_1$（完美完备性变体）刻画 $O(1)$-局域哈密顿量的归一化持续同调复杂性。

Result: 归一化持续同调变体是 $\mathsf{DQC}_1$-hard 且包含于 $\mathsf{BQP}$；$O(1)$-局域哈密顿量的低能归一化子迹与谱密度是 $\mathsf{DQC}_1$-hard；$O(1)$-局域哈密顿量的归一化持续同调是 $\mathsf{SDQC}_1$-hard。

Conclusion: 归一化持续同调在标准复杂性假设下具有指数级量子加速潜力；首次将 $\mathsf{DQC}_1$-hardness 直接应用于 TDA 实例；建立了 TDA 与局域哈密顿量谱问题之间的深层联系。

Abstract: Topological data analysis (TDA) is a machine learning technique that uses topology to extract patterns from data and has shown the potential to exhibit quantum advantage. A key concept in TDA is persistent homology, which measures the robustness of topological information at different lengthscales. In this paper, we introduce and study the problem of normalized persistence, a practically motivated and easily interpretable version of persistent homology that counts the fraction of holes that persist at different lengthscales. We prove that a variant of normalized persistence is $\mathsf{DQC}_1$-hard and contained in $\mathsf{BQP}$, giving evidence of an exponential quantum speedup for TDA under the standard assumption that $\mathsf{DQC}_1 \not\subseteq \mathsf{BPP}$. These are the first $\mathsf{DQC}_1$-hardness results that are directly applicable to TDA instances. We also find a close connection between normalized persistence and the complexity of estimating spectral quantities in the low-energy subspace of local Hamiltonians. We study a family of such problems, including a low-energy normalized subtrace and spectral density. We show that these are $\mathsf{DQC}_1$-hard for $O(1)$-local Hamiltonians, strengthening previous results that required log-local interactions. We also introduce a variant of $\mathsf{DQC}_1$ with perfect completeness ($\mathsf{SDQC}_1$) to characterize the hardness of problems normalized by an exact kernel. This includes normalized persistence for $O(1)$-local Hamiltonians, which we show is $\mathsf{SDQC}_1$-hard.

</details>


### [279] [Quantum Probability and Quantum Information Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03295)
*Hans Maassen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文是关于量子概率与量子信息的入门课程，基于矩阵代数展开教学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为初学者提供从矩阵代数角度理解量子概率与量子信息的系统性入门材料。

Method: 以矩阵代数（而非泛函分析或算子代数）为数学框架，介绍量子概率与量子信息的基本概念与理论。

Result: 形成了一套基于矩阵代数的量子概率与量子信息入门课程体系。

Conclusion: 该课程可作为学习量子概率与量子信息的入门教材，适合具备基础线性代数知识的读者。

Abstract: This is an introductory course on quantum probability and quantum information, based on matrix algebras.

</details>


### [280] [Dissipative preparation and stabilization of d-mode multinomial cat states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03302)
*S. Zhao,A. Metelmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通用方法，通过工程化耗散过程在玻色子系统中生成多模猫态，实现了从真空态到高保真度的制备与稳定，将寿命延长数个数量级，并适用于任意d模态多项式猫态，可扩展至超导电路实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展工程化耗散制备与稳定量子态的方法至多模系统，解决该领域此前未被探索的挑战，利用多模猫态在量子传感和量子信息处理中的强大资源价值。

Method: 提出一种通用方法设计所需的耗散过程，用于在玻色子系统中生成多模猫态；具体聚焦双模二项式猫态的制备与稳定，并讨论超导电路实现路径；方案可扩展至任意d模多项式猫态，与su(d≥2)代数关联。

Result: 工程化耗散能从真空态高保真度地制备多模猫态，并鲁棒地稳定其对抗退相干；猫态寿命相比自然衰减时间延长数个数量级，从而增强其在量子技术中的应用。

Conclusion: 该可扩展框架为稳定紧凑非经典态提供了可行路径，并在超导电路中具有实现潜力。

Abstract: Engineering dissipation with tailored steady states has become a powerful approach for preparing and stabilizing quantum states. In this framework, engineered dissipative processes continuously steer a system towards desired target states while suppressing unwanted noise. However, extending this idea to multimode systems is challenging and remains largely unexplored, although this class of states is a powerful resource for quantum sensing and quantum information processing applications. Here, we propose a general method to design the required dissipative processes for the generation of multimode cat states in bosonic systems. We show that the engineered dissipation prepares such states from the vacuum with high fidelity and robustly stabilizes them against decoherence. As a result, their lifetime is extended by several orders of magnitude compared to natural decay times, which in turn enhances their applications in quantum techonologies. We specifically focus on the preparation and stabilization of two-mode binomial cat states and discuss a pathway for the implementation in superconducting circuit. However, our scheme can also scale up to arbitrary d-mode multinomial cat states associated to $\mathfrak{su}(d\ge2)$ algebras, and thus, our scalable framework provides a feasible route towards stabilizing compact nonclassical states.

</details>


### [281] [High Success Probability, Fidelity, and Purity Nonlinear Optical Two-Qubit Gates on Chip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03313)
*Minghao Shang,Hua-Ying Liu,Ying Wei,Xiaoyi Liu,Qianhao Ning,Lijian Zhang,Shi-Ning Zhu,Zhenda Xie*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于薄膜铌酸锂平台的光学两比特CNOT门方案，利用χ(2)非线性光子-光子相互作用实现100%效率。通过解耦光子相互作用和量子比特翻转操作，并结合全光谱光子相互作用模型的光谱相位预补偿，成功消除了保真度上限并恢复了输出态纯度，实现了84%成功概率、93%纯度和单位保真度的CNOT门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高成功概率、容错保真度和高纯度输出的光学两比特门是大规模光学量子计算实现量子优势的基础性但尚未解决的挑战。

Method: 基于薄膜铌酸锂平台实现χ(2)非线性光子-光子相互作用（效率达100%）；通过解耦光子相互作用和量子比特翻转操作消除保真度上限；基于全光谱光子相互作用模型进行光谱相位预补偿以恢复输出态纯度。

Result: 实现了CNOT门：84%成功概率、93%纯度、单位保真度。

Conclusion: 该方案可行，为大规模光学量子计算提供了关键元件基础。

Abstract: Optical two-qubit gate with high success probability, fault-tolerant fidelity, and high-purity outputs is a fundamental yet unsolved challenge, essential for large-scale optical quantum computing toward quantum advantage. Here, we propose a feasible scheme for such gate using thin-film lithium niobate platform, enabling \c{hi}(2) nonlinear photon-photon interaction with 100% efficiency. By decoupling photon interaction and qubit flip operations, fidelity ceiling is removed, and output state purity is recovered by spectral-phase pre-compensation based on a full-spectral photon interaction model, yielding a CNOT gate with 84% success probability, 93% purity, and unity fidelity.

</details>


### [282] [An End-to-End Multi-Stage Kill-Chain Attack on Quantum Neural Networks: Demonstration on Trapped-Ion Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03337)
*Cedric Brügmann,Daniel Herr,Daniel Ohl de Mello,Pascal Debus,Maximilian Wendlinger,Kilian Tscharke,Juris Ulmanis,Alexander Erhard,Arthur Schmidt,Fabian Petsch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本论文展示了一种针对离子阱量子计算机上执行的量子神经网络（QNN）模型的端到端多阶段攻击，结合了侧信道侦察、串扰表征、对抗样本生成以及物理串扰攻击，并在附录中报告了超导硬件实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示量子神经网络在实际量子计算硬件上面临的安全威胁，特别是通过物理串扰攻击实现对抗性扰动的可行性，提醒量子即服务（QaaS）提供商注意安全风险。

Method: 采用多阶段攻击链：首先通过侧信道侦察获取模型信息，然后进行串扰表征以了解硬件特性，接着生成对抗样本，最后在离子阱量子计算机上实施物理串扰攻击以实现对抗扰动。

Result: 成功在离子阱量子计算机上实现了完整的端到端攻击链，并在附录中报告了对应的超导硬件实验结果。

Conclusion: 该研究对量子即服务提供商的安全实践提出了警示，并讨论了硬件层面的缓解措施。

Abstract: We demonstrate an end-to-end, multi-stage attack against a quantum neural network (QNN) model that is executed on a trapped-ion quantum computer. Our chain combines side-channel reconnaissance, crosstalk characterization, adversarial example generation, and a physical crosstalk attack that realizes the adversarial perturbation on the device. We cover the full attack chain on ion traps and report the corresponding superconducting-hardware experiments in the appendix. We discuss implications for QaaS providers and hardware mitigations.

</details>


### [283] [Measurements Number Scaling in the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm for MaxCut: A Statistical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03340)
*Inbar Chefer,Uri Shaham,Adi Makmal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对量子近似优化算法（QAOA）在MaxCut问题中的测量（shots）需求进行了统计分析，推导了每轮成本算子评估所需shots数量的充分条件，以确保期望成本估计的相对误差和SGD参数优化的收敛性，并发现了一个反直觉的缩放现象：对于特定图类，实现固定相对性能指标所需的总shot预算随实例规模增大而减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化QAOA在MaxCut问题中的测量资源需求（shots数量），为大规模实例下的高效实现提供理论依据和实用指导。

Method: 通过统计分析推导shots数量的充分条件，分别针对期望成本估计（相对误差δ、置信度1-ε）和基于SGD的参数优化（目标相对次优性水平），并给出SGD迭代次数的显式界；对特定图类分析总shot预算的缩放行为。

Result: 发现反直觉的缩放现象：对于特定图类，总shot预算随实例规模增大而减少；提供了shot预算分配的实用经验法则。

Conclusion: QAOA在大型MaxCut实例中具有高性能、低开销的实现潜力，实例规模增长反而可能降低资源需求，为实际部署提供了理论支持和实践指南。

Abstract: We provide a statistical analysis of the measurement (shot) requirements of the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) for the MaxCut problem. We derive sufficient conditions on the number of shots per cost operator evaluation to: (a) estimate the expected cost to within a relative error $δ$ and a confidence $1-ε$, and (b) ensure SGD-based parameter optimization converges to a target relative suboptimality level with high probability. In addition, we provide an explicit bound on the number of SGD iterations required to reach the target accuracy. Our analysis reveals an unexpected scaling phenomenon: for specific graph classes, which we formally characterize, the total shot budget needed to achieve a fixed relative-performance metric decreases as the instance size grows. This result complements earlier cost function concentration arguments regarding parameter optimization redundancy, thereby highlighting the potential for high-performance, low-overhead QAOA implementations for large-scale MaxCut instances. To assist practitioners, we translate our analytical findings into practical rules of thumb for shot-budget allocation and validate these results with numerical simulations, offering new insights into the interplay between graph size, structural complexity, and resource requirements in QAOA.

</details>


### [284] [Nested-Loop Trajectory-Informed Variational Quantum Solver for Interior-Point OPF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03361)
*Farshad Amani,Amin Kargarian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双层可训练量子内点法框架，通过利用早期求解器轨迹（而非单点预测）来加速变分量子线性求解器与内点法的联合优化，将变分更新次数减少高达95%，同时保持与经典内点法相近的最优潮流目标值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内点法求解最优潮流问题时需反复求解牛顿线性系统，而变分量子线性求解器每次内点法迭代都引入额外的嵌套内层变分优化循环，导致整体量子辅助内点法收敛速度显著变慢。

Method: 提出双层可训练框架：1) 量子求解器层：利用变分量子线性求解器参数轨迹的前缀训练参数模型，投影剩余变分搜索到低成本区域；2) 最优潮流求解器层：利用早期原始-对偶内点法迭代轨迹训练模型，投影后期中心路径状态并恢复至可行点后继续内点法精化。

Result: 变分更新次数减少高达95%，最优潮流目标值保持接近经典内点法参考值，并在2节点系统真实量子硬件上验证了工作流实现。

Conclusion: 利用求解器早期轨迹信息可以有效加速量子-经典混合优化，为变分量子算法在电力系统优化中的实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Optimal power flow (OPF) solved by an interior-point method (IPM) requires repeatedly solving Newton linear systems. When variational quantum linear solvers (VQLS) are used, each IPM iteration involves an additional nested inner variational optimization loop, which can significantly slow the overall quantum-assisted IPM convergence. To address this challenge, this paper proposes a dual-level trainable quantum IPM framework for OPF that leverages early solver-generated trajectories rather than relying on single-point prediction. The key observation is that early IPM iterates provide informative primal-dual, slack, and barrier-variable evolution about the path to optimality, while early VQLS parameter updates provide useful information about the later variational search. At the quantum-solver level, a trainable parameter model uses a short prefix of the VQLS parameter trajectory to project the remaining variational search toward a lower-cost region. At the OPF-solver level, a second trainable model uses early primal-dual IPM iterates to project a later central path state, which is restored to an admissible point before IPM refinement continues. Simulation studies show that the proposed approach reduces the number of variational updates by up to $95\%$ while maintaining OPF objective values close to the classical IPM reference. A 2-bus demonstration on real quantum hardware is also included to validate the implementation of the proposed workflow.

</details>


### [285] [Symmetry-Protected Quantum Synchronization in Squeezed-Bath-Engineered Superradiance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03409)
*Juan David Álvarez-Cuartas,Joakim Bergli,John H. Reina*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 压缩耗散浴可将非传统Dicke模型的耦合相位转化为控制参数，抑制静态超辐射阈值，仅保留Hopf不稳定性，使两个自旋系综通过共享腔模完全同步。压缩浴保留Z2奇偶对称性，通过宇称偶的信息论证据（光子数抑制30%、Husimi-Q叶数变化、自旋互信息抑制64%、 discord与互信息比0.50±0.05）认证同步态，为对称保护驱动耗散系统中的量子同步提供无需纠缠的通用认证路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对称性保护的驱动耗散系统中，传统破缺对称性诊断方法失效，需要发展基于宇称偶观测量的信息论证据来认证量子同步态。

Method: 通过压缩耗散浴将耦合相位变为控制参数，利用全量子主方程模拟，采用光子数统计、Husimi-Q分布、自旋互信息和量子discord等宇称偶观测量作为同步认证工具。

Result: 实现了30%的光子数抑制、Husimi-Q叶数变化、64%的自旋互信息抑制以及D/I=0.50±0.05的鲁棒比值。

Conclusion: 宇称偶证据可作为对称保护驱动耗散系统中量子同步认证的通用、无需纠缠的有效途径。

Abstract: A squeezed dissipative bath converts the coupling phase of a bipartite unconventional Dicke model into a control parameter that suppresses both static superradiant thresholds, opening a window where only a Hopf instability survives and the two spin ensembles synchronize completely via the shared cavity mode. The squeezed bath preserves a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ parity symmetry, so conventional broken-symmetry diagnostics vanish identically. We certify the synchronized state instead through parity-even, information-theoretic witnesses: a 30\% photon-number suppression, a Husimi-$Q$ lobe-count change, a 64\% suppression of spin--spin mutual information, and a robust discord-to-mutual-information ratio $D/I = 0.50 \pm 0.05$, confirmed by full quantum master-equation simulations. These results establish parity-even witnesses as a general, entanglement-free route to certifying quantum synchronization in symmetry-protected driven-dissipative systems.

</details>


### [286] [Specifying the operational meaning of quantum reference frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03417)
*Augustin Vanrietvelde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文详细阐释并捍卫了“位置叠加实验室/观察者”这一量子参考框架概念，指出其无需引入量子物理的奇异主张，在诠释难度上不高于普通量子测量。研究表明，位置叠加观察者与Wigner朋友场景中的结果叠加观察者有本质区别，前者可以在不消相干的前提下将结果广播给局域观察者，而后者结果必须保持系统内部性。此外，本文论证了从经典参考框架模拟量子参考框架的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子参考框架的概念（如“叠加实验室的测量”、“量子粒子的视角”、“叠加观察者的观点”）在已有文献中被提出但表述模糊、存在歧义，导致误解和批评。作者旨在提供清晰的技术规范与理论辩护，澄清这一概念的真实含义及其与相关概念（如Wigner朋友悖论）的区别。

Method: 采用理论分析与概念澄清的方法，对位置叠加观察者的操作定义进行详细规范；通过对比分析（与Wigner朋友场景中的结果叠加观察者对比）揭示关键差异；通过思想实验论证结果广播的可能性以及从经典参考框架角色扮演量子参考框架的可行性。

Result: 1. 位置叠加观察者定义的量子参考框架无需引入超越标准量子力学的奇异主张；2. 位置叠加观察者与结果叠加观察者有本质区别，前者问题性更少；3. 位置叠加观察者获得的测量结果可以在不破坏叠加态的情况下广播给局域观察者；4. 从经典参考框架角色扮演量子参考框架是可能的。

Conclusion: 位置叠加观察者定义的量子参考框架具有操作上的一致性和理论上的自洽性，其诠释负担不高于普通量子测量，且与Wigner朋友场景中的结果叠加观察者存在关键差异，后者面临更严重的概念困难。

Abstract: In their strongest usage, quantum reference frames have been described as referring to "the measurements performed by a superposed lab", "the perspective of a quantum particle", "the point of view of a superposed observer", etc. While exciting, these operational proposals have remained brief and ambiguous, leading to misinterpretations and criticism. Here, we provide a detailed specification and defense of the notion of a position-superposed lab or observer. We argue that this requires no exotic claims about quantum physics and raises no greater interpretive difficulty than ordinary quantum measurements. We then derive several consequences of taking this operational meaning seriously. We stress that the position-superposed observers that define quantum references frames are different from, and considerably less problematic than, the outcome-superposed observers considered in Wigners' friend scenarios. In particular, we show that outcomes obtained by a position-superposed observer may (without decohering the superposition) be broadcast to a well-localised one, in contrast with Wigner's friend scenarios, which require the outcomes to remain internal to the system at hand. Finally, we defend the possibility to roleplay a quantum reference frame from a classical reference frame.

</details>


### [287] [Design of an Electrically Tunable Microtoroid for Frequency Selection of Polarization-Entangled Photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03437)
*Yichi Zhang,Enqi Ke,Judith Su*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种电可调谐的二氧化硅微环设计方案，该方案能在不破坏偏振纠缠的情况下选择频率通道，并实现了九个通道的高保真度偏振-频率超纠缠态生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子信息编码到离散光学频率（频率箱）中，利用不同颜色的光作为额外信息通道，使每个光子携带比单纯偏振更多的信息，从而提升量子通信容量。

Method: 设计了一种电可调谐的二氧化硅微环谐振器，通过添加小型铌酸锂调谐元件，施加单电压使微环对水平偏振光和垂直偏振光的响应几乎一致，解决了谐振器双折射导致偏振信息泄露的问题。

Result: 剩余失配仅0.286个光学线宽；光子纠缠保持良好：并发度C=0.969，贝尔态保真度F=0.981，贝尔参数S_max=2.785；可生成九通道超纠缠态，有效维度K=8.97。

Conclusion: 该计算设计为偏振纠缠光子源与未来高容量量子光子学系统之间提供了一种紧凑、电可调谐的桥梁。

Abstract: Encoding quantum information into discrete optical frequencies, or "frequency bins," uses different colors of light as additional information channels, allowing each photon to carry more information than polarization alone. We present a computational design for an electrically tunable silica microtoroid that selects desired frequency channels after a polarization-entangled photon pair has been generated without disturbing the photons' polarization entanglement. In the proposed architecture, the 750 nm signal photon passes through the microtoroid, while its entangled 880 nm partner bypasses the resonator and serves as a reference for the selected frequency channel. The principal challenge is resonator birefringence: because horizontally and vertically polarized light resonate at slightly different frequencies, the selected frequency can reveal the photon's polarization state and weaken the quantum correlation between the photon pair. We solve this problem by adding a small lithium-niobate tuning element controlled with a single applied voltage. The voltage shifts the resonator so that it responds almost identically to horizontally and vertically polarized light, reducing the remaining mismatch to only 0.286 optical linewidths across nine frequency channels. The photons remain strongly entangled after passing through the device, with a concurrence of C = 0.969, a Bell-state fidelity of F = 0.981, and a Bell parameter of S_max = 2.785. If the relative timing between the frequency channels is also controlled, the same device can generate a nine-channel polarization-frequency hyperentangled state with an effective dimension of K = 8.97. This computational design provides a compact, electrically tunable bridge between polarization-entangled photon sources and future high-capacity quantum photonic systems.

</details>


### [288] [Dynamical zero modes, boundary dependence, and numerical instability in dynamical quantum phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03438)
*Siyan Lin,Xu Feng,Xiuhua Tian,Shu Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现动力学的量子相变中，边界条件对Loschmidt率函数的影响由动力学零模（DZMs）编码。DZMs是Loschmidt矩阵中奇异值在热力学极限下趋于零的奇异向量。以SSH模型为例，开边界与周期性边界条件下LRF的差异时间区间与DZMs的出现一致。移除DZMs可恢复周期性边界结果。DZMs的有限尺寸奇异值随系统尺寸指数衰减，导致有限精度数值不稳定，但可通过小尺寸线性外推估计热力学LRF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示动力学量子相变中边界条件依赖性的起源，并解释相关的数值不稳定性问题。

Method: 定义Loschmidt矩阵的动力学零模（DZMs），以SSH和扩展SSH模型为例，比较开边界与周期性边界条件下的LRF，通过奇异值分解分析DZMs的贡献，并利用线性外推处理数值不稳定性。

Result: 边界依赖的LRF差异时间区间与DZMs的出现一致；移除DZMs可恢复周期性边界结果；DZMs的奇异值随系统尺寸指数衰减，导致有限精度计算中数值不稳定；通过小尺寸线性外推可估计热力学LRF。

Conclusion: DZMs既是边界依赖LRF的诊断工具，也是相关数值不稳定性的根源。
