Result: 成功生成了 stabilizer（GHZ）和非 stabilizer（|CCZ⟩）逻辑量子态，并验证了其真正多体纠缠特性；进一步通过基于 CCZ 的资源 gadget 演示了单逻辑量子比特的任意旋转，展示了完整通用门集的访问能力。

Conclusion: 该实验演示了通过晶格手术跨不同纠错码实现通用逻辑门集的核心构建块，为容错量子计算架构中生成长逻辑量子态提供了关键实验依据。

Abstract: Universal quantum computers are expected to generate arbitrary complex quantum states of logical qubits encoded in many physical qubits. This capability hinges on a fault-tolerantly implemented universal gate set, which no single quantum error-correction code admits transversally but which becomes accessible by joining complementary codes via lattice surgery. Here we report on the experimental generation and certification of logical genuine multipartite entanglement in a trapped-ion quantum processor using a transversally implemented universal logical gate set. The gate set is accessed via lattice surgery across two different codes and comprises a Hadamard gate on a four-qubit surface code and a doubly controlled Pauli-$Z$ ($\overline{\mathrm{CCZ}}$) gate on an eight-qubit 3D colour code. To showcase this lattice-surgery toolbox, we generate both stabiliser (Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger) and non-stabiliser ($|\overline{\mathrm{CCZ}}\rangle$) states of three logical qubits and verify their genuine multipartite entanglement--a form of correlation beyond statistical mixtures of bipartite entanglement across any bipartition. We further use these cross-code primitives to demonstrate arbitrary rotations of single logical qubits via a $\overline{\mathrm{CCZ}}$-based resource gadget accessing the full universal gate set through lattice surgery. Together, these demonstrations showcase the core building blocks of an architecture for fault-tolerant quantum computation and its ability to generate complex logical quantum states.

</details>


### [327] [Weak ergodicity breaking without nonthermal eigenstates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04279)
*Boning Huang,Yongguan Ke,Li Zhang,Lin Ling,Chaohong Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在满足本征态热化假说（ETH）的量子系统中实现弱遍历性破缺的新机制：通过空间周期调制产生近线性能带，多粒子Wannier态（MWSs）在该能带中展现出等间距能级结构，从而引发长寿命的集体复归现象，这与传统基于非热本征态（如多体局域化、量子疤痕）的遍历性破缺机制不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在不存在非热本征态的孤立相互作用量子系统中，实现遍历性破缺（弱遍历性破缺）的新机制，突破传统上依赖非热本征态（如多体局域化、量子疤痕）的范式。

Method: 在空间调制的Bose-Hubbard晶格中，通过周期调制将简单晶格的能带折叠分离成多个子带，其中近线性子带继承了原始能带的线性片段；构造多粒子Wannier态（MWSs）作为单个能带内多粒子Bloch态的叠加，产生能带分辨的Wannier扇区碎裂。

Result: 尽管多粒子Bloch态满足ETH，但近线性能带中的MWSs由于涌现的等间距能级结构，仍能展现出长寿命的集体复归现象。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种实现弱遍历性破缺的新路径：长寿命复归源于满足ETH的本征态之间的谱相位相干性，而非类似于量子疤痕的非热本征态。

Abstract: The typical mechanisms of ergodicity breaking in isolated interacting quantum systems, such as many-body localization and quantum many-body scars, originate from the nonthermal nature of the underlying eigenstates. Here, in the absence of nonthermal eigenstates, we identify a mechanism for collective revivals of multiparticle Wannier states (MWSs) associated with nearly linear bands in a spatially modulated Bose-Hubbard lattice. The MWSs, as superpositions of multiparticle Bloch states within individual energy bands, give rise to band-resolved Wannier-sector fragmentation. The key idea is that spatially periodic modulation folds and separates energy bands of a simple lattice into several sub-bands, among which nearly linear sub-bands inherit the linear segments of the original bands. Although multiparticle Bloch states satisfy the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH), the MWSs in the nearly linear band still exhibit long-lived collective revivals, due to emergent equally spaced energy levels. Our work provides a route to weak ergodicity breaking in which long-lived revivals arise from spectral phase coherence among ETH-satisfying eigenstates rather than from scar-like nonthermal eigenstates.

</details>


### [328] [Chromatic Completeness and the Independence of Geometric Obstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04289)
*Karl Svozil*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在正交超图中严格区分了色完备性（全局一致非退化谱的分配）与几何可坐标化性（忠实正交表示的存在性），证明了强色数大于Hilbert空间维数仅阻碍前者，而不阻碍后者。通过两个三维示例（Yu-Oh构型的25射线完备版与Greechie的G₃₂超图）展示了这一分离，两者强色数均为4，但前者有忠实正交表示，后者则无，其障碍源于射影几何而非色数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清正交超图理论中色完备性与几何可坐标化性之间长期未明确区分的逻辑关系，纠正将强色数作为忠实正交表示存在性判据的潜在误解。

Method: 通过构造两个强色数相同（均为4）的三维超图示例进行对比分析：对Yu-Oh构型补充至25射线并显式构造忠实正交表示；对Greechie的G₃₂超图使用初等代数证明其不存在忠实正交表示，揭示射影几何层面的障碍。

Result: Yu-Oh构型的25射线完备版虽强色数为4，但在R³中存在显式忠实正交表示；G₃₂超图强色数同为4且具有分离单值态集，但通过代数证明其在C³中无忠实正交表示。

Conclusion: 正交超图中的忠实正交表示障碍并非色数性（强色数）而是射影几何性：入射关系可能强制两个不同原子坍缩到同一条射线。这建立了色完备性与几何可坐标化性之间的严格逻辑分离。

Abstract: We establish a strict logical separation between two distinct phenomena in orthogonality hypergraphs: chromatic completeness, the possibility of assigning a single globally consistent nondegenerate spectrum to all contexts, and geometric coordinatizability, the existence of a faithful orthogonal representation by rays. A strong chromatic number larger than the Hilbert-space dimension obstructs only the former. It does not, by itself, obstruct the existence of a faithful orthogonal representation. We make this separation explicit by comparing two three-dimensional examples with the same strong chromatic number. A completed 25-ray version of the Yu-Oh configuration has strong chromatic number four and nevertheless possesses an explicit faithful orthogonal representation in R^3. Conversely, Greechie's G_{32} hypergraph also has strong chromatic number four, and has a separating and unital set of two-valued states, but we give an elementary algebraic proof that it admits no faithful orthogonal representation in C^3. The obstruction in G_{32} is therefore not chromatic but projective-geometric: the incidence relations force two distinct atoms to collapse onto the same ray.

</details>


### [329] [Optimal estimation of quantum boundary effect in cosmic string space-time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04295)
*Yao Jin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了宇宙弦和反射边界对两能级原子量子动力学的影响，发现通过单探针原子进行N次最优测量序列，可在固定总探测时间内达到精度极限。原子初始激发态比等权重叠加态的精度高4倍，精度随原子-边界/弦距离呈阻尼振荡。宇宙弦时空中最优偏振方向取决于距离和缺陷角，小缺陷角大距离下沿弦方向偏振变为次优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索宇宙弦对真空涨落的修正如何影响原子动力学，并研究能否通过量子测量估计这些量子边界效应。

Method: 采用两能级极化原子模型，在宇宙弦时空和反射边界条件下分析真空涨落修正；通过序列测量（N次）优化初始态和测量方案，推导精度极限。

Result: 固定总探测时间下，最优策略是每次探针取最佳初始态并执行最优测量；激发态精度是等权重叠加态的4倍；精度随距离呈阻尼振荡；宇宙弦时空的最优偏振由距离和缺陷角共同决定，小缺陷角大距离下沿弦方向偏振不再最优。

Conclusion: 宇宙弦与反射边界类似地影响原子动力学，但最优偏振选择更复杂；通过序列测量可有效估计这些量子边界效应，为探测宇宙弦提供了量子计量学方案。

Abstract: The presence of a cosmic string modifies vacuum fluctuations, making the evolution of a two-level polarizable atom position dependent. Such modifications produce effects on the atomic dynamics analogous to those induced by a reflecting boundary. We show that these quantum boundary effects can be estimated by performing a sequence of $N$ measurements on a single probe atom. For a fixed total probe time, the precision limit is attained by preparing each probe in its optimal initial state, performing the corresponding optimal measurement, and shortening the probe time of each probe. The optimal measurement is uniquely determined by the probe's initial state, and the precision limit obtained with the atom initially in the excited state is four times higher than that for an equal-weight superposition state. The estimation precision displays damped oscillatory behavior as the atom-boundary or atom-string separation increases. While polarization parallel to the reflecting boundary is always optimal in the boundary case, the optimal polarization in cosmic-string space-time depends on both the atom-string separation and the deficit angle. For small deficit angles and sufficiently large separations, polarization along the cosmic-string direction becomes inferior to the other polarization directions.

</details>


### [330] [Incompatibility assisted Zeno-like confinement enables unbounded sharing of nonlocality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04298)
*Prerna Rao,Som Kanjilal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了贝尔非局域性的顺序共享机制，证明要实现无限次共享，每个副本必须同时满足测量不相容性和后处理态非局域性两个条件，并分析了非局域初始态和测量噪声的约束，发现了类似量子芝诺效应的非局域区域约束现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索贝尔非局域性能否被无限多个连续副本顺序共享，以及实现这种共享所需满足的物理条件。

Method: 通过分析两种测量场景（非精准测量对应反对易泡利可观测量、概率投影测量对应反对易泡利可观测量），研究不相容性要求对初始非局域态选择和测量噪声的约束，并考察后处理态在无限副本极限下的行为。

Result: 证明了不相容性要求对初始非局域态和测量噪声施加了非平凡约束；在渐近极限下，所有副本的非局域后处理态几乎相同，导致类似量子芝诺效应的非局域区域约束。

Conclusion: 贝尔非局域性可以被无限多个副本顺序共享，但需要满足严格的测量不相容性和后处理态非局域性条件，且存在量子芝诺效应将态约束在非局域区域内。

Abstract: To enable sequential sharing of Bell-nonlocality by an unbounded number of copies, each copy must satisfy two requirements. First, the measurements must be incompatible to extract nonlocality. Second, the post-processsed state, obtained as an equal mixture of the two post-measurement states, must remain nonlocal. We show that the incompatibility requirements impose nontrivial constraints on the choice of the initial nonlocal state and the amount of measurement noise required to ensure that the post-processed state remains nonlocal for an unbounded number of copies. We establish this result for two measurement scenarios, namely, when each copy performs unsharp measurements corresponding to a pair of anti-commuting Pauli observables, and when each copy performs probabilistic projective measurements (PPMs) of a pair of anti-commuting Pauli observables. Furthermore, we show that, in the asymptotic limit, the nonlocal post-processed states of all the copies are almost identical, leading to a quantum Zeno-like confinement within the nonlocal region.

</details>


### [331] [Fundamental limits on state preparation for an open qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04320)
*D. A. Abraamian,L. V. Lokutsievskiy,A. N. Pechen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解析确定了相干驱动下耗散两能级开放量子系统中态制备的终极极限，给出了布洛赫球中可达集的精确刻画，证明不可达区域具有非平凡几何结构（绕x轴旋转的解析曲面，含两个锥形奇点），并给出了沿边界运动的闭环控制协议及逼近任意边界态的显式协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定开放量子系统中受相干控制驱动的态制备的终极极限，建立可控性和态制备保真度的基本界限。

Method: 基于GKSL主方程的耗散量子比特模型，通过解析方法精确刻画布洛赫球中的可达集，推导边界几何特性，设计闭环控制协议和显式逼近协议。

Result: 耗散排除了布洛赫球中一个具有非平凡几何形状的区域（绕x轴旋转的解析曲面，含两个锥形奇点），该区域即使在任意强相干驱动下也无法逼近；给出了沿该边界运动的闭环协议以及逼近任意边界态的显式协议。

Conclusion: 给出了标准耗散环境下可达量子比特态的完整几何构造性描述，建立了开放两能级量子系统可控性和态制备保真度的基本界限。

Abstract: We analytically determine the ultimate limits of state preparation in two-level open quantum systems driven by coherent control. For a dissipative qubit governed by a GKSL master equation, we give an exact characterization of the reachable set in the Bloch ball. Dissipation excludes a region of states in the Bloch ball which cannot be approached even under arbitrarily strong coherent driving, and we prove that this region has a nontrivial geometry whose boundary is a surface of revolution around the $x$-axis which is analytic except for two conical singularities. We derive a closed-form control protocol for moving on this boundary, and construct an explicit protocol that steers the system arbitrarily close to any prescribed boundary state. These results provide a complete geometric constructive description of reachable qubit states in the standard dissipative environment, establishing fundamental bounds on controllability and state-preparation fidelity for open two-level quantum systems.

</details>


### [332] [Supersymmetry and Entanglement in the Generalized Dirac Oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04348)
*H. P. Laba,V. M. Tkachuk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用广义Dirac振荡器的超对称性质，以纯代数方式确定了自旋与连续变量之间的纠缠熵。纠缠具有相对论起源，在非相对论极限下消失，并在无限能量极限下达到最大值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究广义Dirac振荡器中自旋与连续变量之间的纠缠熵，探索其超对称性质与相对论起源。

Method: 利用广义Dirac振荡器的超对称性质，采用纯代数方法确定纠缠熵。

Result: 纠缠具有相对论起源，在非相对论极限下消失；纠缠熵在无限能量极限下达到最大值。

Conclusion: 广义Dirac振荡器的超对称性可用于纯代数方式求解自旋-连续变量纠缠熵，该纠缠是相对论效应，非相对论极限下消失，高能极限下达到最大。

Abstract: The supersymmetric properties of the generalized Dirac oscillator allow us to determine the entanglement entropy between the spin and the continuous variable in a purely algebraic manner. The entanglement has a relativistic origin and disappears in the nonrelativistic limit. The entanglement entropy attains its maximal value in the limit of infinite energy.

</details>


### [333] [Purcell effect and quantum Zeno effect suppressed self-discharging of quantum battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04363)
*Da-Wei Liu,Guoqing Tian,Zi-Hao Li,Shi-Qi Gan,Ying Wu,Liu-Gang Si*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用珀塞尔效应与量子芝诺效应的协同作用，将充电器引入的损耗转化为保护机制，从而抑制量子电池的自放电效应达四个数量级，提升能量存储与转换效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电池在开放系统中面临自放电这一核心障碍，尤其是充电器本身构成不可避免的损耗通道，严重制约了有用功的存储。

Method: 结合珀塞尔效应与量子芝诺效应，将充电器诱导的损耗转化为保护机制；利用虚光子过程和珀塞尔效应诱导从强耦合到量子芝诺区的转变。

Result: 充电器对量子电池的耗散被抑制了四个数量级；珀塞尔效应诱导的量子芝诺效应避免了量子电池与充电器之间的能量回流；存储能量可充至近乎满态，且几乎全部转化为可提取功。

Conclusion: 该方法大幅提升了能量转换效率，为克服开放量子电池自放电问题提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Quantum batteries (QB), as an energy storage and transfer device, not only show obvious advantages compared to classical electrochemical batteries, but also have important applications in quantum information. Self-discharging is a central obstacle to storing useful work in open QB, especially when the charger itself provides an unavoidable loss channel. Here we show that such charger-induced loss can be converted into a protection mechanism by combining Purcell effect with quantum Zeno effect. We reveal that the virtual photon process and the Purcell effect can induce the strong coupling regime to the quantum Zeno regime, in which the stronger the dissipation of the charger, the weaker the self-discharging effect of the QB. As a result, the dissipation caused by the charger to the QB can be suppressed four orders of magnitude in our scheme. Meanwhile, the quantum Zeno effect induced by the Purcell effect can also avoid the energy backflow between the QB and the charger. Owing to the significantly suppressed dissipation, the stored energy of QB can be charged to a nearly full state and the stored energy is almost converted into extractable work, which greatly improves the energy conversion efficiency.

</details>


### [334] [Topological properties and Majorana Multiplicity in Zigzag Kitaev Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04399)
*Rajiv Kumar,Panch Ram,Levan Chotorlishvili,Sunil Kumar Mishra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了对角耦合的两条一维Kitaev链构成的锯齿Kitaev链在零和有限超导配对相位差下的谱与拓扑性质。通过Bogoliubov-de Gennes公式分析能谱、马约拉纳零模分布、准粒子色散和绕数。零相位差时出现支持两个、四个MZMs的拓扑相及平庸区；相位差φ=π时MZMs简并部分解除，拓扑相发生改变。绕数ν=1和2对应两种拓扑相，ν=0对应平庸区。结果表明锯齿Kitaev链可作为MZM量子计算的最小平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究锯齿Kitaev链中超导配对相位差对拓扑相和马约拉纳零模的影响，寻找MZM量子计算的最小可行平台。

Method: 采用Bogoliubov-de Gennes公式分析能谱、马约拉纳零模分布、准粒子色散和绕数等拓扑不变量，通过间隙闭合点确定拓扑相边界。

Result: 零相位差时存在支持两个和四个MZMs的拓扑相及平庸区；φ=π时MZMs简并部分解除；绕数ν=1和2对应不同拓扑相，ν=0对应平庸区，获得了完整的相图。

Conclusion: 锯齿Kitaev链可作为工程化MZM量子计算的最小平台，具有拓扑相和基于马约拉纳的量子比特物理研究的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We investigate the spectral and topological properties of a zigzag Kitaev chain constructed from two diagonally coupled one dimensional Kitaev chains with a zero and finite superconducting pairing phase difference. Using a Bogoliubov-de Gennes formulation, we analyze the energy spectrum, distribution of Majorana zero modes (MZMs), the quasi-particle dispersion, and the winding number, respectively. For a zero phase difference, the resulting energy spectrum shows topological phases with two, four MZMs, and trivial regions. The phases of gap closure determine the topological phase boundaries. In particular, for the phase difference between $φ=π$, the degeneracy of MZMs is partially lifted, leading to modified topological phases compared to the case $φ=0$. The topological and trivial phase boundaries are further confirmed by evaluating the quasi-particle dispersion and the topological invariant, namely the winding number. We show that the zigzag Kitaev chain contributes independently to the total winding number $ν= 1$ and $2$, giving rise to distinct topological phases that support two and four MZMs. The $ν= 0$ gives rise to a trivial region. The energy spectrum of systems corroborates the analytical phase boundaries and reveals characteristics associated with hybridization, enabling us to obtain the complete phase diagram of the zigzag model. Our results establish the zigzag Kitaev chain as a minimal platform for engineering MZM quantum computations, with potential applications in the study of topological phases and Majorana based qubit physics.

</details>


### [335] [Controlling many-body quantum chaos in a dissipative optical cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04455)
*Filippo Ferrari,Francesca Orsi,Ekaterina Fedotova,Óscar Rios Alves,Michał Zdziennicki,Jean-Philippe Brantut,Vincenzo Savona*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了腔量子电动力学（QED）中超冷费米子平台在耗散环境下实现多体量子混沌的可行性，分析了腔光子损失和原子自发辐射两种耗散源的不同结构及其对量子混沌特征的影响，并基于实验参数给出了观测多体量子混沌的定量约束条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔QED与超冷费米子结合为通过无序、光子介导的长程相互作用实现多体量子混沌提供了有前景的平台，但此类系统天然存在耗散（腔光子损失和原子自发辐射），需系统研究耗散如何影响量子混沌的观测与控制。

Method: 研究驱动-耗散动力学，分析两种耗散源的结构差异：腔损耗简化为单个退相干通道，而自发辐射在实验相关区域产生非局域退相干通道。通过对比线性可观测量与纠缠熵等非线性可观测量对不同耗散机制的响应，结合实验可行参数推导定量约束。

Result: 腔损耗导致的退相干能保留密度矩阵线性可观测量中可积与混沌哈密顿动力学差异的签名，自发辐射则抑制这些签名；纠缠熵等探测多体态结构的可观测量受两种耗散机制强烈影响。

Conclusion: 基于实验现实参数，给出了在腔QED平台观测和控制多体量子混沌的定量约束条件，揭示了不同耗散源对混沌特征的选择性影响。

Abstract: Cavity quantum electrodynamics (QED) with ultracold fermions provides a promising platform for realizing many-body quantum chaos through disordered, photon-mediated long-range interactions. Such setups are inherently open and are therefore subject to dissipation arising from cavity photon loss and atomic spontaneous emission. In this article, we study the driven-dissipative dynamics of a typical cavity QED setting including controllable disorder and long-range interactions. We find that the two dissipation sources have qualitatively different structures. Cavity loss reduces to a single dephasing channel, whereas spontaneous emission in the experimentally relevant regime generates a collection of nonlocal dephasing channels. Cavity-induced dephasing preserves signatures distinguishing integrable from chaotic Hamiltonian dynamics in observables that depend linearly on the density matrix, while spontaneous emission suppresses these signatures. By contrast, quantities that probe the structure of the many-body state, such as the entanglement entropy, are strongly affected by both dissipation mechanisms. Assuming experimentally realistic parameters, we derive quantitative constraints for the observation and control of many-body quantum chaos in cavity-QED platforms.

</details>


### [336] [Noise-Aware Synthesis of Quantum LDPC Encoder Circuits via Two-Sided Hamming Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04462)
*Aditya Sodhani,Keshab K. Parhi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对量子LDPC编码器电路的再综合优化方法，通过双面汉明下降和噪声感知优化流水线，在多个CSS LDPC编码器家族上将门数量平均减少53.8%，双量子比特深度减少高达71%，并提升了噪声下的态制备保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子LDPC码是实现容错量子计算的重要途径，但其编码态的制备效率是关键瓶颈。标准编码器构造方法生成的电路中存在大量冗余，需要优化以适配目标硬件并抑制噪声。

Method: 将LDPC编码器制备问题形式化为电路再综合问题，针对编码器CNOT块实现的线性可逆矩阵，寻求更低成本的等价电路。提出双面汉明下降优化方法和噪声感知优化流水线。

Result: 在Bivariate Bicycle、超图积和纠缠辅助编码等多个CSS LDPC编码器家族上，门数量平均减少53.8%，Bivariate Bicycle家族减少高达68%；路由后双量子比特深度减少最多71%；结合存活区间调度进一步将制备失败率降低13.7%。

Conclusion: 编码器矩阵再综合结合硬件校准选择和调度，是制备量子LDPC码态的有效编译器级工具。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes are a promising route to fault-tolerant quantum computation, but their use requires efficient preparation of encoded states. Standard encoder constructions generate circuits through fixed algebraic procedures, yet the resulting circuit can contain substantial redundancy. We formulate LDPC encoder preparation as a circuit-resynthesis problem: given the linear-reversible matrix implemented by the encoder's CNOT block, we seek a lower-cost equivalent circuit that can be routed efficiently on the target hardware and which mitigates noise. We propose a novel optimization approach referred as two-sided Hamming descent and a noise-aware optimization pipeline for this task.
  Across several families of Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) LDPC encoders, including Bivariate Bicycle, hypergraph-product, and entanglement-assisted codes, the proposed pipeline produces substantially smaller and shallower encoder circuits than the standard constructions and the synthesis baselines considered, cutting gate counts by 53.8% in aggregate across the benchmark and by up to 68% on the Bivariate Bicycle family. The gains remain visible after routing, where the two-qubit depth is reduced by up to 71% and translate into higher-fidelity state preparation under circuit-level noise. On the Bivariate Bicycle family, live-range scheduling further reduces routed preparation failure by up to 13.7% without adding two-qubit gates to the selected circuit. These results indicate that encoder-matrix resynthesis, combined with hardware-calibrated selection and scheduling, is an effective compiler-level tool for preparing quantum LDPC code states.

</details>


### [337] [Trace-to-Hilbert-Schmidt Speed Ratio in Quantum Dynamics: Universal Bounds and Effective Rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04488)
*Hossein Rangani Jahromi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了可微量子态迹速度与希尔伯特-施密特速度之比R(φ)=‖∂_φρ(φ)‖₁/‖∂_φρ(φ)‖₂，给出了其严格界：√2 ≤ R ≤ √r（r为切算子秩），并建立了有效秩与量子Fisher信息之间的不等式关系，推导了有效秩控制的量子速度极限层次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态演化的两种速度度量——迹速度（L1范数）和希尔伯特-施密特速度（L2范数）之间的比率缺乏系统研究，特别是考虑到切算子具有厄米性和无迹性的特殊约束，需要建立严格的界并揭示其物理意义。

Method: 利用厄米无迹算子的奇异值结构分析，证明L1/L2范数比的严格界；将R²解释为奇异值分布的逆参与比；将有效秩分解为经典（本征值）和量子（本征矢）贡献；通过不等式建立有效秩与量子Fisher信息的联系。

Result: 1) 对任意秩r的切算子，证明严格界√2 ≤ R ≤ √r；2) 下界在秩2时达到，上界在所有非零奇异值相等时达到（需偶数秩）；3) 纯态族非驻点处R=√2；4) 奇维数d时全局最大值R≤√(d-1/d)；5) 建立有效秩分解r_eff ≤ r_C + r_Q；6) 推导有效秩与QFI的不等式及量子速度极限层次。

Conclusion: R²作为奇异值分布逆参与比，是局部量子动力学的有效秩诊断工具；有效秩控制着以希尔伯特-施密特速度表达的量子速度极限的紧致性，为量子计量学和量子动力学提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We study the ratio between the trace speed and the Hilbert-Schmidt speed for differentiable finite-dimensional quantum states, $\mathcal R(φ)=\|\partial_φρ(φ)\|_1/\|\partial_φρ(φ)\|_2$. Because $\partial_φρ(φ)$ is always Hermitian and traceless, this ratio is constrained more strongly than for a generic operator. For any nonzero tangent operator $X=\partial_φρ$ of rank $r$, we prove the sharp bounds $\sqrt{2}\le \|X\|_1/\|X\|_2\le \sqrt r$. The lower bound is attained exactly for rank-two tangents, while the upper bound is attained exactly when all nonzero singular values are equal, which in the traceless Hermitian setting requires even rank. At every nonstationary point of a pure-state family, the tangent has rank two, implying $\mathcal R=\sqrt2$. For odd Hilbert-space dimension $d$, we further prove the sharp global maximum $\mathcal R\le \sqrt{d-1/d}$, with equality characterized by full-rank spectra whose positive and negative eigenvalues are separately degenerate and have multiplicities differing by one. We identify $\mathcal R^2$ as the inverse participation ratio of the singular-value distribution of the tangent operator, giving $\mathcal R$ a natural interpretation as an effective-rank diagnostic for local quantum dynamics. Furthermore, we decompose the effective rank into classical (eigenvalue) and quantum (eigenvector) contributions and prove the bound $r_{\mathrm{eff}}\le r_C + r_Q$, with equality guaranteed when either component vanishes. We establish a direct inequality linking the effective rank to the quantum Fisher information (QFI), which forces a large number of active singular modes when the QFI is small relative to the squared trace speed. Finally, we derive a hierarchy of quantum speed limits in which the effective rank controls the tightness of bounds expressed through the Hilbert-Schmidt speed.

</details>


### [338] [Schrodinger Oscillator and its Thermal Properties in a Dynamical Noncommutative Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04519)
*Ilyas Haouam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在动力学非对易空间中研究了二维薛定谔振子，利用微扰理论推导了能量本征值和本征向量，分析了动力学与非动力学非对易设置的影响，得到了本征系统的一阶修正，并探讨了系统的热力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动力学非对易空间中的二维薛定谔振子，探索非对易参数τ对能量本征值的具体影响，填补动力学非对易框架下量子振子系统研究的空白。

Method: 采用微扰理论推导能量本征值和本征向量；利用二维Bopp-shift将非对易问题映射到对易对应问题；通过配分函数分析系统热力学性质；进行数值计算分析系统行为。

Result: 得到了能量本征系统的一阶修正，发现能量偏移显式依赖于动力学非对易参数τ；在动力学非对易框架下，对易关系和形变参数均具有位置依赖性。

Conclusion: 动力学非对易空间中的二维薛定谔振子与标准非对易情况存在本质区别，其热力学性质受τ参数调控，Bopp-shift方法可有效处理该问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the two-dimensional Schrodinger oscillator within a dynamical noncommutative (DNC) space. By leveraging perturbation theory, we derive the energy eigenvalues and eigenvectors and systematically analyze the effects of both dynamical and non-dynamical noncommutative settings. First-order corrections to the eigensystem are obtained, revealing that the energy shift explicitly depends on the DNC parameter tau. Furthermore, we explore the thermal properties of the system by employing the partition function. Numerical results are presented to provide a comprehensive analysis of the system behavior under the considered effects. Notably, in the DNC framework, the commutation relations and the deformation parameter are position-dependent. Using the two-dimensional Bopp-shift, we effectively map the noncommutative problem to its commutative counterpart.

</details>


### [339] [Security of Quantum Conference Key Agreement with Two-Way Classical Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04538)
*Shun Kawakami,Mori Watanabe,Takuya Ikuta,Koichi Takasugi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了基于三体GHZ态和两个测量基的量子会议密钥协商（QCKA）在双向经典通信下的渐近安全性，通过迭代B步骤将容错阈值从无双向通信的约11%提升至超过20%，显著增强了协议鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际QCKA实现通常比传统双向量子密钥分发（QKD）面临更高的噪声水平，提升容错阈值是一个重要挑战。

Method: 对使用三体GHZ态和两个测量基的QCKA进行渐近安全分析，引入双向经典通信中的多轮B步骤和P步骤预处理过程，并推导了相应的安全密钥率公式。

Result: 迭代B步骤可将容错阈值提升至超过20%，显著高于无双向通信时的约11%和仅单次B步骤时的约15%。

Conclusion: 双向经典通信能够大幅增强实际QCKA协议的鲁棒性，为未来量子网络中的安全通信提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: Quantum conference key agreement (QCKA) enables multiple users to establish a common secret key with information-theoretic security and is regarded as a key primitive for secure communication in future quantum networks. However, practical implementations of QCKA typically suffer from higher noise levels than conventional bipartite quantum key distribution (QKD), making the improvement of the tolerable error threshold an important challenge. Gottesman and Lo proposed two preprocessing procedures for QKD with two-way classical communication, known as the B-step and the P-step, which enhance the tolerable error threshold. In this paper, we analyze the asymptotic security of QCKA with tripartite GHZ states and two measurement bases using two-way classical communication, including multiple B-steps and P-steps. We derive the corresponding secure key rate analytically and demonstrate that iterative B-steps can increase the tolerable error threshold beyond 20%, significantly improving upon the approximately 11% threshold achievable without two-way classical communication and the approximately 15% threshold obtained with only a single B-step. Our results show that two-way classical communication can substantially enhance the robustness of practical QCKA protocols.

</details>


### [340] [Equiangular weighted frames and conical 2-designs with unequal traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04555)
*Katarzyna Siudzińska*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非投影算子的等角加权框架泛化方法来构建迹不相等的锥形2-designs，详细分析了其性质、与量子测量的关系及应用，为信息过完备量子测量的完整表征迈出了重要一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锥形2-designs在表征重要量子度量方面具有关键对称性，但其一般结构和性质尚不明确，需要更深入的理解和构造方法。

Method: 基于等角加权框架向非投影算子的泛化，提出了一种构造元素迹不相等的锥形2-designs的方法。

Result: 详细分析了所构造锥形2-designs的性质、与量子测量的关系及应用，并给出了超越已知类别的示例。

Conclusion: 该工作为全面表征信息过完备量子测量奠定了重要基础，是理解锥形2-designs一般结构的关键进展。

Abstract: Conical 2-designs are families of positive operators possessing crucial symmetry properties that allow them to efficiently characterize important quantum measures. However, little is known about their general structures and properties. We propose a construction method of conical 2-designs whose elements are not of equal traces, based on a generalization of equiangular weighted frames to non-projective operators. We provide a detailed analysis of their properties, relations to quantum measurements, and applications, together with examples that go beyond the known classes. This constitutes a major step toward a full characterization of informationally overcomplete quantum measurements.

</details>


### [341] [Quantum random walks on d-regular graphs with Haar-random coin operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04584)
*Alice C. Quillen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使用Haar随机酉矩阵作为硬币算子的离散量子随机游走变体，证明了平均信道会使硬币子空间退极化，但顶点子空间仍可测量初始量子态信息，并以阿贝尔群Cayley图（循环图、超立方体图）为例进行说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索当硬币算子为随机Haar酉矩阵时，离散量子随机游走的动力学行为，研究其与经典随机游走的关联，以及量子信息在强扰动子系统中的可恢复性。

Method: 构造d-正则无向连通简单图上的离散量子随机游走，每步独立采样Haar分布随机酉矩阵作为硬币算子；计算量子信道在随机酉矩阵分布上的期望，得到退极化信道；在阿贝尔群Cayley图（循环图、超立方体图）上进行实例分析。

Result: 平均信道使硬币子空间退极化，行为类似公平硬币/平衡d面骰子决定的经典随机游走；尽管硬币子空间退极化，顶点子空间仍可设计测量来揭示初始量子态信息，且该信息在多次迭代后仍可测量。

Conclusion: Haar随机硬币的量子随机游走因退极化不适用于搜索算法，但可作为理解强扰动子系统（一子系统被强烈扰动，另一子系统信息可能永久可测量）的量子系统工具。

Abstract: With unitary coin operator that is a random matrix drawn from a uniform distribution with respect to the Haar measure, we construct a variant of a discrete quantum random walk using a d-regular undirected connected simple graph. With each step of the walk, the coin operator random matrix is drawn independently from the distribution. The expectation value over the distribution of random unitaries for the associated quantum channel gives a depolarization channel for the coin subspace and resembles the associated classical random walk where the direction a walker steps depends upon the outcome of a fair coin or balanced d-sided dice. Remarkably, despite the fact that the averaged channel depolarizes the coin subspace, measurements in the vertex subspace can be designed that would reveal information about the initial quantum state, even after many iterations of the channel. We illustrate with examples of quantum walks on Cayley graphs of Abelian groups, such as the cycle and hypercube graphs. These quantum walks are examples of bipartite strongly interacting systems, where one subsystem is strongly perturbed, yet information about the initial state in the other subsystem is potentially measurable forever. Due to its decoherence, a quantum random walk with a Haar-random coin would not be useful in search algorithms but could aid in understanding quantum systems with strongly perturbed subsystems.

</details>


### [342] [QuTuner: Feature- and Learning-Guided Optimization Pass Tuning for Quantum Compilers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04586)
*Ming Zhong,Xiangyu Ren,Jinglei Cheng,Shaohua Li,Zhiding Liang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QuTuner 提出了一种基于特征的量子编译器调优框架，通过静态电路特征和优化感知嵌入来预测并排序最优的编译优化序列，显著提升了编译效果并大幅缩短调优时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子编译器调优方法仅探索了优化传递空间的一小部分，且主要依赖静态特征，未能反映电路对优化的实际响应。

Method: 构建大规模优化数据集，从静态电路特征和优化感知传递嵌入两个视角表征电路；训练两个离线模型用于检索和排序候选传递序列，并进行轻量级精调。

Result: 在 Qiskit 上指标提升最高达 84.85%，调优时间减少 73.59%；在 PyTKET 上指标提升最高达 18.68%，调优时间减少 64.49%。

Conclusion: QuTuner 提供了一种有效的自适应传递调优方法，可跨编译器推广并兼顾效果与效率。

Abstract: Quantum compilers play a key role in transforming quantum circuits into lower-cost implementations with improved execution fidelity. This process is commonly guided by circuit-level metrics, such as gate counts and circuit depth. Although compiler pass tuning has been widely studied in classical compilation, directly transferring these techniques to quantum compilers is challenging, because quantum programs are expressed as circuits and exhibit optimization behaviors that are shaped by quantum-specific structures. Prior quantum compiler tuning approaches have begun to use circuit features to guide pass selection, but they remain limited in two aspects: they search only a small portion of the optimization-pass space, and they mainly rely on static features that do not explicitly reflect how a circuit reacts to compiler optimizations.
  We present QuTuner, a feature-guided quantum compiler pass tuning framework that generalizes across compilers and tuning objectives. QuTuner first builds a large optimization dataset. It then characterizes each circuit from two complementary views: static circuit features that describe circuit structure, and optimization-aware pass embeddings that summarize the circuit's responses to individual optimization passes. Using these representations, QuTuner trains two offline models to retrieve and rank candidate pass sequences for unseen circuits, followed by lightweight refinement. We evaluate QuTuner on Qiskit and PyTKET using two benchmark suites. On Qiskit, QuTuner improves the evaluation-metric reduction by up to 84.85% over the strongest baseline while reducing tuning time by 73.59%. On PyTKET, it improves metric reduction by up to 18.68% with a 64.49% reduction in tuning time. These results show that QuTuner provides an effective approach to adaptive pass tuning for quantum compilers.

</details>


### [343] [Breaking the One-Dimensional Expressibility-Trainability Tradeoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04598)
*Kyoungho Cho,Yu-Seong Jeon,Jinhyoung Lee,Jeongho Bang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了参数化量子电路中表达能力与可训练性之间并非一维折衷关系，通过引入纠缠能力（EP）和纠缠能力偏差（EPD）两个指标，建立了双旋钮设计规则：在保持高覆盖率的同时避免贫瘠高原（BP）条件，从而实现表达性与可训练性的解耦与共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为，增强参数化量子电路的表达能力和纠缠能力会提高希尔伯特空间覆盖率，但同时也可能导致电路随机化并激活贫瘠高原条件，形成一种表达性与可训练性之间的折衷困境。作者旨在打破这种一维折衷叙事，探索两者是否可以独立调控。

Method: 作者通过矩层级分析（moment hierarchy）将三个原本混为一谈的概念进行了解构：参数系综覆盖（全局两拷贝均值）、固定电路纠缠响应（全局四拷贝涨落描述子）和局部梯度矩（局域两拷贝收缩）。利用Haar乘积输入探测固定电路，从理论上分离了EP、EPD与梯度方差之间的关系。

Result: 证明了相等的EP不一定意味着相同的可训练性——存在EP相等但EPD和梯度方差不同的电路实例。发现ansatz路径可以在EPD和梯度方差崩溃之前达到高Haar-like覆盖率，表明覆盖率和BP激活是两个不同的交叉事件。

Conclusion: 将EP和EPD转化为PQC ansatz设计的双旋钮规则：EP衡量电路在覆盖率旋钮上的移动程度，EPD监测输入依赖的可变性是否保留。这打破了表达性-可训练性的一维折衷，提供了一个实用设计准则——在高覆盖率但BP均质化尚未抹去可训练结构的窗口中搜索高表达性PQC。

Abstract: Expressive parameterized quantum circuits (PQCs) are often designed under a dilemma: the growth of expressibility and entangling power (EP) that improves Hilbert-space coverage is also expected to randomize an ansatz and activate barren-plateau (BP) conditions. We show that this dilemma is not a one-dimensional tradeoff. The usual picture collapses three inequivalent objects -- parameter-ensemble coverage, fixed-circuit entangling response, and local gradient moments -- into one scalar narrative. For a fixed circuit probed by Haar-product inputs, EP is a global two-copy mean of the output-entanglement distribution, whereas entangling-power deviation (EPD) is a global four-copy fluctuation descriptor. Gradient variance, however, is a local two-copy contraction selected by a parameter light cone and a cost observable. This moment hierarchy yields an analytic separation: equal EP need not imply equal trainability, as witnessed by equal-EP circuits with different EPDs and different gradient variances. These separations turn EP and EPD into a two-dial design rule for PQC ansatzes: EP measures how far the circuit has moved along the coverage dial, while EPD monitors whether input-dependent variability remains. We find that ansatz routes can reach high, Haar-like coverage before EPD and gradient variance collapse, showing that coverage and BP activation are distinct crossover events. The EP/EPD framework thus breaks the apparent one-dimensional expressibility-trainability tradeoff into a practical design rule: search for highly expressive PQCs in the window where coverage is high but BP-like homogenization has not yet erased trainable structure.

</details>


### [344] [Measurement Geometry as a Resource for Certifying Network Nonlocality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04656)
*Leon Adachi,Le Bin Ho*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在双局域Alice-Bob-Charlie量子网络中，利用辅助量子比特辅助测量构建非局域观测算子，成功在156量子比特超导处理器上验证了双局域非局域性和完全网络非局域性，发现测量几何结构是网络非局域性的独立资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络可展现经典独立源模型无法解释的非经典关联，但测量设计对网络非局域性的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出基于辅助量子比特辅助测量（ancilla-assisted meters）的操作性框架，用于在双局域Alice-Bob-Charlie网络中认证网络非局域性；通过仿真复现双局域和完全网络非局域关联；在ibm_kingston超导处理器上进行实验验证。

Result: 在156量子比特处理器上，经读错误缓解后观测到双局域非局域性（S_BLHV=1.067(6)>1）；完全网络非局域性证认阈值达到99%和96%；Bob的联合测量决定了可达的网络非局域性水平，不同测量设置分别优化双局域和完全网络非局域性证认，且即使最大纠缠态下两种违反均可能消失。

Conclusion: 测量几何结构是网络非局域性的独立资源，该框架为在可编程量子处理器上认证网络非局域性提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Quantum networks can exhibit nonclassical correlations that cannot be explained by classical models with independent sources. While the role of entanglement is well understood, the impact of measurement design remains largely unexplored. Here we develop an operational framework for certifying network nonlocality in the bilocal Alice--Bob--Charlie network using ancilla-assisted meters to evaluate the nonlocal observables required for bilocal and fully network nonlocal (FNN) witnesses. The approach successfully reproduces both bilocal and FNN correlations in simulation. On the 156-qubit superconducting processor \textit{ibm\_kingston}, we observe bilocal nonlocality with $\mathcal{S}_{\rm BLHV}=1.067(6)>1$ after readout-error mitigation, while the FNN witnesses reach $99\%$ and $96\%$ of their certification thresholds, implying the substantially stronger requirements for FNN certification. We further show that Bob's joint measurement determines the accessible level of network nonlocality: bilocal and FNN certification are optimized by different measurement settings, while both violations can disappear even for maximally entangled states. These results identify measurement geometry as an independent resource for network nonlocality and provide a practical route toward its certification on programmable quantum processors.

</details>


### [345] [Krylov complexity, mode-resolved complexity and entanglement entropy across phase transitions in the non-Hermitian extended Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04659)
*Ling-Feng Zhang,Wing Chi Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩展SSH模型中非厄米性导致的相变，利用Krylov展开复杂度和纠缠熵分析两种动力学协议，并引入动量分辨复杂度揭示相变细节，同时发现纠缠熵可作为动力学相的可实验探测手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非厄米扩展SSH模型中的相变特征，并建立可实验观测的动力学相变探测方法。

Method: 通过Krylov展开复杂度、纠缠熵以及动量分辨复杂度，分析两种动力学协议：(i)通过幺正变换制备非厄米基态；(ii)在非厄米哈密顿量下演化系统。

Result: 在协议(i)中，展开复杂度能有效标记相变；在协议(ii)中，长时间展开复杂度和纠缠熵能标记相变。动量分辨复杂度可识别特征模并追踪其随驱动参数的演化。在纯虚数谱区域，基于展开复杂度的饱和行为识别出动力学相，纠缠熵也呈现类似饱和行为。

Conclusion: Krylov展开复杂度和纠缠熵均可有效探测非厄米SSH模型中的相变与动力学相，其中纠缠熵更具实验可操作性。

Abstract: We investigate phase transitions in the extended Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) model with next-nearest-neighbor hoppings and an imaginary staggered chemical potential. In the presence of small non-Hermiticity, exceptional points emerge in pairs from the gap-closing momenta near the topological phase boundaries of the Hermitian limit. Utilizing the Krylov spread complexity and entanglement entropy, we analyze two dynamical protocols: (i) preparing the non-Hermitian ground state via a unitary transformation, and (ii) evolving the system under the non-Hermitian Hamiltonian. We show that the spread complexity, and long-time spread complexity as well as entanglement entropy can effectively signal phase transitions in the first and second protocols, respectively. To unravel the detailed structure of the transitions, we introduce the momentum-resolved complexity that identifies the characteristic modes and tracks their evolution with the driving parameter. In the regime where the system possesses a purely imaginary spectrum, we further identify dynamical phases based on the saturation behavior of the spread complexity. The entanglement entropy is also found to exhibit similar saturation behavior, thereby providing a more experimentally accessible probe of the dynamical phases.

</details>


### [346] [Estimation of a sparse multi-qubit Hamiltonian via compressed sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04669)
*Juntao Tu,Yuanlong Wang,Shuming Cheng,Shuixin Xiao,Zhibo Hou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于压缩感知的多量子比特哈密顿量估计方法，通过"尺度变换"技术解决高维系统中限制等距性质（RIP）失效的问题，仅需O(k log(4^N/k))个方程即可实现k-稀疏哈密顿量的压缩估计，并在6和30量子比特系统中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N量子比特哈密顿量含有4^N-1个未知参数，需要指数级数量的方程进行信息提取，传统的压缩感知方法在N≥3时RIP条件常常失效，亟需解决高维量子系统哈密顿量估计的可行性与效率问题。

Method: 提出"尺度变换"技术，通过变换恢复RIP条件，使得压缩感知可用于多量子比特系统；利用k-稀疏性假设，将所需方程数从指数级降低至O(k log(4^N/k))。

Result: 在6量子比特和30量子比特系统的数值实验中，成功估计了哈密顿量，验证了所提方法在高维量子系统中的有效性和压缩感知的可行性。

Conclusion: 尺度变换技术有效解决了压缩感知在多量子比特哈密顿量估计中RIP失效的问题，实现了高维稀疏哈密顿量的高效估计，为大规模量子系统的结构研究提供了新途径。

Abstract: Hamiltonian estimation is an effective approach in studying the structure and dynamical evolution of quantum systems. The difficulty in estimating the Hamiltonian is that an $N$-qubit Hamiltonian has $4^N-1$ unknown parameters, requiring exponentially many equations for information extraction. In this paper we develop a method based on compressed sensing to estimate the Hamiltonian of a multi-qubit system. We identify a problem where as $N$ increases, the common sufficient condition (Restricted Isometry Property) for compressed sensing often fails, obstructing the application of compressed sensing in ($N\geq 3$)-qubit Hamiltonian estimation. To solve this problem, we propose a ``scale transformation" technique to restore RIP and ensure a compressive estimation of a $k$-sparse Hamiltonian using only $O(k\log(4^N/k))$ equations. In the numerical examples, we estimate the Hamiltonians of two 6- and 30-qubit systems, demonstrating the effectiveness of the method.

</details>


### [347] [A Path-Superposition Framework for Quantum Gate Teleportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04672)
*Santiago Ávila,Marco Enríquez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于路径叠加的确定性量子门隐形传态通用框架，通过预共享最大纠缠资源和路径依赖的局域幺正操作来实现非局域量子门，并展示了CNOT和CZ门的协议实例及光子学实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现分布式量子计算中非局域量子操作的高效执行，避免物理传输参与量子比特，从而降低通信开销和保真度损失。

Method: 基于路径叠加（path superposition）机制，利用预共享最大纠缠资源的相位和一族路径依赖的局域幺正操作来指定目标非局域操作；通过设计条件保证控制量子比特测量和局域纠正操作后的确定性隐形传态。

Result: 成功构建了CNOT门和CZ门的隐形传态协议，证明不同非局域操作可在同一协议架构下通过选择不同设计参数来实现；给出了基于空间路径和偏振自由度的光子学概念验证方案。

Conclusion: 路径叠加是一种用于量子门隐形传态和分布式量子信息处理的通用资源，所提框架为构建确定性非局域量子操作提供了系统化设计方法。

Abstract: Quantum gate teleportation enables distant parties to implement nonlocal quantum operations without physically transferring the participating qubits, making it a promising primitive for distributed quantum computing. We introduce a general framework for deterministic quantum gate teleportation based on path superposition, in which the target nonlocal operation is specified through the phase of a preshared maximally entangled resource and a suitable family of path-dependent local unitary operators. The framework establishes general design conditions that guarantee deterministic teleportation after measurement of the control qubits and the application of local correction operations. As representative realizations, we construct teleportation protocols for controlled-NOT (CNOT) and controlled-Z (CZ) gates, demonstrating that different nonlocal operations can be implemented within the same protocol architecture through appropriate choices of the design parameters. We further outline a proof-of-concept photonic realization based on spatial-path and polarization degrees of freedom. The proposed framework identifies path superposition as a versatile resource for quantum gate teleportation and distributed quantum information processing.

</details>


### [348] [Magnetic graphs for cavity quantum electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04736)
*Sunkyu Yu,Xianji Piao,Namkyoo Park*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种磁性图模型用于单原子腔QED，通过图连通性解释从弱耦合到深强耦合的量子动力学，揭示了亚图拓扑引起的相位阻挫是连通性转变的主要驱动力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强光-物质耦合是腔QED的核心挑战，随着耦合进入超强乃至深强区域，即使最简单的两能级原子与量子化场相互作用的系统也需要精确处理（如规范不变的量子拉比模型），亟需新的理论框架来统一描述不同耦合强度下的量子动力学。

Method: 提出单原子腔QED的磁性图模型，将广义QRM映射为满足Floquet边界条件的完全二分图，通过"断开非磁性亚图的代价"这一单一指标来捕捉从弱耦合到深强耦合的渡越。

Result: 建立了图论与腔QED之间的桥梁，揭示了亚图拓扑诱导的相位阻挫是连通性转变的驱动机制，即使在最简单的单原子设置中也能展现高度复杂的图动力学。

Conclusion: 该图论框架可扩展至多体系统，为理解超强耦合腔QED中的量子动力学提供了全新的视角和统一的分析工具。

Abstract: Strengthening light-matter coupling has become a central challenge in cavity quantum electrodynamics (QED), enabling ultrafast gate operations, qubit protection, and deterministic nonlinear optics. As the coupling increases, even the simplest configuration, a two-level atom interacting with a quantized field, requires careful treatment, as exemplified by the gauge-invariant quantum Rabi model (QRM). Here we propose a magnetic graph model for single-atom cavity QED, which enables the interpretation of quantum dynamics across the ultrastrong coupling regime through graph connectivity. We demonstrate that the generalized QRM maps onto a complex bipartite graph of identical sites under Floquet boundary conditions. This framework captures the crossover from weak to deep-strong coupling via a single metric: the cost of disconnecting a nonmagnetic subgraph. We examine the mechanism underlying this connectivity transition, establishing phase frustration induced by subgraph topology as the primary driver. Scalable to many-body systems, this approach bridges graph theory and cavity QED, revealing highly complex-graph dynamics even in the simplest setting.

</details>


### [349] [Quantum-Optical Bound States in the Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04742)
*Ruo Kun Cai,Zhi Jiao Deng,Chun Wang Wu,Ping Xing Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个量子光学模型，利用Fock态晶格将驱动多能级Jaynes-Cummings系统映射为两个耦合到共同连续谱的半无限非均匀SSH链，通过拓扑零模的量子叠加形成连续谱中的束缚态（BIC），并发展出通过傅里叶变换手性对称性算符动力学提取BIC谱特征的方法，最后提出了基于单离子阱的实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BICs在经典波系统中已被广泛实现和利用，但在量子光学环境中——其中波场本身被量子化为玻色子激发——BIC的类似物是什么以及如何提取其实验信号仍不清楚。本文旨在填补这一空白，构建一个可解析且实验上可行的量子光学BIC模型。

Method: (1)构建驱动多能级Jaynes-Cummings系统模型，利用Fock态晶格概念将其映射为两个半无限非均匀SSH链耦合到共同连续谱的结构；(2)证明通过两个拓扑零模的量子叠加可形成与连续谱完全解耦（通过相消量子干涉）的BIC；(3)发展通过傅里叶变换系统手性对称性算符的时间依赖动力学提取BIC谱特征（嵌入连续背景中的分立峰）的方法；(4)提出基于单离子阱的实验实现方案。

Result: 成功构建了具有少量量子自由度却能承载BIC的量子光学模型，实现BIC在Fock态维度中完美局域化且与连续谱完全解耦，并开发了提取BIC谱学信号的方法。

Conclusion: 这项工作桥接了BIC物理与量子光学，为在量子极限下利用这类奇特态开辟了路径，并为量子光学系统中BIC的实验观测提供了可行的方案。

Abstract: Bound states in the continuum (BICs) are counterintuitive localized states that lie within the continuum of extended states. While extensively realized and utilized in classical wave systems, it is still unclear what a close analog of BICs would be, and how to extract their experimental signature in quantum-optical settings -- where the wave field itself is quantized into bosonic excitations. Here, we present a paradigmatic quantum-optical model consisting of a driven multi-level Jaynes-Cummings (JC) system, featuring few quantum degrees of freedom yet capable of hosting a BIC. Using the concept of a Fock-state lattice (FSL), this model can be mapped to an extended structure comprising two semi-infinite inhomogeneous Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) chains coupled to a common continuum. An appropriate quantum superposition of two topological zero modes from the separate chains forms a BIC that remains perfectly localized in the Fock-state dimension within the continuum spectrum, due to complete decoupling from the common continuum via destructive quantum interference. We further develop a method to extract the spectroscopic signature of the BIC -- a discrete peak embedded in a continuous background -- by Fourier-transforming the time-dependent dynamics of the system's chiral-symmetry operator. A highly feasible experimental proposal using a single trapped ion is provided. Our work bridges BIC physics with quantum optics, opening a pathway to harnessing such exotic states at the quantum limit.

</details>


### [350] [Sector-memory obstruction to probe-level bath emergence in finite programmable qubit environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04791)
*Gaurav Sarmah,Ramakrishna Podila*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过数值模拟和IBM量子硬件实验，研究了有限量子环境中一个探测量子比特与N个浴量子比特在激发数守恒动力学下的弛豫行为。结果表明，在电荷守恒约束下，系统在各固定电荷扇区内趋于平衡，但无法形成单一的、扇区无关的吉布斯态，即有限量子环境不能作为标准热浴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究有限量子环境（非无穷大热浴）能否使局域探针弛豫到与标准吉布斯态一致的状态。具体关注激发数守恒动力学下，系统因电荷守恒被分割为不同扇区，这对探针的平衡态有何影响。

Method: 1. 使用Haar随机纯态在固定电荷扇区内进行精确模拟，计算扇区分辨的晚期布居数p_e^{(q)}、扇区记忆方差M_N和全局吉布斯拟合误差Δ_G^{global}。2. 构建电荷守恒的Floquet电路（R_z相位门和XX+YY交换门），在理想和噪声Qiskit模拟中验证。3. 在IBM Fez量子硬件上对N=4系统进行有限深度实验，采用读错缓解和对称性破缺扫描（R_x(ε)旋转）。

Result: 1. 扇区依赖的布居数接近最大混合扇区基准p_e^{(q)}=q/(N+1)，但存在非零的吉布斯阻塞。2. 对N=4, ε=0，硬件数据给出M_N≈0.044, Δ_G^{global}≈0.558，电荷守恒经读错缓解后接近0.90。3. 对称性破缺扫描（R_x(ε)旋转）虽降低两个诊断指标并增加电荷泄漏，但在可达深度内无法消除扇区排序。

Conclusion: 在有限量子环境中，即使系统在电荷守恒约束的扇区内达到平衡，也不足以产生一个单一的、扇区无关的吉布斯态。有限量子环境不能替代标准热浴，这揭示了有限量子环境的弛豫行为与标准热力学极限之间的本质区别。

Abstract: Finite quantum environments can relax local probes without acting as canonical baths. We study this distinction for a probe qubit coupled to a programmable bath of ($N$) qubits under excitation-number-conserving dynamics. The conserved charge partitions the Hilbert space into sectors. We characterize probe-level bath emergence using the sector-resolved late-time population ($p_e^{(q)}$), the sector-memory variance ($M_N$), and a global Gibbs-fit error ($Δ_G^{\mathrm{global}}$). Exact simulations with Haar-random pure states in each complete fixed-charge sector yield sector-dependent populations close to the maximally mixed-sector benchmark ($p_e^{(q)}=q/(N+1)$), producing a nonzero Gibbs obstruction. We then construct charge-preserving Floquet circuits using ($R_z$) phases and ($XX+YY$) exchange gates, validate them with ideal and noisy Qiskit simulations, and implement finite-depth experiments on IBM Fez. For ($N=4$) and ($ε=0$), the hardware data give ($M_N \simeq 0.044$), ($Δ_G^{\mathrm{global}} \simeq 0.558$), and charge preservation near 0.90 after readout mitigation. A paired symmetry-breaking scan using bath ($R_x(ε)$) rotations reduces both diagnostics while increasing charge leakage, but does not erase sector ordering over the accessible depths. These results show that equilibration within constrained sectors is insufficient to produce a single sector-independent Gibbs state for the probe.

</details>


### [351] [HamQASBench: A Hamiltonian-Informed Diagnostic Benchmark for Evaluating Quantum Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04845)
*Jiayang Niu,Akib Karim,Yan Wang,Jie Li,Ke Deng,Azadeh Alavi,Muhammad Usman,Yongli Ren*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 HamQASBench，一个基于哈密顿量信息诊断的量子架构搜索基准，通过五层结构分类分子并进行后验关键结构提取，揭示了传统能量指标无法检测的五种失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 QAS 基准按分子身份或量子比特数组织实例，这些标准与哈密顿量结构无关；且仅依赖能量精度评估，无法检测过参数化等结构失效问题。

Method: 将11个分子通过泡利算子基、计算基表示和基态纠缠指纹分为五个结构层级；提出后验关键结构抽取过程，识别满足各层级需求的最小电路；结合每量子比特纠缠分析和成对态保真度补充能量评估。

Result: 对五种 QAS 方法（涵盖四个范式）的基准测试揭示了传统指标不可见的五种失效模式：极简主义下的过参数化、简并下的本征态承诺、强关联系统中的表示瓶颈、拓扑诱导的路由失效以及电路搜索空间增长带来的可扩展性瓶颈。

Conclusion: HamQASBench 通过哈密顿量结构感知的层级分类和多维度评估，能够有效诊断 QAS 方法在传统能量指标下隐藏的结构性失效，为更鲁棒的量子架构搜索提供了诊断性基准工具。

Abstract: Quantum Architecture Search (QAS) automates the design of parameterized quantum circuits for variational quantum algorithms, yet existing benchmarks organize instances by molecular identity or qubit count -- criteria agnostic to Hamiltonian structure -- and rely solely on energy accuracy, which cannot detect structural failures such as over-parameterization on near-product ground states. We introduce HamQASBench, a Hamiltonian-informed diagnostic benchmark organizing 11 molecules into five structural tiers via fingerprints derived from the Pauli operator basis, computational basis representation, and ground-state entanglement. A post-hoc critical-structure extraction procedure identifies minimal circuits consistent with each tier's requirements, complementing energy-based evaluation with per-qubit entanglement analysis and pairwise state fidelity. Benchmarking five QAS methods across four paradigms reveals failure modes invisible to conventional metrics: over-parameterization in the minimalism regime, eigenstate commitment under degeneracy, a representation bottleneck in strongly correlated systems, topology-induced routing failure, and circuit search space growth as a scalability bottleneck.

</details>


### [352] [Emergence of the Scrooge Ensemble in the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04864)
*Zeyu Liu,Pengfei Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用可解的Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK)模型，通过路径积分方法解析证明了投影系综在任意演化时间下均精确收敛到Scrooge系综，揭示了深度热化现象的微观机制，并建立了测量路径积分中的鞍点结构与Scrooge统计中复制置换之间的自然联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子测量提供了解多体波函数结构及其复杂性的直接窗口。当系统仅部分被测量时，剩余自由度形成后测量态系综，其统计结构可揭示一种更强的热化形式——深度热化。近期数值证据表明该现象表现为投影系综收敛到Scrooge系综（在给定密度矩阵下最大随机的系综），但其背后的理论机制尚不清晰，需要从可解模型中建立解析理解。

Method: 利用可解的Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK)模型，将测量概率和后测量态表述为路径积分形式，解析刻画投影系综的所有矩，并与Scrooge系综的矩进行精确比较。进一步将结果与测量路径积分的鞍点结构建立联系，自然导出Scrooge统计中关键的复制置换结构。

Result: 解析证明了在SYK模型中，投影系综的所有矩在任意演化时间下均精确匹配Scrooge系综的矩，即使在短时间演化下也成立。建立了测量路径积分的鞍点结构与Scrooge统计中复制置换之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 建立了可解的SYK模型作为研究混沌多体动力学中量子测量普适统计的可驾驭框架，为理解深度热化现象中Scrooge系综涌现的微观机制提供了解析基础。

Abstract: The probabilistic nature of quantum measurement provides a direct window into the structure and complexity of many-body wave functions. When only part of a system is measured, the remaining degrees of freedom form an ensemble of post-measurement states whose statistical structure can reveal a stronger form of thermalization, known as deep thermalization. Recent numerical evidence suggests that this phenomenon is characterized by convergence of the projected ensemble to the Scrooge ensemble, a maximally random ensemble compatible with a given density matrix. In this Letter, we use the solvable Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model to unveil the mechanism by which the Scrooge ensemble emerges in many-body systems. By formulating measurement probabilities and post-measurement states in terms of path integrals, we analytically characterize all moments of the projected ensemble and show that they exactly match those of the Scrooge ensemble, even at short evolution times. We further connect this result to the saddle-point structure of the measurement path integral, which naturally generates the replica permutations underlying Scrooge statistics. Our results establish the solvable SYK model as a tractable setting for exploring universal statistics of quantum measurements in chaotic many-body dynamics.

</details>


### [353] [Photonic Cluster State Generation from a Quantum Dot Emitting in the Telecom C-band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04896)
*Giora Peniakov,Reza Hekmati,Johannes Michl,Mohamed Helal,Moritz Meinecke,Jochen Kaupp,Yorick Reum,Martin Kamp,Monika Emmerling,Andreas Theo Pfenning,Sven Hoefling,Tobias Huber-Loyola*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了在电信C波段直接确定性生成光量子簇态，通过重复激发磁场中的砷化铟量子点空穴自旋实现，过程保真度0.71，自旋-光子纠缠负性0.27，光子不可区分度≥83%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光量子簇态是光子量子信息处理的关键资源，但此前确定性生成仅限于近红外波段。为实现量子通信优势同时兼容硅基光子学，需要将工作波长扩展到电信C波段。

Method: 通过重复激发置于外磁场中的砷化铟量子点空穴自旋，在电信C波段直接确定性生成光量子簇态。通过测量过程映射图（process map）表征簇态生成过程，同时测量自旋-光子偏振纠缠的负性和光子的不可区分度。

Result: 过程保真度F = 0.71 ± 0.01；自旋-光子偏振纠缠负性N = 0.27 ± 0.02；光子不可区分度至少83%。

Conclusion: 成功在电信C波段实现确定性光量子簇态生成，高光子不可区分度表明具备实现未来融合门（fusion gates）的潜力，可支持超越线性连通性的光量子簇态生成。

Abstract: Photonic cluster states are a key resource for photonic quantum information processing. So far, deterministic generation of these states has been limited to the near-infrared wavelength range. To achieve quantum advantage in communication while maintaining compatibility with silicon photonics, operation in the telecom wavelength range is required. In this work, we demonstrate deterministic cluster state generation directly in the telecom C-band. This is achieved through repetitive excitation of a hole spin confined in an indium-arsenide quantum dot subjected to an external magnetic field. We characterize the quantum process that generates the cluster state by measuring its process map, obtaining a fidelity of $\mathrm{F} = 0.71 \pm 0.01$ to the ideal case. As part of this characterization, we observe spin--photon polarization entanglement with a negativity of $\mathrm{N} = 0.27 \pm 0.02$. The emitted photons exhibit indistinguishability of at least 83%, demonstrating the potential for future fusion gates necessary for photonic cluster state generation beyond linear connectivity.

</details>


### [354] [Quantum orientation, Noether structure, composition of systems and operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04899)
*Heinz-Jürgen Schmidt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文论证除了量子理论的统计结构外，还需要一种称为"Noether结构"的额外结构来描述系统组合和定义完全正操作。该结构反映了厄米算符作为可观测量和对称性生成元的双重角色，在有限维情形下建立了与完全正操作的联系，纯操作可表征为保定向映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子理论框架仅关注统计结构，不足以完整描述系统组合和完全正操作。论文旨在揭示Noether结构在量子理论中的必要性，并建立其与完全正操作的联系。

Method: 论文在有限维框架下，借鉴Alfsen和Shultz关于Jordan乘积扩展为算子代数结合乘积的研究，通过分析厄米算符的双重角色来构建Noether结构，并建立与完全正操作的联系。

Result: 证明了Noether结构对于描述系统组合和定义完全正操作是必要的；在纯操作情形下，完全正操作可表征为保定向映射。

Conclusion: 量子理论需要统计结构和Noether结构共同支撑；Noether结构为理解系统组合和完全正操作提供了新的数学基础。

Abstract: In this paper we argue that, in addition to the statistical structure of quantum theory, another structure, referred to here as the ``Noether structure," is necessary to describe the composition of systems and to define completely positive operations. A Noether structure reflects the dual role of Hermitian operators as observables on the one hand and as generators of symmetry transformations on the other. This idea has been expressed in a similar form in the works of Alfsen and Shultz, who investigated the conditions under which the Jordan product can be extended to an associative product of operator algebras. Our investigations into the Noether structure and the composition of systems are limited to the finite-dimensional case and establish a connection to completely positive operations. In the case of pure operations, the latter can be characterized as orientation-preserving maps.

</details>


### [355] [Error Mitigation in Bosonic Systems via Virtual Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04914)
*Leonardo Finocchiaro,Marco Robbio,Diogo Gomes,David Gunn,Adithi Udupa,Axel M. Eriksson,Leonardo Novo,Giulia Ferrini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了连续变量和玻色子量子信息处理中虚拟蒸馏（virtual distillation）的理论框架，利用被动线性光学干涉仪实现循环移位算符的对角化，给出了针对玻色子体系关键可观测量（如粒子数算符、相移算符、任意正交分量）的噪声抑制协议，并量化了光子损耗和退相干的抑制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟蒸馏最初针对量子比特系统提出，但玻色子量子计算和连续变量量子信息处理中同样面临噪声问题，尚缺乏系统化的虚拟蒸馏方案。

Method: 基于被动线性光学干涉仪实现循环移位算符的对角化，推导多副本测量下玻色子可观测量（粒子数算符、相移算符、正交分量）的虚拟蒸馏期望值估计协议；利用光子数分布特征函数实现任意阶粒子数关联函数的高阶估计。

Result: 给出了光子损耗和退相干两种主要噪声机制下的噪声抑制量化结果，证明该框架能有效压制玻色子量子计算中的噪声贡献。

Conclusion: 将虚拟蒸馏从量子比特系统推广到玻色子系统，为连续变量量子处理器中利用现有线性光学资源实现纠错测量提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Virtual distillation is a promising error-mitigation technique that exploits multiple copies of a noisy quantum state to estimate observables as if measured on a purified state. Although originally introduced in the context of bosonic many-body systems under the name of virtual cooling, its development and applications have largely focused on qubit-based quantum computation. Here, we establish a framework for virtual distillation in bosonic quantum information processing and continuous-variable quantum computing. Building on a diagonalization of cyclic shift operators implemented with passive linear-optical interferometers, we derive experimentally accessible protocols for estimating virtually distilled expectation values of observables relevant to bosonic architectures. In particular, we show how to recover noise-mitigated expectation values of number operators, phase-shift operators, and arbitrary quadratures from multi-copy measurements. For number operators, we further demonstrate the estimation of virtually distilled correlators of arbitrary order through the characteristic function of the photon-number distribution. We apply the framework to states affected by photon loss and dephasing, two of the dominant noise mechanisms in bosonic quantum computation, and quantify the resulting suppression of noise contributions. Our results extend virtual distillation beyond its original setting and provide a practical route toward error-mitigated measurements in bosonic quantum processors using experimentally available linear-optical resources.

</details>


### [356] [How Hard Is Quantum Advantage? A Cloud Microphysics Stress Test for Variational Quantum Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04915)
*Felix Herbort,Ellen Sarauer,Daniel Ohl de Mello,Paul Christiansen,Steffen Hien,Cedric Brügmann,Dieter Jaksch,Veronika Eyring,Martin Kiffner,Mierk Schwabe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探索了混合量子神经网络（QNN）在云微观物理数据集上的应用，发现尽管通过超参数优化和频率谱增强提升了性能，但QNN仍被简单的经典全连接神经网络超越，揭示了当前变分量子模型在机器学习中的瓶颈和局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习（QML）在理论上具有超越经典计算的潜力，但缺乏实际改进和可扩展性的有力证据，且受到贫瘠高原等现象的阻碍。本文旨在探索QNN在复杂物理系统（云微观物理）中的实际可行性。

Method: 采用混合量子神经网络（QNN），结合丰富的可训练频率谱和增强表达能力的经典后处理，并在云微观物理数据集（包含大气中水的相变过程及相关温度变化）上进行实验，同时进行广泛的超参数优化。

Result: 经过超参数优化后，QNN在云微观物理数据集上表现出可行性，但性能仍被简单的经典全连接神经网络基线所超越。

Conclusion: 当前变分量子模型在学习复杂物理系统数据方面存在瓶颈，需要进一步理解和改进，才能在经典模型失败或低效的领域发挥优势。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning (QML) could have the potential to leverage advantages of quantum over classical computing but still lacks strong evidence of actual improvements and scalability, partly due to phenomena such as barren plateaus. In this paper, we employ a hybrid quantum neural network (QNN) on a dataset on cloud microphysics, containing processes for phase transitions of water in the atmosphere and its related temperature changes, which are highly relevant for accurate climate predictions and projections. To reach optimal performance of our QNNs, we employ a rich and trainable frequency spectrum together with expressivity enhancing classical postprocessing. We find that our QNNs strongly benefit from extensive hyperparameter optimization and thereby demonstrate the feasibility of applying QNNs to complex physical systems. At the same time, the QNNs are outperformed by classical baselines in the form of simple fully-connected neural networks. We discuss identified bottlenecks of this class of quantum models to learn the full complexity of the cloud microphysics dataset to show that there is a need to further understand and improve variational quantum models for machine learning such that they might fill the gap where classical models fail or are inefficient.

</details>


### [357] [Noise-limited secret key agreement with twin optical physically unclonable functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04936)
*Georgios M. Nikolopoulos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究利用孪生光学PUF（物理不可克隆函数）产生的相关散斑图案，作为硬件基础实现密码学密钥生成与分发。提出了一种信息论意义上的密钥协商协议，结合安全草图、纠错和隐私放大，分析了公共辅助数据的信息泄露，推导了最终密钥长度的下界，并探讨了其在QKD网络中作为预共享密钥生成机制的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密码学密钥分发通常依赖计算复杂性假设或可信第三方，存在安全隐患。本文旨在利用光学PUF的物理不可克隆性和相关性，提供一种不依赖计算假设的硬件级密钥协商方案，以增强密钥分发的安全性。

Method: 采用相关光学PUF产生的孪生散斑图案作为随机源，设计信息论密钥协商协议，包含三个核心组件：安全草图用于处理PUF输出间的差异性，纠错算法进行信息 reconciliation，隐私放大消除泄露信息。理论分析公共辅助数据的信息泄露量，并推导最终密钥长度的下界。

Result: 确定了在何种噪声水平下能够实现安全密钥协商，比较了实际纠错方案与近容量纠错方案的性能差异。给出了密钥长度的理论下界，并量化了辅助数据泄露对安全性的影响。

Conclusion: 孪生光学PUF能够在无需计算假设或可信第三方的情况下，为双方建立共享密钥，并可作为QKD网络中的预共享密钥生成机制，提升量子密钥分发系统的实际安全性。

Abstract: We investigate the use of twin optical fingerprints derived from correlated physical unclonable functions (PUFs), as a hardware-based platform for cryptographic key generation and distribution. Each fingerprint is associated with a random, yet reproducible speckle pattern, generated when coherent light is scattered by a disordered optical structure. We consider a pair of correlated optical PUFs, and study the conditions under which two honest parties can establish a common secret key, despite fabrication-induced variability and environmental noise. An explicit information-theoretic key-agreement protocol is developed, incorporating secure sketches, error reconciliation, and privacy amplification. We quantify information leakage due to public helper data, and derive lower bounds on the length of the final secret key. The analysis identifies the noise regimes in which secure key agreement is feasible, and examines the performance of both practical and near-capacity reconciliation schemes. Finally, we discuss how twin optical PUFs could be integrated into quantum key distribution (QKD) networks, as a mechanism for establishing an initial pre-shared secret key between two honest users, without relying on computational assumptions or trusted third parties.

</details>


### [358] [Contraction and Expansion Values of Quantum Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04950)
*Ruben Ibarrondo,Mikel Sanz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入了“收缩值”和“扩张值”两个单调序列，它们以类似奇异值细化算子范数的方式细化了量子信道的收缩系数与扩张系数，提供了信道在态区分能力下降方面的更精细刻画，并在信道组合下提供比单一收缩系数更优的界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的迹距离收缩系数作为极值比率，只刻画了信道最乐观的行为，即使在噪声信道中也常常是平凡的；且单个标量无法很好地描述收缩在信道组合下的累积效应。因此需要更精细的定量工具。

Method: 通过在无迹厄米算子的子空间上建立min-max变分原理，定义收缩值和扩张值两个单调序列；将它们与Pietsch的s-数理论中的Gel'fand数和Bernstein数建立等价关系；并针对单量子比特信道、d维振幅阻尼信道和直和信道进行具体计算或估计。

Result: 证明了收缩值和扩张值与信道限制在无迹算子上的Gel'fand数和Bernstein数一致，从而可应用s-数理论的已知结果；给出了它们在信道组合下的界限（单一收缩系数无法提供）；计算了单量子比特信道、振幅阻尼信道和直和信道的具体值。

Conclusion: 收缩值和扩张值为量子信息中迹距离收缩的刻画提供了更精细、更具结构性的理论框架，弥补了传统收缩系数的不足，并在信道组合分析中展现出优势。

Abstract: The contraction coefficient of the trace distance is a central tool in quantum information, quantifying how strongly a quantum channel degrades the distinguishability of states. However, being an extremal ratio, it captures only the most optimistic behaviour of the channel and is often trivial, even for very noisy channels. Moreover, a single scalar is poorly suited to describe how contraction accumulates under channel composition. In this work we introduce the \emph{contraction and expansion values}, two monotone sequences that refine the contraction and expansion coefficients in the same way singular values refine the operator norm. They arise from a min--max variational principle over subspaces of traceless Hermitian operators, admit an operational interpretation in terms of two state-discrimination games, and are shown to coincide with the Gel'fand or Bernstein numbers of the channel restricted to traceless operators. This identification places the sequences within Pietsch's theory of $s$-numbers and yields, in particular, bounds under channel composition that the contraction coefficient alone cannot provide. We establish their main structural properties and compute or estimate them for single-qubit channels, $d$-dimensional amplitude damping channels, and direct-sum channels.

</details>


### [359] [Extending the Bloch sphere model to an N-qubit system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04979)
*Francisco Piñero,Cristian Franco,Hernán I. de la Cruz,Fernando L. Pelayo,Vicente Pascual,Mauro Mezzini,Jose Javier Paulet,Fernando Cuartero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将Bloch球面表示推广到任意N量子比特系统的几何模型，使用2^N-1个球面来表征多量子比特态及其纠缠结构，并证明了该模型与标准态矢量表示之间的严格双射关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bloch球是表示单量子比特状态的优雅工具，但缺乏被广泛接受的多量子比特系统（含纠缠）推广表示方法。

Method: 提出使用2^N-1个球面扩展Bloch球面表示到任意N量子比特系统；通过沿Z轴和Y轴的控制旋转门分层应用实现N量子比特参数化；证明该模型的角度参数与标准态矢量表示之间存在严格双射。

Result: 任何纯2量子比特状态由三个球面唯一描述：两个用于单个量子比特，第三个用于封装两体纠缠；建立了N量子比特的完整参数化方案。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了多重纠缠的直观可视化，为量子模拟器带来潜在计算优势，并为量子门分析提供新的视角。

Abstract: The Bloch sphere is an elegant tool for representing single-qubit states. However, a widely accepted generalization for multi-qubit systems with entanglement remains absent. We propose a novel geometric model extending the Bloch sphere representation to arbitrary $N$-qubit systems using $2^N-1$ spheres. We demonstrate that any pure 2-qubit state is uniquely described by three spheres: two for individual qubits and a third encapsulating bipartite entanglement. Generalizing this, we establish an $N$-qubit parameterization through the hierarchical application of controlled rotation gates along the $Z$ and $Y$ axes. We formally prove a strict bijection between the standard state vector representation and our model's angular parameters. This framework provides an intuitive visualization of multiple entanglement, offering potential computational advantages for quantum simulators and new analytical perspectives on quantum gates.

</details>


### [360] [Quantum Hashing via Constrained Rydberg Many-Body Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04991)
*Han-Chao Chen,Xin Liu,Zheng-Yuan Zhang,Dong-Sheng Ding,Bao-Sen Shi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了里德伯原子阵列中的约束多体动力学如何自然产生量子哈希机制，通过将三元字符串编码为状态空间中的确定性轨迹，将经典信息空间映射到希尔伯特空间中的量子态系综，该系综具有高概率近正交性、随机分布和广泛几何覆盖等特征，从而自然地实现了量子哈希的密码学功能（低碰撞概率、单向性、篡改敏感性和隐私保护），表明量子动力学本身可作为密码信息处理的物理资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子哈希的密码学功能是否可以不必依赖精心设计的算法，而是从约束多体动力学中自然涌现，从而识别量子动力学本身作为一种密码信息处理的物理资源。

Method: 利用里德伯原子阵列中的约束多体动力学，将三元字符串编码为状态空间中的确定性轨迹，将经典信息空间映射到希尔伯特空间中的量子态系综，并对其几何特性进行统计分析。

Result: 该量子态系综展现出高概率近正交性、随机分布和广泛几何覆盖的特性，这些几何特征自然地产生了量子哈希的核心密码学性质：低碰撞概率、单向性、篡改敏感性和隐私保护。

Conclusion: 量子哈希的密码学功能可以从约束多体动力学中自然涌现，无需依赖人为设计的算法，量子动力学本身可以作为一种物理资源用于密码学信息处理。

Abstract: In this Letter, we show that constrained many-body dynamics in Rydberg atom arrays naturally gives rise to a quantum hashing mechanism. By encoding ternary strings into deterministic trajectories in the state space, the classical information space is mapped onto a quantum state ensemble in the Hilbert space with an induced geometric structure. Statistical analysis reveals that this ensemble exhibits high probability near-orthogonality, random-like distribution, and broad geometric coverage. These geometric features naturally give rise to the essential cryptographic properties of quantum hashing, including low collision probability, one-wayness, tamper sensitivity, and privacy preservation. Our results demonstrate that the cryptographic functionality of quantum hashing need not rely on deliberately engineered algorithms, but can instead emerge naturally from constrained many-body dynamics, identifying quantum dynamics itself as a physical resource for cryptographic information processing.

</details>


### [361] [Canonical quantization of neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05000)
*Alexander He,Nana Liu,Mark M. Wilde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将正则量子化应用于机器学习中的神经元，通过将能量函数替换为量子哈密顿量并利用矩阵函数微积分应用激活函数，构建量子神经元模型，并开发了混合量子-经典算法进行训练与评估，实验表明该量子神经元相比经典神经元具有更强的表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子机器学习建立系统化的构造框架，将经典机器学习中的基本计算原语（神经元）通过正则量子化原则转化为量子模型，从而为处理量子数据设计专门的神经架构。

Method: 将神经元视为能量函数与激活函数的复合，通过正则量子化将能量函数替换为量子哈密顿量，利用矩阵函数微积分应用激活函数得到可观测的激活算子；使用经典随机采样、Hadamard测试和哈密顿模拟进行梯度估计，利用"单qumode之力"和薛定谔化算法测量激活可观测量。

Result: 数值实验表明量子神经元在代表性学习任务上相比经典神经元具有更强的表达能力。

Conclusion: 正则量子化可作为构建量子机器学习原语的原则性框架，为处理量子数据的神经架构开发奠定基础。

Abstract: Canonical quantization provides a systematic procedure for constructing quantum models from classical Hamiltonians. Here, we apply this principle to a fundamental computational primitive of machine learning: the neuron. Specifically, by viewing a neuron as a composition of an energy function and an activation function, we quantize this model by replacing the energy function with a quantum Hamiltonian and applying the activation function to it through matrix functional calculus. This results in an activation observable that can be measured on an input quantum state. We investigate the use of these quantized neurons for function approximation, where the objective is to learn an unknown observable from labeled quantum data. For this purpose, we develop hybrid quantum-classical algorithms for training and evaluation, including procedures for measuring the activation observable and estimating gradients of the squared loss error. Our algorithms for gradient estimation rely on basic primitives like classical random sampling, the Hadamard test, and Hamiltonian simulation, and those for measuring an activation observable rely on quantum algorithms known as the power of one qumode and Schroedingerization. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our quantized neurons exhibit enhanced expressive capabilities relative to corresponding classical neurons on representative learning tasks. Our work establishes canonical quantization as a principled framework for constructing quantum machine learning primitives and provides a foundation for developing neural architectures tailored to quantum data.

</details>


### [362] [Transmon Phase Gates Controlled by Superconducting Soliton DAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05072)
*Derek Reitz,Tony X. Zhou,Aditya Sharma,Ryan Bilotta,John McFarland,Aref Fouladi,Jacob Glasby,Aruna Ramanayaka,Zachary Stegen,Aaron Pesetski,Mark Covington,Gregory Boyd,Jeremy Clark*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种超导数模转换器（DAC），通过将梯形驱动脉冲转换为超导孤子并存储在负载环路中，实现对超导量子比特的高保真度、纳秒级门操作。初始演示成功在transmon上实现了5.6 ns的S门，每个门的激发概率为0.05%，但多比特扩展时存在非局域DAC诱导相位误差（1.6%/门），并讨论了缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超导量子比特控制方案存在控制噪声、缺乏原生复用能力、时钟速度受限等问题，且通常需要复杂的低温控制线路。作者希望设计一种新型DAC，既能滤波控制噪声、提供原生复用，又能实现纳秒级量子门操作，并兼容CMOS控制。

Method: 将梯形驱动脉冲转换为超导孤子，将孤子存储在DAC负载环路中，通过互感向超导量子比特或门耦合器施加磁通。模拟输出通量可通过改变孤子保持时间或使用直流偏置可调DAC-量子比特耦合器控制。实验在transmon上演示了5.6 ns的S门，并通过Interleaved Randomized Benchmarking (IRB)测量了扩展性能。

Result: 成功实现5.6 ns的S门；每个门的量子态激发概率为0.05%；DAC诱导的弛豫率低于transmon的固有T1极限；量子模拟预测通过过阻尼约瑟夫森结可将激发率再降低10倍；多比特扩展时，非耦合的辅助量子比特上出现1.6%/门的非局域DAC诱导相位误差。

Conclusion: 超导DAC能够实现快速、高保真度的量子门操作，但多比特扩展面临非局域电磁干扰（EMI）导致的相位误差问题。未来需要优化多DAC芯片布局以缓解EMI，并引入直流可调耦合器进行相位校正。

Abstract: We introduce a superconducting digital-to-analog converter (DAC) that filters control noise, provides native multiplexing, performs quantum gates in nanoseconds, and can be controlled by CMOS. This is achieved by transducing a trapezoidal drive pulse into a superconducting soliton, which is then held in the DAC load loop, applying flux to a mutually-coupled superconducting qubit or gate coupler. The analog flux output by the DAC can be easily controlled by varying the soliton hold time, or with a DC-biased tunable DAC-qubit coupler, allowing the DAC to perform a fixed-time, high-fidelity gate that's robust to fabrication variance or flux offsets in the quantum circuit. Our initial demonstration shows that the DAC can successfully perform 5.6 ns S-gates on transmons. We measure the DAC-induced quantum state excitation probability per gate to be 0.05%, and find that the DAC-induced relaxation rate from the qubit 1 state is below the intrinsic T1 rate limit of the transmon. Quantum simulations show qualitative agreement with the measured data, and predict that the DAC excitation rate can be lowered 10 times further by overdamping the Josephson junction (JJ) in the DAC load loop. may be limited by a Interleaved Randomized Benchmarking (IRB) sequences on an observer qubit reveal that, when scaling to many qubits, the DAC's performance may be limited by a non-local, DAC-induced phase error of 1.6% per gate, appearing in ancilla qubits that are not directly coupled to any of the 30 DACs on the chip. We discuss strategies for future layouts of multi-DAC chips that focus on mitigating the source of these non-local, high-frequency electromagnetic interactions (EMI), and how to incorporate a DC-tunable coupler for phase correction.

</details>


### [363] [Brownian Motion in Orthogonal and Symplectic Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05094)
*Zhiyang Tan,Piet W. Brouwer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对具有粒子-空穴对称性的正交和辛随机矩阵，建立了统一的图解框架来刻画布朗系综，计算了最高四阶的多项式平均，构建了正交群断开SO^-(q)扇区的不变插值，并探讨了在量子信息、量子混沌和量子输运中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵布朗运动是研究量子混沌中交叉系综、量子输运以及多体动力学中热化和信息 scrambling 的有力工具，但现有框架主要针对酉系综，缺乏对具有粒子-空穴对称性（正交和辛系综）的统一处理。

Method: 发展了一套统一的图解框架来刻画正交和辛随机矩阵的布朗系综；计算了最高四阶的多项式平均；构建了正交群断开SO^-(q)扇区的正交不变插值。

Result: 成功建立了适用于具有粒子-空穴对称性的布朗系综的图解方法，得到了低阶多项式平均的解析表达式，并给出了正交群特定扇区的不变插值构造。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了正交和辛系综的布朗运动描述，为量子信息、量子混沌和量子输运中的相关问题提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: Matrix Brownian motion provides a powerful framework for studying crossover ensembles in quantum chaos and quantum transport, as well as thermalization and information scrambling in many-body dynamics. Here, we develop a unified diagrammatic framework to characterize Brownian ensembles for orthogonal and symplectic random matrices, which describe systems with particle-hole symmetry. We compute polynomial averages up to fourth order and construct an orthogonally invariant interpolation for the disconnected $\mathrm{SO}^-(q)$ sector of the orthogonal group. We consider applications relating to the fields of quantum information, quantum chaos, and quantum transport.

</details>


### [364] [Complementary 3D color codes for transversal quantum logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05107)
*Friederike Butt,Luis Colmenarez,Erik Weilandt,Tom Peham,Robert Wille,Markus Müller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于四面体三维颜色码及其Hadamard变换码（H-四面体码）的混合架构，通过互补的横向非Clifford操作和比特级Hadamard变换实现近乎普适的横向逻辑门集，并利用分段容错的round-robin结构完成逻辑C-Z门以构建通用门集，该方案将资源开销集中在少量非横向Clifford纠缠操作上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eas tin-Knill定理禁止单个量子纠错码拥有普适的横向逻辑门集，因此需要寻找新的架构以突破这一限制，实现容错量子计算。

Method: 采用四面体三维颜色码与H-四面体码的混合编码，二者支持互补的横向非Clifford操作；通过比特级Hadamard变换在两种编码间切换，并结合从四面体码到H-四面体码的单向横向逻辑CNOT，实现近乎普适的横向逻辑门集；最后通过分段容错的round-robin结构构造两个H-四面体码之间的逻辑C-Z门，并利用二维颜色码逻辑辅助量子比特进行Steane型症候提取。

Result: 实现了近乎普适的横向逻辑门集，能够创建纠缠和魔法逻辑态；通用门集通过分段容错的round-robin逻辑C-Z门完成，大部分魔法和纠缠操作是横向的，资源开销集中在少量非横向Clifford纠缠操作上。

Conclusion: 该混合架构为在容错量子计算中实现经典难模拟的量子算法提供了新路径，有效降低了资源开销。

Abstract: Transversal logical gates provide a direct route to fault-tolerant quantum computation, but the Eastin-Knill theorem forbids a universal transversal gate set within a single quantum error-correcting code. We propose a hybrid architecture based on the tetrahedral three-dimensional color code and its Hadamard-transformed counterpart, which we call the H-tetrahedral code. The two encodings support complementary transversal non-Clifford operations. Combined with bitwise Hadamard transformations that switch between the two encodings and a one-way transversal logical CNOT from the tetrahedral code to the H-tetrahedral code, these operations realize an almost-universal transversal logical gate set that enables both the creation of entanglement and logical states with magic. We complete a universal gate set through a pieceably fault-tolerant round-robin construction of a logical controlled-$Z$ gate between two H-tetrahedral codes. This logical entangling gate is interleaved with reduced-overhead Steane-type syndrome extraction using logical two-dimensional color-code auxiliary qubits. Our construction provides a new route toward implementing classically hard-to-simulate quantum algorithms where magic and most entangling operations are transversal while the resource overhead is concentrated in a small number of non-transversal Clifford entangling operations.

</details>


### [365] [Characterisation of a satellite-to-ground channel for continuous variable quantum key distribution protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05109)
*Emma Tien Hwai Medlock,Vinod N. Roa,Timothy Spiller,Rupesh Kumar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了星基连续变量量子密钥分发（CV-QKD）中动态量子信道的损耗特性，分析了随天顶角变化的信道损耗对参数估计和散粒噪声的影响，并在实用假设下对不同湍流强度和波长进行了表征，结果表明在受限Eve安全假设下可以生成正的安全密钥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星基QKD协议中量子信道具有动态特性，信道损耗随天顶角变化，这会给CV-QKD的参数估计带来问题，特别是对于传输本地振荡器方案，还会导致散粒噪声波动，因此有必要对信道损耗及其来源进行表征。

Method: 在实用假设下对变化的信道损耗进行表征，考虑了不同场景、湍流强度以及波长条件。

Result: 对于所考虑的信道参数，在受限Eve安全假设下能够生成正的安全密钥。

Conclusion: 动态信道损耗虽对CV-QKD构成挑战，但在实用条件下仍然可以实现正向安全密钥生成。

Abstract: In space based quantum key distribution (QKD) protocols, the quantum channel will be dynamic in nature and the channel loss will change with respect to the zenith angle. In the context of continuous variable (CV)-QKD, this will cause issues with parameter estimation and for a transmitted local oscillator in particular it will also fluctuate the shot noise. Therefore, it is vital to characterise this channel loss and the sources of this loss. In this paper the varying channel loss is characterised under practical assumptions. This is shown for various different scenarios, turbulence strengths, as well as wavelengths. This work shows, for the channel parameters considered, it is possible to generate a positive secret key if restricted Eve security assumptions are made.

</details>


### [366] [Fragile single-cone Dirac quantum walks in two dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05112)
*C. W. J. Beenakker,J. Sánchez Férnan,J. Tworzydło*

Main category: quant-ph
