TL;DR: 该论文指出一维量子行走可实现无费米子加倍的质量less Dirac方程离散化，但二维类似构造更脆弱：基于Ho-Chalker网络模型的二维狄拉克量子行走的未配对狄拉克锥受非对称（非点式）对称性保护，这种对称性包含半格点平移，会被空间不均匀性破坏，势垒散射即可打开能隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索二维量子行走能否像一维那样实现无费米子加倍的质量less Dirac方程离散化，并检验其对称性保护机制的稳定性。

Method: 通过分析二维局域两带量子行走的对称性结构，特别是基于Ho-Chalker网络模型的狄拉克量子行走，研究其对称性性质及对空间不均匀性的响应。

Result: 二维局域两带量子行走可存在未配对的狄拉克锥，但保护对称性为非点式对称性（包含半格点平移），被空间不均匀性破坏；势垒散射即可使二维狄拉克量子行走打开能隙。

Conclusion: 二维量子行走的狄拉克锥稳定性远弱于一维，其对杂质散射更敏感，无法像一维情况那样鲁棒地实现无费米子加倍的低能费米子离散化。

Abstract: It is known that a one-dimensional (1D) quantum walk gives a local space-time discretization of the massless Dirac equation with a single quasi-energy cone (no fermion doubling at low energies), keeping the fundamental symmetries (chiral and time-reversal) of the continuum theory. We show that the analogous 2D construction is fundamentally more fragile. Local two-band quantum walks can have an unpaired Dirac cone, but the protecting symmetries then cease to be ordinary on-site symmetries: they become non-symmorphic, involving half-lattice translations, and are broken by generic spatial inhomogeneities. In particular, we demonstrate that the 2D Dirac quantum walk based on the Ho-Chalker network model can be gapped by potential scattering.

</details>


### [367] [Efficient classical simulation of two-dimensional long-range systems: Rydberg arrays and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05178)
*Jia-Lin Chan,Tao Xiang,Yantao Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的变分蒙特卡洛框架，将张量网络态计算局部能量的复杂度从O(N³)降至O(N)，成功模拟了此前无法经典模拟的10×10偶极XY模型绝热实时动力学，并解决了从乘积态演化的病态问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在变分蒙特卡洛（VMC）计算中，对具有任意全连接两体相互作用的N位点量子系统，评估局部能量的计算成本通常高达O(N³)，严重限制了可模拟的系统规模。此外，张量网络VMC中从乘积态演化存在病态问题亟待解决。

Method: 提出一种新框架，利用张量网络态的结构特性将局部能量计算复杂度降至O(N)；解决了从乘积态演化的病理问题。

Result: 成功模拟了此前经典方法无法企及的10×10偶极XY模型绝热实时动力学（Rydberg模拟器实验对照）；实现了基态VMC计算并与绝热态制备进行对比。

Conclusion: 张量网络VMC可作为长程量子平台（如Rydberg和离子阱模拟器）的高效经典基准测试工具，填补了可扩展经典模拟方法的迫切需求。

Abstract: In variational Monte Carlo (VMC) calculations of $N$-site quantum systems with arbitrary all-to-all two-body interactions, evaluating the local energy generally costs $O(N^3)$. We introduce a new framework that reduces this cost to $O(N)$ for tensor network states, capable of scalable and accurate computation of real-time dynamics and ground states. As a result, we obtain accurate simulations of the adiabatic real-time protocol of a $10\times10$ dipolar XY model realized in a Rydberg simulator [C. Chen et al., Nature 616, 691 (2023)], which was previously beyond the reach of classical simulation. Going beyond quantum experiments, we also directly perform ground state VMC to compare with the adiabatic state preparation. Our work demonstrates tensor network VMC as a powerful classical simulator for long-range quantum platforms such as Rydberg and ion-trap simulators, which are currently in urgent need of scalable classical benchmarking tools. As a separate technical contribution, we resolve the pathology of evolving from product states within of tensor network VMC.

</details>


### [368] [Quasi-holonomy in non-adiabatic quantum evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05218)
*Erik Sjöqvist,Adam Fredriksson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了非绝热子空间量子时间演化中拟和乐（quasi-holonomy）的理论框架。通过将薛定谔演化分解为动力学部分和移动基矢下的联络诱导部分，得到了依赖于动力学传播子的有效几何生成元。该拟联络不构成原始格拉斯曼丛上的真正联络，因其规范变换律包含历史依赖的非定域项。研究表明子空间的几何与动力学效应通常相互纠缠，无法彻底分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从规范场论视角澄清量子子空间演化中几何（和乐）部分与动力学部分的分离问题，揭示非绝热情形下拟和乐的本质。

Method: 将薛定谔演化因子分解为动力学传播子和移动基矢下的联络诱导贡献，构造有效几何生成元；分析拟联络的规范变换性质并讨论其他分解方式。

Result: 拟联络不满足丛上真正联络的规范变换律，其变换包含历史依赖的非定域项；所有因子分解方式均存在类似的历史依赖性，导致几何与动力学效应不可彻底分离。

Conclusion: 非绝热子空间演化中，几何与动力学效应一般相互纠缠，无法通过简单的因子分解实现完全分离，这对理解和乐量子计算等应用具有重要启示。

Abstract: We develop a framework for quasi-holonomy in non-adiabatic quantum time evolution of subspaces along loops in a complex Grassmannian. By factoring the Schrödinger evolution into dynamical and connection-induced contributions in a moving basis, we obtain an effective geometric generator that depends explicitly on the dynamical propagator. This quasi-connection does not define a genuine connection on the original Grassmann bundle, since its gauge transformation law acquires a history-dependent, nonlocal term. Other ways of factoring the Schrödinger evolution are briefly discussed. All these approaches suffer from the same type of history-dependence, thereby defining transport of subspaces in which geometric and dynamical effects are generally intertwined, just as in the case of the quasi-holonomy. Our work sheds light on the issue of separating quantum evolution of subspaces into holonomic and dynamical parts from an essentially gauge-theoretic perspective.

</details>


### [369] [Routing Anonymity and Identifiability of Noisy Quantum Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05281)
*Ben Priestley,Mina Doosti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次为量子云计算中的后端可识别性及其隐私影响建立了正式框架，证明路由匿名性以Chernoff速率指数衰减，并揭示了效用-匿名性权衡以及指纹作为中等深度现象的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子云计算中，用户提交的电路在服务商专有后端硬件上执行。服务商希望隐藏实现细节，但噪声有限次输出会携带后端特有的指纹信息，可能泄露后端身份。此前研究主要从基准测试角度关注此类指纹，缺乏对用户和提供者隐私的系统性考虑。

Method: 1. 提出后端可识别性游戏，将路由匿名性形式化为量子云服务的安全概念；2. 将后端可识别性建模为假设检验问题，证明被动i.i.d.访问下单后端路由匿名性以Chernoff速率指数衰减；3. 建立效用-匿名性权衡的理论界限；4. 利用Pauli传输矩阵工具揭示噪声量子硬件中的指纹是中等深度现象；5. 在Amazon Braket上的离子阱和超导量子处理器上进行实验验证。

Result: 实验结果显示：超导后端间分类准确率87-90%，跨物理平台（离子阱vs超导）分类准确率96-100%，且可识别性在自然后处理下仍能保持。

Conclusion: 路由匿名性是量子云计算中独立的安全需求，本文提供了量化与控制效用-匿名性权衡的理论框架。

Abstract: Present-day quantum computing is cloud-based, where a user submits a circuit to a service provider's proprietary backend hardware. While providers may wish to hide implementation details, scheduling choices, or even which physical device was used, noisy finite-shot outputs can carry backend-specific fingerprints: information imprinted in the classical output distribution that can reveal the backend identity. So far, such fingerprints have mostly been studied from a benchmarking perspective, with limited attention to privacy considerations for users and providers. This work develops the first formal framework for backend identifiability and its privacy implications. We introduce a backend-identifiability game and use it to formalise routing anonymity as a security notion for quantum cloud services. We show that backend identifiability is a hypothesis-testing problem and prove that, under passive i.i.d. access to a single backend, routing anonymity decays exponentially at the Chernoff rate. We also establish a utility-anonymity trade-off, imposing fundamental limits on how much backend-specific information can be removed from classical outputs without degrading their usefulness. In addition, we observe that, for noisy quantum hardware, identifying fingerprints are inherently an intermediate-depth phenomenon, and establish a depth principle using Pauli-transfer-matrix tools. We complement the theory with experiments on Amazon Braket on AWS, using ion-trap and superconducting quantum processors. We observe 87-90% classification between superconducting backends and 96-100% classification across physical platforms, and find that identifiability can survive natural forms of post-processing. Overall, these results establish routing anonymity as a distinct security requirement for quantum cloud computing, and provide a framework for quantifying and controlling the utility-anonymity trade-off.

</details>


### [370] [Quantum Spectral Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05307)
*Yewei Yuan,Michele Minervini,Mark M. Wilde,Nana Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子谱异常检测(QSPADE)，通过直接计算正常数据集平均态的谱来得到类似PCA的异常分数，避免了显式恢复主特征向量、构建完整Gram矩阵或加载QRAM数据的高成本，并采用平滑的温度控制谱阈值替代硬PCA秩选择，使异常分数连续变化且对噪声不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子异常检测中，传统PCA方法需要数据居中、显式恢复主特征向量或构建完整Gram矩阵，这些操作对于量子数据成本高昂，且量子数据缺乏标准居中对齐方式。

Method: QSPADE通过计算正常数据集的平均态的谱直接获得PCA类异常分数，用平滑的温度控制谱阈值替代硬PCA秩选择，使近阈值谱分量部分贡献于异常分数。在零温极限下恢复硬投影PCA分数。

Result: 数值模拟表明QSPADE行为类似于编码经典数据上的核PCA，能检测横向场伊辛转变而无需预设序参量。可校准的样本复杂度与数据维度无关。

Conclusion: QSPADE为编码经典数据的量子核异常检测和诊断观测量未知的量子原生系统监测提供了一种高效框架。

Abstract: A core task in quantum anomaly detection is to compute an anomaly score that quantifies how strongly a test quantum state deviates from a given quantum dataset assumed to be normal. Classically, principal component analysis (PCA) for centered data computes the anomaly score by evaluating the test sample relative to the subspace spanned by the selected leading eigenvectors. However, for quantum data that lack a standard centering, explicitly recovering principal eigenvectors, constructing full Gram matrices, or loading quantum-random-access-memory-style data can be more costly than estimating the anomaly score itself. To avoid these costs, we propose Quantum Spectral Anomaly Detection (QSPADE), which computes PCA-like anomaly scores directly from the spectrum of the average state of the normal dataset. By replacing hard PCA rank selection with a smooth, temperature-controlled spectral threshold, QSPADE makes near-threshold spectral components contribute partially to the anomaly score. This makes the score vary continuously rather than jump when a borderline component is included or excluded, and makes it less sensitive to noise or arbitrary hard cutoffs near the threshold. In the zero-temperature limit, QSPADE recovers the hard-projector PCA score. The proposed measurement-based quantum detector can be calibrated with a sample complexity independent of the data dimension. Numerical simulations show that QSPADE behaves like kernel-PCA on encoded classical data and detects changes across a transverse-field Ising transition without predefined order parameters. Consequently, QSPADE gives an efficient framework for both quantum-kernel anomaly detection on encoded classical data and the monitoring of quantum-native systems where diagnostic observables are unknown.

</details>


### [371] [Quantum Computational Resources and Conformal Field Theory: Unifying Spins, Bosons, and Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05343)
*Ryota Matsuda,Masahiro Hoshino,Yuto Ashida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一度量——魔法Rényi熵(MRE)，用于在自旋、玻色子和费米子系统中量化计算资源（非稳定性和非高斯性）。通过共形场论分析，发现MRE的普适贡献由Affleck-Ludwig边界熵决定，并揭示了非高斯性可通过边界重整化群流连续重整化这一普适贡献或驱动边界相变。以Tomonaga-Luttinger液体为例，给出了解析和数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自旋系统中，CFT对稳定子Rényi熵的分析已揭示非稳定性的普适特征，但玻色子和费米子系统中尚缺乏类似的计算资源（非高斯性）的统一框架。本文旨在建立一种跨系统的统一度量，揭示临界多体态中非稳定性和非高斯性的共同普适方面。

Method: 引入魔法Rényi熵(MRE)作为统一度量，涵盖自旋、玻色子和费米子系统。利用共形场论分析MRE的标度行为，将普适贡献与Affleck-Ludwig边界熵关联。通过Tomonaga-Luttinger液体中的相互作用无自旋费米子进行具体CFT分析，并结合数值计算验证。

Result: MRE的普适贡献表现为与系统大小无关的项，由Affleck-Ludwig边界熵决定。在Tomonaga-Luttinger液体中，发现在Luttinger参数K=1/3和K=3处发生边界相变。数值结果与场论预言一致。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个统一的场论框架，用以理解自旋、玻色子和费米子系统中多体魔法的普适行为，揭示了非高斯性对边界熵的重整化效应和边界相变现象。

Abstract: Characterizing a quantum state through the lens of quantum resources provides an information-theoretic perspective on many-body systems. While quantum entanglement serves as the paradigmatic example of a quantum resource, recent studies have shown that quantum magic, a resource for universal quantum computation, can capture aspects of many-body states complementary to those described by entanglement. For instance, in spin systems, conformal field theory (CFT) analysis of the stabilizer Rényi entropy has revealed universal features of nonstabilizerness that are qualitatively distinct from entanglement. In bosonic and fermionic systems, however, a comparable formulation for their computational resource, non-Gaussianity, has yet to be established. In this work, we introduce a unified measure, the magic Rényi entropy (MRE), to quantify computational resources in spins, bosons, and fermions on an equal footing. This allows us to reveal common universal aspects of nonstabilizerness and non-Gaussianity in critical many-body states. In particular, our CFT analysis shows that the universal contribution to the MRE appears as the size-independent term determined by the Affleck-Ludwig boundary entropy. We find that non-Gaussianity can continuously renormalize this universal contribution or drive a boundary phase transition through bulk-induced boundary renormalization-group flows. As a concrete demonstration, we present a detailed CFT analysis of non-Gaussianity in interacting spinless fermions described by the Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid, showing boundary transitions at the Luttinger parameters $K=1/3$ and $K=3$. We perform numerical calculations that confirm our field-theoretical predictions. These results provide a unified field-theoretical understanding of many-body magic across spins, bosons, and fermions.

</details>


### [372] [Coherent Control of Energy Transport at Room Temperature in a Noisy Bath](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05361)
*Davinder Singh,Paul Brumer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在非平衡稳态（NESS）下，通过相位可控的相干场对反应中心连接的供体-受体对进行能量传输的相干控制方法，利用激发路径间的干涉效应实现能量通量的增强或抑制，展示了在耗散环境（如室温）中实现量子控制的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子控制从传统瞬态、低耗散领域拓展到噪声环境中非平衡稳态下的室温操作，实现光学能量开关等应用。

Method: 将色素系统与连续非相干辐射及声子浴相互作用，同时施加相位可控的相干场驱动供体-受体对，利用激发路径间的量子干涉效应调节能量通量。

Result: 相干激发诱导的路径干涉产生相位依赖的能量通量调制，可在耗散性NESS下实现能量的增强或抑制（如光学能量开关）。

Conclusion: 在耗散环境（如室温）的非平衡稳态下，量子相干控制仍可保持有效，从而扩展了量子控制的操作范围。

Abstract: Coherent control of energy transport in a non-equilibrium steady-state (NESS) in a reaction-center-connected donor-acceptor pair is proposed. The pigments are considered to be continuously interacting with incoherent radiation and a phonon bath while being driven by phase-controlled coherent fields. Coherent excitation of the donor-acceptor pair is shown to induce interference between excitation pathways, resulting in phase dependent modulation of the flux. As a consequence one can enhance or suppress energy transfer via interference, e.g. an optical energy switch. The persistence of such interference enables coherent control at a NESS in dissipative regime suggests an extension of the operational scope of quantum control from traditional transient domain with low dissiaption to noisy environment NESS at room temperature.

</details>


### [373] [Logical Spectroscopy: Lifted-Product Codes with Addressable Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05386)
*Jong Yeon Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"逻辑光谱学"方法，利用Frobenius特征包和CRT分解，为有限阿贝尔提升积量子LDPC码构造显式的、可寻址的共轭逻辑算子基，同时提供扇区诊断、宽度上界和擦除分析等设计工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图积码的逻辑结构透明因其输入矩阵在域上可行约简；但阿贝尔提升积码的种子条目是群代数环中的移位，全局行约简失效，需要新方法构造显式共轭逻辑算子。

Method: 利用CRT将群代数分解为Frobenius特征包；在每个包内进行有限域上的核、商及乘积复同调计算；通过CRT幂等元提升回原空间，并通过互逆迹对偶包配对X和Z逻辑算子。

Result: 获得了有限阿贝尔提升积LP(A,B)的完整可寻址共轭逻辑基。包秩揭示逻辑扇区分裂，提升代表给出基宽度的确定性上界，整轨道擦除可分解为包属性擦除逻辑维数。

Conclusion: CRT特征包不仅提供逻辑算子的构造坐标，还作为扇区标签、基宽度认证和擦除分析的工作坐标系，使阿贝尔提升积码兼具超图积码的透明性和群代数提升的布局自由度。

Abstract: Quantum LDPC memories can encode many logical qubits, but dimension alone does not make them usable: applications need explicit conjugate logical operators with structured labels and physical representatives. For hypergraph-product (HGP) codes this structure is transparent, since the input matrices are binary and can be row-reduced over $\mathbb{F}_2$. Abelian lifted-product codes are subtler. Their seed entries are shifts, or sparse sums of shifts, in a group-algebra ring rather than a field, so pivot blocks need not be invertible and global row reduction can fail.
  We address this with \emph{logical spectroscopy}, a spectral construction that replaces global row reduction by finite-field computations in the Frobenius character packets of the Abelian lift group. The Chinese remainder theorem (CRT) decomposes the group algebra into these packets. In each packet, we compute kernels, quotients, and product-complex homology; we then lift the resulting representatives back with CRT idempotents and pair $X$ and $Z$ logicals through reciprocal trace-dual packets. This gives complete addressable conjugate logical bases for finite Abelian lifted products $\mathsf{LP}(A,B)$.
  The same packet data also gives design diagnostics. Packet ranks show how logical sectors split, the lifted representatives give certified upper bounds on the width of the constructed conjugate basis, and whole-orbit erasures decompose into packet-attributed erased-logical dimensions. Thus, CRT packets also serve as working coordinates: they label logical sectors, certify the constructed basis width, and attribute structured erasure failures. Under bounded seed-shape and group-basis-support assumptions, this construction gives Abelian lifted-product qLDPC families an HGP-like feature while preserving the layout freedom of group-algebra lifts.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [374] [Global Distinctions Between New Electrovacuum and Kundt Class](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02602)
*Liang Ma,H. Lu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文分析了两类局部等价但全局性质不同的引力解：一类是电磁场支持的电真空解，另一类是由(加速)电磁荷产生的非电真空Petrov D型解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究局部等价的引力解在全局结构上的本质差异，理解电真空解与荷电生成解之间的物理区别。

Method: 对比分析两种局部等价解在全局拓扑、渐进结构、奇点性质等方面的差异。

Result: 揭示了两种解虽然局部等价，但在全局性质（如完整性、渐进结构）上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 局部等价的引力解可能具有根本不同的全局性质，需谨慎对待局部变换保持的等价性。

Abstract: It was observed [2606.30426] that our recently constructed electrovacuum [2606.23782], supported by electromagnetic fields, can be locally transformed to a special Petrov type D solution within the general Kundt class, which is not an electrovacuum but instead describes a spacetime generated by (accelerating) electric and magnetic charges. In this short note, which serves as a supplemental to [2606.23782], we analyse the major global differences between the two locally-equivalent solutions.

</details>


### [375] [Observational Constraints on Kazakov-Solodukhin Quantum-Deformed Black Holes from M87$^*$ and Sgr A$^*$ Shadows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02631)
*A. Errehymy,Y. Khedif,M. Daoud,B. Turimov,S. Usanov,Z. Yasakov,Z. Avezmuratova*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了Kazakov-Solodukhin量子变形黑洞时空，该模型通过单一变形参数η编码对经典Schwarzschild解的量子修正。核心发现包括：曲率不变量在最小半径r=η处保持有限，实现经典奇点的正则化；视界位置、表面引力、霍金温度及热力学稳定性均被修正；光子球位移和强引力透镜特征改变，阴影尺寸对η敏感；弱场偏转角获得η²阶二次修正。该模型在理论上吸引人且可观测检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子引力效应对经典黑洞解的影响，特别是通过Kazakov-Solodukhin模型解决经典广义相对论中的奇点问题，并寻找可观测检验的量子引力信号。

Method: 基于单一变形参数η的Kazakov-Solodukhin度规，通过分析曲率不变量、视界结构、热力学量（表面重力、霍金温度）、光子轨道动力学和引力透镜效应，系统研究量子变形对黑洞时空的修正。

Result: 曲率不变量在r=η处有限，奇点被正则化；视界位置和质量-半径关系改变；表面重力降低导致霍金温度下降和蒸发减缓；光子球位移且阴影尺寸依赖于η；弱场偏转角获得η²阶修正；可通过观测约束给出变形参数上限。

Conclusion: Kazakov-Solodukhin量子变形黑洞模型在保留正确广义相对论极限的同时，在强场区域引入可观测的量子修正，有效解决奇点问题，并通过阴影和透镜效应提供可检验的预言，是理论与观测衔接的候选量子引力模型。

Abstract: We explore the Kazakov-Solodukhin quantum-deformed black hole spacetime, characterized by a single deformation parameter \( η\) that encodes quantum corrections to the classical Schwarzschild solution. The model preserves the correct general-relativistic limit as \( η\to 0 \), while introducing significant and physically meaningful deviations in the strong-field regime. A central and remarkable feature of the geometry is the regularization of the classical singularity: curvature invariants remain finite near the minimal radius \( r = η\), effectively replacing the divergent core with a smooth and well-behaved region. This behavior naturally introduces a minimal length scale into the spacetime structure, offering a geometrically motivated resolution of the singularity problem. The deformation modifies the horizon structure, shifts the event horizon location, and alters the mass-radius relation. It also reduces the surface gravity, leading to a lower Hawking temperature and a slower evaporation process, thereby enhancing the thermodynamic stability of the black hole. Photon dynamics are correspondingly affected, resulting in a displaced photon sphere and modified strong-lensing characteristics. While the shadow remains perfectly circular due to spherical symmetry, its size depends sensitively on \( η\). Observational constraints can be expressed through \( \left| R_{sh}(η) - R_{obs} \right| \leq ΔR_{obs}, \) which places an upper bound on the deformation parameter. In the weak-field limit, the deflection angle acquires a quadratic correction proportional to \( η^2 \), ensuring consistency with precision tests while allowing potentially detectable deviations in strong-gravity observations. These features make the model both theoretically appealing and observationally testable.

</details>


### [376] [Interacting Holographic Dark Energy in $f(Q)$ Gravity: Cosmological Evolution and Gravitational Wave Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02792)
*N. Amiriborkhani,Alireza Amani,M. A. Ramzanpour*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在修正引力 \(f(Q)\) 框架下研究了交互式全息暗能量（HDE）模型，通过参数化哈勃参数重建场方程，利用观测数据 \(H(z)\) 约束模型参数，分析了暗能量在宇宙历史中的动力学行为，并研究了引力波在宇宙学背景下的传播特性。结果表明该模型在描述宇宙加速膨胀和引力波宇宙学后果方面具有一致性和可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在修正引力 \(f(Q)\) 框架下构建交互式全息暗能量模型，以描述宇宙加速膨胀并探索与标准宇宙学不同的引力波信号特征。

Method: 采用哈勃参数的幂律参数化形式重建 \(f(Q)\) 引力下的场方程；利用 \(H(z)\) 观测数据对模型参数进行约束；通过声速分析评估经典稳定性；研究张量扰动的演化以分析引力波传播特点。

Result: 暗能量在晚期宇宙自然趋近于宇宙学常数行为，而在过去呈现不同的动力学演化；声速分析表明模型在当前时期具有良好的经典稳定性；物质和暗能量的相对密度参数与观测约束相容；\(f(Q)\) 引力和物质-暗能量相互作用可在张量扰动演化中产生不同于标准场景的特征信号。

Conclusion: 交互式全息暗能量在 \(f(Q)\) 修正引力框架下能够为宇宙加速膨胀提供一致的理论描述，并为引力波的宇宙学后果研究提供新的视角。

Abstract: In this paper, an interactive Holographic Dark Energy (HDE) model is studied in the framework of modified gravity \(f(Q)\). By adopting a power parameterization for the Hubble parameter, the field equations are reconstructed and the evolution of the universe at the background level and tensor perturbations are investigated. Then, using observational data \(H(z)\), the model parameters are constrained and the dynamical behavior of dark energy throughout the history of the universe is analyzed. Also, the study of the evolution of energy density, pressure and the Equation of State (EoS) parameter of dark energy shows that dark energy in the late universe naturally tends to a region close to the cosmological constant behavior, while in the past it followed a distinct dynamical evolution. Stability analysis based on the speed of sound also indicates that the model has good classical stability around the present era. In addition, the compatibility of the current values of the relative density parameters of matter and dark energy with the observational constraints confirms the ability of the model to reproduce the main features of the observed universe. Next, the propagation of gravitational waves in the cosmological context of the model is investigated. The results show that the corrections due to \(f(Q)\) gravity and the interaction between matter and dark energy can affect the evolution of tensor perturbations and produce signatures distinct from the standard scenario. Overall, the findings of this study indicate that the interactive HDE in the gravitational framework \(f(Q)\) can provide a consistent framework for describing the cosmic acceleration and studying the cosmological consequences of gravitational waves.

</details>


### [377] [Distinguishing wormholes via Einstein rings and global curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02889)
*M. B. Cruz,C. R. Muniz,R. M. P. Neves,Jonathan A. Rebouças*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了嵌入弯曲FLRW宇宙中的静态Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞的引力透镜特性，发现虫洞爱因斯坦环半径与宇宙学距离呈三次方标度关系（不同于史瓦西黑洞的平方根行为），并揭示了局域虫洞几何与FLRW背景之间的非对称响应。虫洞的透镜效率远低于同等尺度的史瓦西黑洞，微角秒级爱因斯坦环需要宏观尺度的喉部半径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为虫洞和黑洞透镜场景提供模型无关的几何诊断方法，以区分两者，并探索虫洞透镜信号作为宇宙学探针的可行性。

Method: 采用曲率依赖的宇宙学距离，推导弯曲FLRW背景下的弱场透镜方程；通过数值分析研究局域虫洞几何与FLRW背景的相互作用，分析空间曲率的非对称响应及其红移演化。

Result: 虫洞爱因斯坦环半径呈三次方标度（区别于黑洞的平方根标度）；空间曲率响应在中红移处发生反转且具有非可忽略的敏感性；虫洞透镜效率显著低于史瓦西黑洞。

Conclusion: 如果存在宇宙学虫洞族群，其透镜信号可作为探测奇异时空拓扑和宇宙总体几何的灵敏互补探针。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the gravitational lensing properties of a static Ellis-Bronnikov wormhole embedded in a curved Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker (FLRW) universe. By employing curvature-dependent cosmological distances, we derive the corresponding weak-field lens equation and demonstrate that the wormhole Einstein ring radius follows a characteristic cubic scaling with cosmological distances, in sharp contrast to the square-root behavior found for Schwarzschild black holes. This distinct scaling leads to a qualitatively different redshift evolution of the lensing signal, providing a model-independent geometric diagnostic to discriminate between wormhole and black hole lensing scenarios. Numerical analysis reveals that the interplay between the local wormhole geometry and the FLRW background produces an asymmetric response to spatial curvature that inverts at intermediate redshifts, exhibiting a non-negligible sensitivity even under tight modern constraints such as those from DESI 2024. We also find that Ellis-Bronnikov wormholes are substantially less efficient gravitational lenses than Schwarzschild black holes of comparable physical scale, implying that microarcsecond-scale Einstein rings require macroscopic throat radii. These results suggest that, should a population of cosmological wormholes exist, their lensing signatures could provide a sensitive, complementary probe of both exotic spacetime topology and the global geometry of the Universe.

</details>


### [378] [Decoherence Effects on Primordial Black Holes and Scalar-Induced Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02935)
*Waqas Ahmed*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种保守扩展的原初黑洞(PBH)和标量诱导引力波(SIGW)框架，利用高斯量子失谐作为残余量子关联的诊断工具，而非PBH形成的新判据。通过林德blad开放系统描述，引入高斯损失通道来刻画退相干效应，发现退相干会通过协方差抑制因子Q_dec(k)影响PBH丰度并修正SIGW能谱，最清晰的量子-经典转变印记出现在标量诱导引力波中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原初黑洞产生的扰动起源于暴涨真空的量子涨落，但现有框架缺乏对量子关联残余效应的定量描述。作者希望发展一套方法，用量子信息工具（特别是量子失谐）诊断产生PBH的标量扇区中残留的量子关联，并研究退相干效应如何影响PBH丰度和引力波信号。

Method: 将每个(k,-k)动量对描述为双模高斯态；在纯压缩极限下计算量子失谐随压缩参数的快速增长行为；利用林德blad开放系统方程引入高斯损失通道描述退相干；定义协方差生存因子Q_dec(k)区分失谐与退相干效应；推导退相干对标量两点协方差和SIGW功率谱的修正公式。

Result: 纯压缩极限下低失谐阈值自动满足；混合态（退相干）是导致非平凡行为的来源；退相干通道通过Q_dec(k)抑制标量两点协方差，从而影响PBH形成所需的经典塌缩方差；SIGW谱受Q_dec(ku)Q_dec(kv)乘积修正，对于窄标量峰且Q_dec缓变情形，基准标度为Ω_GW^eff ≃ Q_dec^2 Ω_GW^class。

Conclusion: 量子失谐和退相干为表征PBH产生扰动的量子-经典转变提供了一种受控途径，最清晰的印记出现在标量诱导引力波中。这项研究为连接量子引力理论、早期宇宙宇宙学和引力波观测提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Primordial black holes (PBHs) form when large primordial curvature perturbations re-enter the Hubble radius and exceed the classical collapse threshold. These perturbations originate as quantum fluctuations of the inflationary vacuum, motivating a quantum-information description of the PBH-producing scalar sector. We develop a conservative extension of the standard PBH and scalar-induced gravitational-wave (SIGW) framework in which Gaussian quantum discord is used as a diagnostic of residual quantum correlations, not as a new PBH-formation criterion. We describe each \((\bm k,-\bm k)\) pair as a two-mode Gaussian state and show that, in the pure squeezed limit, discord grows rapidly with the squeezing parameter, so low discord thresholds are automatically satisfied for strongly squeezed modes. The nontrivial regime is the mixed state produced by decoherence. Using a Lindblad open-system description, we motivate a Gaussian loss channel for the scalar covariance matrix and distinguish the discord from a covariance-survival factor \(Q_{\rm dec}(k)\). If the decoherence channel suppresses the scalar two-point covariance, PBH abundance can be affected through the classical collapse variance, while the SIGW spectrum is modified more directly by the factors \(Q_{\rm dec}(ku)Q_{\rm dec}(kv)\) inside the radiation-era convolution. For a narrow scalar peak and slowly varying \(Q_{\rm dec}\), this gives the benchmark scaling \(Ω_{\rm GW}^{\rm eff}\simeq Q_{\rm dec}^2Ω_{\rm GW}^{\rm class}\). Thus quantum discord and decoherence provide a controlled way to characterize the quantum-to-classical transition of PBH-producing perturbations, with the clearest imprint appearing in scalar-induced gravitational waves.

</details>


### [379] [Black holes in f(R) theory of gravity with compact extra dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03034)
*Arkady Popov,Sergey Rubin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究 $f(R)$ 引力理论中 $D=4+n$ 维时空的静态球对称解，其中额外维度紧化为恒定半径的 $n$ 维球面。四维部分对应于Schwarzschild-de Sitter度规，但内部空间尺度与有效四维宇宙学常数 $\Lambda_4 = (n-1)/L_0^2$ 之间的约束关系导致 $\Lambda_4$ 过大而与观测不符。通过引入非最小耦合量子场的真空极化效应（半经典方法），可得到渐近平坦的四维Schwarzschild解作为极限情形，此时额外维半径由量子场的真空期望值决定。最后讨论了有效四维普朗克质量和额外维半径对径向坐标的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 $f(R)$ 高维引力框架下，寻找能稳定额外维度的静态球对称解，并解决由此导致的宇宙学常数过大问题，使理论预测与观测相符。

Method: 在 $D=4+n$ 维 $f(R)$ 引力中假设额外维紧化为恒定半径的 $n$ 维球面，求解场方程得到精确解；引入非最小耦合量子场的真空极化效应，采用半经典方法处理。

Result: 获得四维部分为Schwarzschild-de Sitter度规的精确解，额外维稳定在 $L_0$；但一致性条件给出 $\Lambda_4 = (n-1)/L_0^2$，数值过大。加入量子真空极化后，可得到渐近平坦的Schwarzschild解，额外维半径由量子真空期望值决定。

Conclusion: $f(R)$ 高维引力中，量子真空极化效应可有效修正经典解中的宇宙学常数问题，实现额外维的稳定化并获得与观测兼容的四维渐近平坦引力解；有效普朗克质量和额外维半径随径向坐标变化。

Abstract: We study static, spherically symmetric solutions in $f(R)$ gravity within a $D = 4+n$-dimensional spacetime, where the extra dimensions form a compact $n$-sphere of constant radius. We derive an exact solution in which the four-dimensional part of the metric corresponds to the Schwarzschild - de Sitter metric, while the extra dimensions are stabilized at a constant radius $L_0$. A consistency condition relates the size of the internal space to the effective four-dimensional cosmological constant $Λ_4 = (n-1)/L_0^2$, which is generally too large to be compatible with observations. To overcome this issue, we include the vacuum polarization effects of quantized matter fields nonminimally coupled to curvature. Using the semiclassical approach, we obtain an asymptotically flat four-dimensional Schwarzschild solution as a limiting case, where the size of the extra sphere is determined by the vacuum expectation values of the quantum fields. Finally, we discuss the dependence of the effective four-dimensional Planck mass and the extra-dimensional radius on the radial coordinate.

</details>


### [380] [Conformal symmetries and MOTS stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03128)
*Abbas M. Sherif*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究时空中的类空叶状结构上的MOTS（边缘闭类空曲面），证明在温和能量条件下，与过去指向共形Killing向量场相交的MOTS是严格稳定的，并光滑演化为类空视界；同时给出了MOTS稳定性的判定条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在广义相对论中，类空超曲面上的MOTS在特定向量场条件下的稳定性与演化行为，为视界形成和黑洞物理提供理论基础。

Method: 利用MOTS的稳定性分析，结合过去指向共形Killing向量场的性质、散度条件以及能量条件，通过几何分析推导稳定性结论。

Result: (1) 在温和能量条件下，若MOTS与过去指向共形Killing向量场（位于MOTS的法空间中）相交，则MOTS严格稳定且光滑演化为类空视界；(2) 若向量场散度在MOTS上的限制非负，则MOTS不稳定；若为负且MOTS为2-球面，则MOTS严格稳定。

Conclusion: 建立了MOTS稳定性与共形Killing向量场、散度条件及拓扑之间的联系，为理解黑洞视界的生成与稳定性提供了新的几何判据。

Abstract: Let $\{Σ_t\}$ be a spacelike foliation of a spacetime $\mathcal{M}$, and suppose each $Σ_t$ is foliated by 2-surfaces $\mathcal{S}$ with spacelike unit normal in $Σ_t$. We show that under mild energy conditions, a MOTS $\mathcal{S}$ that intersects integral curves of past-pointing conformal Killing vector field lying in the normal space of $\mathcal{S}$ is strictly stable and evolves smoothly to a spacelike horizon. We also show that if the restriction of the divergence of the vector field to $\mathcal{S}$ is non-negative, $\mathcal{S}$ is unstable, and if negative and $\mathcal{S}$ is a 2-sphere, $\mathcal{S}$ must be strictly stable.

</details>


### [381] [Odd-parity ringdown gravitational waves of a spherically symmetric black hole with perfect fluid accretion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03231)
*Rikuto Ohashi,Yasutaka Koga,Hiroyuki Nakano,Kota Ogasawara,Chul-Moon Yoo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究在稀薄稳态球对称完美流体吸积下，史瓦西黑洞的奇偶极环信号。在一阶吸积率下，通过在时域直接计算波形，发现频率虚部与实部之比消除了黑洞增长导致的长期变化和红移因子，其偏离史瓦西值的部分纯粹反映环境；而频率的时间依赖性反映吸积率，可作为第二个可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际黑洞周围存在物质分布，使背景时空动态化，环信号频率随时间变化。传统环信号分析通常假设静态背景，忽略了吸积等环境效应，作者希望探究物质吸积如何影响环信号，并定义新的可观测参数来约束黑洞周围环境。

Method: 采用奇偶极微扰理论，在史瓦西黑洞背景下引入稀薄稳态球对称完美流体吸积（一阶吸积率近似）。由于奇偶极物质扰动与度规扰动解耦，波模式可描述为在吸积背景上的纯张量模式。直接在时域计算环信号波形。

Result: 频率虚部与实部之比（品质因子相关量）消除了黑洞质量增长引起的长期变化和红移效应，其偏离纯史瓦西值的部分纯粹由周围环境引起；频率的时间依赖性则直接反映吸积率。这两个可观测量可同时从观测中提取。

Conclusion: 测量这些可观测量的多模信息，可能为约束黑洞周围环境提供重要手段，有助于区分不同的吸积模型和环境密度分布。

Abstract: The ringdown waves from a black hole offer a clean probe of strong-field gravity, but a matter distribution that may be present around a realistic black hole renders the background spacetime dynamical and the ringdown frequencies time-dependent. We study the odd-parity ringdown of a Schwarzschild black hole that grows through the dilute, steady, spherically symmetric accretion of a perfect fluid. Working to first order in the accretion rate, we compute the ringdown waveform directly in the time domain on this dynamical background. Since the odd-parity matter perturbation decouples from the metric perturbation, the wave mode can be described by a purely tensorial mode on the accreting background. In particular, the ratio of the imaginary to the real part of the frequency cancels both the secular variation caused by the growth of the black hole and the redshift factor, so that its deviation from the Schwarzschild value purely reflects the surrounding environment. The time dependence of the frequency, on the other hand, reflects the accretion rate and allows us to define a second observable tied to it. We argue that measuring these observables across multiple modes may provide significant information to constrain the surrounding environment of the black hole.

</details>


### [382] [Dark energy genesis: modeling dissipative effects in primordial cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03272)
*Pietro Pellecchia,Alejandro Perez,Salvatore Ribisi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在单模引力框架下，通过引入量子引力缺陷作为有效热库，发展了一种由耗散导致变宇宙学常数的有效宇宙学模型，研究表明从无暗能量的原初宇宙出发，耗散可以产生一个小的正宇宙学常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力理论认为时空几何从普朗克尺度的离散结构中涌现，这些结构可能对光滑有效系统产生耗散效应。本文旨在探索引力中非守恒的物理机制，建立耗散与宇宙学常数产生之间的联系。

Method: 在单模引力框架下引入隐藏自由度（量子引力缺陷）作为物质场的有效热库，类比Caldeira-Leggett布朗运动模型，采用欧姆热库假设，导出物质能量密度的扩散方程。

Result: 从无暗能量的原初宇宙出发，耗散效应可以产生一个小的正宇宙学常数。

Conclusion: 耗散机制能够解释暗能量的动态起源，量子引力缺陷作为有效热库的框架具有可行性，为理解宇宙学常数的产生提供了新的动力学途径。

Abstract: In various approaches to quantum gravity, spacetime geometry is understood to emerge from more fundamental discrete structures at the Planck scale. As sometimes posited, their presence could lead to dissipative effects in the smooth effective sector. In this paper, we develop the idea of non-conservation in gravity, by introducing an effective cosmological model within unimodular gravity, in which a varying cosmological constant arises as a consequence of dissipation. We show that this requires to incorporate hidden degrees of freedom -- termed quantum gravity defects -- that act as an effective bath for the matter fields. To illustrate the viability of the framework, we study the case of an Ohmic bath inspired by the Caldeira-Leggett model for Brownian motion, leading to a diffusion equation for the matter energy density. The results show that, starting from a primordial universe with no dark energy, dissipation can account for the generation of a small positive cosmological constant.

</details>


### [383] [Reconstructing $f(R)$ gravity from Viaggiu Holographic Dark Energy under Hubble, Event Horizon and Granda Oliveros cutoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03308)
*Arushi Jhunjhunwala,Sayani Maity*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Viaggiu全息暗能量框架下重构了f(R)引力理论，通过考虑宇宙学视界动态性带来的熵修正，建立了VHDE与曲率诱导能量密度之间的对应关系。针对不同红外截断（哈勃视界、未来事件视界和Granda-Oliveros截断）生成了显式的f(R)形式，并通过状态方程、减速参数等宇宙学参数进行图形化分析，成功描述了从减速膨胀到加速膨胀的转变。稳定性分析和局部引力约束表明模型无鬼影和快子不稳定性，证明了VHDE启发的f(R)引力模型是解释晚期宇宙加速膨胀的一致且灵活的几何框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何将考虑视界动态熵修正的Viaggiu全息暗能量模型与f(R)引力理论建立对应关系，以构建能够解释宇宙晚期加速膨胀的几何框架。

Method: 建立VHDE与曲率诱导能量密度之间的对应关系；针对哈勃视界、未来事件视界和Granda-Oliveros截断三种不同红外截断生成显式f(R)形式；通过状态方程和减速参数等宇宙学参数进行图形化分析；利用稳定性条件和局部引力约束检验模型可行性。

Result: 成功构建了VHDE启发的f(R)引力模型，该模型能够描述从减速膨胀到加速膨胀的宇宙学转变；模型无鬼影和快子不稳定性，满足局部引力约束。

Conclusion: VHDE启发的f(R)引力模型是解释宇宙晚期加速膨胀的一致且灵活的几何框架。

Abstract: This work explores the reconstruction of $f(R)$ gravity within the framework of newly proposed Viaggiu holographic dark energy (VHDE), which incorporates entropy corrections due to the dynamical nature of cosmological horizons. By implementing a correspondence between VHDE and curvature-induced energy density, we generate explicit forms of $f(R)$ for different infrared cutoffs, namely the Hubble horizon, future event horizon, and Granda Oliveros cutoff. The resulting models are analyzed graphically through the cosmological parameters such as the equation of state and deceleration parameter, revealing a successful description of the transition from decelerated to accelerated expansion. The viability of the reconstructed models is further examined through stability conditions and local gravity constraints, that yields the nature of the models as free from ghost and tachyonic instabilities and demonstrates VHDE-inspired $f(R)$ gravity model as a consistent and flexible geometric framework for explaining the late-time cosmic acceleration of the Universe.

</details>


### [384] [Black Hole Memory Burden and its Signatures in Gravitational Waves from Mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03560)
*Gia Dvali,Michael Zantedeschi,Sebastian Zell*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究黑洞记忆负担如何受坍缩源特征影响，发现双粒子碰撞形成的黑洞具有最大记忆负担，并分析了记忆负担对引力波准正规模频率的偏移效应，表明引力波观测可探测黑洞信息存储机制和形成历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究黑洞记忆负担与坍缩源特征之间的关系，理解记忆负担如何影响黑洞的经典动力学及引力波辐射。

Method: 通过理论分析对比不同坍缩源（如恒星级坍缩与双粒子碰撞）形成的黑洞记忆负担量级，推导约束条件，并计算记忆负担对黑洞准正规模频率的偏移。

Result: 记忆负担可远超其前身星的信息含量；双粒子碰撞形成的黑洞表现出最大记忆负担；记忆负担导致引力波准正规模频率发生可量化的偏移。

Conclusion: 引力波观测为探测黑洞信息存储的基本机制及其形成历史提供了新途径。

Abstract: Swift memory burden (MB) implies that the information stored in a black hole (BH) can modify its classical dynamics when the BH is perturbed. This influences the gravitational waves (GWs) emitted during BH mergers. In this paper, we investigate how the BH memory load is determined by the features of the collapsing source. We show that the memory load can vastly exceed the information content of its progenitor. An extreme example is a BH formed in a two-particle collision, which exhibits maximal MB. We then derive bounds for BHs formed through stellar collapse and examine the impact of swift MB on BH quasinormal modes, quantifying the MB-induced frequency shift of GWs. These findings imply that GW observations probe the fundamental mechanisms of BH information storage as well as their formation history.

</details>


### [385] [Generalized Unruh temperature for dynamical thin-shell wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03577)
*Francisco S. N. Lobo,Manuel E. Rodrigues*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文统一了动力学薄壳虫洞中的两种有效温度——喉部的局部加速度温度和类霍金粒子产生温度，并推导了广义Unruh恒等式。应用于Schwarzschild-Schwarzschild虫洞后，得到了显式的类霍金谱解析表达式，并展示了壳温度在准视界处的发散与局域Tolman温度的发散具有相同起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一动力学薄壳虫洞中两种独立定义的有效温度（加速度温度与类霍金粒子产生温度），将Hawking效应与Unruh效应之间的概念联系扩展到非稳态黑洞以外的时空结构。

Method: 通过平均虫洞连接面两侧的加速度标度定义热力学壳温度；利用渐近指数零射线映射的剥离函数定义有效粒子产生温度；推导广义Unruh恒等式建立两者相等关系；应用于对称和非对称Schwarzschild-Schwarzschild虫洞构型进行解析求解。

Result: 获得了对称和非对称Schwarzschild-Schwarzschild虫洞的类霍金谱、重整化量子通量及准视界极限的显式解析表达式；证明了壳温度在准视界附近发散与局域Tolman温度发散同源，均源于无限引力蓝移效应。

Conclusion: 该工作为无视界的动力学时空提供了加速度、粒子产生与温度的统一解释，将Hawking与Unruh效应之间的概念联系超越了稳态黑洞范畴。

Abstract: We unify two independent notions of effective temperature in dynamical thin-shell wormholes: the local acceleration temperature of the throat and the Hawking-like particle-creation temperature governed by the peeling of null rays. The effective acceleration scale, obtained by averaging over both sides of the junction, defines the thermodynamic shell temperature, while peeling functions for each asymptotic universe yield an effective particle-creation temperature. For asymptotically exponential null-ray maps, we derive the generalized Unruh identity that equates these two temperatures. Applied to Schwarzschild-Schwarzschild wormholes, the formalism yields explicit analytical expressions for the Hawking-like spectra, renormalized quantum fluxes, and quasi-horizon limits in both symmetric and asymmetric configurations. The divergence of the shell temperature near the quasi-horizon is shown to mirror the divergence of the local Tolman temperature, both originating from the infinite gravitational blueshift at a horizon. These results provide a unified interpretation of acceleration, particle creation, and temperature in horizonless dynamical spacetimes, extending the conceptual link between the Hawking and Unruh effects beyond stationary black holes.

</details>


### [386] [Additional Observational Signatures of Asymmetric Thin-Shell Wormholes within 4D Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03673)
*J. Song,X. G. Lan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过Visser剪切粘贴构造研究4D爱因斯坦-高斯-邦内特不对称薄壳虫洞的光学外观，发现其图像呈现额外光子环和透镜带（尺寸随耦合参数α增大而增大），这些特征与黑洞情况截然不同，可作为区分和表征薄壳虫洞时空的可靠判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索4D爱因斯坦-高斯-邦内特不对称薄壳虫洞的光学外观，寻找区分虫洞与黑洞的观测特征。

Method: 采用Visser的剪切粘贴构造法确定光子球半径和临界碰撞参数；分析有效势和光子运动；考虑光学薄吸积盘作为唯一光源进行成像模拟。

Result: 不对称薄壳虫洞的图像呈现额外光子环和透镜带，这些在4D爱因斯坦-高斯-邦内特黑洞中不存在；额外环的尺寸随高斯-邦内特耦合参数α增大而增大（与黑洞情况相反）；质量比和喉半径显著调控额外光子环的形态。

Conclusion: 额外光子环可作为区分和表征薄壳虫洞时空的可靠判据，为通过观测识别虫洞提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the optical appearance of a 4D Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet asymmetric thin-shell wormhole. Using Visser's cut-and-paste construction, we determine the photon sphere radius and critical impact parameter for different values of the Gauss-Bonnet coupling $α$. We then investigate the effective potential and photon motion inside the wormhole spacetime. It is found that the effective potential, light ray paths, and azimuthal angle are closely tied to the mass ratio of the two spacetimes. Considering an optically thin accretion disk as the only light source, we find that the asymmetric thin-shell wormhole's images exhibit additional photon rings and lensing bands that are absent for a 4D Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet black hole. Furthermore, the size of these extra rings increases with $α$, contrary to the black hole case. Such exceptionally bright rings provide a reliable criterion for distinguishing and characterizing a thin-shell wormhole spacetime. We also verify that the mass ratio and throat radius significantly tune the morphology of these extra photon rings.

</details>


### [387] [Equatorial periodic orbits and gravitational waveforms in Bardeen black holes surrounded by perfect fluid dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03714)
*Sohan Kumar Jha*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究在完美流体暗物质（PFDM）环境中的Bardeen黑洞（BPFDM BH），分析非线性电动力学（NED）和暗物质对粒子轨道动力学（MBO、ISCO、周期轨道）的影响，并通过Kludge方法计算极端质量比旋进（EMRI）产生的引力波波形，发现NED和暗物质在轨道动力学和引力波中留下独特特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究暗物质（DM）与非线性电动力学（NED）之间的相互作用，具体通过分析Bardeen黑洞在PFDM环境中的粒子轨道行为和引力波信号，揭示DM和NED对黑洞物理的可观测效应。

Method: 1) 计算有效势；2) 通过条件约束求解MBO和ISCO；3) 限制在赤道面上，利用有理数q和(z,w,v)分类法分析周期轨道；4) 采用数值Kludge方法模拟EMRI引力波波形。

Result: DM参数β和磁荷g（NED参数）显著影响MBO和ISCO的位置、周期轨道的能量/角动量分布以及引力波波形，产生可区分的特征信号。

Conclusion: NED和暗物质在轨道动力学和引力波中均留下独特可观测特征，为通过引力波探测暗物质性质和非线性电动力学效应提供了理论依据。

Abstract: To probe the interplay between dark matter (DM) and non-linear electrodynamics (NED), we consider the Bardeen black hole (BH) surrounded by perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM). We first compute the effective potential governing the particle trajectory, and then, by imposing suitable conditions on the potential, examine the effects of DM and NED on the marginally bound orbit (MBO) and innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO). In this study, we confine the particle's trajectory to the equatorial plane. We then investigate periodic orbits around the Bardeen BH surrounded by PFDM (BPFDM BH), considering the rational number $q$ associated with each periodic orbit. We use the $(z,w,v)$ taxonomy, which is widely used to systematically organize periodic orbits. We examine the variation of $q$ with energy and angular momentum, and also the variation of the angular momentum and energy required for a specific $(z,w,v)$ configuration with the magnetic charge $g$ and DM parameter $\b$. Finally, with the help of the numerical "Kludge" method, we examine gravitational waveforms emitted from EMRIs where the central supermassive BH is modeled as a BPFDM BH. Our study reveals distinct signatures of NED and DM on orbital dynamics and gravitational waveforms.

</details>


### [388] [Radiated Energy Spectrum, Radiated Angular Distribution and Non-linear Memory from the One-loop Gravitational Bremsstrahlung Waveform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03879)
*Donato Bini,Thibault Damour,Stefano De Angelis,Andrea Geralico*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于单圈精确的频域引力波形，推导了非自旋大质量粒子散射过程中谱引力波辐射率、能量谱和角分布至G⁴阶，并将非线性记忆的多极展开推广至G⁵阶，在质心系中达到7.5PN精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已有的下一阶（单圈）后闵可夫斯基近似下散射引力波形基础上，进一步推导更高阶的引力波辐射能量和角分布，并扩展非线性记忆效应的多极展开。

Method: 基于单圈精确的频域引力波形h(ω,θ,φ)~G²+G³，依次推导谱辐射率dE^gw/(dωdΩ)、能量谱dE^gw/dω和角分布dE^gw/dΩ至G⁴阶，并从角分布推导非线性记忆的多极展开至G⁵阶，在质心系中计算，达到7.5PN精度。

Result: 获得了至G⁴阶的引力波谱辐射率、能量谱和角分布，以及至G⁵阶的非线性记忆多极展开，精度达到7.5PN。

Conclusion: 本文结果将非线性记忆效应扩展到了更高阶，为后闵可夫斯基近似下引力波辐射提供了更完整的理论描述。

Abstract: The frequency-domain gravitational waveform emitted by the scattering of two non-spinning massive particles has recently been derived at next-to-leading, \textit{i.e.} one-loop, post-Minkowskian order, $h(ω, θ,φ) \sim G^2 + G^3$. Building on this one-loop-accurate frequency-domain gravitational waveform, we successively derive the spectral gravitational-wave (GW) radiance, $dE^{\rm gw}/(dωdΩ)$, the radiated GW energy spectrum, $dE^{\rm gw}/dω$, and the radiated GW angular distribution, $dE^{\rm gw}/dΩ$, up to order $G^4$ included. We deduce from the radiated angular distribution the multipole expansion of the non-linear memory up to order $G^5$ included, thereby extending previous results. We work in the center-of-mass frame, and our results reach the fractional 7.5PN accuracy. For completeness, we include the tree-level information (considered in the center-of-mass frame).

</details>


### [389] [Identifying lensed gravitational waves with physics-informed posterior learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03885)
*Tian-Yang Sun,Xiao Guo,Jing-Fei Zhang,Xin Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息的后验学习方法来识别引力波透镜事件，通过融合后验样本与波形特征，在1%假阳性率下将检测效率从20.8%提升至35.2%，并将50%检测效率所需的信噪比从45.3降至33.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波引力透镜效应可以探测致密天体透镜、暗物质子结构和宇宙学距离，但识别透镜事件极具挑战性，因为无关的双星并合事件会在同一分析窗口内重叠，难以区分真正的透镜事件和无关的多并合事件。

Method: 开发物理信息后验学习（Physics-informed Posterior Learning）方法，利用几何光学一致性——透镜可以改变振幅、到达时间和莫尔斯相位偏移，但保持源的本征相位演化。训练模拟逼近后验，推断公共探测器框架下的啁啾质量和对称质量比，并将后验样本与直接波形特征融合。基于通用多图像仿真训练，用点质量、SIS、SIE和剪切扰动透镜进行留出族评估。

Result: 在1%参考假阳性率阈值下，融合排名将检测效率从20.8%提升至35.2%；50%检测效率所需网络信噪比从45.3降至33.5，相当于信噪比等效距离尺度扩大1.35倍。

Conclusion: 物理一致性可以成为密集引力波目录中机器学习搜索的指导原则。当前增益受限于部分源一致的响亮无关多并合事件以及对无关多并合群体标定的需求。

Abstract: Gravitational lensing of gravitational waves can probe compact lenses, dark matter substructure, and cosmological distances, but identifying lensed events is difficult when unrelated binary mergers overlap in the same analysis window. We develop physics-informed posterior learning for ranking lensed multi-image signals against unrelated multiple-merger events. The method exploits the geometric-optics consistency that lensing can change amplitudes, arrival times, and Morse phase offsets while preserving the intrinsic phase evolution of the source. We infer a simulation-trained approximate posterior for the common detector-frame chirp mass and symmetric mass ratio, and fuse posterior samples with direct waveform features. Training uses generic multi-image simulations, while point-mass, singular-isothermal-sphere, singular-isothermal-ellipsoid, and shear-perturbed lenses are reserved for held-out lens-family evaluation. For the observationally motivated binary-black-hole population, the fusion ranking raises the detection efficiency from $20.8\%$ to $35.2\%$ at a $1\%$ reference false-positive-rate threshold calibrated on the corresponding unrelated multiple-merger sample. It lowers the network signal-to-noise ratio needed for $50\%$ detection efficiency from 45.3 to 33.5, which corresponds to a 1.35 times larger signal-to-noise-ratio-equivalent distance scale. The gain is limited by loud unrelated multiple-merger events that are partly source consistent, and by the need to calibrate the unrelated multiple-merger population. These results suggest that physical consistency can become a guiding principle for machine learning searches in dense gravitational-wave catalogs.

</details>


### [390] [Semiclassical regularity of compact trapped regions: From dynamical horizons to inner extremality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03916)
*Raúl Carballo-Rubio,Francesco Di Filippo,Stefano Liberati,Matt Visser*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了黑洞内视界在动力学演化中的稳定性。对于形成和蒸发的黑洞，内视界不再是柯西视界，因此重整化应力-能量张量（RSET）保持有限。非极端内视界会出现RSET指数增长，而极端内视界仅呈现幂律增长，表明此类时空可作为经典和半经典亚稳态黑洞内部的自然候选者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典黑洞内视界存在质量膨胀不稳定性（非极端）和半经典RSET发散问题。极端内视界（表面引力为零）虽规避了经典质量膨胀，但在稳态下仍存在RSET奇异性。作者希望探究动力学情况下（黑洞形成与蒸发过程）内视界的稳定性是否有所不同。

Method: 在s波Polyakov近似下，考虑描述紧凑束缚区域形成和蒸发的有限时时空，计算in-真空态下的应力-能量张量期望值。分别分析非极端和极端内视界两种情况下的RSET行为。

Result: 动力学情况下内视界不是柯西视界，RSET保持有限。非极端内视界：RSET随时间指数增长，但仅当束缚区域永久存在时才出现发散。极端内视界：指数增长被更温和的幂律增长替代。

Conclusion: 动力学黑洞内视界比稳态情形更稳定，尤其是极端内视界仅呈现幂律增长，这些时空可作为经典和半经典亚稳态黑洞内部的自然候选者。

Abstract: In eternal black-hole spacetimes, inner horizons are Cauchy horizons and are generically unstable. For non-extremal inner horizons, this includes both the classical mass-inflation instability and a semiclassical instability associated with divergences in the renormalized stress-energy tensor (RSET). Inner-extremal geometries, for which the inner-horizon surface gravity vanishes, evade classical mass inflation, but in stationary settings still suffer from singular behavior of the RSET. In this work, we show that the dynamical case is qualitatively different. Considering spacetimes describing the formation and evaporation of a compact trapped region in finite time, and working in the $s$-wave Polyakov approximation, we compute the expectation value of the stress-energy tensor in the in-vacuum state. Given that in this case the inner horizon is not a Cauchy horizon, the RSET remains finite everywhere. For generic non-extremal inner horizons, however, the RSET grows exponentially in time at the inner horizon, with a divergence emerging only in the asymptotic limit of an ever-lasting trapped region. For inner-extremal geometries this exponential growth is replaced by a considerably milder power-law growth. Such spacetimes may therefore be considered natural candidates for classically and semiclassically meta-stable black-hole interiors.

</details>


### [391] [On the structure of black hole interior in a model of scalar quasi-particles within the GR framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03938)
*S. Bondarenko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一个无奇点的黑洞内部模型，用具有非线性自相互作用势的标量场描述。黑洞内部由三层结构组成——核心、过渡层和壳层，每层由局域准粒子密度和势的极值决定。壳层是零势阱上方的正能量热激发层，位于史瓦西视界之下；核心是AdS型负能量密度区域，模拟准粒子凝聚体，具有负逆温参数。通过Israel匹配连接两层，并求解静态爱因斯坦方程得到各层的度规、质量函数、状态方程、稳定性条件及双温热力学。该框架将核心与壳层统一于单一场描述，黑洞形成与霍金蒸发表现为以负能量核心为工作物质的准静态绝热热力学循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑洞内部模型存在奇点问题，且缺乏对黑洞内部结构的统一场论描述。本文旨在构建一个无奇点的黑洞内部模型，用单一标量场统一描述核心与壳层，并揭示负能量AdS核心和负温度概念在黑洞内部中的作用。

Method: 采用具有非线性自相互作用势的标量场描述黑洞内部，将内部划分为核心、过渡层和壳层三层结构，每层由局域准粒子密度和势的极值确定。通过Israel匹配条件在过渡层连接核心与壳层，求解静态爱因斯坦方程得到各层的度规和质量函数，推导壳层的边沿状态方程、零引力面的线性稳定性条件，以及联系动能激发与负温度基态的双温热力学。

Result: 获得了各层的度规与质量函数、壳层的边沿状态方程、零引力面对普通物质和有效负质量物质的线性稳定性条件，以及双温热力学关系。模型将核心和壳层统一于单一场描述，黑洞形成与霍金蒸发呈现为准静态绝热热力学循环，负能量核心作为工作物质。

Conclusion: 该无奇点模型有效描述了黑洞内部结构，负能量AdS核心和负温度概念在解释黑洞内部物理中起关键作用，黑洞热力学过程可理解为以负能量核心为工作物质的准静态循环。

Abstract: We propose an effective, singularity-free model of the black hole interior described entirely by a scalar field with a non-linear self-interaction potential. The interior consists of three layers -- a core, a transition layer, and a crust -- each fixed by the local quasi-particle density and the corresponding extremum of the potential of the field. The crust is a layer of massive, positive-energy thermal excitations above the zero-potential well, beneath a genuine Schwarzschild horizon at $r = 2GM_{\rm ADM}$. The core is an AdS-type region of negative energy density, it simulates a condensate of quasi-particles which carry zero classical kinetic energy and form a negative potential well governed by a negative inverse temperature parameter. The two regions are joined through Israel matching across the transition layer, which sits at an approximately null-gravity hypersurface of maximal regular matter density and where both the sign of the energy density and the type of thermal excitations change. Solving the static Einstein equations, we obtain the metric and mass functions of each layer, the edge equation of state of the crust, the linear stability condition of the null-gravity surface for ordinary and effectively negative mass matter, and the two-temperature thermodynamics linking the kinetic excitations to the negative temperature ground state. The framework unifies core and crust within a single field description and highlights the role of the negative energy AdS core and the associated negative temperature notion in describing black hole interior. In this picture the formation and Hawking evaporation of a black hole appear as a quasi-static cycle of an almost adiabatic thermodynamic engine, with the negative energy core as the working substance.

</details>


### [392] [Transient Dynamical Wormholes with Decaying Radial Energy Flux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04041)
*Allah Ditta,Phongpichit Channuie*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义相对论中形状函数随时间变化的可穿越虫洞，通过径向能量流驱动几何演化，获得了随时间衰减至静态的精确解，分析了能量条件违反程度及稳定性，并探讨了与宇宙尺度因子驱动方法的区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿越虫洞研究多集中在静态或由宇宙尺度因子驱动的演化模型，缺乏由物质能量流直接驱动几何演化的自然机制。本文旨在建立一种由径向能量通量驱动虫洞动力学的简单物理框架。

Method: 允许形状函数含时，通过爱因斯坦场方程的非对角分量将几何演化与径向能量流耦合，求解得到静态背景加衰减瞬态项的精确解；通过体积积分量化奇异物质总量，并采用小扰动分析评估稳定性。

Result: 得到了含暂态衰减项的精确虫洞解，满足喉部存在、flare-out条件和渐近平坦性；零能量条件在喉部附近被违反但违反程度随时间减小；奇异物质总量在晚期趋于常数；在合适参数下扰动衰减，系统稳定。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了一种由能量输运控制的动力学虫洞框架，系统逐渐过渡到静态构型，为广义相对论及扩展引力理论中的更现实场景提供了简单且物理动机明确的研究途径。

Abstract: We investigate a class of time-dependent traversable wormholes within the framework of general relativity by allowing the shape function to vary with time. In this setting, the evolution of the geometry is directly connected to a radial energy flux through the off-diagonal component of the Einstein field equations, providing a natural mechanism for non-static configurations. We obtain exact solutions in which the geometry consists of a static background supplemented by a transient term that diminishes with time. The resulting spacetime satisfies the standard conditions required for a traversable wormhole, including the presence of a throat, the flaring-out condition, and asymptotic flatness. An analysis of the energy conditions indicates that the null energy condition is violated in the vicinity of the throat, although the degree of violation decreases as the system evolves. This feature is examined further through a volume integral that measures the total amount of exotic matter, demonstrating that it approaches a constant value at late times. We also study the response of the system to small perturbations and find that, for a suitable choice of parameters, the configuration remains stable with perturbations decaying over time. We discuss how this flux-driven mechanism relates to the alternative and extensively studied approach in which wormhole evolution is instead carried by a cosmological scale factor, and we identify possible physical origins of the assumed radial flux in terms of null-fluid matter sources. Overall, the model describes a wormhole spacetime whose evolution is controlled by energy transport, leading to a gradual transition toward a static configuration. This framework offers a simple and physically motivated approach to dynamical wormholes and may be useful for exploring more realistic scenarios in both general relativity and extended theories of gravity.

</details>


### [393] [Where Thermodynamics Meets Geometry: Critical-Radius Coincidences in Confining-NED Black Holes with Barrow Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04173)
*Erdem Sucu,İzzet Sakallı*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究爱因斯坦引力耦合非线性电动力学（含夸克禁闭相互作用）得到的静态球对称黑洞。该黑洞度规在Reissner-Nordström基础上引入对数修正（参数ζ），修改了视界结构和奇点附近行为。采用Hamilton-Jacobi隧穿方法计算狄拉克费米子的Hawking温度；通过Barrow熵（形变指数Δ）引入量子引力涨落。在扩展相空间中计算了内能、自由能、压强、热容、等温压缩率和Joule-Thomson系数。核心发现是一个四重巧合：Hawking温度峰值、热容发散、Joule-Thomson反转和径向潮汐力零点均位于由A''(r⋆)=0定义的同一半径r⋆处；而极端视界与角向潮汐力零点通过A'(rh)=0重合。Event Horizon Telescope对Sgr A*的观测给出约束ζ≲0.7（Q/M=0.5时）。禁闭项对测地偏离产生可观测修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非线性电动力学中夸克禁闭相互作用对黑洞热力学和几何结构的影响，特别是对数修正项如何改变经典RN黑洞的视界结构、奇点行为以及热力学稳定性，并通过事件视界望远镜观测数据获得参数约束。

Method: 采用Hamilton-Jacobi隧穿方法计算狄拉克费米子的Hawking温度；使用Barrow熵（形变指数Δ）引入量子引力涨落；在扩展相空间中计算内能、自由能、压强、热容、等温压缩率和Joule-Thomson系数；通过A''(r⋆)=0和A'(rh)=0两个标量方程简化临界点分析；将几何潮汐加速度映射到热力学临界曲线；利用EHT对Sgr A*的观测数据约束参数ζ。

Result: 发现四重巧合：Hawking温度峰值、热容发散、Joule-Thomson反转和径向潮汐力零点均位于同一半径r⋆（由A''(r⋆)=0定义）；极端视界与角向潮汐力零点通过A'(rh)=0重合。热容定位了Δ依赖的稳定区域；等温压缩率在全部分析域内保持负值，表明该分支为力学刚性相，无van der Waals临界性。EHT观测给出ζ≲0.7（Q/M=0.5时）。

Conclusion: ζ依赖项抑制了小视界发散并预示残留物存在。热力学与几何潮汐力之间存在深刻的统一结构，可将完整的临界点分析简化为两个标量方程。禁闭项对测地偏离产生可观测的修正，在EHT约束下仍保留有限参数窗口。

Abstract: We study a static, spherically symmetric black hole obtained from Einstein gravity coupled to a nonlinear electrodynamics model with a quark--antiquark confinement interaction. The metric extends Reissner--Nordström by a logarithmic correction controlled by $ζ$, modifying both horizon structure and the near-singularity regime. The Hamilton--Jacobi tunneling method for Dirac fermions yields the Hawking temperature; the $ζ$-dependent terms suppress the small-horizon divergence and signal a remnant. Quantum-gravitational fluctuations are incorporated through Barrow entropy with deformation index $Δ$. Within the extended phase space we compute the internal energy, free energy, pressure, heat capacity, isothermal compressibility, and Joule--Thomson coefficient. The heat capacity locates $Δ$-dependent stability regions; the compressibility stays negative across the domain analysed here, marking a mechanically rigid phase with no van der Waals criticality in this branch. The central result is a quadruple coincidence: the peak Hawking temperature, the heat-capacity divergence, the Joule--Thomson inversion, and the zero of the radial tidal force all sit at one radius $r_\star$ defined by $A''(r_\star)=0$, while the extremal horizon and the angular tidal-force zero coincide via $A'(r_h)=0$. These reduce the full critical-point analysis to two scalar equations on $A(r)$. Geometric tidal accelerations are mapped against the thermodynamic critical curves. Event Horizon Telescope observations of Sgr~A* translate into a constraint $ζ\lesssim 0.7$ at $Q/M=0.5$, leaving a finite window open. The confinement term induces observable corrections to geodesic deviation.

</details>


### [394] [Tests of general relativity using analytic derivatives of parametrized post-Einsteinian gravitational waveforms within the Fisher-matrix framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04238)
*Jie Wu,Mengfei Sun,Jin Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在参数化后爱因斯坦框架下推导了Fisher矩阵中波形导数的解析表达式，避免了有限差分方案，显著降低了计算成本。该方法应用于天基、地基和多波段引力波探测器配置及不同双黑洞种群模型，系统展示了非GR效应约束随后牛顿阶数、探测器类型和源种群的变化趋势，揭示了空间与地基探测器的互补性，为未来引力波观测检验引力理论提供了稳健预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越广义相对论的引力检验对于在强场和高动态区域探测基础物理至关重要。现有Fisher矩阵计算依赖有限差分方案，存在数值系统性和计算成本高的问题，亟需发展解析方法以提高效率和稳定性。

Method: 在参数化后爱因斯坦框架下，使用频域旋进波形，推导Fisher矩阵中波形偏导数的解析表达式。将此方法应用于天基、地基和多波段探测器配置，并结合不同双黑洞种群模型进行系统性分析。

Result: 解析方法避免了有限差分的步长选择问题，大幅降低计算成本；揭示了非GR效应约束随后牛顿阶数、探测器类型和源种群的清晰系统趋势；展示了空间与地基探测器在低频旋进累积效应方面的互补性。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于大规模参数研究，为未来引力波观测检验非GR效应和环境影响提供了稳健预测，凸显了即将建成的探测器网络在精密引力检验中的科学潜力。

Abstract: Testing gravity beyond general relativity (GR) is essential for probing fundamental physics in the strong-field and highly dynamical regime accessed by gravitational-wave (GW) observations. In this work, we derive analytic expressions for waveform derivatives in the Fisher-matrix formalism within the parametrized post-Einsteinian framework, using the frequency-domain inspiral waveform. These analytic derivatives enable stable and efficient Fisher-matrix calculations without relying on finite-difference schemes. We apply this method to a wide range of detector configurations, including space-based, ground-based, and multiband observations, and combine it with different binary black hole population models. Our results reveal clear and systematic trends in the constraints on non-GR effects as functions of post-Newtonian order, detector type, and source population. They also demonstrate the complementarity between space- and ground-based detectors, particularly for effects that accumulate during the low-frequency inspiral. The analytic approach substantially reduces computational cost and avoids numerical systematics associated with step-size choices, making it well suited for large-scale parameter studies. These results provide robust forecasts for the capability of future GW observations to constrain a broad class of non-GR effects and environmental influences, highlighting the scientific potential of upcoming detector networks for precision tests of gravity.

</details>


### [395] [Rigidity and the interpretation of mass with a positive cosmological constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04291)
*Eneko Aranguren,Raül Vera*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个满足强、主能量条件且不含表面层的正则静态球对称星体构型，其外部Schwarzschild-de Sitter区域的质量参数可正、可零、可负。零质量情形对应外部真空区退化为纯de Sitter时空，使de Sitter域与正则理想流体共存——这直接推翻了Λ>0时与正质量定理相关的刚性质，证明Λ=0下的刚性质无法推广到Λ>0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验在正宇宙学常数Λ>0条件下，与质量解释相关的刚性质是否仍然成立，特别是Minkowski/de Sitter域能否与满足能量条件的理想流体区域共存。

Method: 显式构造一族正则静态球对称星体解，验证其满足主导能量条件和强能量条件，并分析不同质量参数下外部Schwarzschild-de Sitter区域的几何性质。

Result: 成功构造出质量参数可正、可零、可负的星体构型；零质量时外部真空区为纯de Sitter时空，且与内部理想流体平滑匹配；该结果与Λ=0情形形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: Λ>0时，与正质量定理相关的静态球对称刚性质不成立，de Sitter域可以与满足能量条件的物质共存，打破了Λ=0下Minkowski域与物质不能共存的刚性结论。

Abstract: We provide an explicit counterexample to the rigidity properties underlying the interpretation of mass in the presence of a positive cosmological constant $Λ$. Specifically, we construct a family of regular static stellar configurations satisfying the dominant and strong energy conditions, and containing no surface layers, for which the mass parameter of the outer Schwarzschild-de Sitter region can be positive, zero, or negative. The zero-mass configuration is precisely the one for which the outer vacuum region becomes exactly de Sitter, yielding a spacetime in which a de Sitter domain coexists with regular perfect-fluid matter. This contrasts sharply with the $Λ=0$ case, where a Minkowski domain cannot coexist with perfect-fluid regions satisfying the energy conditions. These results show that the static, spherically symmetric realization of the rigidity principle associated with the positive mass theorem for $Λ=0$ does not carry over to $Λ>0$.

</details>


### [396] [Complete Quantum Stress Tensor Inside a Four Dimensional Schwarzschild Black Hole: A Divergent Focusing Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04386)
*Shun Jiang,Jie Jiang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次计算了四维Schwarzschild黑洞内部（从事件视界到靠近奇点r/M≈10⁻⁴）完整重整化应力能量张量（RSET），包含Unruh态和Hartle-Hawking态，并给出了相应的真空极化⟨Φ²⟩_ren。结果通过协变守恒和迹恒等式检验。在类空奇点附近，两种态趋近于相同的标度解，主导源是局域真空极化应力而非霍金辐射通量。该张量破坏主导能量条件但满足零能量条件，表明在半经典背景下量子源是发散聚焦的，而非平滑奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四维黑洞内部的完整RSET长期缺失，导致半经典回反应的局域源项未知，尤其在类空奇点附近这一空白更为突出。本文旨在填补这一空白，为半经典引力研究提供必要的局域源项。

Method: 采用角分裂重整化方案（angular-splitting renormalization scheme）结合高阶大ℓ渐近减除，从事件视界计算到r/M≈10⁻⁴，确定所有独立分量的⟨T^a_b⟩_ren及真空极化⟨Φ^2⟩_ren。

Result: RSET通过协变守恒和迹恒等式检验；在奇点附近两种态趋近相同标度解M⁴⟨T^a_b⟩_ren ≃ (r/M)⁻⁶ τ^a_b；Unruh通量相对对角混合分量被(r/M)⁴压制；主导紫外源为局域真空极化应力；张量破坏主导能量条件但满足零能量条件。

Conclusion: 在固定背景Schwarzschild RSET层面，半经典源提供的是发散聚焦源而非平滑奇点的退聚焦效应。但真正的全局结论需要求解反作用的半经典几何。

Abstract: We compute the complete renormalized stress-energy tensor (RSET) of a massless minimally coupled scalar field throughout the interior of a four-dimensional Schwarzschild black hole, in both the Unruh and Hartle--Hawking states. The complete RSET inside four-dimensional black holes has long been unavailable, leaving the local source term required for semiclassical backreaction unknown. This gap is even sharper near spacelike singularities. Taking the Schwarzschild interior as a concrete example, we close both gaps for the first time. Using an angular-splitting renormalization scheme together with a high-order large-$\ell$ asymptotic subtraction, we determine all independent components of $\langle T^a{}_{b}\rangle_{\rm ren}$ from the event horizon down to $r/M\simeq10^{-4}$, and simultaneously obtain the corresponding vacuum polarization $\langleΦ^2\rangle_{\rm ren}$. The tensor passes the cross-checks of the covariant conservation and the trace identity. Near the spacelike singularity, the Unruh and Hartle--Hawking states approach the same conserved scaling solution, \ba M^4\langle T^a{}_{b}\rangle_{\rm ren} \simeq \left(\frac{r}{M}\right)^{-6}τ^a{}_{b},\nn \ea while the state-dependent Unruh flux is suppressed by $(r/M)^4$ relative to the diagonal mixed components. The leading ultraviolet source is therefore a local vacuum-polarization stress rather than transported Hawking flux. The limiting tensor violates the dominant energy condition but satisfy the null energy condition. Thus, at the level of the complete fixed-background Schwarzschild RSET, the leading semiclassical source does not support the intuition that quantum defocusing smooths the singularity; instead, it supplies a divergent focusing source in the local Raychaudhuri equation. A genuine global conclusion, however, requires solving the backreacted semiclassical geometry.

</details>


### [397] [Neutron stars in $f(Q) = Q +ξQ^2$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04596)
*J. C. N. de Araujo,H. G. M. Fortes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在f(Q)引力框架下，利用四种真实物态方程（FPS、SLy、ENG、MPA1）研究中子星结构，推导了TOV方程并数值求解得到质量-半径关系和最大质量，与观测约束进行比较，同时分析了非度量标量在星体内外的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称平行引力框架下的修正引力理论（如f(Q)引力）作为广义相对论的可行替代方案近来备受关注，本文旨在探究这类理论中中子星的结构特性。

Method: 采用f(Q)引力的协变形式，推导相应的Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程；使用FPS、SLy、ENG和MPA1四种真实物态方程；通过数值积分场方程获得质量-半径关系及最大质量。

Result: 得到了每种物态方程对应的质量-半径关系和最大质量，并与当前观测约束进行了直接比较；同时给出了非度量标量在星体内外的分布行为。

Conclusion: f(Q)引力框架下中子星的结构与标准广义相对论存在差异，通过真实物态方程的模拟可与观测数据进行对比，为检验修正引力理论提供了新的天体物理约束。

Abstract: Modified theories of gravity based on the symmetric teleparallel framework have recently attracted considerable attention as viable alternatives to General Relativity. In this context, f(Q) gravity, in which the gravitational interaction is encoded in the nonmetricity scalar Q, provides a consistent geometrical formulation that differs from the standard curvature-based description. In this work, we investigate the structure of neutron stars within a family of f(Q) gravity models by employing realistic equations of state, namely FPS, SLy, ENG and MPA1. Using the covariant formulation of f(Q) gravity, we derive the corresponding Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equations and apply them to model compact stellar configurations. Numerical integration of the field equations provides the mass-radius relations and the maximum masses supported by each equation of state, enabling a direct comparison with current observational constraints. Furthermore, we analyze the behavior of the nonmetricity scalar both inside and outside the stellar object, providing additional insight into the gravitational structure of compact stars in this framework.

</details>


### [398] [Beyond the Bounce: Multiple Tidal Sign Reversals and Turning-Point Bifurcations in Multi-Horizon Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04642)
*Mohammad Ali S. Afshar,J. Sadeghi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦引力与非线性电动力学（NED）耦合产生的多视界黑洞中，中性试验粒子的径向运动与潮汐力。发现NED场导致径向和角向潮汐力分量出现多次零点交叉，产生拉伸与压缩潮汐区间的连续转换；径向运动方程中存在经典禁区，阻止粒子进入潮汐力发散区域；在超极端情况下，禁区可能延伸至事件视界外，阻止远处静止释放的粒子穿越视界；还揭示了与潮汐力零点、额外视界和反弹点相关的临界电荷值的层次有序性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非线性电动力学对多视界黑洞时空因果结构、径向测地运动及潮汐力分布的影响，并与Schwarzschild和Reissner-Nordstrom时空进行对比。

Method: 通过分析三视界和四视界黑洞构型，求解非线性电动力学修正下的径向运动方程和潮汐力分量，研究零交叉点、禁区、反弹点等特征，并系统比较临界电荷值的层次关系。

Result: NED场使潮汐力分量出现多次零交叉，导致拉伸与压缩区间交替；径向运动存在经典禁区阻止粒子进入潮汐发散区；超极端下禁区可延伸至视界外阻止粒子穿越；临界电荷值满足层次有序性，潮汐结构变化先于视界结构变化。

Conclusion: 非线性电动力学通过禁区效应、多重潮汐转换和视界结构变化，显著改变了多视界黑洞时空中中性粒子的经典动力学行为。

Abstract: We investigate the radial motion and tidal forces experienced by neutral test particles in multi-horizon black hole solutions arising from Einstein gravity coupled to nonlinear electrodynamics (NED). Focusing on the three- and four-horizon configurations, we examine how nonlinear electromagnetic corrections modify the causal structure, radial geodesic motion, and tidal-force profiles in comparison with the Schwarzschild and Reissner-Nordstrom (R-N) spacetimes.
  Our analysis shows that the NED field gives rise to multiple zero crossings in both the radial and angular tidal-force components, leading to successive transitions between stretching and compressive tidal regimes. More importantly, the radial equation of motion contains classically forbidden regions bounded by bounce-back points. For the class of trajectories considered in this work, these forbidden regions prevent particles from entering the spacetime domain where the tidal forces become divergent. In the super-extremal regime admitted by these solutions, the forbidden region may extend beyond the event horizon, preventing particles released from rest at sufficiently large distances from crossing the horizon.
  We further identify a systematic ordering of the critical charge values associated with the appearance of tidal-force zero crossings, additional horizons, and bounce-back points. For both the three- and four-horizon configurations, these critical values satisfy a hierarchical ordering, indicating that changes in the tidal-force structure precede the corresponding modifications of the horizon configuration.
  These results demonstrate that nonlinear electrodynamics can substantially modify the classical dynamics of neutral particles in multi-horizon black hole spacetimes through the combined effects of forbidden regions, multiple tidal transitions, and changes in the horizon structure.

</details>


### [399] [Constraining inflationary models via de-Sitterization of Bianchi Cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04701)
*Apurba Samanta,Rahul Kothari*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明在Bianchi宇宙中，不需要宇宙常数，仅通过满足特定条件的均匀暴胀场即可实现各向同性化（de Sitterization），并利用该条件约束暴胀模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在观测上宇宙在大尺度上是各向同性和均匀的，但早期宇宙可能具有各向异性（如Bianchi宇宙）。已有研究通过宇宙常数实现各向同性化，本文希望探索是否可以通过暴胀场而非宇宙常数达到同样效果，并进一步用该条件约束暴胀模型。

Method: 作者在Bianchi宇宙框架下，引入一个具有一般势能的均匀暴胀场，推导出实现宇宙各向同性化的条件，并通过具体暴胀模型实例进行验证和约束。

Result: 发现不需要宇宙常数，仅需暴胀场满足特定判据即可使Bianchi宇宙各向同性化；该条件可用于筛选和约束可行的暴胀模型。

Conclusion: 暴胀场本身可以替代宇宙常数实现Bianchi宇宙的de Sitterization，且该条件对暴胀模型具有限制作用，为理解宇宙早期各向异性演化和暴胀模型选择提供了新视角。

Abstract: Our Universe is isotropic and homogeneous when we observe it on $\gtrsim$ Mpc length scales. It is desirable that present state of the Universe has no dependence on its initial geometry. In case of Bianchi Universes, i.e., anisotropic but homogeneous Universe, this has already been demonstrated via cosmological constant in a process that we call \textit{de Sitterization}. In this letter, we show that for Bianchi Universe, the same state can be achieved by a homogeneous inflaton field with a general potential and satisfying a criterion without the need of a cosmological constant. More importantly, we show that the same condition can constrain models of inflation and explain our idea with examples.

</details>


### [400] [Rotating Fermion-Boson Stars in $R$-squared Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04744)
*Saeed Fakhry,Jorge Castelo Mourelle,Nicolas Sanchis-Gual,Daniela Doneva,Stoytcho Yazadjiev,José A. Font*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在R平方f(R)引力框架下构建了静态和均匀旋转的费米子-玻色子星，发现标量自由度会修正物质分布、扩大平衡解域并增加最大支持质量，且模型与当前天体物理和引力波观测约束兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出标准中子星模型的偏差，包括暗物质在中子星内可能的积累，以及低质量黑洞间隙附近的致密天体现象，同时结合修改引力理论（f(R)引力）研究强场修正效应。

Method: 在f(R)=R+aR²框架下构建静态和均匀旋转的费米子-玻色子星模型；费米子部分采用零温表格化状态方程的理想流体描述，玻色子部分由自相互作用的复标量场表示，两者仅通过引力相互作用。

Result: 标量自由度（参数a）修正了玻色子场和费米子压力的空间分布；扩大了允许的平衡解参数空间；相较于广义相对论增加了最大支持质量；模型与当前天体物理和引力波观测约束兼容。

Conclusion: R平方引力中的费米子-玻色子星为研究暗物质、旋转和强场引力修正对致密天体的联合效应提供了有前景的理论框架。

Abstract: Fermion-boson stars are compact equilibrium configurations composed of ordinary fermionic matter and a bosonic dark component interacting only through gravity. Such systems provide a natural framework for exploring deviations from standard neutron-star models, including the possible accumulation of dark matter inside neutron stars, and may be relevant for compact objects near the low-mass black-hole gap. We construct static and uniformly rotating fermion-boson stars within the framework of $R$-squared $f(R)$ gravity, characterized by the functional form $f(R)=R+aR^{2}$, where $a$ is a positive parameter governing the effective mass scale from the scalar degree of freedom. The fermionic sector is modeled as a perfect fluid described by a tabulated equation of state at zero temperature, while the bosonic component is represented by a self-interacting complex bosonic field. Our results show that the scalar degree of freedom modifies the spatial distribution of both the bosonic field and the fermionic pressure, enlarges the domain of admissible equilibrium solutions, and increases the maximum supported masses relative to general relativity. Our models remain compatible with current astrophysical and gravitational-wave constraints, suggesting that fermion-boson stars in $R$-squared gravity offer a promising framework to investigate the combined effects of dark bosonic matter, rotation, and strong-field modifications of gravity in compact objects.

</details>


### [401] [Measurability of Quadrupole Deviations from Kerr in Binary black hole Mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04762)
*Song Li,Wen-Biao Han*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA合作的五个双黑洞并合事件，使用超越广义相对论的全波形模型Ψ_FD检验了黑洞四极矩偏离Kerr度规的可测量性。早期低信噪比事件温和地支持非零四极矩偏离，但新纳入的高信噪比GWTC-4事件（GW231226、GW230814和GW250114）的结果越来越与Kerr预言一致。特别是两个最高信噪比事件GW230814和GW250114的偏离与零一致。全波形、旋近和旋近后分析均未发现超越无毛定理的可观测偏离。尽管受限于事件数量无法得出最终结论，未来更多高信噪比双黑洞并合事件的探测将能对黑洞的Kerr性质进行更严格和稳健的检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验黑洞是否严格遵循Kerr度规（即无毛定理），通过测量黑洞四极矩偏离来寻找超越广义相对论的新物理信号。

Method: 使用LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA合作观测的五个双黑洞并合事件，采用超越广义相对论的全波形模型Ψ_FD进行数据分析，对每个事件分别进行全波形、旋近阶段和旋近后阶段的多层次分析，测量四极矩偏离参数ΔQ/Q。

Result: 早期低信噪比事件温和地支持非零四极矩偏离，但高信噪比事件GW230814和GW250114的ΔQ/Q后验分布中心接近零，全波形、旋近和旋近后分析均未发现与Kerr预言的可观测偏离。

Conclusion: 在当前数据灵敏度和Ψ_FD框架下，未观测到黑洞四极矩偏离无毛定理的迹象；未来更多高信噪比双黑洞并合事件将使对黑洞Kerr性质的检验更加严格和可靠。

Abstract: We investigate the measurability of black hole quadrupole deviations from Kerr using five binary black hole mergers observed by the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA Collaboration with the beyond-general-relativity full-waveform model $Ψ_{\mathrm{FD}}$. While earlier lower-SNR events mildly favored nonzero quadrupole deviations, the newly included high-SNR GWTC-4 events GW231226, GW230814, and GW250114 yield results increasingly consistent with the Kerr prediction. In particular, GW230814 and GW250114, the two highest-SNR events in our sample, yield deviations consistent with zero. We further perform separate inspiral and post-inspiral analyses and find both the posterior distributions centered close to $ΔQ/Q=0$ for GW230814 and GW250114. Overall, the full-waveform, inspiral, and post-inspiral results for GW230814 and GW250114 reveal no observable departure from the no-hair theorem within the sensitivity of the current data and the $Ψ_{\mathrm{FD}}$ framework. Although the limited number of events prevents a definitive conclusion, future detections of additional high-SNR binary black hole mergers will enable increasingly stringent and robust tests of the Kerr nature of black holes.

</details>


### [402] [Chaotic particle dynamics near a traversable wormhole throat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04799)
*Xin Zhao,Xing-Kun Zhang,Ya-Peng Hu,Yu-Sen An*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究分析了测试粒子在可穿越虫洞喉部附近、受外部谐和势作用下的非线性动力学。一维径向微扰分析显示粒子在平衡位置局部线性稳定；但在二维和高能情况下出现非线性响应，导致大尺度混沌。粒子被限制在虫洞一侧时，即使极高能量下庞加莱截面仍保留KAM环面，这与黑洞事件视界导致的混沌不同。通过另一组形状函数验证了该相空间结构的普适性。研究阐明了可穿越虫洞独特的非线性动力学机制，为强场天体物理观测中识别黑洞模拟体提供了基于混沌动力学的新判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究可穿越虫洞的非线性动力学机制，区分虫洞与黑洞在强引力环境下的动力学行为差异，为天文观测中识别黑洞模拟体提供新判据。

Method: 采用一维径向微扰分析线性稳定性，结合二维高能情况下的非线性动力学分析、庞加莱截面方法研究相空间结构，并通过改变形状函数验证结果的普适性。

Result: 一维情况下粒子局部线性稳定；二维高能情况下出现大尺度混沌；但粒子被约束在虫洞一侧时，极高能量下庞加莱截面仍保留KAM环面；该现象在不同形状函数下具有普适性。

Conclusion: 可穿越虫洞具有与黑洞不同的非线性动力学特征（KAM环面的保持），该差异可作为基于混沌动力学识别黑洞模拟体的新观测判据。

Abstract: This study investigates the nonlinear dynamics of a test particle near the traversable wormhole throat under an external harmonic potential. One-dimensional radial perturbation analysis shows that the particle is locally linearly stable at the equilibrium position. However, for two-dimensional and high-energy cases, the system exhibits a nonlinear response, leading to large-scale chaos. The analysis indicates that, if the particle is confined on one side of the wormhole, the Poincare section will still retain Kolmogorov-Arnold-Moser (KAM) tori under extremely high-energy conditions, which is distinct from the chaos caused by the event horizon in the black hole. By studying another set of shape functions, the universality of this phase space structure is confirmed. This research clarifies the unique nonlinear dynamical mechanism of a traversable wormhole. It provides a new criterion, based on chaotic dynamics, for identifying black hole mimickers in strong-field astrophysical observations.

</details>


### [403] [A stepping stone toward detecting gravitational wave memory: a cumulative analysis with the full $(\ell=2, m=0)$ spherical harmonic using events from GWTC-4.0 and GWTC-5.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04909)
*Maria Rosselló-Sastre,Sascha Husa,Yumeng Xu,Jorge Valencia,Joan Llobera-Querol,Antoni Ramos-Buades*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过贝叶斯模型选择，在GWTC-4.0和部分GWTC-5.0引力波事件中检验(ℓ=2,m=0)球谐模的存在。使用包含振荡和位移记忆贡献的IMRPhenomTHM_20波形模型，发现GWTC-4.0目录累积对数贝叶斯因子log₁₀ℬ=1.38±0.79支持该模态存在。记忆成分信噪比达到SNR_memory=0.89⁺⁰·²⁹₋₀.₁₁。研究认为决定性证据需要更大事件目录（乐观估计N_events=166⁺⁸²₋₅₅）和更广泛的波形系统误差研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波记忆效应是广义相对论的重要预言，但尚未被直接探测。本文旨在通过分析(2,0)球谐模（包含振荡和记忆成分）在现有LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA数据中的存在证据，为最终探测引力波记忆奠定基础。

Method: 采用贝叶斯模型选择，比较包含(2,0)模的IMRPhenomTHM_20波形模型与不含该模的基线模型。对GWTC-4.0全部事件和GWTC-5.0选定事件进行参数估计和模型比较，计算对数贝叶斯因子和信噪比堆叠分析。对进动候选体GW241127_061008使用IMRphenomTPHM_20模型进行额外检验。

Result: GWTC-4.0目录累积log₁₀ℬ=1.38±0.79支持(2,0)模存在；堆叠记忆SNR达到0.89⁺⁰·²⁹₋₀.₁₁（约7.5个月O4a观测）；进动候选体GW241127_061008未发现额外证据；乐观估计需N_events=166⁺⁸²₋₅₅事件才能获得决定性统计证据。

Conclusion: 当前数据为(2,0)模存在提供了一定支持但非决定性证据。未来需要更大观测事件目录和更完善的波形系统误差研究来实现引力波记忆的决定性探测。

Abstract: We perform Bayesian model selection to test for the presence of the $(\ell=2,m=0)$ spherical harmonic mode in gravitational wave events that have previously been identified as binary black hole mergers. As our signal model we use the quasi-circular, non-precessing IMRPhenomTHM_20 waveform model, which includes the oscillatory and displacement memory contributions. Including the oscillatory component of the (2,0) mode increases the signal-to-noise ratio and evidence for this mode, compared to testing only for the presence of gravitational wave memory. Our analysis thus constitutes a natural stepping stone toward detecting gravitational wave memory. We perform our analysis for the binary black hole signals identified in the GWTC-4.0 catalog, and for selected GWTC-5.0 events. In our Bayesian model comparison we find a cumulative $\log_{10}\mathcal{B}=1.38\pm0.79$ in favor of the presence of the (2,0) mode for the GWTC-4.0 catalog.
